Atharva-veda-saṃhitā

;Saunaka-Rezension

Auf der Grundlage der āusgabe
Gli inni dell' Atharvaveda (Saunaka),
trasliterazione a cura di Chatia Orlandi, Pisa 1991
und unter Kollationierung der Edition Roth/Whitney
bearbeitet von Vladimir Petr und Petr Vavrousek.
Vorlaeufige Endredaktion: Jost Gippert
Frankfurt aṂ., 31.1.97

(1.1.1’) ye triṣaptāḥ pariyanti viśvā rūpāṇi bibhrataḥ |
(1.1.1.c) vācas patir balā teṣāṃ tanvo adya dadhātu me ‖1‖
(1.1.2’) punar ehi vacas pate devena manasā saha |
(1.1.2.c) vasoṣ pate ni ramaya mayy evāstu mayi śrutam ‖2‖
(1.1.3’) ihaivābhi vi tanūbhe ārtnī iva jyayā |
(1.1.3.c) vācas patir ni yachatu mayy evāstu mayi śrutam ‖3‖
(1.1.4’) upahūto vācas patir upāsmān vācaspatir hvayatām |
(1.1.4.c) saṃ śrutena gamemahi mā śrutena vi rādhiṣi ‖4‖

(1.2.1’) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ parjanyaṃ bhūridhāyasam |
(1.2.1.c) vidmo ṣv asya mātaraṃ pṛthivīṃ bhūrivarpasam ‖1‖
(1.2.2’) jyāke pari ṇo namāśmānaṃ tanvaṃ kṛdhi |
(1.2.2.c) vīḍur varīyo 'rātīr apa dveṣāṃsy ā kṛdhi ‖2‖
(1.2.3’) vṛkṣaṃ yad gāvaḥ pariṣasvajānā anusphuraṃ śaraṃ arcanty ṛbhum |
(1.2.3.c) śarum asmad yāvaya didyum indra ‖3‖
(1.2.4’) yathā dyāṃ ca pṛthivīṃ cāntas tiṣṭhati tejanam |
(1.2.4.c) evā rogaṃ cāsrāvaṃ cāntas tiṣṭhatu muñja it ‖4‖

(1.3.1’) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ parjanyaṃ śatavṛṣṇyaṃ |
(1.3.1.c) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti ‖1‖
(1.3.2’) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ mitraṃ śatavṛṣṇyaṃ |
(1.3.2.c) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti ‖2‖
(1.3.3’) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ varuṇaṃ śatavṛṣṇyaṃ |
(1.3.3.c) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti ‖3‖
(1.3.4’) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ candraṃ śatavṛṣṇyaṃ |
(1.3.4.c) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti ‖4‖
(1.3.5’) vidmā śarasya pitaraṃ sūryaṃ śatavṛṣṇyaṃ |
(1.3.5.c) tenā te tanve śaṃ karaṃ pṛthivyāṃ te niṣecanaṃ bahiṣ ṭe astu bāl iti ‖5‖
(1.3.6’) yad āntreṣu gavīnyor yad vastāv adhi saṃśritam |
(1.3.6.c) evā te mūtraṃ mucyatāṃ bahir bāl iti sarvakam ‖6‖
(1.3.7’) pra te bhinadmi mehanaṃ vartraṃ veśantyā iva |
(1.3.7.c) evā te mūtraṃ mucyatāṃ bahir bāl iti sarvakam ‖7‖
(1.3.8’) viṣitaṃ te vastibilaṃ samudrasyodadher iva |
(1.3.8.c) evā te mūtraṃ mucyatāṃ bahir bāl iti sarvakam ‖8‖
(1.3.9’) yatheṣukā parāpatad avasṛṣṭādhi dhanvanaḥ |
(1.3.9.c) evā te mūtraṃ mucyatāṃ bahir bāl iti sarvakam ‖9‖

(1.4.1’) ambayo yanty adhvabhir jāmayo adhvarīyatām |
(1.4.1.c) pṛñcatīr madhunā payaḥ ‖1‖
(1.4.2’) amūr yā upa sūrye yābhir vā sūryaḥ saha |
(1.4.2.c) tā no hinvantv adhvaram ‖2‖
(1.4.3’) apo devīr upa hvaye yatra gāvaḥ pibanti naḥ |
(1.4.3.c) sindhubhyaḥ kartvaṃ haviḥ ‖3‖
(1.4.4’) apsv antar amṛtam apsu bheṣajam |
(1.4.4.c) apām uta praśastibhir aśvā bhavatha vājino gāvo bhavatha vājinīḥ ‖4‖

(1.5.1’) āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvas tā na ūrje dadhātana |
(1.5.1.c) mahe raṇāya cakṣase ‖1‖
(1.5.2’) yo vaḥ śivatamo rasas tasya bhājayateha naḥ |
(1.5.2.c) uśatīr iva mātaraḥ ‖2‖
(1.5.3’) tasmā araṃ gamāma vo yasya kṣayāya jinvatha |
(1.5.3.c) āpo janayathā ca naḥ ‖3‖
(1.5.4’) īśānā vāryāṇāṃ kṣayantīś carṣaṇīnām |
(1.5.4.c) apo yācāmi bheṣajam ‖4‖

(1.6.1’) śaṃ no devīr abhiṣṭaya āpo bhavantu pītaye |
(1.6.1.c) śaṃ yor abhi sravantu naḥ ‖1‖
(1.6.2’) apsu me somo abravīd antar viśvāni bheṣajā |
(1.6.2.c) agniṃ ca viśvaśaṃbhuvam ‖2‖
(1.6.3’) āpaḥ pṛṇīta bheṣajaṃ varūthaṃ tanve mama |
(1.6.3.c) jyok ca sūryaṃ dṛśe ‖3‖
(1.6.4’) śaṃ na āpo dhanvanyāḥ śam u santv anūpyāḥ |
(1.6.4.c) śaṃ naḥ khanitrimā āpaḥ śam u yāḥ kumbha ābhṛtāḥ |
(1.6.4.e) śivā naḥ santu vārṣikīḥ ‖4‖

(1.7.1’) stuvānam agna ā vaha yātudhānaṃ kimīdinam |
(1.7.1.c) tvaṃ hi deva vandito hantā dasyor babhūvitha ‖1‖
(1.7.2’) ājyasya parameṣṭhin jātavedas tanūvaśin |
(1.7.2.c) agne taulasya prāśāna yātudhānān vi lāpaya ‖2‖
(1.7.3’) vi lapantu yātudhānā attriṇo ye kimīdinaḥ |
(1.7.3.c) athedam agne no havir indraś ca prati haryatam ‖3‖
(1.7.4’) agniḥ pūrva ā rabhatāṃ prendro nudatu bāhumān |
(1.7.4.c) bravītu sarvo yātumān ayam asmīty etya ‖4‖
(1.7.5’) paśyāma te vīryaṃ jātavedaḥ pra ṇo brūhi yātudhānān nṛcakṣaḥ |
(1.7.5.c) tvayā sarve paritaptāḥ purastāt ta ā yantu prabruvāṇā upedam ‖5‖
(1.7.6’) ā rabhasva jātavedo 'smākārthāya jajñiṣe |
(1.7.6.c) dūto no agne bhūtvā yātudhānān vi lāpaya ‖6‖
(1.7.7’) tvam agne yātudhānān upabaddhā;m ihā vaha |
(1.7.7.c) athaiṣām indro vajreṇāpi śīrṣāṇi vṛścatu ‖7‖

(1.8.1’) idaṃ havir yātudhānān nādī phenam iva ā vahat |
(1.8.1.c) ya idaṃ strī pumān akar iha sa stuvatāṃ janaḥ ‖1‖
(1.8.2’) ayaṃ stuvāna āgamad imaṃ sma prati haryata |
(1.8.2.c) bṛhaspate vaśe labdhvāgnīṣomā vi vidhyatam ‖2‖
(1.8.3’) yātudhānasya somapa jahi prajāṃ nayasva ca |
(1.8.3.c) ni stuvānasya pātaya param akṣy utāvaram ‖3‖
(1.8.4’) yatraiṣām agne janimāni vettha guhā satām attriṇāṃ jātavedaḥ |
(1.8.4.c) tāṃs tvaṃ brahmaṇā vāvṛdhāno jahy eṣāṃ śatatarham agne ‖4‖

(1.9.1’) asmin vasu vasavo dhārayantv indraḥ pūṣā varuṇo mitro agniḥ |
(1.9.1.c) imam ādityā uta viśve ca devā uttarasmin jyotiṣi dhārayantu ‖1‖
(1.9.2’) asya devāḥ pradiśi jyotir astu sūryo agnir uta vā hiraṇyam |
(1.9.2.c) sapatnā asmad adhare bhavantūttamaṃ nākam adhi rohayemam ‖2‖
(1.9.3’) yenendrāya samabharaḥ payāṃsy uttamena brahmaṇā jātavedaḥ |
(1.9.3.c) tena tvam agna iha vardhayemaṃ sajātānāṃ śraiṣṭhya ā dhehy enam ‖3‖
(1.9.4’) aiṣāṃ yajñam uta varco dade 'haṃ rāyas poṣam uta cittāny agne |
(1.9.4.c) sapatnā asmad adhare bhavantūttamaṃ nākam adhi rohayemam ‖4‖

(1.10.1’) ayaṃ devānām asuro vi rājati vaśā hi satyā varuṇasya rājñaḥ |
(1.10.1.c) tatas pari brahmaṇā śāśadāna ugrasya manyor ud imaṃ nayāmi ‖1‖
(1.10.2’) namas te rajan varuṇāstu manyave viśvaṃ hy ugra nicikeṣi drugdham |
(1.10.2.c) sahasram anyān pra suvāmi sākaṃ śataṃ jīvāti śaradas tavāyam ‖2‖
(1.10.3’) yad uvakthānṛtam jihvayā vṛjinaṃ bahu |
(1.10.3.c) rājñas tvā satyadharmaṇo muñcāmi varuṇād aham ‖3‖
(1.10.4’) muñcāmi tvā vaiśvānarād arṇavān mahatas pari |
(1.10.4.c) sajātān ugrehā vada brahma cāpa cikīhi naḥ ‖4‖

(1.11.1’) vaṣaṭ te pūṣann asmint sūtāv aryamā hotā kṛṇotu vedhāḥ |
(1.11.1.c) sisratāṃ nāry ṛtaprajātā vi parvāṇi jihatāṃ sūtavā u ‖1‖
(1.11.2’) catasro divaḥ pradiśaś catasro bhūmyā uta |
(1.11.2.c) devā garbhaṃ sam airayan taṃ vy ūrṇuvantu sūtave ‖2‖
(1.11.3’) sūṣā vy ūrṇotu vi yoniṃ hāpayāmasi |
(1.11.3.c) śrathayā sūṣaṇe tvam ava tvaṃ biṣkale sṛja ‖3‖
(1.11.4’) neva māṃse na pīvasi neva majjasv āhatam |
(1.11.4.c) avaitu pṛśni śevalaṃ śune jarāyv attave 'va jarāyu padyatām ‖4‖
(1.11.5’) vi te bhinadmi mehanaṃ vi yoniṃ vi gavīnike |
(1.11.5.c) vi mātaraṃ ca putraṃ ca vi kumāraṃ jarāyuṇāva jarāyu padyatām ‖5‖
(1.11.6’) yathā vāto yathā mano yathā patanti pakṣiṇaḥ |
(1.11.6.c) evā tvaṃ daśamāsya sākaṃ jarāyuṇā patāva jarāyu padyatām ‖6‖

(1.12.1’) jarāyujaḥ prathama usriyo vṛṣā vātābhrajā stanayann eti vṛṣṭyā |
(1.12.1.c) sa no mṛḍāti tanva ṛjugo rujan ya ekam ojas tredhā vicakrame ‖1‖
(1.12.2’) aṅgeaṅge śociṣā śiśriyāṇaṃ namasyantas tvā haviṣā vidhema |
(1.12.2.c) aṅkānt samaṅkān haviṣā vidhema yo agrabhīt parvāsyā grabhītā ‖2‖
(1.12.3’) muñca śīrṣaktyā uta kāsa enaṃ paruṣparur āviveśā yo asya |
(1.12.3.c) yo abhrajā vātajā yaś ca śuṣmo vanaspatīnt sacatāṃ parvatāṃś ca ‖3‖
(1.12.4’) śaṃ me parasmai gātrāya śam astv avarāya me |
(1.12.4.c) śaṃ me caturbhyo aṅgebhyaḥ śam astu tanve mama ‖4‖

(1.13.1’) namas te astu vidyute namas te stanayitnave |
(1.13.1.c) namas te astv aśmane yenā dūḍāśe asyasi ‖1‖
(1.13.2’) namas te pravato napād yatas tapaḥ samūhasi |
(1.13.2.c) mṛḍayā nas tanūbhyo mayas tokebhyas kṛdhi ‖2‖
(1.13.3’) pravato napān nama evāstu tubhyaṃ namas te hetaye tapuṣe ca kṛṇmaḥ |
(1.13.3.c) vidma te dhāma paramaṃ guhā yat samudre antar nihitāsi nābhiḥ ‖3‖
(1.13.4’) yāṃ tvā devā asṛjanta viśva iṣuṃ kṛṇvānā asanāya dhṛṣṇum |
(1.13.4.c) sā no mṛḍa vidathe gṛṇānā tasyai te namo astu devi ‖4‖

(1.14.1’) bhagam asyā varca ādiṣy adhi vṛkṣād iva srajam |
(1.14.1.c) mahābudhna iva parvato jyok pitṛṣv āstām ‖1‖
(1.14.2’) eṣā te rājan kanyā vadhūr ni dhūyatām yama |
(1.14.2.c) sā mātur badhyatāṃ gṛhe 'tho bhrātur atho pituḥ ‖2‖
(1.14.3’) eṣā te kulapā rājan tām u te pari dadmasi |
(1.14.3.c) jyok pitṛṣv āsātā ā śīrṣṇaḥ śamopyāt ‖3‖
(1.14.4’) asitasya te brahmaṇā kaśyapasya gayasya ca |
(1.14.4.c) antaḥkośam iva jāmayo 'pi nahyāmi te bhagam ‖4 ‖

(1.15.1’) saṃ saṃ sravantu sindhavaḥ saṃ vātāḥ saṃ patatriṇaḥ |
(1.15.1.c) imaṃ yajñaṃ pradivo me juṣantāṃ saṃsrāvyeṇa haviṣā juhomi ‖1‖
(1.15.2’) ihaiva havam ā yāta ma iha saṃsrāvaṇā utemaṃ vardhayatā giraḥ |
(1.15.2.c) ihaitu sarvo yaḥ paśur asmin tiṣṭhatu yā rayiḥ ‖2‖
(1.15.3’) ye nadīnāṃ saṃsravanty utsāsaḥ sadam akṣitāḥ |
(1.15.3.c) tebhir me sarvaiḥ saṃsrāvair dhanaṃ saṃ srāvayāmasi ‖3‖
(1.15.4’) ye sarpiṣaḥ saṃsravanti kṣīrasya codakasya ca
(1.15.4.b) tebhir me sarvaiḥ saṃsrāvair dhanaṃ saṃ srāvayāmasi ‖4‖

(1.16.1’) ye 'māvāsyāṃ rātrim udasthur vrājam attriṇaḥ |
(1.16.1.c) agnis turīyo yātuhā so asmabhyam adhi bravat ‖1‖
(1.16.2’) sīsāyādhy āha varuṇaḥ sīsāyāgnir upāvati |
(1.16.2.c) sīsaṃ ma indraḥ prāyachat tad aṅga yātucātanam ‖2‖
(1.16.3’) idaṃ viṣkandhaṃ sahata idaṃ bādhate attriṇaḥ |
(1.16.3.c) anena viśvā sasahe yā jātāni piśācyāḥ ‖3‖
(1.16.4’) yadi no gāṃ haṃsi yady aśvaṃ yadi pūruṣam |
(1.16.4.c) taṃ tvā sīsena vidhyāmo yathā no 'so avīrahā ‖4‖

(1.17.1’) amūr yā yanti yoṣito hirā lohitavāsasaḥ |
(1.17.1.c) abhrātara iva jāmayas tiṣṭhantu hatavarcasaḥ ‖1‖
(1.17.2’) tiṣṭhāvare tiṣṭha para uta tvaṃ tiṣṭha madhyame |
(1.17.2.c) kaniṣṭhikā ca tiṣṭhati tiṣṭhād id dhamanir mahī ‖2‖
(1.17.3’) śatasya dhamanīnāṃ sahasrasya hirāṇām |
(1.17.3.c) asthur in madhyamā imāḥ sākam antā araṃsata ‖3‖
(1.17.4’) pari vaḥ sikatāvatī dhanūr bṛhaty akramīt |
(1.17.4.c) tiṣṭhatelayatā su kam ‖4‖

(1.18.1’) nir lakṣmyaṃ lalāmyaṃ nir arātiṃ suvāmasi |
(1.18.1.c) atha yā bhadrā tāni naḥ prajāyā arātiṃ nayāmasi ‖1‖
(1.18.2’) nir araṇiṃ savitā sāviṣak pador nir hastayor varuṇo mitro aryamā |
(1.18.2.c) nir asmabhyam anumatī rarāṇā premāṃ devā asāviṣuḥ saubhagāya ‖2‖
(1.18.3’) yat ta ātmani tanvāṃ ghoram asti yad vā keśeṣu praticakṣaṇe vā |
(1.18.3.c) sarvaṃ tad vācāpa hanmo vayaṃ devas tvā savitā sūdayatu ‖3‖
(1.18.4’) riśyapadīṃ vṛṣadatīṃ goṣedhāṃ vidhamām uta |
(1.18.4.c) vilīḍhyaṃ lalāmyaṃ tā asman nāśayāmasi ‖4‖

(1.19.1’) mā no vidan vivyādhino mo abhivyādhino vidan |
(1.19.1.c) ārāc charavyā asmad viṣūcīr indra pātaya ‖1‖
(1.19.2’) viṣvañco asmac charavaḥ patantu ye astā ye cāsyāḥ |
(1.19.2.c) daivīr manuṣyesavo mamāmitrān vi vidhyata ‖2‖
(1.19.3’) yo naḥ svo yo araṇaḥ sajāta uta niṣṭyo yo asmā;m abhidāsati |
(1.19.3.c) rudraḥ śaravyayaitān mamāmitrān vi vidhyatu ‖3‖
(1.19.4’) yaḥ sapatno yo 'sapatno yaś ca dviṣan chapāti naḥ |
(1.19.4.c) devās taṃ sarve dhūrvantu brahma varma mamāntaram ‖4‖

(1.20.1’) adārasṛd bhavatu deva somāsmin yajñe maruto mṛḍatā naḥ |
(1.20.1.c) mā no vidad abhibhā mo aśastir mā no vidad vṛjinā dveṣyā yā ‖1‖
(1.20.2’) yo adya senyo vadho 'ghāyūnām udīrate |
(1.20.2.c) yuvaṃ taṃ mitrāvaruṇāv asmad yāvayataṃ pari ‖2‖
(1.20.3’) itaś ca yad amutaś ca yad vadhaṃ varuṇa yāvaya |
(1.20.3.c) vi mahac charma yacha varīyo yāvayā vadham ‖3‖
(1.20.4’) śāsa itthā mahā;m asy amitrasāho astṛtaḥ |
(1.20.4.c) na yasya hanyate sakhā na jīyate kadā cana ‖4‖

(1.21.1’) svastidā viśāṃ patir vṛtrahā vimṛdho vaśī |
(1.21.1.c) vṛṣendraḥ pura etu naḥ somapā abhayaṃkaraḥ ‖1‖
(1.21.2’) vi na indra mṛdho jahi nīcā yacha pṛtanyataḥ |
(1.21.2.c) adhamaṃ gamayā tamo yo asmā;m abhidāsati ‖2‖
(1.21.3’) vi rakṣo vi mṛdho jahi vi vṛtrasya hanū ruja |
(1.21.3.c) vi manyum indra vṛtrahann amitrasyābhidāsataḥ ‖3‖
(1.21.4’) apendra dviṣato mano 'pa jijyāsato vadham |
(1.21.4.c) vi mahac charma yacha varīyo yāvayā vadham ‖4‖

(1.22.1’) anu sūryam ud ayatāṃ hṛddyoto harimā ca te |
(1.22.1.c) go rohitasya varṇena tena tvā pari dadhmasi ‖1‖
(1.22.2’) pari tvā rohitair varṇair dīrghāyutvāya dadhmasi |
(1.22.2.c) yathāyam arapā asad atho aharito bhuvat ‖2‖
(1.22.3’) yā rohiṇīr devatyā gāvo yā uta rohiṇīḥ |
(1.22.3.c) rūpaṃrūpaṃ vayovayas tābhiṣ ṭvā pari dadhmasi ‖3‖
(1.22.4’) śukeṣu te harimāṇaṃ ropaṇākāsu dadhmasi |
(1.22.4.c) atho hāridraveṣu te harimāṇaṃ ni dadhmasi ‖4‖

(1.23.1’) naktaṃjātāsi oṣadhe rāme kṛṣṇe asikni ca |
(1.23.1.c) idaṃ rajani rajaya kilāsaṃ palitaṃ ca yat ‖1‖
(1.23.2’) kilāsaṃ ca palitaṃ ca nir ito nāśayā pṛṣat |
(1.23.2.c) ā tvā svo viśatāṃ varṇaḥ parā śuklāni pātaya ‖2‖
(1.23.3’) asitaṃ te pralayanam āsthānam asitaṃ tava |
(1.23.3.c) asiknī asy oṣadhe nir ito nāśayā pṛṣat ‖3‖
(1.23.4’) asthijasya kilāsasya tanūjasya ca yat tvaci |
(1.23.4.c) dūsyā kṛtasya brahmaṇā lakṣma śvetam anīnaśam ‖4‖

(1.24.1’) suparṇo jātaḥ prathamas tasya tvaṃ pittam āsitha |
(1.24.1.c) tad āsurī yudhā jitā rūpaṃ cakre vanaspatīn ‖1‖
(1.24.2’) āsurī cakre prathamedaṃ kilāsabheṣajam idaṃ kilāsanāśanam |
(1.24.2.c) anīnaśat kilāsaṃ sarūpām akarat tvacam ‖2‖
(1.24.3’) sarūpā nāma te mātā sarūpo nāma te pitā |
(1.24.3.c) sarūpakṛt tvam oṣadhe sā sarūpam idaṃ kṛdhi ‖3‖
(1.24.4’) śyāmā sarūpaṃkaraṇī pṛthivyā adhy udbhṛtā |
(1.24.4.c) idam ū ṣu pra sādhaya punā rūpāṇi kalpaya ‖4‖

(1.25.1’) yad agnir āpo adahat praviśya yatrākṛṇvan dharmadhṛto namāṃsi |
(1.25.1.c) tatra ta āhuḥ paramaṃ janitraṃ sa naḥ saṃvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman ‖1‖
(1.25.2’) yady arcir yadi vāsi śociḥ śakalyeṣi yadi vā te janitram |
(1.25.2.c) hrūḍur nāmāsi haritasya deva sa naḥ saṃvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman ‖2‖
(1.25.3’) yadi śoko yadi vābhiśoko yadi vā rājño varuṇasyāsi putraḥ |
(1.25.3.c) hrūḍur nāmāsi haritasya deva sa naḥ saṃvidvān pari vṛṅgdhi takman ‖3‖
(1.25.4’) namaḥ śītāya takmane namo rūrāya śociṣe kṛṇomi |
(1.25.4.c) yo anyedyur ubhayadyur abhyeti tṛtīyakāya namo astu takmane ‖4‖

(1.26.1’) āre 'sāv asmad astu hetir devāso asat |
(1.26.1.c) āre aśmā yam asyatha ‖1‖
(1.26.2’) sakhāsāv asmabhyam astu rātiḥ sakhendro bhagaḥ |
(1.26.2.c) savitā citrarādhāḥ ‖2‖
(1.26.3’) yūyam naḥ pravato napān marutaḥ sūryatvacasaḥ |
(1.26.3.c) śarma yachatha saprathāḥ ‖3‖
(1.26.4’) suṣūdata mṛḍata mṛḍayā nas tanūbhyo |
(1.26.4.c) mayas tokebhyas kṛdhi ‖4‖

(1.27.1’) amūḥ pāre pṛdākvas triṣaptā nirjarāyavaḥ |
(1.27.1.c) tāsām jarāyubhir vayam akṣyāv api vyayāmasy aghāyoḥ paripanthinaḥ ‖1‖
(1.27.2’) viṣūcy etu kṛntatī pinākam iva bibhratī |
(1.27.2.c) viṣvak punarbhuvā mano 'samṛddhā aghāyavaḥ ‖2‖
(1.27.3’) na bahavaḥ sam aśakan nārbhakā abhi dādhṛṣuḥ |
(1.27.3.c) veṇor adgā ivābhito 'samṛddhā aghāyavaḥ ‖3‖
(1.27.4’) pretaṃ pādau pra sphurataṃ vahataṃ pṛṇato gṛhān |
(1.27.4.c) indrāny etu prathamājītāmuṣitā puraḥ ‖4‖

(1.28.1’) upa prāgād devo agnī rakṣohāmīvacātanaḥ |
(1.28.1.c) dahann apa dvayāvino yātudhānān kimīdinaḥ ‖1‖
(1.28.2’) prati daha yātudhānān prati deva kimīdinaḥ |
(1.28.2.c) pratīcīḥ kṛṣṇavartane saṃ daha yātudhānyaḥ ‖2‖
(1.28.3’) yā śaśāpa śapanena yāghaṃ mūram ādadhe |
(1.28.3.c) yā rasasya haraṇāya jātam ārebhe tokam attu sā ‖3‖
(1.28.4’) putram attu yātudhānīḥ svasāram uta naptyam |
(1.28.4.c) adhā mitho vikeśyo vi ghnatāṃ yātudhānyo vi tṛhyantām arāyyaḥ ‖4‖

(1.29.1’) abhīvartena maṇinā yenendro abhivavṛdhe |
(1.29.1.c) tenāsmān brahmaṇas pate 'bhi rāṣṭrāya vardhaya ‖1‖
(1.29.2’) abhivṛtya sapatnān abhi yā no arātayaḥ |
(1.29.2.c) abhi pṛtanyantaṃ tiṣṭhābhi yo no durasyati ‖2‖
(1.29.3’) abhi tvā devaḥ savitābhi ṣomo avīvṛdhat |
(1.29.3.c) abhi tvā viśvā bhūtāny abhīvarto yathāsasi ‖3‖
(1.29.4’) abhīvarto abhibhavaḥ sapatnakṣayaṇo maṇiḥ |
(1.29.4.c) rāṣṭrāya mahyaṃ badhyatāṃ sapatnebhyaḥ parābhuve ‖4‖
(1.29.5’) ud asau sūryo agād ud idaṃ māmakaṃ vacaḥ |
(1.29.5.c) yathāhaṃ śatruho 'sāny asapatnaḥ sapatnahā ‖5‖
(1.29.6’) sapatnakṣayaṇo vṛṣābhiraṣṭro viṣāsahiḥ |
(1.29.6.c) yathāham eṣāṃ vīrāṇāṃ virājāni janasya ca ‖6‖

(1.30.1’) viśve devā vasavo rakṣatemam utādityā jāgṛta yūyam asmin |
(1.30.1.c) memaṃ sanābhir uta vānyanābhir memaṃ prāpat pauruṣeyo vadho yaḥ ‖1‖
(1.30.2’) ye vo devāḥ pitaro ye ca putrāḥ sacetaso me śṛṇutedam uktam |
(1.30.2.c) sarvebhyo vaḥ pari dadāmy etaṃ svasty enaṃ jarase vahātha ‖2‖
(1.30.3’) ye devā divi ṣṭha ye pṛthivyāṃ ye antarikṣa oṣadhīṣu paśuṣv apsv antaḥ |
(1.30.3.c) te kṛṇuta jarasam āyur asmai śatam anyān pari vṛṇaktu mṛtyūn ‖3‖
(1.30.4’) yeṣāṃ prayājā uta vānuyājā hutabhāgā ahutādaś ca devāḥ |
(1.30.4.c) yeṣāṃ vaḥ pañca pradiśo vibhaktās tān vo asmai satrasadaḥ kṛṇomi ‖4‖

(1.31.1’) āśānām āśāpālebhyaś caturbhyo amṛtebhyaḥ |
(1.31.1.c) idaṃ bhūtasyādhyakṣebhyo vidhema haviṣā vayam ‖1‖
(1.31.2’) ya āśānām āśāpālāś catvāra sthana devāḥ |
(1.31.2.c) te no nirṛtyāḥ pāśebhyo muñcatāṃhasoaṃhasaḥ ‖2‖
(1.31.3’) asrāmas tvā haviṣā yajāmy aśloṇas tvā ghṛtena juhomi |
(1.31.3.c) ya āśānām āśāpālas turīyo devaḥ sa naḥ subhūtam eha vakṣat ‖3‖
(1.31.4’) svasti mātra uta pitre no astu svasti gobhyo jagate puruṣebhyaḥ |
(1.31.4.c) viśvam subhūtam suvidatraṃ no astu jyog eva dṛśema sūryam ‖4‖

(1.32.1’) idaṃ janāso vidatha mahad brahma vadiṣyati |
(1.32.1.c) na tat pṛthivyāṃ no divi yena prāṇanti vīrudhaḥ ‖1‖
(1.32.2’) antarikṣa āsāṃ sthāma śrāntasadām iva |
(1.32.2.c) āsthānam asya bhūtasya viduṣ ṭad vedhaso na vā ‖2‖
(1.32.3’) yad rodasī rejamāne bhūmiś ca niratakṣatam |
(1.32.3.c) ārdraṃ tad adya sarvadā samudrasyeva śrotyāḥ ‖3‖
(1.32.4’) viśvam anyām abhīvāra tad anyasyām adhi śritam |
(1.32.4.c) dive ca viśvavedase pṛthivyai cākaraṃ namaḥ ‖4‖

(1.33.1’) hiraṇyavarṇāḥ śucayaḥ pāvakā yāsu jātaḥ savitā yāsv agniḥ |
(1.33.1.c) yā agniṃ garbhaṃ dadhire suvarṇās tā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu ‖1‖
(1.33.2’) yāsāṃ rājā varuṇo yāti madhye satyānṛte avapaśyan janānām |
(1.33.2.c) yā agniṃ garbhaṃ dadhire suvarṇās tā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu ‖2‖
(1.33.3’) yāsāṃ devā divi kṛṇvanti bhakṣaṃ yā antarikṣe bahudhā bhavanti |
(1.33.3.c) yā agniṃ garbhaṃ dadhire suvarṇās tā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu ‖3‖
(1.33.4’) śivena mā cakṣuṣā paśyatāpaḥ śivayā tanvopa spṛśata tvacaṃ me |
(1.33.4.c) ghṛtaścutaḥ śucayo yāḥ pāvakās tā na āpaḥ śaṃ syonā bhavantu ‖4‖

(1.34.1’) iyaṃ vīrun madhujātā madhunā tvā khanāmasi |
(1.34.1.c) madhor adhi prajātāsi sā no madhumatas kṛdhi ‖1‖
(1.34.2’) jihvāyā agre madhu me jihvāmūle madhūlakam |
(1.34.2.c) mamed aha kratāv aso mama cittam upāyasi ‖2‖
(1.34.3’) madhuman me nikramaṇaṃ madhuman me parāyaṇam |
(1.34.3.c) vācā vadāmi madhumad bhūyāsaṃ madhusaṃdṛśaḥ ‖3‖
(1.34.4’) madhor asmi madhutaro madughān madhumattaraḥ |
(1.34.4.c) mām it kila tvaṃ vanāḥ śākhāṃ madhumatīm iva ‖4‖
(1.34.5’) pari tvā paritatnunekṣuṇāgām avidviṣe |
(1.34.5.c) yathā māṃ kaminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ‖5‖

(1.35.1’) yad ābadhnan dākṣāyaṇā hiraṇyaṃ śatānīkāya sumanasyamānāḥ |
(1.35.1.c) tat te badnāmy āyuṣe varcase balāya dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ‖1‖
(1.35.2’) nainaṃ rakṣāṃsi na piśācāḥ sahante devānām ojaḥ prathamajam hy etat |
(1.35.2.c) yo bibharti dākṣāyaṇaṃ hiraṇyaṃ sa jīveṣu kṛṇute dīrgham āyuḥ ‖2‖
(1.35.3’) apāṃ tejo jyotir ojo balaṃ ca vanaspatīnām uta vīryāṇi |
(1.35.3.c) indra ivendriyāṇy adhi dhārayāmo asmin tad dakṣamāṇo bibharad dhiraṇyam ‖3‖
(1.35.4’) samānāṃ māsām ṛtubhiṣ ṭvā vayaṃ saṃvatsarasya payasā piparmi |
(1.35.4.c) indrāgnī viśve devās te 'nu manyantām ahṛṇīyamānāḥ ‖4‖

(2.1.1’) venas tat paśyat paramaṃ guhā yad yatra viśvaṃ bhavaty ekarūpam |
(2.1.1.c) idaṃ pṛśnir aduhaj jāyamānāḥ svarvido abhy anūṣata vrāḥ ‖1‖
(2.1.2’) pra tad voced amṛtasya vidvān gandharvo dhāma paramaṃ guhā yat |
(2.1.2.c) trīṇi padāni nihitā guhāsya yas tāni veda sa pituṣ pitāsat ‖2‖
(2.1.3’) sa naḥ pitā janitā sa uta bandhur dhāmāni veda bhuvanāni viśvā |
(2.1.3.c) yo devānāṃ nāmadha eka eva taṃ saṃpraśnaṃ bhuvanā yanti sarvā ‖3‖
(2.1.4’) pari dyāvāpṛthivī sadya āyam upātiṣṭhe prathamajām ṛtasya |
(2.1.4.c) vācam iva vaktari bhuvaneṣṭhā dhāsyur eṣa nanv eṣo agniḥ ‖4‖
(2.1.5’) pari viśvā bhuvanāny āyam ṛtasya tantuṃ vitataṃ dṛśe kam |
(2.1.5.c) yatra devā amṛtam ānaśānāḥ samāne yonāv adhy airayanta ‖5‖

(2.2.1’) divyo gandharvo bhuvanasya yas patir eka eva namasyo vikṣv īḍyaḥ |
(2.2.1.c) taṃ tvā yaumi brahmaṇā divya deva namas te astu divi te sadhastham ‖1‖
(2.2.2’) divi spṛṣṭo yajataḥ sūryatvag avayātā haraso daivyasya |
(2.2.2.c) mṛḍāt gandharvo bhuvanasya yas patir eka eva namasyaḥ suśevāḥ ‖2‖
(2.2.3’) anavadyābhiḥ sam u jagma ābhir apsarāsv api gandharva āsīt |
(2.2.3.c) samudra āsāṃ sadanaṃ ma āhur yataḥ sadya ā ca parā ca yanti ‖3‖
(2.2.4’) abhriye didyun nakṣatriye yā viśvāvasuṃ gandharvaṃ sacadhve |
(2.2.4.c) tābhyo vo devīr nama it kṛṇomi ‖4‖
(2.2.5’) yāḥ klandās tamiṣīcayo 'kṣakāmā manomuhaḥ |
(2.2.5.c) tābhyo gandharvabhyo 'psarābhyo 'karam namaḥ ‖5‖

(2.3.1’) ado yad avadhāvaty avatkam adhi parvatāt |
(2.3.1.c) tat te kṛṇomi bheṣajaṃ subheṣajaṃ yathāsasi ‖1‖
(2.3.2’) ād aṅgā kuvid aṅga śataṃ yā bheṣajāni te |
(2.3.2.c) teṣām asi tvam uttamam anāsrāvam arogaṇam ‖2‖
(2.3.3’) nīcaiḥ khananty asurā arusrāṇam idaṃ mahat |
(2.3.3.c) tad āsrāvasya bheṣajaṃ tad u rogam anīnaśat ‖3‖
(2.3.4’) upajīkā ud bharanti samudrād adhi bheṣajam |
(2.3.4.c) tad āsrāvasya bheṣajaṃ tad u rogam aśīśamat ‖4‖
(2.3.5’) arusrāṇam idaṃ mahat pṛthivyā adhy udbhṛtam |
(2.3.5.c) tad āsrāvasya bheṣajaṃ tad u rogam anīnaśat ‖5‖
(2.3.6’) śaṃ no bhavantv apa oṣadhayaḥ śivāḥ |
(2.3.6.c) indrasya vajro apa hantu rakṣasa ārād visṛṣṭā iṣavaḥ patantu rakṣasām ‖6‖

(2.4.1’) dīrghāyutvāya bṛhatṃ raṇāyāriṣyanto dakṣamāṇāḥ sadaiva |
(2.4.1.c) maṇiṃ viṣkandhadūṣaṇaṃ jaṅgiḍaṃ bibhṛmo vayam ‖1‖
(2.4.2’) jaṅgiḍo jambhād viśarād viṣkandhād abhiśocanāt |
(2.4.2.c) maṇiḥ sahasravīryaḥ pari ṇaḥ pātu viśvataḥ ‖2‖
(2.4.3’) ayaṃ viṣkandhaṃ sahate 'yaṃ bādhate attriṇaḥ |
(2.4.3.c) ayaṃ no viśvabheṣajo jaṅgiḍaḥ pātv aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(2.4.4’) devair dattena maṇinā jaṅgiḍena mayobhuvā |
(2.4.4.c) viṣkandhaṃ sarvā rakṣāṃsi vyāyāme sahāmahe ‖4‖
(2.4.5’) śaṇaś ca mā jaṅgiḍaś ca viṣkandhād abhi rakṣatām |
(2.4.5.c) araṇyād anya ābhṛtaḥ kṛṣyā anyo rasebhyaḥ ‖5‖
(2.4.6’) kṛtyādūṣir ayaṃ maṇir atho arātidūṣiḥ |
(2.4.6.c) atho sahasvān jaṅgiḍaḥ pra ṇa āyuṃṣi tāriṣat ‖6‖

(2.5.1’) indra juṣasva pra vahā yāhi śūra haribhyām |
(2.5.1.c) pibā sutasya mater iha madhoś cakānaś cārur madāya ‖1‖
(2.5.2’) indra jaṭharaṃ navyo na pṛṇasva madhor divo na |
(2.5.2.c) asya sutasya svar ṇopa tvā madāḥ suvāco aguḥ ‖2‖
(2.5.3’) indras turāṣāṇ mitro vṛtraṃ yo jaghāna yatīr na |
(2.5.3.c) bibheda valaṃ bhṛgur na sasahe śatrūn made somasya ‖3‖
(2.5.4’) ā tvā viśantu sutāsa indra pṛṇasva kukṣī viḍḍhi śakra dhiyehy ā naḥ |
(2.5.4.c) śrudhī havaṃ giro me juṣasvendra svayugbhir matsveha mahe raṇāya ‖4‖
(2.5.5’) indrasya nu pra vocaṃ vīryāṇi yāni cakāra prathamāni vajrī |
(2.5.5.c) ahann ahim anu apas tatarda pra vakṣaṇā abhinat parvatānām ‖5‖
(2.5.6’) ahann ahiṃ parvate śiśriyāṇaṃ tvaṣṭāsmai vajraṃ svaryaṃ tatakṣa |
(2.5.6.c) vāśrā iva dhenavaḥ syandamānā añjaḥ samudram ava jagmur āpaḥ ‖6‖
(2.5.7’) vṛṣāyamāṇo avṛṇīta somaṃ trikadrukeṣu apibat sutasya |
(2.5.7.c) ā sāyakaṃ maghavādatta vajram ahann enaṃ prathamajām ahīnām ‖7‖

(2.6.1’) samās tvāgna ṛtavo vardhayantu saṃvatsarā ṛṣayo yāni satyā |
(2.6.1.c) saṃ divyena dīdihi rocanena viśvā ā māhi pradiśaś catasraḥ ‖1‖
(2.6.2’) saṃ cedhyasvāgne pra ca vardhayemam uc ca tiṣṭha mahate saubhagāya |
(2.6.2.c) mā te riṣann upasattāro agne brahmāṇas te yaśasaḥ santu mānye ‖2‖
(2.6.3’) tvām agne vṛṇate brāhmaṇā ime śivo agne saṃvaraṇe bhavā naḥ |
(2.6.3.c) sapatnahāgne abhimātijid bhava sve gaye jāgṛhy aprayuchan ‖3‖
(2.6.4’) kṣatreṇāgne svena saṃ rabhasva mitreṇāgne mitradhā yatasva |
(2.6.4.c) sajātānāṃ madhyameṣṭhā rājñām agne vihavyo dīdihīha ‖4‖
(2.6.5’) ati niho ati sṛdho 'ty acittīr ati dviṣaḥ |
(2.6.5.c) viśvā hy agne duritā tara tvam athāsmabhyaṃ sahavīraṃ rayiṃ dāḥ ‖5‖

(2.7.1’) aghadviṣṭā devajātā vīruc chapathayopanī |
(2.7.1.c) āpo malam iva prāṇaikṣīt sarvān mac chapathā;m adhi ‖1‖
(2.7.2’) yaś ca sāpatnaḥ śapatho jāmyāḥ śapathaś ca yaḥ |
(2.7.2.c) brahmā yan manyutaḥ śapāt sarvaṃ tan no adhaspadam ‖2‖
(2.7.3’) divo mūlam avatataṃ pṛthivyā adhy uttatam |
(2.7.3.c) tena sahasrakāṇḍena pari ṇaḥ pāhi viśvataḥ ‖3‖
(2.7.4’) pari māṃ pari me prajāṃ pari ṇaḥ pāhi yad dhanam |
(2.7.4.c) arātir no mā tārīn mā nas tāriśur abhimātayaḥ ‖4‖
(2.7.5’) śaptāram etu śapatho yaḥ suhārt tena naḥ saha |
(2.7.5.c) cakṣurmantrasya durhārdaḥ pṛṣṭīr api śṛṇīmasi ‖5‖

(2.8.1’) ud agātāṃ bhagavatī vicṛtau nāma tārake |
(2.8.1.c) vi kṣetriyasya muñcatām adhamaṃ pāśam uttamam ‖1‖
(2.8.2’) apeyaṃ rātry uchatv apochantv abhikṛtvarīḥ |
(2.8.2.c) vīrut kṣetriyanāśany apa kṣetriyam uchatu ‖2‖
(2.8.3’) babhror arjunakāṇḍasya yavasya te palālyā tilasya tilapiñjyā |
(2.8.3.c) vīrut kṣetriyanāśany apa kṣetriyam uchatu ‖3‖
(2.8.4’) namas te lāṅgalebhyo nama īṣāyugebhyaḥ |
(2.8.4.c) vīrut kṣetriyanāśany apa kṣetriyam uchatu ‖4‖
(2.8.5’) namaḥ sanisrasākṣebhyo namaḥ saṃdeśyebhyaḥ |

(2.9.1’) daśavṛkṣa muñcemaṃ rakṣaso grāhyā adhi yainaṃ jagrāha parvasu |

(2.10.1’) kṣetriyāt tvā nirṛtyā jāmiśaṃsād druho muñcāmi varuṇasya pāśāt |

(2.12.1’) dyāvāpṛthivī urv antarikṣaṃ kṣetrasya patny urugāyo 'dbhutaḥ |

(2.27.3.c) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe ‖3‖
(2.27.4’) pāṭām indro vy āśnād asurebhya starītave |
(2.27.4.c) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe ‖4‖
(2.27.5’) tayāhaṃ śatrūnt sākṣe indraḥ sālāvṛkā;m iva |
(2.27.5.c) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe ‖5‖
(2.27.6’) rudra jalāṣabheṣaja nīlaśikhaṇḍa karmakṛt |
(2.27.6.c) prāśaṃ pratiprāśo jahy arasān kṛṇv oṣadhe ‖6‖
(2.27.7’) tasya prāśaṃ tvaṃ jahi yo na indrābhidāsati |
(2.27.7.c) adhi no brūhi śaktibhiḥ prāśi mām uttaraṃ kṛdhi ‖7‖

(2.28.1’) tubhyam eva jariman vardhatām ayam memam anye mṛtyavo hiṃsiṣuḥ śataṃ ye |
(2.28.1.c) māteva putraṃ pramanā upasthe mitra enaṃ mitriyāt pātv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(2.28.2’) mitra enaṃ varuṇo vā riśādā jarāmṛtyuṃ kṛṇutāṃ saṃvidānau |
(2.28.2.c) tad agnir hotā vayunāni vidvān viśvā devānāṃ janimā vivakti ‖2‖
(2.28.3’) tvam īśiṣe paśūnām pārthivānāṃ ye jātā uta vā ye janitrāḥ |
(2.28.3.c) memaṃ prāṇo hāsīn mo apāno memaṃ mitrā vadhiṣur mo amitrāḥ ‖3‖
(2.28.4’) dyauṣ ṭvā pitā pṛthivī mātā jarāmṛtyuṃ kṛṇutāṃ saṃvidāne |
(2.28.4.c) yathā jīvā aditer upasthe prāṇāpānābhyāṃ gupitaḥ śataṃ himāḥ ‖4‖
(2.28.5’) imam agne āyuṣe varcase naya priyaṃ reto varuṇa mitra rājan |
(2.28.5.c) mātevāsmā adite śarma yacha viśve devā jaradaṣṭir yathāsat ‖5‖

(2.29.1’) pārthivasya rase devā bhagasya tanvo bale |
(2.29.1.c) āyuṣyam asmā agniḥ sūryo varca ā dhād bṛhaspatiḥ ‖1‖
(2.29.2’) āyur asmai dhehi jātavedaḥ prajāṃ tvaṣṭar adhinidhehi asmai |
(2.29.2.c) rāyas poṣaṃ savitar ā suvāsmai śataṃ jīvāti śaradas tavāyam ‖2‖
(2.29.3’) āśīr ṇa ūrjam uta sauprajāstvaṃ dakṣaṃ dhattaṃ draviṇaṃ sacetasau |
(2.29.3.c) jayam kṣetrāṇi sahasāyam indra kṛṇvāno anyān adharānt sapatnān ‖3‖
(2.29.4’) indreṇa datto varuṇena śiṣṭo marudbhir ugraḥ prahito no āgan |
(2.29.4.c) eṣa vāṃ dyāvāpṛthivī upasthe mā kṣudhan mā tṛṣat ‖4‖
(2.29.5’) ūrjam asmā ūrjasvatī dhattaṃ payo asmai payasvatī dhattam |
(2.29.5.c) ūrjam asmai dyāvapṛthivī adhātāṃ viśve devā maruta ūrjam āpaḥ ‖5‖
(2.29.6’) śivābhiṣ ṭe hṛdayaṃ tarpayāmy anamīvo modiṣīṣṭhāḥ suvarcāḥ |
(2.29.6.c) savāsinau pibatāṃ mantham etam aśvino rūpaṃ paridhāya māyām ‖6‖
(2.29.7’) indra etāṃ sasṛje viddho agra ūrjāṃ svadhām ajarāṃ sā ta eṣā |
(2.29.7.c) tayā tvaṃ jīva śaradah suvarcā mā ta ā susrod bhiṣajas te akran ‖7‖

(2.30.1’) yathedaṃ bhūmyā adhi tṛṇaṃ vāto mathāyati |
(2.30.1.c) evā mathnāmi te mano yathā māṃ kāminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ‖1‖
(2.30.2’) saṃ cen nayātho aśvinā kāminā saṃ ca vakṣathaḥ |
(2.30.2.c) saṃ vāṃ bhagāso agmata saṃ cittāni sam u vratā ‖2‖
(2.30.3’) yat suparṇā vivakṣavo anamīvā vivakṣavaḥ |
(2.30.3.c) tatra me gachatād dhavaṃ śalya iva kulmalaṃ yathā ‖3‖
(2.30.4’) yad antaraṃ tad bāhyaṃ yad bāhyaṃ tad antaram |
(2.30.4.c) kanyānāṃ viśvarūpāṇāṃ mano gṛbhāyauṣadhe ‖4‖
(2.30.5’) eyam agan patikāmā janikāmo 'ham āgamam |
(2.30.5.c) aśvaḥ kanikradad yathā bhagenāhaṃ sahāgamam ‖5‖

(2.31.1’) indrasya yā mahī dṛṣat krimer viśvasya tarhaṇī |
(2.31.1.c) tayā pinaṣmi saṃ krimīn dṛṣadā khalvā;m iva ‖1‖
(2.31.2’) dṛṣṭam adṛṣṭam atṛham atho kurūrum atṛham |
(2.31.2.c) algaṇḍūnt sarvān chalunān krimīn vacasā jambhayāmasi ‖2‖
(2.31.3’) algaṇḍūn hanmi mahatā vadhena dūnā adūnā arasā abhūvan |
(2.31.3.c) śiṣṭān aśiṣṭān ni tirāmi vācā yathā krimīṇāṃ nakir uchiṣātai ‖3‖
(2.31.4’) anvāntryaṃ śīrṣaṇyam atho pārṣṭeyaṃ krimīn	|
(2.31.4.c) avaskavaṃ vyadhvaraṃ krimīn vacasā jambhayāmasi ‖4‖
(2.31.5’) ye krimayaḥ parvateśu vaneṣv oṣadhīṣu paśuṣv apsv antaḥ |
(2.31.5.c) ye asmākaṃ tanvam āviviśuḥ sarvaṃ tad dhanmi janima krimīṇām ‖5‖

(2.32.1’) udyann ādityaḥ krimīn hantu nimrocan hantu raśmibhiḥ |
(2.32.1.c) ye antaḥ krimayo gavi ‖1‖
(2.32.2’) viśvarūpaṃ caturakṣaṃ krimiṃ sāraṅgam arjunam |
(2.32.2.c) śṛṇāmy asya pṛṣṭīr api vṛścāmi yac chiraḥ ‖2‖
(2.32.3’) atrivad vaḥ krimayo hanmi kaṇvavaj jamadagnivat |
(2.32.3.c) agastyasya brahmaṇā saṃ pinaṣmy ahaṃ krimīn ‖3‖
(2.32.4’) hato rājā krimīṇām utaiṣāṃ sthapatir hataḥ |
(2.32.4.c) hato hatamātā krimir hatabhrātā hatasvasā ‖4‖
(2.32.5’) hatāso asya veśaso hatāsaḥ pariveśasaḥ |
(2.32.5.c) atho ye kṣullakā iva sarve te krimayo hatāḥ ‖5‖
(2.32.6’) pra te śṛṇāmi śṛṅge yābhyāṃ vitudāyasi |
(2.32.6.c) bhinādmi te kuṣumbhaṃ yas te viṣadhānaḥ ‖6‖

(2.33.1’) akṣībhyāṃ te nāsikābhyāṃ karṇābhyāṃ chubukād adhi |
(2.33.1.c) yakṣmaṃ śīrṣaṇyaṃ mastiṣkāj jihvāyā vi vṛhāmi te ‖1‖
(2.33.2’) grīvābhyas ta uṣṇihābhyaḥ kīkasābhyo anūkyāt |
(2.33.2.c) yakṣmaṃ doṣaṇyam aṃsābhyāṃ bāhubhyāṃ vi vṛhāmi te ‖2‖
(2.33.3’) hṛdayāt te pari klomno halīkṣṇāt pārśvābhyām |
(2.33.3.c) yakṣmaṃ matasnābhyāṃ plīhno yaknas te vi vṛhāmasi ‖3‖
(2.33.4’) āntrebhyas te gudābhyo vaniṣṭhor udarād adhi |
(2.33.4.c) yakṣmaṃ kukṣibhyām plāśer nābhyā vi vṛhāmi te ‖4‖
(2.33.5’) ūrubhyāṃ te aṣṭhīvadbhyāṃ pārṣṇibhyāṃ prapadābhyām |
(2.33.5.c) yakṣmaṃ bhasadyaṃ śroṇibhyāṃ bhāsadaṃ bhaṃsaso vi vṛhāmi te ‖5‖
(2.33.6’) asthibhyas te majjabhyaḥ snāvabhyo dhamanibhyaḥ |
(2.33.6.c) yakṣmam pāṇibhyām aṅgulibhyo nakhebhyo vi vṛhāmi te ‖6‖
(2.33.7’) aṅgeaṅge lomnilomni yas te parvaṇiparvaṇi |
(2.33.7.c) yakṣmaṃ tvacasyaṃ te vayaṃ kaśyapasya vībarheṇa viṣvañcaṃ vi vṛhāmasi ‖7‖

(2.34.1’) ya īśe paśupatiḥ paśūnām catuṣpadām uta yo dvipadām |
(2.34.1.c) niṣkrītaḥ sa yajñiyaṃ bhāgam etu rāyas poṣā yajamānaṃ sacantām ‖1‖
(2.34.2’) pramuñcanto bhuvanasya reto gātuṃ dhatta yajamānāya devāḥ |
(2.34.2.c) upākṛtaṃ śaśamānaṃ yad asthāt priyam devānām apy etu pāthaḥ ‖2‖
(2.34.3’) ye badhyamānam anu dīdhyānā anvaikṣanta manasā cakṣuṣā ca |
(2.34.3.c) agniṣ ṭān agre pra mumoktu devo viśvakarmā prajayā saṃrarāṇaḥ ‖3‖
(2.34.4’) ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpā virūpāḥ santo bahudhaikarūpāḥ |
(2.34.4.c) vāyuṣ ṭān agre pra mumoktu devaḥ prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṃrarāṇaḥ ‖4‖
(2.34.5’) prajānantaḥ prati gṛhṇantu pūrve prāṇam aṅgebhyaḥ pary ācarantam |
(2.34.5.c) divaṃ gacha prati tiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ svargaṃ yāhi pathibhir devayānaiḥ ‖5‖

(2.35.1’) ye bhakṣayanto na vasūny ānṛdhur yān agnayo anvatapyanta dhiṣṇyāḥ |
(2.35.1.c) yā teṣām avayā duriṣṭiḥ sviṣṭiṃ nas tāṃ kṛṇavad viśvakarmā ‖1‖
(2.35.2’) yajñapatim ṛṣayaḥ enasāhur nirbhaktaṃ prajā anutapyamānam |
(2.35.2.c) mathavyānt stokān apa yān rarādha saṃ naṣ ṭebhiḥ sṛjatu viśvakarmā ‖2‖
(2.35.3’) adānyānt somapān manyamāno yajñasya vidvānt samaye na dhīraḥ |
(2.35.3.c) yad enaś cakṛvān baddha eṣa taṃ viśvakarman pra muñcā svastaye ‖3‖
(2.35.4’) ghorā ṛṣayo namo astv ebhyaś cakṣur yad eṣāṃ manasaś ca satyam |
(2.35.4.c) bṛhaspataye mahiṣa dyumann namo viśvakarman namas te pāhy asmān ‖4‖
(2.35.5’) yajñasya cakṣuḥ prabhṛtir mukhaṃ ca vācā śrotreṇa manasā juhomi |
(2.35.5.c) imaṃ yajñaṃ vitataṃ viśvakarmaṇā devā yantu sumanasyamānāḥ ‖5‖

(2.36.1’) ā no agne sumatiṃ saṃbhalo gamed imāṃ kumārīṃ saha no bhagena |
(2.36.1.c) juṣṭā vareṣu samaneṣu valgur oṣaṃ patyā saubhagam astu asyai ‖1‖
(2.36.2’) somajuṣṭaṃ brahmajuṣṭam aryamnā saṃbhṛtaṃ bhagam |
(2.36.2.c) dhātur devasya satyena kṛṇomi pativedanam ‖2‖
(2.36.3’) iyam agne nārī patim videṣṭa somo hi rājā subhagāṃ kṛṇoti |
(2.36.3.c) suvānā putrān mahiṣī bhavāti gatvā patiṃ subhagā vi rājatu ‖3‖
(2.36.4’) yathākharo maghavaṃś cārur eṣa priyo mṛgāṇāṃ suṣadā babhūva |
(2.36.4.c) evā bhagasya juṣṭeyam astu nārī saṃpriyā patyāvirādhayantī ‖4‖
(2.36.5’) bhagasya nāvam ā roha pūrṇām anupadasvatīm |
(2.36.5.c) tayopapratāraya yo varaḥ pratikāmyaḥ ‖5‖
(2.36.6’) ā krandaya dhanapate varam āmanasaṃ kṛṇu |
(2.36.6.c) sarvaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛṇu yo varaḥ pratikāmyaḥ ‖6‖
(2.36.7’) idaṃ hiraṇyaṃ gulgulv ayam aukṣo atho bhagaḥ |
(2.36.7.c) ete patibhyas tvām aduḥ pratikāmāya vettave ‖7
(2.36.7.e) ā te nayatu savitā nayatu patir yaḥ pratikāmyaḥ |

(3.1.1’) agnir naḥ śatrūn praty etu vidvān pratidahann abhiśastim arātim |
(3.1.1.c) sa senāṃ mohayatu pareṣāṃ nirhastāṃś ca kṛṇavaj jātavedāḥ ‖1‖
(3.1.2’) yūyam ugrā maruta īdṛśe sthābhi preta mṛṇata sahadhvam |
(3.1.2.c) amīmṛṇan vasavo nāthitā ime agnir hy eṣāṃ dūtaḥ pratyetu vidvān ‖2‖
(3.1.3’) amitrasenāṃ maghavann asmān chatrūyatīm abhi |
(3.1.3.c) yuvaṃ tām indra vṛtrahann agniś ca dahataṃ prati ‖3‖
(3.1.4’) prasūta indra pravatā haribhyāṃ pra te vajraḥ pramṛṇann etu śatrūn |
(3.1.4.c) jahi pratīco anūcaḥ parāco viṣvak satyaṃ kṛṇuhi cittam eṣām ‖4‖
(3.1.5’) indra senāṃ mohayāmitrāṇām |
(3.1.5.c) agner vātasya dhrājyā tān viṣūco vi nāśaya ‖5‖
(3.1.6’) indraḥ senāṃ mohayatu maruto ghnantv ojasā |
(3.1.6.c) cakṣūṃsy agnir ā dattāṃ punar etu parājitā ‖6‖

(3.2.1’) agnir no dūtaḥ pratyetu vidvān pratidahann abhiśastim arātim |
(3.2.1.c) sa cittāni mohayatu pareṣāṃ nirhastāṃś ca kṛṇavaj jātavedāḥ ‖1‖
(3.2.2’) ayam agnir amūmuhad yāni cittāni vo hṛdi |
(3.2.2.c) vi vo dhamatv okasaḥ pra vo dhamatu sarvataḥ ‖2‖
(3.2.3’) indra cittāni mohayann arvāṅ ākūtyā cara |
(3.2.3.c) agner vātasya dhrājyā tān viṣūco vi nāśaya ‖3‖
(3.2.4’) vy ākūtaya eṣām itātho cittāni muhyata |
(3.2.4.c) atho yad adyaiṣāṃ hṛdi tad eṣāṃ pari nir jahi ‖4‖
(3.2.5’) amīṣāṃ cittāni pratimohayantī gṛhāṇāṅgāny apve parehi |
(3.2.5.c) abhi prehi nir daha hṛtsu śokair grāhyāmitrāṃs tamasā vidhya śatrūn ‖5‖
(3.2.6’) asau yā senā marutaḥ pareṣām asmān aity abhy ojasā spardhamānā |
(3.2.6.c) tām vidhyata tamasāpavratena yathaiṣām anyo anyaṃ na jānāt ‖6‖

(3.3.1’) acikradat svapā iha bhuvad agne vy acasva rodasī urūcī |
(3.3.1.c) yuñjantu tvā maruto viśvavedasa āmuṃ naya namasā rātahavyam ‖1‖
(3.3.2’) dūre cit santam aruṣāsa indram ā cyāvayantu sakhyāya vipram |
(3.3.2.c) yad gāyatrīṃ bṛhatīm arkam asmai sautrāmaṇyā dadhṛṣanta devāḥ ‖2‖
(3.3.3’) adbhyas tvā rāja varuṇo hvayatu somas tvā hvayatu parvatebhyaḥ |
(3.3.3.c) indras tvā hvayatu viḍbhya ābhyaḥ śyeno bhūtvā viśa ā patemāḥ ‖3‖ {4}
(3.3.4’) śyeno havyaṃ nayatv ā parasmād anyakṣetre aparuddhaṃ carantam |
(3.3.4.c) aśvinā panthāṃ kṛṇutāṃ sugaṃ ta imaṃ sajātā abhisaṃviśadhvam ‖4‖ {5}
(3.3.5’) hvayantu tvā pratijanāḥ prati mitrā avṛṣata |
(3.3.5.c) indrāgnī viśve devās te viśi kṣemam adīdharan ‖5‖ {6}
(3.3.6’) yas te havaṃ vivadat sajāto yaś ca niṣṭyaḥ |
(3.3.6.c) apāñcam indra taṃ kṛtvāthemam ihāva gamaya ‖6‖ {7}

(3.4.1’) ā tvā gan rāṣtraṃ saha varcasod ihi prāṅ viśāṃ patir ekarāṭ tvaṃ vi rāja |
(3.4.1.c) sarvās tvā rājan pradiśo hvayantūpasadyo namasyo bhaveha ‖1‖
(3.4.2’) tvāṃ viśo vṛṇatāṃ rājyāya tvām imāḥ pradiśaḥ pañca devīḥ |
(3.4.2.c) varṣman rāṣṭrasya kakudi śrayasva tato na ugro vi bhajā vasūni ‖2‖
(3.4.3’) acha tvā yantu havinaḥ sajātā agnir dūto ajiraḥ saṃ carātai |
(3.4.3.c) jāyāḥ putrāḥ sumanaso bhavantu bahuṃ baliṃ prati paśyāsā ugraḥ ‖3‖
(3.4.4’) aśvinā tvāgre mitrāvaruṇobhā viśve devā marutas tvā hvayantu |
(3.4.4.c) adhā mano vasudeyāya kṛṇuṣva tato na ugro vi bhajā vasūni ‖4‖
(3.4.5’) ā pra drava paramasyāḥ parāvataḥ śive te dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe stām |
(3.4.5.c) tad ayaṃ rājā varuṇas tathāha sa tvāyam ahvat sa upedam ehi ‖5‖
(3.4.6’) indrendra manuṣyāḥ parehi saṃ hy ajñāsthā varuṇaiḥ saṃvidānaḥ |
(3.4.6.c) sa tvāyam ahvat sve sadhasthe sa devān yakṣat sa u kalpayad viśaḥ ‖6‖
(3.4.7’) pathyā revatīr bahudhā virūpāḥ sarvāḥ saṃgatya varīyas te akran |
(3.4.7.c) tās tvā sarvāḥ saṃvidānā hvayantu daśamīm ugraḥ sumanā vaśeha ‖7‖

(3.5.1’) āyam agan parṇamaṇir balī balena pramṛṇant sapatnān |
(3.5.1.c) ojo devānāṃ paya oṣadhīnāṃ varcasā mā jinvantv aprayāvan ‖1‖
(3.5.2’) mayi kṣatraṃ parṇamaṇe mayi dhārayatād rayim |
(3.5.2.c) ahaṃ rāṣṭrasyābhīvarge nijo bhūyāsam uttamaḥ ‖2‖
(3.5.3’) yaṃ nidadhur vanaspatau guhyaṃ devāḥ priyaṃ maṇim |
(3.5.3.c) tam asmabhyaṃ sahāyuṣā devā dadatu bhartave ‖3‖
(3.5.4’) somasya parṇaḥ saha ugram āgann indreṇa datto varuṇena śiṣṭaḥ |
(3.5.4.c) taṃ priyāsaṃ bahu rocamāno dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ‖4‖
(3.5.5’) ā mārukṣat parṇamaṇir mahyā ariṣṭatātaye |
(3.5.5.c) yathāham uttaro 'sāny aryamṇa uta saṃvidaḥ ‖5‖
(3.5.6’) ye dhīvāno rathakārāḥ karmārā ye manīṣiṇaḥ |
(3.5.6.c) upastīn parṇa mahyaṃ tvaṃ sarvān kṛṇv abhito janān ‖6‖
(3.5.7’) ye rājāno rājakṛtaḥ sūtā grāmaṇyaś ca ye |
(3.5.7.c) upastīn parṇa mahyaṃ tvaṃ sarvān kṛṇv abhito janān ‖7‖
(3.5.8’) parṇo 'si tanūpānaḥ sayonir vīro vīreṇa mayā |
(3.5.8.c) saṃvatsarasya tejasā tena badhnāmi tvā maṇe ‖8‖

(3.6.1’) pumān puṃsaḥ parijāto 'śvatthaḥ khadirād adhi |
(3.6.1.c) sa hantu śatrūn māmakān yān ahaṃ dveṣmi ye ca mām ‖1‖
(3.6.2’) tān aśvattha niḥ śṛṇīhi śatrūn vaibādhadodhataḥ |
(3.6.2.c) indreṇa vṛtraghnā medī mitreṇa varuṇena ca ‖2‖
(3.6.3’) yathāśvattha nirabhano 'ntar mahaty arṇave |
(3.6.3.c) evā tānt sarvān nir bhaṅgdhi yān ahaṃ dveṣmi ye ca mām ‖3‖
(3.6.4’) yaḥ sahamānaś carasi sāsahāna iva ṛṣabhaḥ |
(3.6.4.c) tenāśvattha tvayā vayaṃ sapatnānt sahiṣīmahi ‖4‖
(3.6.5’) sinātv enān nirṛtir mṛtyoḥ pāśair amokyaiḥ |
(3.6.5.c) aśvattha śatrūn māmakān yān ahaṃ dveṣmi ye ca mām ‖5‖
(3.6.6’) yathāśvattha vānaspatyān ārohan kṛṇuṣe 'dharān |
(3.6.6.c) evā me śatror mūrdhānaṃ viṣvag bhinddhi sahasva ca ‖6‖
(3.6.7’) te 'dharāñcaḥ pra plavantāṃ chinnā naur iva bandhanāt |
(3.6.7.c) na vaibādhapraṇuttānāṃ punar asti nivartanam ‖7‖
(3.6.8’) praiṇān nude manasā pra cittenota brahmaṇā |
(3.6.8.c) praiṇān vṛkṣasya śākhayāśvatthasya nudāmahe ‖8‖

(3.7.1’) hariṇasya raghuṣyado 'dhi śīrṣaṇi bheṣajam |
(3.7.1.c) sa kṣetriyaṃ viṣāṇayā viṣūcīnam anīnaśat ‖1‖
(3.7.2’) anu tvā hariṇo vṛṣā padbhiś caturbhir akramīt |
(3.7.2.c) viṣāṇe vi ṣya guṣpitaṃ yad asya kṣetriyaṃ hṛdi ‖2‖
(3.7.3’) ado yad avarocate catuṣpakṣam iva chadiḥ |
(3.7.3.c) tenā te sarvaṃ kṣetriyam aṅgebhyo nāśayāmasi ‖3‖
(3.7.4’) amū ye divi subhage vicṛtau nāma tārake |
(3.7.4.c) vi kṣetriyasya muñcatām adhamaṃ pāśam uttamam ‖4‖
(3.7.5’) āpa id vā u bheṣajīr āpo amīvacātanīḥ |
(3.7.5.c) āpo viśvasya bheṣajīs tās tvā muñcantu kṣetriyāt ‖5‖
(3.7.6’) yad āsuteḥ kriyamānāyāḥ kṣetriyaṃ tvā vyānaśe |
(3.7.6.c) vedāhaṃ tasya bheṣajaṃ kṣetriyaṃ nāśayāmi tvat ‖6‖
(3.7.7’) apavāse nakṣatrāṇām apavāsa uṣasām uta |
(3.7.7.c) apāsmat sarvaṃ durbhūtam apa kṣetriyam uchatu ‖7‖

(3.8.1’) ā yātu mitra ṛtubhiḥ kalpamānaḥ saṃveśayan pṛthivīm usriyābhiḥ |
(3.8.1.c) athāsmabhyam varuṇo vāyur agnir bṛhad rāṣṭraṃ saṃveśyam dadhātu ‖1‖
(3.8.2’) dhātā rātiḥ savitedaṃ juśantām indras tvaṣṭā prati haryantu me vacaḥ |
(3.8.2.c) huve devīm aditiṃ śūraputrāṃ sajātānāṃ madhyameṣṭhā yathāsāni ‖2‖
(3.8.3’) huve somaṃ savitāraṃ namobhir viśvān ādityā;m aham uttaratve |
(3.8.3.c) ayam agnir dīdāyad dīrgham eva sajātair iddho 'pratibruvadbhiḥ ‖3‖
(3.8.4’) ihed asātha na paro gamātheryo gopāḥ puṣṭapatir va ājat |
(3.8.4.c) asmai kāmāyopa kāminīr viśve vo devā upasaṃyantu ‖4‖
(3.8.5’) saṃ vo manāṃsi saṃ vratā sam ākūtīr namāmasi |
(3.8.5.c) amī ye vivratā sthana tān vaḥ saṃ namayāmasi ‖5‖
(3.8.6’) ahaṃ gṛbhṇāmi manasā manāṃsi mama cittam anu cittebhir eta |
(3.8.6.c) mama vaśeṣu hṛdayāni vaḥ kṛṇomi mama yātam anuvartmāna eta ‖6‖

(3.9.1’) karśaphasya viśaphasya dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā |
(3.9.1.c) yathābhicakra devās tathāpa kṛṇutā punaḥ ‖1‖
(3.9.2’) aśreṣmāṇo adhārayan tathā tan manunā kṛtam |
(3.9.2.c) kṛṇomi vadhri viṣkandhaṃ muṣkābarho gavām iva ‖2‖
(3.9.3’) piśaṅge sūtre khṛgalaṃ tad ā badhnanti vedhasaḥ |
(3.9.3.c) śravasyuṃ śuṣmaṃ kābavaṃ vadhriṃ kṛṇvantu bandhuraḥ ‖3‖
(3.9.4’) yenā śravasyavaś caratha devā ivāsuramāyayā |
(3.9.4.c) śunāṃ kapir iva dūṣaṇo bandhurā kābavasya ca ‖4‖
(3.9.5’) duṣṭyai hi tvā bhatsyāmi dūṣayiṣyāmi kābavam |
(3.9.5.c) ud āśavo rathā iva śapathebhiḥ sariṣyatha ‖5‖
(3.9.6’) ekaśataṃ viṣkandhāni viṣṭhitā pṛthivīm anu |
(3.9.6.c) teṣāṃ tvām agre uj jaharur maṇiṃ viṣkandhadūṣaṇam ‖6‖

(3.10.1’) prathamā ha vy uvāsa sā dhenur abhavad yame |
(3.10.1.c) sā naḥ payasvatī duhām uttarāmuttarām samām ‖1‖
(3.10.2’) yāṃ devāḥ pratinandanti rātrim dhenum upāyatīm |
(3.10.2.c) saṃvatsarasya yā patnī sā no astu sumaṅgalī ‖2‖
(3.10.3’) saṃvatsarasya pratimāṃ yāṃ tvā rātry upāsmahe |
(3.10.3.c) sā na āyuṣmatīṃ prajāṃ rāyas poṣeṇa saṃ sṛja ‖3‖
(3.10.4’) iyam eva sā yā prathamā vyauchad āsv itarāsu carati praviṣṭā |
(3.10.4.c) mahānto asyāṃ mahimāno antar vadhūr jigāya navagaj janitrī ‖4‖
(3.10.5’) vānaspatyā grāvāṇo ghoṣam akrata haviṣ kṛṇvantaḥ parivatsarīṇam |
(3.10.5.c) ekāṣṭake suprajasaḥ suvīrā vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ‖5‖
(3.10.6’) iḍāyās padaṃ ghṛtavat sarīsṛpaṃ jātavedaḥ prati havyā gṛbhāya |
(3.10.6.c) ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpās teṣāṃ saptānāṃ mayi rantir astu ‖6‖
(3.10.7’) ā mā puṣṭe ca poṣe ca rātri devānāṃ sumatau syāma |
(3.10.7.c) pūrṇā darve parā pata supūrṇā punar ā pata |
(3.10.7.e) sarvān yajñānt saṃbhuñjatīṣam ūrjaṃ na ā bhara ‖7‖
(3.10.8’) āyam agant saṃvatsaraḥ patir ekāṣṭake tava |
(3.10.8.c) sā na āyuṣmatīṃ prajāṃ rāyas poṣeṇa saṃ sṛja ‖8‖
(3.10.9’) ṛtūn yaja ṛtupatīn ārtavān uta hāyanān |
(3.10.9.c) samāḥ saṃvatsarān māsān bhūtasya pataye yaje ‖9‖
(3.10.10’) ṛtubhyaṣ ṭvārtavebhyo mādbhyaḥ saṃvatsarebhyaḥ |
(3.10.10.c) dhātre vidhātre samṛdhe bhūtasya pataye yaje ‖10‖
(3.10.11’) iḍayā juhvato vayaṃ devān ghṛtavatā yaje |
(3.10.11.c) gṛhān alubhyato vayaṃ saṃ viśemopa gomataḥ ‖11‖
(3.10.12’) ekāṣṭakā tapasā tapyamānā jajāna garbhaṃ mahimānam indram |
(3.10.12.c) tena devā vy asahanta śatrūn hantā dasyūnām abhavac chacīpatiḥ ‖12‖
(3.10.13’) indraputre somaputre duhitāsi prajāpateḥ |
(3.10.13.c) kāmān asmākaṃ pūraya prati gṛhṇāhi no haviḥ ‖13‖

(3.11.1’) muñcāmi tvā haviṣā jīvanāya kam ajñātayakṣmād uta rājayakṣmāt |
(3.11.1.c) grāhir jagrāha yady etad enaṃ tasyā indrāgnī pra mumuktam enam ‖1‖
(3.11.2’) yadi kṣitāyur yadi vā pareto yadi mṛtyor antikaṃ eva |
(3.11.2.c) tam ā harāmi nirṛter upasthād aspārśam enaṃ śataśāradāya ‖2‖
(3.11.3’) sahasrākṣeṇa śatavīryeṇa śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣam enam |
(3.11.3.c) indro yathainaṃ śarado nayāty ati viśvasya duritasya pāram ‖3‖
(3.11.4’) śataṃ jīva śarado vardhamānaḥ śataṃ hemantān chatam u vasantān |
(3.11.4.c) śataṃ te indro agniḥ savitā bṛhaspatiḥ śatāyuṣā haviṣāhārṣam enam ‖4‖
(3.11.5’) pra viśataṃ prāṇāpānāv anaḍvāhāv iva vrajam |
(3.11.5.c) vy anye yantu mṛtyavo yān āhur itarān chatam ‖5‖
(3.11.6’) ihaiva staṃ prāṇāpānau māpa gātam ito yuvam |
(3.11.6.c) śarīram asyāṅgāni jarase vahataṃ punaḥ ‖6‖
(3.11.7’) jarāyai tvā pari dadāmi jarāyai ni dhuvāmi tvā |
(3.11.7.c) jarā tvā bhadrā neṣṭa vy anye yantu mṛtyavo yān āhur itarān chatam ‖7‖
(3.11.8’) abhi tvā jarimāhita gām ukṣaṇam iva rajjvā |
(3.11.8.c) yas tvā mṛtyur abhyadhatta jāyamānaṃ supāśayā |
(3.11.8.e) taṃ te satyasya hastābhyām ud amuñcad bṛhaspatiḥ ‖8‖

(3.12.1’) ihaiva dhruvāṃ ni minomi śālāṃ kṣeme tiṣṭhāti ghṛtam ukṣamāṇā |
(3.12.1.c) tāṃ tvā śāle sarvavīrāḥ suvīrā ariṣṭavīrā upa saṃ carema ‖1‖
(3.12.2’) ihaiva dhruvā prati tiṣṭha śāle 'śvāvatī gomatī sūnṛtāvatī |
(3.12.2.c) ūrjasvatī ghṛtavatī payasvaty uc chrayasva mahate saubhagāya ‖2‖
(3.12.3’) dharuṇy asi śāle bṛhachandāḥ pūtidhānyā |
(3.12.3.c) ā tvā vatso gamed ā kumāra ā dhenavaḥ sāyam āspandamānāḥ ‖3‖
(3.12.4’) imāṃ śālāṃ savitā vāyur indro bṛhaspatir ni minotu prajānan |
(3.12.4.c) ukṣantūdrā maruto ghṛtena bhago no rājā ni kṛṣiṃ tanotu ‖4‖
(3.12.5’) mānasya patni śaraṇā syonā devī devebhir nimitāsy agre |
(3.12.5.c) tṛṇaṃ vasānā sumanā asas tvam athāsmabhyaṃ sahavīraṃ rayiṃ dāḥ ‖5‖
(3.12.6’) ṛtena sthūṇām adhi roha vaṃśogro virājann apa vṛṅkṣva śatrūn |
(3.12.6.c) mā te riṣann upasattāro gṛhāṇāṃ śāle śataṃ jīvema śaradaḥ sarvavīrāḥ ‖6‖
(3.12.7’) emām kumāras taruṇa ā vatso jagatā saha |
(3.12.7.c) emām parisrutaḥ kumbha ā dadhnaḥ kalaśair aguḥ ‖7‖
(3.12.8’) pūrṇaṃ nāri pra bhara kumbham etaṃ ghṛtasya dhārām amṛtena saṃbhṛtām |
(3.12.8.c) imāṃ pātṝn amṛtena sam aṅgdhīṣṭāpūrtam abhi rakṣāty enām ‖8‖
(3.12.9’) imā āpaḥ pra bharāmy ayakṣmā yakṣmanāśanīḥ |
(3.12.9.c) gṛhān upa pra sīdāmy amṛtena sahāgninā ‖9‖

(3.13.1’) yad adaḥ saṃprayatīr ahāv anadatā hate |
(3.13.1.c) tasmād ā nadyo nāma stha tā vo nāmāni sindhavaḥ ‖1‖
(3.13.2’) yat preṣitā varuṇenāc chībham samavalgata |
(3.13.2.c) tad āpnod indro vo yatīs tasmād āpo anu ṣṭhana ‖2‖
(3.13.3’) apakāmaṃ syandamānā avīvarata vo hi kam |
(3.13.3.c) indro vaḥ śaktibhir devīs tasmād vār nāma vo hitam ‖3‖
(3.13.4’) ekaḥ vo devo 'py atiṣṭhat syandamānā yathāvaśam |
(3.13.4.c) ud āniṣur mahīr iti tasmād udakam ucyate ‖4‖
(3.13.5’) āpo bhadrā ghṛtam id āpa āsann agnīṣomau bibhraty āpa it tāḥ |
(3.13.5.c) tīvro raso madhupṛcām araṃgama ā mā prāṇena saha varcasā gamet ‖5‖
(3.13.6’) ād it paśyāmy uta vā śṛṇomy ā mā ghoṣo gachati vāṅ māsām |
(3.13.6.c) manye bhejāno amṛtasya tarhi hiraṇyavarṇā atṛpaṃ yadā vaḥ ‖6‖
(3.13.7’) idaṃ va āpo hṛdayam ayaṃ vatsa ṛtāvarīḥ |
(3.13.7.c) ihettham eta śakvarīr yatredaṃ veśayāmi vaḥ ‖7‖

(3.14.1’) saṃ vo goṣṭhena suṣadā saṃ rayyā saṃ subhūtyā |
(3.14.1.c) aharjātasya yan nāma tenā vaḥ saṃ sṛjāmasi ‖1‖
(3.14.2’) saṃ vaḥ sṛjatv aryamā saṃ pūṣā saṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |
(3.14.2.c) sam indro yo dhanaṃjayo mayi puṣyata yad vasu ‖2‖
(3.14.3’) saṃjagmānā abibhyuṣīr asmin goṣṭhe karīṣiṇīḥ |
(3.14.3.c) bibhratīḥ somyaṃ madhv anamīvā upetana ‖3‖
(3.14.4’) ihaiva gāva etaneho śakeva puṣyata |
(3.14.4.c) ihaivota pra jāyadhvaṃ mayi saṃjñānam astu vaḥ ‖4‖
(3.14.5’) śivo vo goṣṭho bhavatu śāriśākeva puṣyata |
(3.14.5.c) ihaivota pra jāyadhvaṃ mayā vaḥ saṃ sṛjāmasi ‖5‖
(3.14.6’) mayā gāvo gopatinā sacadhvam ayaṃ vo goṣṭha iha poṣayiṣṇuḥ |
(3.14.6.c) rāyas poṣeṇa bahulā bhavantīr jīvā jīvantīr upa vaḥ sadema ‖6‖

(3.15.1’) indram ahaṃ vaṇijaṃ codayāmi sa na aitu puraetā no astu |
(3.15.1.c) nudann arātiṃ paripanthinaṃ mṛgaṃ sa īśāno dhanadā astu mahyam ‖1‖
(3.15.2’) ye panthāno bahavo devayānā antarā dyāvāpṛthivī saṃcaranti |
(3.15.2.c) te mā juṣantāṃ payasā ghṛtena yathā krītvā dhanam āharāṇi ‖2‖
(3.15.3’) idhmenāgna ichamāno ghṛtena juhomi havyaṃ tarase balāya |
(3.15.3.c) yāvad īśe brahmaṇā vandamāna imāṃ dhiyaṃ śataseyāya devīm ‖3‖
(3.15.4’) imām agne śaraṇiṃ mīmṛṣo no yam adhvānam agāma dūram |
(3.15.4.c) śunaṃ no astu prapaṇo vikrayaś ca pratipaṇaḥ phalinaṃ mā kṛṇotu |
(3.15.4.e) idaṃ havyaṃ saṃvidānau juṣethāṃ śunaṃ no astu caritam utthitaṃ ca ‖4‖
(3.15.5’) yena dhanena prapaṇaṃ carāmi dhanena devā dhanam ichamānaḥ |
(3.15.5.c) tan me bhūyo bhavatu mā kanīyo 'gne sātaghno devān haviṣā ni ṣedha ‖5‖
(3.15.6’) yena dhanena prapaṇaṃ carāmi dhanena devā dhanam ichamānaḥ |
(3.15.6.c) tasmin ma indro rucim ā dadhātu prajāpatiḥ savitā somo agniḥ ‖6‖
(3.15.7’) upa tvā namasā vayaṃ hotar vaiśvānara stumaḥ |
(3.15.7.c) sa naḥ prajāsv ātmasu goṣu prāṇeṣu jāgṛhi ‖7‖
(3.15.8’) viśvāhā te sadam id bharemāśvāyeva tiṣṭhate jātavedaḥ |
(3.15.8.c) rāyas poṣeṇa sam iṣā madanto mā te agne prativeśā riṣāma ‖8‖

(3.16.1’) prātar agniṃ prātar indram havāmahe prātar mitrāvaruṇā prātar aśvinā |
(3.16.1.c) prātar bhagaṃ pūṣaṇaṃ brahmaṇas patiṃ prātaḥ somam uta rudraṃ havāmahe ‖1‖
(3.16.2’) prātarjitaṃ bhagam ugram havāmahe vayaṃ putram aditer yo vidhartā |
(3.16.2.c) ādhraś cid yaṃ manyamānas turaś cid rājā cid yaṃ bhagaṃ bhakṣīty āha ‖2‖
(3.16.3’) bhaga praṇetar bhaga satyarādho bhagemāṃ dhiyam ud avā dadan naḥ |
(3.16.3.c) bhaga pra ṇo janaya gobhir aśvair bhaga pra nṛbhir nṛvantaḥ syāma ‖3‖
(3.16.4’) utedānīṃ bhagavantaḥ syāmota prapitva uta madhye ahnām |
(3.16.4.c) utoditau maghavant sūryasya vayaṃ devānāṃ sumatau syāma ‖4‖
(3.16.5’) bhaga eva bhagavā;m astu devas tenā vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |
(3.16.5.c) taṃ tvā bhaga sarva ij johavīmi sa no bhaga puraetā bhaveha ‖5‖
(3.16.6’) sam adhvarāyoṣaso namanta dadhikrāveva śucaye padāya |
(3.16.6.c) arvācīnaṃ vasuvidaṃ bhagaṃ me ratham ivāśvā vājina ā vahantu ‖6‖
(3.16.7’) aśvāvatīr gomatīr na uṣāso vīravatīḥ sadam uchantu bhadrāḥ |
(3.16.7.c) ghṛtaṃ duhānā viśvataḥ prapītā yūyaṃ pāta svastibhiḥ sadā naḥ ‖7‖

(3.17.1’) sīrā yuñjanti kavayo yugā vi tanvate pṛthak |
(3.17.1.c) dhīrā deveṣu sumnayau ‖1‖
(3.17.2’) yunakta sīrā vi yugā tanota kṛte yonau vapateha bījam |
(3.17.2.c) virājaḥ śnuṣṭiḥ sabharā asan no nedīya it sṛṇyaḥ pakvam ā yavan ‖2‖
(3.17.3’) lāṅgalaṃ pavīravat suśīmaṃ somasatsaru |
(3.17.3.c) ud id vapatu gām aviṃ prasthāvad rathavāhanaṃ pībarīṃ ca prapharvyam ‖3‖
(3.17.4’) indraḥ sītāṃ ni gṛhṇātu tāṃ pūṣābhi rakṣatu |
(3.17.4.c) sā naḥ payasvatī duhām uttarāmuttarāṃ samām ‖4‖
(3.17.5’) śunaṃ suphālā vi tudantu bhūmiṃ śunaṃ kīnāśā anu yantu vāhān |
(3.17.5.c) śunāsīrā haviṣā tośamānā supippalā oṣadhīḥ kartam asmai ‖5‖
(3.17.6’) śunaṃ vāhāḥ śunaṃ naraḥ śunaṃ kṛṣatu lāṅgalam |
(3.17.6.c) śunaṃ varatrā badhyantāṃ śunam aṣṭrām ud iṅgaya ‖6‖
(3.17.7’) śunāsīreha sma me juṣethām |
(3.17.7.c) yad divi cakrathuḥ payas tenemām upa siñcatam ‖7‖
(3.17.8’) sīte vandāmahe tvārvācī subhage bhava |
(3.17.8.c) yathā naḥ sumanā aso yathā naḥ suphalā bhuvaḥ ‖8‖
(3.17.9’) ghṛtena sītā madhunā samaktā viśvair devair anumatā marudbhiḥ |
(3.17.9.c) sā naḥ sīte payasābhyāvavṛtsvorjasvatī ghṛtavat pinvamānā ‖9‖

(3.18.1’) imāṃ khanāmy oṣadhiṃ vīrudhāṃ balavattamām |
(3.18.1.c) yayā sapatnīṃ bādhate yayā saṃvindate patim ‖1‖
(3.18.2’) uttānaparṇe subhage devajūte sahasvati |
(3.18.2.c) sapatnīṃ me parā ṇuda patiṃ me kevalaṃ kṛdhi ‖2‖
(3.18.3’) nahi te nāma jagrāha no asmin ramase patau |
(3.18.3.c) parām eva parāvataṃ sapatnīṃ gamayāmasi ‖3‖
(3.18.4’) uttarāham uttara uttared uttarābhyaḥ |
(3.18.4.c) adhaḥ sapatnī yā mamādharā sādharābhyaḥ ‖4‖
(3.18.5’) aham asmi sahamānātho tvam asi sāsahiḥ |
(3.18.5.c) ubhe sahasvatī bhūtvā sapatnīṃ me sahāvahai ‖5‖
(3.18.6’) abhi te 'dhāṃ sahamānām upa te 'dhāṃ sahīyasīm |
(3.18.6.c) mām anu pra te mano vatsaṃ gaur iva dhāvatu pathā vār iva dhāvatu ‖6‖

(3.19.1’) saṃśitaṃ ma idaṃ brahma saṃśitaṃ vīryaṃ balam |
(3.19.1.c) saṃśitaṃ kṣatram ajaram astu jiṣṇur yeṣām asmi purohitaḥ ‖1‖
(3.19.2’) sam aham eṣāṃ rāṣṭraṃ syāmi sam ojo vīryaṃ balam |
(3.19.2.c) vṛścāmi śatrūṇāṃ bāhūn anena haviṣā aham ‖2‖
(3.19.3’) nīcaiḥ padyantām adhare bhavantu ye naḥ sūriṃ maghavānaṃ pṛtanyān |
(3.19.3.c) kṣiṇāmi brahmaṇāmitrān un nayāmi svān aham ‖3‖
(3.19.4’) tīkṣṇīyāṃsaḥ paraśor agnes tīkṣṇatarā uta |
(3.19.4.c) indrasya vajrāt tīkṣṇīyāṃso yeṣām asmi purohitaḥ ‖4‖
(3.19.5’) eṣām aham āyudhā saṃ syāmy eṣāṃ rāṣṭraṃ suvīraṃ vardhayāmi |
(3.19.5.c) eṣām kṣatram ajaram astu jiṣṇv eṣāṃ cittaṃ viśve 'vantu devāḥ ‖5‖
(3.19.6’) ud dharṣantāṃ maghavan vājināny ud vīrāṇāṃ jayatām etu ghoṣaḥ |
(3.19.6.c) pṛthag ghoṣā ululayaḥ ketumanta ud īratām |
(3.19.6.e) devā indrajyeṣṭhā maruto yantu senayā ‖6‖
(3.19.7’) pretā jayatā nara ugrā vaḥ santu bāhavaḥ |
(3.19.7.c) tīkṣṇeṣavo 'baladhanvano hatogrāyudhā abalān ugrabāhavaḥ ‖7‖
(3.19.8’) avasṛṣṭā parā pata śaravye brahmasaṃśite |
(3.19.8.c) jaya amitrān pra padyasva jahy eṣāṃ varaṃvaraṃ māmīṣāṃ moci kaś cana ‖8‖

(3.20.1’) ayaṃ te yonir ṛtviyo yato jāto arocathāḥ |
(3.20.1.c) taṃ jānann agna ā rohādhā no vardhaya rayim ‖1‖
(3.20.2’) agne achā vadeha naḥ pratyaṅ naḥ sumanā bhava |
(3.20.2.c) pra ṇo yacha viśāṃ pate dhanadā asi nas tvam ‖2‖
(3.20.3’) pra ṇo yachatv aryamā pra bhagaḥ pra bṛhaspatiḥ |
(3.20.3.c) pra devīḥ prota sūnṛtā rayiṃ devī dadhātu me ‖3‖
(3.20.4’) somaṃ rājānam avase 'gniṃ gīrbhir havāmahe |
(3.20.4.c) ādityam viṣṇum sūryaṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca bṛhaspatim ‖4‖
(3.20.5’) tvaṃ no agne agnibhir brahma yajñaṃ vardhaya |
(3.20.5.c) tvaṃ no deva dātave rayiṃ dānāya codaya ‖5‖
(3.20.6’) indravāyū ubhāv iha suhaveha havāmahe |
(3.20.6.c) yathā naḥ sarva ij janaḥ saṃgatyāṃ sumanā asad dānakāmaś ca no bhuvat ‖6‖
(3.20.7’) aryamaṇaṃ bṛhaspatim indraṃ dānāya codaya |
(3.20.7.c) vātaṃ viṣṇuṃ sarasvatīṃ savitāraṃ ca vājinam ‖7‖
(3.20.8’) vājasya nu prasave saṃ babhūvimemā ca viśvā bhuvanāni antaḥ |
(3.20.8.c) utāditsantaṃ dāpayatu prajānan rayiṃ ca naḥ sarvavīraṃ ni yacha ‖8‖
(3.20.9’) duhrām me pañca pradiṣo duhrām urvīr yathābalam |
(3.20.9.c) prāpeyaṃ sarvā ākūtīr manasā hṛdayena ca ‖9‖
(3.20.10’) gosaniṃ vācam udeyaṃ varcasā mābhyudihi |
(3.20.10.c) ā rundhāṃ sarvato vāyus tvaṣṭā poṣaṃ dadhātu me ‖10‖

(3.21.1’) ye agnayo apsv antar ye vṛtre ye puruṣe ye aśmasu |
(3.21.1.c) ya āviveśoṣadhīr yo vanaspatīṃs tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat ‖1‖
(3.21.2’) yaḥ some antar yo goṣv antar ya āviṣṭo vayaḥsu yo mṛgeṣu |
(3.21.2.c) ya āviveśa dvipado yas catuṣpadas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat ‖2‖
(3.21.3’) ya indreṇa sarathaṃ yāti devo vaiśvānara uta viśvadāvyaḥ |
(3.21.3.c) yaṃ johavīmi pṛtanāsu sāsahiṃ tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat ‖3‖
(3.21.4’) yo devo viśvād yam u kāmam āhur yaṃ dātāraṃ pratigṛhṇantam āhuḥ |
(3.21.4.c) yo dhīraḥ śakraḥ paribhūr adābhyas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat ‖4‖
(3.21.5’) yaṃ tvā hotāraṃ manasābhi saṃvidus trayodaśa bhauvanāḥ pañca mānavāḥ |
(3.21.5.c) varcodhase yaśase sūnṛtāvate tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat ‖5‖
(3.21.6’) ukṣānnāya vaśānnāya somapṛṣṭhāya vedhase |
(3.21.6.c) vaiśvānarajyeṣṭhebhyas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat ‖6‖
(3.21.7’) divaṃ pṛthivīm anv antarikṣam ye vidyutam anusaṃcaranti |
(3.21.7.c) ye dikṣv antar ye vāte antas tebhyo agnibhyo hutam astv etat ‖7‖
(3.21.8’) hiraṇyapāṇiṃ savitāram indraṃ bṛhaspatiṃ varuṇaṃ mitram agnim |
(3.21.8.c) viśvān devān aṅgiraso havāmahe imaṃ kravyādaṃ śamayantv agnim ‖8‖
(3.21.9’) śānto agniḥ kravyāc chāntaḥ puruṣareṣaṇaḥ |
(3.21.9.c) atho yo viśvadāvyas taṃ kravyādam aśīśamam ‖9‖
(3.21.10’) ye parvatāḥ somapṛṣṭhā āpa uttānaśīvarīḥ |
(3.21.10.c) vātaḥ parjanya ād agnis te kravyādam aśīśaman ‖10‖

(3.22.1’) hastivarcasaṃ prathatāṃ bṛhad yaśo adityā yat tanvaḥ saṃbabhūva |
(3.22.1.c) tat sarve sam adur mahyam etad viśve devā aditiḥ sajoṣāḥ ‖1‖
(3.22.2’) mitraś ca varuṇaś cendro rudraś ca cetatu |
(3.22.2.c) devāso viśvadhāyasas te māñjantu varcasā ‖2‖
(3.22.3’) yena hastī varcasā saṃbabhūva yena rājā manuṣyesv apsv antaḥ |
(3.22.3.c) yena devā devatām agra āyan tena mām adya varcasāgne varcasvinaṃ kṛṇu ‖3‖
(3.22.4’) yat te varco jātavedo bṛhad bhavaty āhuteḥ |
(3.22.4.c) yāvat sūryasya varca āsurasya ca hastinaḥ |
(3.22.4.e) tāvan me aśvinā varca ā dhattāṃ puṣkarasrajā ‖4‖
(3.22.5’) yāvac catasraḥ pradiśaś cakṣur yāvat samaśnute |
(3.22.5.c) tāvat samaitv indriyaṃ mayi tad dhastivarcasam ‖5‖
(3.22.6’) hastī mṛgāṇāṃ suṣadām atiṣṭhāvān babhūva hi |
(3.22.6.c) tasya bhagena varcasābhi ṣiñcāmi mām aham ‖6‖

(3.23.1’) yena vehad babhūvitha nāśayāmasi tat tvat |
(3.23.1.c) idaṃ tad anyatra tvad apa dūre ni dadhmasi ‖1‖
(3.23.2’) ā te yoniṃ garbha etu pumān bāṇa iveṣudhim |
(3.23.2.c) ā vīro 'tra jāyatāṃ putras te daśamāsyaḥ ‖2‖
(3.23.3’) pumāṃsaṃ putraṃ janaya taṃ pumān anu jāyatām |
(3.23.3.c) bhavāsi putrāṇāṃ mātā jātānāṃ janayāś ca yān ‖3‖
(3.23.4’) yāni badrāṇi bījāny ṛṣabhā janayanti ca |
(3.23.4.c) tais tvaṃ putraṃ vindasva sā prasūr dhenukā bhava ‖4‖
(3.23.5’) kṛṇomi te prājāpatyam ā yoniṃ garbha etu te |
(3.23.5.c) vindasva tvaṃ putraṃ nāri yas tubhyaṃ śam asac cham u tasmai tvam bhava ‖5‖
(3.23.6’) yāsām dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā samudro mūlaṃ vīrudhāṃ babhūva |
(3.23.6.c) tās tvā putravidyāya daivīḥ prāvantv oṣadhayaḥ ‖6‖

(3.24.1’) payasvatīr oṣadhayaḥ payasvan māmakaṃ vacaḥ |
(3.24.1.c) atho payasvatīnām ā bhare 'haṃ sahasraśaḥ ‖1‖
(3.24.2’) vedāhaṃ payasvantaṃ cakāra dhānyam bahu |
(3.24.2.c) saṃbhṛtvā nāma yo devas taṃ vayaṃ havāmahe yoyo ayajvano gṛhe ‖2‖
(3.24.3’) imā yāḥ pañca pradiśo mānavīḥ pañca kṛṣṭayaḥ |
(3.24.3.c) vṛṣṭe śāpaṃ nadīr iveha sphātiṃ samāvahān ‖3‖
(3.24.4’) ud utsaṃ śatadhāraṃ sahasradhāram akṣitam |
(3.24.4.c) evāsmākedaṃ dhānyaṃ sahasradhāram akṣitam ‖4‖
(3.24.5’) śatahasta samāhara sahasrahasta saṃ kira |
(3.24.5.c) kṛtasya kāryasya ceha sphātiṃ samāvaha ‖5‖
(3.24.6’) tisro mātrā gandharvāṇāṃ catasro gṛhapatnyāḥ |
(3.24.6.c) tāsāṃ yā sphātimattamā tayā tvābhi mṛśāmasi ‖6‖
(3.24.7’) upohaś ca samūhaś ca kṣattārau te prajāpate |
(3.24.7.c) tāv ihā vahatāṃ sphātiṃ bahuṃ bhūmānam akṣitam ‖7‖

(3.25.1’) uttudas tvot tudatu mā dhṛthāḥ śayane sve |
(3.25.1.c) iṣuḥ kāmasya yā bhīmā tayā vidhyāmi tvā hṛdi ‖1‖
(3.25.2’) ādhīparṇāṃ kāmaśalyām iṣuṃ saṃkalpakulmalām |
(3.25.2.c) tāṃ susaṃnatāṃ kṛtvā kāmo vidhyatu tvā hṛdi ‖2‖
(3.25.3’) yā plīhānaṃ śoṣayati kāmasyeṣuḥ susaṃnatā |
(3.25.3.c) prācīnapakṣā vyoṣā tayā vidhyāmi tvā hṛdi ‖3‖
(3.25.4’) śucā viddhā vyoṣayā śuṣkāsyābhi sarpa mā |
(3.25.4.c) mṛdur nimanyuḥ kevalī priyavādiny anuvratā ‖4‖
(3.25.5’) ājāmi tvājanyā pari mātur atho pituḥ |
(3.25.5.c) yathā mama kratāv aso mama cittam upāyasi ‖5‖
(3.25.6’) vy asyai mitrāvaruṇau hṛdaś cittāny asyatam |
(3.25.6.c) athainām akratuṃ kṛtvā mamaiva kṛṇutaṃ vaśe ‖6‖

(3.26.1’) ye 'syāṃ stha prācyāṃ diśi hetayo nāma devās teṣāṃ vo agnir iṣavaḥ |
(3.26.1.c) te no mṛḍata te no 'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā ‖1‖
(3.26.2’) ye 'syāṃ stha dakṣiṇāyāṃ diśy aviṣyavo nāma devās teṣāṃ vaḥ kāma iṣavaḥ |
(3.26.2.c) te no mṛḍata te no 'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā ‖2‖
(3.26.3’) ye 'syāṃ stha pratīcyāṃ diśi vairājā nāma devās teṣāṃ va āpa iṣavaḥ |
(3.26.3.c) te no mṛḍata te no 'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā ‖3‖
(3.26.4’) ye 'syāṃ sthodīcyāṃ diśi pravidhyanto nāma devās teṣāṃ vo vāta iṣavaḥ |
(3.26.4.c) te no mṛḍata te no 'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā ‖4‖
(3.26.5’) ye 'syāṃ stha dhruvāyāṃ diśi nilimpā nāma devās teṣāṃ va oṣadhīr iṣavaḥ |
(3.26.5.c) te no mṛḍata te no 'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā ‖5‖
(3.26.6’) ye 'syāṃ sthordhvāyāṃ diśy avasvanto nāma devās teṣāṃ vo bṛhaspatir iṣavaḥ |
(3.26.6.c) te no mṛḍata te no 'dhi brūta tebhyo vo namas tebhyo vaḥ svāhā ‖6‖

(3.27.1’) prācī dig agnir adhipatir asito rakṣitādityā iṣavaḥ |
(3.27.1.c) tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3.27.1.e) yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmas taṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ‖1‖
(3.27.2’) dakṣiṇā dig indro 'dhipatis tiraścirājī rakṣitā pitara iṣavaḥ |
(3.27.2.c) tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3.27.2.e) yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmas taṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ‖2‖
(3.27.3’) pratīcī dig varuṇo 'dhipatiḥ pṛdākū rakṣitānnam iṣavaḥ |
(3.27.3.c) tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3.27.3.e) yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmas taṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ‖3‖
(3.27.4’) udīcī dik somo 'dhipatiḥ svajo rakṣitāśanir iṣavaḥ |
(3.27.4.c) tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3.27.4.e) yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmas taṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ‖4‖
(3.27.5’) dhruvā dig viṣṇur adhipatiḥ kalmāṣagrīvo rakṣitā vīrudha iṣavaḥ |
(3.27.5.c) tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3.27.5.e) yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayam dviṣmas taṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ‖5‖
(3.27.6’) ūrdhvā dig bṛhaspatir adhipatiḥ śvitro rakṣitā varṣam iṣavaḥ |
(3.27.6.c) tebhyo namo 'dhipatibhyo namo rakṣitṛbhyo nama iṣubhyo nama ebhyo astu |
(3.27.6.e) yo 'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ vayaṃ dviṣmas taṃ vo jambhe dadhmaḥ ‖6‖

(3.28.1’) ekaikayaiṣā sṛṣṭyā saṃ babhūva yatra gā asṛjanta bhūtakṛto viśvarūpāḥ |
(3.28.1.c) yatra vijāyate yaminy apartuḥ sā paśūn kṣiṇāti riphatī ruśatī ‖1‖
(3.28.2’) eṣā paśūnt saṃ kṣiṇāti kravyād bhūtvā vyadvarī |
(3.28.2.c) utaināṃ brahmaṇe dadyāt tathā syonā śivā syāt ‖2‖
(3.28.3’) śivā bhava puruṣebhyo gobhyo aśvebhyaḥ śivā |
(3.28.3.c) śivāsmai sarvasmai kṣetrāya śivā na ihaidhi ‖3‖
(3.28.4’) iha puṣṭir iha rasa iha sahasrasātamā bhava |
(3.28.4.c) paśūn yamini poṣaya ‖4‖
(3.28.5’) yatrā suhārdaḥ sukṛto madanti vihāya rogaṃ tanvaḥ svāyāh |
(3.28.5.c) taṃ lokaṃ yaminy abhisaṃbabhūva sā no mā hiṃsīt puruṣān paśūṃś ca ‖5‖
(3.28.6’) yatrā suhārdāṃ sukṛtām agnihotrahutām yatra lokaḥ |
(3.28.6.c) taṃ lokaṃ yaminy abhisaṃbabhūva sā no mā hiṃsīt puruṣān paśūṃś ca ‖6‖

(3.29.1’) yad rājāno vibhajanta iṣṭāpūrtasya ṣoḍaśam yamasyāmī sabhāsadaḥ |
(3.29.1.c) avis tasmāt pra muñcati dattaḥ śitipāt svadhā ‖1‖
(3.29.2’) sarvān kāmān pūrayaty ābhavan prabhavan bhavan |
(3.29.2.c) ākūtipro 'vir dattaḥ śitipānn nopa dasyati ‖2‖
(3.29.3’) yo dadāti śitipādam aviṃ lokena saṃmitam |
(3.29.3.c) sa nākam abhyārohati yatra śulko na kriyate abalena balīyase ‖3‖
(3.29.4’) pañcāpūpaṃ śitipādam aviṃ lokena saṃmitam |
(3.29.4.c) pradātopa jīvati pitṝṇāṃ loke 'kṣitam ‖4‖
(3.29.5’) pañcāpūpaṃ śitipādam aviṃ lokena saṃmitam |
(3.29.5.c) pradātopa jīvati sūryāmāsayor akṣitam ‖5‖
(3.29.6’) ireva nopa dasyati samudra iva payo mahat |
(3.29.6.c) devau savāsināv iva śitipān nopa dasyati ‖6‖
(3.29.7’) ka idaṃ kasmā adāt kāmaḥ kāmāyādāt |
(3.29.7.c) kāmo dātā kāmaḥ pratigrahītā kāmaḥ samudram ā viveśa |
(3.29.7.e) kāmena tvā prati gṛhnāmi kāmaitat te ‖7‖
(3.29.8’) bhūmiṣ ṭvā prati gṛhṇātv antarikṣam idaṃ mahat |
(3.29.8.c) māhaṃ prāṇena mātmanā mā prajayā pratigṛhya vi rādhiṣi ‖8‖

(3.30.1’) sahṛdayaṃ sāṃmanasyam avidveṣaṃ kṛṇomi vaḥ |
(3.30.1.c) anyo anyam abhi haryata vatsaṃ jātam ivāghnyā ‖1‖
(3.30.2’) anuvrataḥ pituḥ putro mātrā bhavatu saṃmanāḥ |
(3.30.2.c) jāyā patye madhumatīṃ vācaṃ vadatu śantivām ‖2‖
(3.30.3’) mā bhrātā bhrātaraṃ dvikṣan mā svasāram uta svasā |
(3.30.3.c) samyañcaḥ savratā bhūtvā vācaṃ vadata bhadrayā ‖3‖
(3.30.4’) yena devā na viyanti no ca vidviṣate mithaḥ |
(3.30.4.c) tat kṛṇmo brahma vo gṛhe saṃjñānaṃ puruṣebhyaḥ ‖4‖
(3.30.5’) jyāyasvantaś cittino mā vi yauṣṭa saṃrādhayantaḥ sadhurāś carantaḥ |
(3.30.5.c) anyo anyasmai valgu vadanta eta sadhrīcīnān vaḥ saṃmanasas krṇomi ‖5‖
(3.30.6’) samānī prapā saha vo 'nnabhāgaḥ samāne yoktre saha vo yunajmi |
(3.30.6.c) samyañco 'gniṃ saparyatārā nābhim ivābhitaḥ ‖6‖
(3.30.7’) sadhrīcīnān vaḥ saṃmanasas kṛṇomy ekaśnuṣṭīnt saṃvananena sarvān |
(3.30.7.c) devā ivāmṛtaṃ rakṣamāṇāḥ sāyaṃprātaḥ saumanaso vo astu ‖7‖

(3.31.1’) vi devā jarasāvṛtan vi tvam agne arātyā |
(3.31.1.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖1‖
(3.31.2’) vy ārtyā pavamāno vi śakraḥ pāpakṛtyayā |
(3.31.2.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖2‖
(3.31.3’) vi grāmyāḥ paśava āraṇyair vy āpas tṛṣṇayāsaran |
(3.31.3.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖3‖
(3.31.4’) vī me dyāvāpṛthivī ito vi panthāno diśaṃdiśam |
(3.31.4.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖4‖
(3.31.5’) tvaṣṭā duhitre vahatuṃ yunaktītīdaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ vi yāti |
(3.31.5.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖5‖
(3.31.6’) agniḥ prāṇānt saṃ dadhāti candraḥ prāṇena saṃhitaḥ |
(3.31.6.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖6‖
(3.31.7’) prāṇena viśvatovīryaṃ devāḥ sūryaṃ sam airayan |
(3.31.7.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖7‖
(3.31.8’) āyuṣmatām āyuṣkṛtāṃ prāṇena jīva mā mṛthāḥ |
(3.31.8.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖8‖
(3.31.9’) prāṇena prāṇatāṃ prāṇehaiva bhava mā mṛthāḥ |
(3.31.9.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖9‖
(3.31.10’) ud āyuṣā sam āyuṣod oṣadhīnāṃ rasena |
(3.31.10.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖10‖
(3.31.11’) ā parjanyasya vṛṣṭyod asthāmāmṛtā vayam |
(3.31.11.c) vy ahaṃ sarveṇa pāpmanā vi yakṣmeṇa sam āyuṣā ‖11‖

(4.1.1’) brahma jajñānaṃ prathamaṃ purastād vi sīmataḥ suruco vena āvaḥ |
(4.1.1.c) sa budhnyā upamā asya viṣṭhāḥ sataś ca yonim asataś ca vi vaḥ ‖1‖
(4.1.2’) iyaṃ pitryā rāṣṭry etv agre prathamāya januṣe bhuvaneṣṭhāḥ |
(4.1.2.c) tasmā etaṃ surucaṃ hvāram ahyaṃ gharmaṃ śrīṇantu prathamāya dhāsyave ‖2‖
(4.1.3’) pra yo jajñe vidvān asya bandhur viśvā devānāṃ janimā vivakti |
(4.1.3.c) brahma brahmaṇa uj jabhāra madhyān nicair uccaiḥ svadhā abhi pra tasthau ‖3‖
(4.1.4’) sa hi vidaḥ sa pṛthivyā ṛtasthā mahī kṣemaṃ rodasī askabhāyat |
(4.1.4.c) mahān mahī askabhāyad vi jāto dyāṃ sadma pārthivaṃ ca rajaḥ ‖4‖
(4.1.5’) sa bhudhnyād āṣṭra januṣo 'bhy agram bṛhaspatir devatā tasya samrāṭ |
(4.1.5.c) ahar yac chukraṃ jyotiṣo janiṣṭātha dyumanto vi vasantu viprāḥ ‖5‖
(4.1.6’) nūnaṃ tad asya kāvyo hinoti maho devasya pūrvyasya dhāma |
(4.1.6.c) eṣa jajñe bahubhiḥ sākam itthā pūrve ardhe viṣite sasan nu ‖6‖
(4.1.7’) yo 'tharvāṇaṃ pitaraṃ devabandhuṃ bṛhaspatiṃ namasāva ca gachāt |
(4.1.7.c) tvaṃ viśveṣāṃ janitā yathāsaḥ kavir devo na dabhāyat svadhāvān ‖7‖

(4.2.1’) ya ātmadā baladā yasya viśva upāsate praśiṣaṃ yasya devāḥ |
(4.2.1.c) yo 'syeśe dvipado yaś catuṣpadaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ‖1‖
(4.2.2’) yaḥ prāṇato nimiṣato mahitvaiko rājā jagato babhūva |
(4.2.2.c) yasya chāyāmṛtaṃ yasya mṛtyuḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ‖2‖
(4.2.3’) yaṃ krandasī avataś caskabhāne bhiyasāne rodasī ahvayethām |
(4.2.3.c) yasyāsau panthā rajaso vimānaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ‖3‖
(4.2.4’) yasya dyaur urvī pṛthivī ca mahī yasyāda urv antarikṣam |
(4.2.4.c) yasyāsau sūro vitato mahitvā kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ‖4‖
(4.2.5’) yasya viśve himavanto mahitvā samudre yasya rasām id āhuḥ |
(4.2.5.c) imāś ca pradiśo yasya bāhū kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ‖5‖
(4.2.6’) āpo agre viśvam āvan garbhaṃ dadhānā amṛtā ṛtajñāḥ |
(4.2.6.c) yāsu devīṣv adhi deva āsīt kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ‖6‖
(4.2.7’) hiraṇyagarbhaḥ sam avartatāgre bhūtasya jātaḥ patir eka āsīt |
(4.2.7.c) sa dādhāra pṛthivīm uta dyāṃ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ‖7‖
(4.2.8’) āpo vatsaṃ janayantīr garbham agre sam airayan |
(4.2.8.c) tasyota jāyamānasyolba āsīd dhiraṇyayaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ‖8‖

(4.3.1’) ud itas trayo akraman vyāghraḥ puruṣo vṛkaḥ |
(4.3.1.c) hirug ghi yanti sindhavo hirug devo vanaspatir hiruṅ namantu śatravaḥ ‖1‖
(4.3.2’) pareṇaitu pathā vṛkaḥ parameṇota taskaraḥ |
(4.3.2.c) pareṇa datvatī rajjuḥ pareṇāghāyur arṣatu ‖2‖
(4.3.3’) akṣyau ca te mukhaṃ ca te vyāghra jambhayāmasi |
(4.3.3.c) āt sarvān viṃśatiṃ nakhān ‖3‖
(4.3.4’) vyāghraṃ datvatāṃ vayaṃ prathamaṃ jambhayāmasi |
(4.3.4.c) ād u ṣṭenam atho ahiṃ yātudhānam atho vṛkam ‖4‖
(4.3.5’) yo adya stena āyati sa saṃpiṣṭo apāyati |
(4.3.5.c) pathām apadhvaṃsenaitv indro vajreṇa hantu tam ‖5‖
(4.3.6’) mūrṇā mṛgasya dantā apiśīrṇā u pṛṣṭayaḥ |
(4.3.6.c) nimruk te godhā bhavatu nīcāyac chaśayur mṛgaḥ ‖6‖
(4.3.7’) yat saṃyamo na vi yamo vi yamo yan na saṃyamaḥ |
(4.3.7.c) indrajāh somajā ātharvaṇam asi vyāghrajambhanam ‖7‖

(4.4.1’) yāṃ tvā gandharvo akhanad varuṇāya mṛtabhraje |
(4.4.1.c) tāṃ tvā vayaṃ khanāmasy oṣadhiṃ śepaharṣaṇīm ‖1‖
(4.4.2’) ud uṣā ud u sūrya ud idaṃ māmakaṃ vacaḥ |
(4.4.2.c) ud ejatu prajāpatir vṛṣā śuṣmeṇa vājinā ‖2‖
(4.4.3’) yathā sma te virohato 'bhitaptam ivānati |
(4.4.3.c) tatas te śuṣmavattaram iyaṃ kṛṇotv oṣadhiḥ ‖3‖
(4.4.4’) uc chuṣmauṣadhīnāṃ sārā ṛṣabhāṇām |
(4.4.4.c) saṃ puṃsām indra vṛṣṇyam asmin dhehi tanūvaśin ‖4‖
(4.4.5’) apāṃ rasaḥ prathamajo 'tho vanaspatīnām |
(4.4.5.c) uta somasya bhrātāsy utārśam asi vṛṣṇyam ‖5‖
(4.4.6’) adyāgne adya savitar adya devi sarasvati |
(4.4.6.c) adyāsya brahmaṇas pate dhanur ivā tānayā pasaḥ ‖6‖
(4.4.7’) āhaṃ tanomi te paso adhi jyām iva dhanvani |
(4.4.7.c) kramasvarśa iva rohitam anavaglāyatā sadā ‖7‖
(4.4.8’) aśvasyāśvatarasyājasya petvasya ca |
(4.4.8.c) atha ṛṣabhasya ye vājās tān asmin dhehi tanūvaśin ‖8‖

(4.5.1’) sahasraśṛṅgo vṛṣabho yaḥ samudrād udācarat |
(4.5.1.c) tenā sahasyenā vayaṃ ni janānt svāpayāmasi ‖1‖
(4.5.2’) na bhūmiṃ vāto ati vāti nāti paśyati kaś cana |
(4.5.2.c) striyaś ca sarvāḥ svāpaya śunaś cendrasakhā caran ‖2‖
(4.5.3’) proṣṭheśayās talpeśayā nārīr yā vahyaśīvarīḥ |
(4.5.3.c) striyo yāḥ puṇyagandhayas tāḥ sarvāḥ svāpayāmasi ‖3‖
(4.5.4’) ejadejad ajagrabhaṃ cakṣuḥ prāṇam ajagrabham |
(4.5.4.c) aṅgāny ajagrabhaṃ sarvā rātrīṇām atiśarvare ‖4‖
(4.5.5’) ya āste yaś carati yaś ca tiṣṭhan vipaśyati |
(4.5.5.c) teṣāṃ saṃ dadhmo akṣīṇi yathedaṃ harmyaṃ tathā ‖5‖
(4.5.6’) svaptu mātā svaptu pitā svaptu śvā svaptu viśpatiḥ |
(4.5.6.c) svapantv asyai jñātayaḥ svaptv ayam abhito janaḥ ‖6‖
(4.5.7’) svapna svapnābhikaraṇena sarvaṃ ni svāpayā janam |
(4.5.7.c) otsūryam anyānt svāpayāvyuṣaṃ jāgṛtād aham indra ivāriṣṭo akṣitaḥ ‖7‖

(4.6.1’) brāhmaṇo jajñe prathamo daśaśīrṣo daśāsyaḥ |
(4.6.1.c) sa somaṃ prathamaḥ papau sa cakārārasaṃ viṣam ‖1‖
(4.6.2’) yāvatī dyāvāpṛthivī varimṇā yāvat sapta sindhavo vitaṣṭhire |
(4.6.2.c) vācaṃ viṣasya dūṣaṇīṃ tām ito nir avādiṣam ‖2‖
(4.6.3’) suparṇas tvā garutmān viṣa prathamam āvayat |
(4.6.3.c) nāmīmado nārūrupa utāsmā abhavaḥ pituḥ ‖3‖
(4.6.4’) yas ta āsyat pañcāṅgurir vakrāc cid adhi dhanvanaḥ |
(4.6.4.c) apaskambhasya śalyān nir avocam ahaṃ viṣam ‖4‖
(4.6.5’) śalyād viṣaṃ nir avocam prāñjanād uta parṇadheḥ |
(4.6.5.c) apāṣṭhāc chṛṅgāt kulmalān nir avocam aham viṣam ‖5‖
(4.6.6’) arasas ta iṣo śalyo 'tho te arasaṃ viṣam |
(4.6.6.c) utārasasya vṛkṣasya dhanuṣ ṭe arasārasam ‖6‖
(4.6.7’) ye apīṣan ye adihan ya āsyan ye avāsṛjan |
(4.6.7.c) sarve te vadhrayaḥ kṛtā vadhrir viṣagiriḥ kṛtaḥ ‖7‖
(4.6.8’) vadhrayas te khanitāro vadhris tvam asy oṣadhe |
(4.6.8.c) vadhriḥ sa parvato girir yato jātam idaṃ viṣam ‖8‖

(4.7.1’) vār idam vārayātai varaṇāvatyām adhi |
(4.7.1.c) tatrāmṛtasyāsiktaṃ tenā te vāraye viṣam ‖1‖
(4.7.2’) arasaṃ prācyaṃ viṣam arasaṃ yad udīcyam |
(4.7.2.c) athedam adharācyaṃ karambheṇa vi kalpate ‖2‖
(4.7.3’) karambhaṃ kṛtvā tiryaṃ pībaspākam udārathim |
(4.7.3.c) kṣudhā kila tvā duṣṭano jakṣivānt sa na rūrupaḥ ‖3‖
(4.7.4’) vi te madaṃ madāvati śaram iva pātayāmasi |
(4.7.4.c) pra tvā carum iva yeṣantaṃ vacasā sthāpayāmasi ‖4‖
(4.7.5’) pari grāmam ivācitaṃ vacasā sthāpayāmasi |
(4.7.5.c) tiṣṭhā vṛkṣa iva sthāmny abhrikhāte na rūrupaḥ ‖5‖
(4.7.6’) pavastais tvā pary akrīṇan dūrśebhir ajinair uta |
(4.7.6.c) prakrīr asi tvam oṣadhe 'bhrikhāte na rūrupaḥ ‖6‖
(4.7.7’) anāptā ye vaḥ prathamā yāni karmāṇi cakrire |
(4.7.7.c) vīrān no atra mā dabhan tad va etat puro dadhe ‖7‖

(4.8.1’) bhūto bhūteṣu paya ā dadhāti sa bhūtānām adhipatir babhūva |
(4.8.1.c) tasya mṛtyuś carati rājasūyaṃ sa rājā anu manyatām idaṃ ‖1‖
(4.8.2’) abhi prehi māpa vena ugraś cettā sapatnahā |
(4.8.2.c) ā tiṣṭha mitravardhana tubhyam devā adhi bruvan ‖2‖
(4.8.3’) ātiṣṭhantaṃ pari viśve abhūṣaṃ chriyaṃ vasānaś carati svarociḥ |
(4.8.3.c) mahat tad vṛṣṇo asurasya nāmā viśvarūpo amṛtāni tasthau ‖3‖
(4.8.4’) vyāghro adhi vaiyāghre vi kramasva diśo mahīḥ |
(4.8.4.c) viśas tvā sarvā vāñchantv āpo divyāḥ payasvatīḥ ‖4‖
(4.8.5’) yā āpo divyāḥ payasā madanty antarikṣa uta vā pṛthivyām |
(4.8.5.c) tāsāṃ tvā sarvāsām apām abhi ṣiñcāmi varcasā ‖5‖
(4.8.6’) abhi tvā varcasāsicann āpo divyāḥ payasvatīh |
(4.8.6.c) yathāso mitravardhanas tathā tvā savitā karat ‖6‖
(4.8.7’) enā vyāghraṃ pariṣasvajānāḥ siṃhaṃ hinvanti mahate saubhagāya |
(4.8.7.c) samudraṃ na subhuvas tasthivāṃsaṃ marmṛjyante dvīpinam apsv antaḥ ‖7‖

(4.9.1’) ehi jīvaṃ trāyamāṇaṃ parvatasyāsy akṣyam |
(4.9.1.c) viśvebhir devair dattaṃ paridhir jīvanāya kam ‖1‖
(4.9.2’) paripāṇaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ paripāṇaṃ gavāṃ asi |
(4.9.2.c) aśvānām arvatām paripāṇāya tasthiṣe ‖2‖
(4.9.3’) utāsi paripāṇam yātujambhanam āñjana |
(4.9.3.c) utāmṛtasya tvaṃ vetthātho asi jīvabhojanam atho haritabheṣajam ‖3‖
(4.9.4’) yasyāñjana prasarpasy aṅgamaṅgam paruṣparuḥ |
(4.9.4.c) tato yakṣmaṃ vi bādhasa ugro madhyamaśīr iva ‖4‖
(4.9.5’) nainaṃ prāpnoti śapatho na kṛtyā nābhiśocanam |
(4.9.5.c) nainaṃ viṣkandham aśnute yas tvā bibharty āñjana ‖5‖
(4.9.6’) asanmantrād duṣvapnyād duṣkṛtāc chamalād uta |
(4.9.6.c) durhārdaś cakṣuṣo ghorāt tasmān naḥ pāhy āñjana ‖6‖
(4.9.7’) idaṃ vidvān āñjana satyaṃ vakṣyāmi nānṛtam |
(4.9.7.c) saneyam aśvaṃ gām aham ātmānaṃ tava pūruṣa ‖7‖
(4.9.8’) trayo dāsā āñjanasya takmā balāsa ād ahiḥ |
(4.9.8.c) varṣiṣṭhaḥ parvatānāṃ trikakun nāma te pitā ‖8‖
(4.9.9’) yad āñjanaṃ traikakudam jātaṃ himavatas pari |
(4.9.9.c) yātūṃś ca sarvāñ jambhayat sarvāś ca yātudhānyaḥ ‖9‖
(4.9.10’) yadi vāsi traikakudaṃ yadi yāmunam ucyase |
(4.9.10.c) ubhe te bhadre nāmnī tābhyāṃ naḥ pāhy āñjana ‖10‖

(4.10.1’) vātāj jāto antarikṣād vidyuto jyotiṣas pari |
(4.10.1.c) sa no hiraṇyajāḥ śaṅkhaḥ kṛśanaḥ pātv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(4.10.2’) yo agrato rocanānāṃ samudrād adhi jajñiṣe |
(4.10.2.c) śaṅkhena hatvā rakṣāṃsy attriṇo vi ṣahāmahe ‖2‖
(4.10.3’) śaṅkhenāmīvām amatiṃ śaṅkhenota sadānvāḥ |
(4.10.3.c) śaṅkho no viśvabheṣajaḥ kṛśanaḥ pātv aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.10.4’) divi jātaḥ samudrajaḥ sindhutas pary ābhṛtaḥ |
(4.10.4.c) sa no hiraṇyajāḥ śaṅkha āyuṣprataraṇo maṇiḥ ‖4‖
(4.10.5’) samudrāj jāto maṇir vṛtrāj jāto divākaraḥ |
(4.10.5.c) so asmānt sarvataḥ pātu hetyā devāsurebhyaḥ ‖5‖
(4.10.6’) hiraṇyānām eko 'si somāt tvam adhi jajñiṣe |
(4.10.6.c) rathe tvam asi darśata iṣudhau rocanas tvaṃ pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat ‖6‖
(4.10.7’) devānām asthi kṛśanaṃ babhūva tad ātmanvac caraty apsv antaḥ |
(4.10.7.c) tat te badhnāmy āyuṣe varcase balāya dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya kārśanas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖7‖

(4.11.1’) anaḍvān dādhāra pṛthivīm uta dyām anaḍvān dādhārorv antarikṣam |
(4.11.1.c) anaḍvān dādhāra pradiśaḥ ṣaḍ urvīr anaḍvān viśvaṃ bhuvanam ā viveśa ‖1‖
(4.11.2’) anaḍvān indraḥ sa paśubhyo vi caṣṭe trayāṃ chakro vi mimīte adhvanaḥ |
(4.11.2.c) bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyad bhuvanā duhānaḥ sarvā devānām carati vratāni ‖2‖
(4.11.3’) indro jāto manuṣyeṣv antar gharmas taptaś carati śośucānaḥ |
(4.11.3.c) suprajāḥ sant sa udāre na sarṣad yo nāśnīyād anaḍuho vijānan ‖3‖
(4.11.4’) anaḍvān duhe sukṛtasya loka ainaṃ pyāyayati pavamānaḥ purastāt |
(4.11.4.c) parjanyo dhārā maruta ūdho asya yajñaḥ payo dakṣiṇā doho asya ‖4‖
(4.11.5’) yasya neśe yajñapatir na yajño nāsya dāteśe na pratigrahītā |
(4.11.5.c) yo viśvajid viśvabhṛd viśvakarmā gharmaṃ no brūta yatamaś catuṣpāt ‖5‖
(4.11.6’) yena devāḥ svar āruruhur hitvā śarīram amṛtasya nābhim |
(4.11.6.c) tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokaṃ gharmasya vratena tapasā yaśasyavaḥ ‖6‖
(4.11.7’) indro rūpeṇāgnir vahena prajāpatiḥ parameṣṭhī virāṭ |
(4.11.7.c) viśvānare akramata vaiśvānare akramatānaduhy akramata |
(4.11.7.e) so 'dṛṃhayata so 'dhārayata ‖7‖
(4.11.8’) madhyam etad anaḍuho yatraiṣa vaha āhitaḥ |
(4.11.8.c) etāvad asya prācīnaṃ yāvān pratyaṅ samāhitaḥ ‖8‖
(4.11.9’) yo vedānaduho dohān saptānupadasvataḥ |
(4.11.9.c) prajāṃ ca lokaṃ cāpnoti tathā saptaṛṣayo viduḥ ‖9‖
(4.11.10’) padbhiḥ sedim avakrāmann irāṃ jaṅghābhir utkhidan |
(4.11.10.c) srameṇānaḍvān kīlālaṃ kīnāśaś cābhi gachataḥ ‖10‖
(4.11.11’) dvādaśa vā etā rātrīr vratyā āhuḥ prajāpateḥ |
(4.11.11.c) tatropa brahma yo veda tad vā anaḍuho vratam ‖11‖
(4.11.12’) duhe sāyaṃ duhe prātar duhe madhyaṃdinaṃ pari |
(4.11.12.c) dohā ye asya saṃyanti tān vidmānupadasvataḥ ‖12‖

(4.12.1’) rohaṇy asi rohaṇy asthnaś chinnasya rohaṇī |
(4.12.1.c) rohayedam arundhati ‖1‖
(4.12.2’) yat te riṣṭaṃ yat te dyuttam asti peṣṭraṃ ta ātmani |
(4.12.2.c) dhātā tad bhadrayā punaḥ saṃ dadhat paruṣā paruḥ ‖2‖
(4.12.3’) saṃ te majjā majñā bhavatu sam u te paruṣā paruḥ |
(4.12.3.c) saṃ te māṃsasya visrastaṃ sam asthy api rohatu ‖3‖
(4.12.4’) majjā majñā saṃ dhīyatāṃ carmaṇā carma rohatu |
(4.12.4.c) asṛk te asthi rohatu māṃsaṃ māṃsena rohatu ‖4‖
(4.12.5’) loma lomnā saṃ kalpayā tvacā saṃ kalpayā tvacam |
(4.12.5.c) asṛk te asthi rohatu chinnaṃ saṃ dhehy oṣadhe ‖5‖
(4.12.6’) sa ut tiṣṭha prehi pra drava rathaḥ sucakraḥ |
(4.12.6.c) supaviḥ sunābhiḥ prati tiṣṭhordhvaḥ ‖6‖
(4.12.7’) yadi kartaṃ patitvā saṃśaśre yadi vāsmā prahṛto jaghāna |
(4.12.7.c) ṛbhū rathasyevāṅgāni saṃ dadhat paruṣā paruḥ ‖7‖

(4.13.1’) uta devā avahitaṃ devā un nayathā punaḥ |
(4.13.1.c) utāgaś cakruṣaṃ devā devā jīvayathā punaḥ ‖1‖
(4.13.2’) dvāv imau vātau vāta ā sindhor ā parāvataḥ |
(4.13.2.c) dakṣaṃ te anya āvātu vy anyo vātu yad rapaḥ ‖2‖
(4.13.3’) ā vāta vāhi bheṣajaṃ vi vāta vāhi yad rapaḥ |
(4.13.3.c) tvaṃ hi viśvabheṣaja devānāṃ dūta īyase ‖3‖
(4.13.4’) trāyantām imaṃ devās trāyantāṃ marutāṃ gaṇāḥ |
(4.13.4.c) trāyantāṃ viśvā bhūtāni yathāyam arapā asat ‖4‖
(4.13.5’) ā tvāgamaṃ śaṃtātibhir atho ariṣṭatātibhiḥ |
(4.13.5.c) dakṣaṃ ta ugram ābhāriṣaṃ parā yakṣmaṃ suvāmi te ‖5‖
(4.13.6’) ayaṃ me hasto bhagavān ayaṃ me bhagavattaraḥ |
(4.13.6.c) ayaṃ me viśvabheṣajo 'yaṃ śivābhimarśanaḥ ‖6‖
(4.13.7’) hastābhyāṃ daśaśākhābhyāṃ jihvā vācaḥ purogavī |
(4.13.7.c) anāmayitnubhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ tābhyāṃ tvābhi mṛśāmasi ‖7‖

(4.14.1’) ajo hy agner ajaniṣṭa śokāt so apaśyaj janitāram agre |
(4.14.1.c) tena devā devatām agrā āyan tena rohān ruruhur medhyāsaḥ ‖1‖
(4.14.2’) kramadhvam agninā nākam ukhyān hasteṣu bibhrataḥ |
(4.14.2.c) divas pṛṣṭhaṃ svar gatvā miśrā devebhir ādhvam ‖2‖
(4.14.3’) pṛṣṭhāt pṛthivyā aham antarikṣam āruham antarikṣād divam āruham |
(4.14.3.c) divo nākasya pṛṣṭhāt svar jyotir agām aham ‖3‖
(4.14.4’) svar yanto nāpekṣanta ā dyāṃ rohanti rodasī |
(4.14.4.c) yajñaṃ ye viśvatodhāraṃ suvidvāṃso vitenire ‖4‖
(4.14.5’) agne prehi prathamo devatānāṃ cakṣur devānām uta mānuṣānām |
(4.14.5.c) iyakṣamāṇā bhṛgubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ svar yantu yajamānāḥ svasti ‖5‖
(4.14.6’) ajam anajmi payasā ghṛtena divyaṃ suparnaṃ payasaṃ bṛhantam |
(4.14.6.c) tena geṣma sukṛtasya lokaṃ svar ārohanto abhi nākam uttamam ‖6‖
(4.14.7’) pañcaudanaṃ pañcabhir aṅgulibhir darvyoddhara pañcadhaitam odanam |
(4.14.7.c) prācyāṃ diśi śiro ajasya dhehi dakṣiṇāyāṃ diśi dakṣiṇaṃ dhehi pārśvam ‖7‖
(4.14.8’) pratīcyāṃ diśi bhasadam asya dhehy uttarasyāṃ diśy uttaraṃ dhehi pārśvam |
(4.14.8.c) ūrdhvāyāṃ diśy ajasyānūkaṃ dhehi diśi dhruvāyāṃ dhehi pājasyam antarikṣe madhyato madhyam asya ‖8‖
(4.14.9’) śṛtam ajaṃ śṛtayā prorṇuhi tvacā sarvair aṅgaiḥ saṃbhṛtaṃ viśvarūpam |
(4.14.9.c) sa ut tisṭheto abhi nākam uttamaṃ padbhiś caturbhiḥ prati tiṣṭha dikṣu ‖9‖

(4.15.1’) samutpatantu pradiśo nabhasvatīḥ sam abhrāṇi vātajūtāni yantu |
(4.15.1.c) mahaṛṣabhasya nadato nabhasvato vāśrā āpaḥ pṛthivīṃ tarpayantu ‖1‖
(4.15.2’) sam īkṣayantu taviṣāḥ sudānavo 'pāṃ rasā oṣadhībhiḥ sacantām |
(4.15.2.c) varṣasya sargā mahayantu bhūmiṃ pṛthag jāyantām oṣadhayo viśvarūpāḥ ‖2‖
(4.15.3’) sam īkṣayasva gāyato nabhāṃsy apām vegāsaḥ pṛthag ud vijantām |
(4.15.3.c) varṣasya sargā mahayantu bhūmiṃ pṛthag jāyantām vīrudho viśvarūpāḥ ‖3‖
(4.15.4’) gaṇās tvopa gāyantu mārutāḥ parjanya ghoṣiṇaḥ pṛthak |
(4.15.4.c) sargā varṣasya varṣato varṣantu pṛthivīm anu ‖4‖
(4.15.5’) ud īrayata marutaḥ samudratas tveṣo arko nabha ut pātayātha |
(4.15.5.c) mahaṛṣabhasya nadato nabhasvato vāśrā āpaḥ pṛthivīṃ tarpayantu ‖5‖
(4.15.6’) abhi kranda stanayārdayodadhiṃ bhūmiṃ parjanya payasā sam aṅdhi |
(4.15.6.c) tvayā sṛṣṭaṃ bahulam aitu varṣam āśāraiṣī kṛśagur etv astam ‖6‖
(4.15.7’) saṃ vo 'vantu sudānava utsā ajagarā uta |
(4.15.7.c) marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghā varṣantu pṛthivīm anu ‖7‖
(4.15.8’) āśāmāśāṃ vi dyotatāṃ vātā vāntu diśodiśaḥ |
(4.15.8.c) marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghāḥ saṃ yantu pṛthivīm anu ‖8‖
(4.15.9’) āpo vidyud abhraṃ varṣaṃ saṃ vo 'vantu sudānava utsā ajagarā uta |
(4.15.9.c) marudbhiḥ pracyutā meghāḥ prāvantu pṛthivīm anu ‖9‖
(4.15.10’) apām agnis tanūbhiḥ saṃvidāno ya oṣadhīnām adhipā babhūva |
(4.15.10.c) sa no varṣaṃ vanutāṃ jātavedāḥ prāṇaṃ prajābhyo amṛtaṃ divas pari ‖10‖
(4.15.11’) prajāpatiḥ salilād ā samudrād āpa īrayann udadhim ardayāti |
(4.15.11.c) pra pyāyatāṃ vṛṣṇo aśvasya reto 'rvān etena stanayitnunehi ‖11‖
(4.15.12’) apo niṣiñcann asuraḥ pitā naḥ śvasantu gargarā apāṃ varuṇāva nīcīr apaḥ sṛja |
(4.15.12.c) vadantu pṛśnibāhavo maṇḍūkā iriṇānu ‖12‖
(4.15.13’) saṃvatsaraṃ śaśayānā brāhmaṇā vratacāriṇaḥ |
(4.15.13.c) vācam parjanyajinvitāṃ pra maṇdūkā avādiṣuḥ ‖13‖
(4.15.14’) upapravada maṇḍūki varṣaṃ ā vada tāduri |
(4.15.14.c) madhye hradasya plavasva vigṛhya caturaḥ padaḥ ‖14‖
(4.15.15’) khaṇvakhā3i khaimakhā3i madhye taduri |
(4.15.15.c) varṣaṃ vanudhvaṃ pitaro marutāṃ mana ichata ‖15‖
(4.15.16’) mahāntaṃ kośam ud acābhi ṣiñca savidyutaṃ bhavatu vātu vātaḥ |
(4.15.16.c) tanvatāṃ yajñaṃ bahudhā visṛṣṭā ānandinīr oṣadhayo bhavantu ‖16‖

(4.16.1’) bṛhan eṣām adhiṣṭhātā antikād iva paśyati |
(4.16.1.c) ya stāyan manyate carant sarvaṃ devā idaṃ viduḥ ‖1‖
(4.16.2’) yas tiṣṭhati carati yaś ca vañcati yo nilāyaṃ carati yaḥ prataṅkam |
(4.16.2.c) dvau saṃniṣadya yan mantrayete rājā tad veda varuṇas tṛtīyaḥ ‖2‖
(4.16.3’) uteyaṃ bhūmir varuṇasya rājña utāsau dyaur bṛhatī dūreantā |
(4.16.3.c) uto samudrau varuṇasya kukṣī utāsminn alpa udake nilīnaḥ ‖3‖
(4.16.4’) uta yo dyām atisarpāt parastān na sa mucyātai varuṇasya rājñaḥ |
(4.16.4.c) diva spaśaḥ pra carantīdam asya sahasrākṣā ati paśyanti bhūmim ‖4‖
(4.16.5’) sarvaṃ tad rājā varuṇo vi caṣṭe yad antarā rodasī yat parastāt |
(4.16.5.c) saṃkhyātā asya nimiṣo janānām akṣān iva śvaghnī ni minoti tāni ‖5‖
(4.16.6’) ye te pāśā varuṇa saptasapta tredhā tiṣṭhanti viṣitā ruṣantaḥ |
(4.16.6.c) chinantu sarve anṛtaṃ vadantaṃ yaḥ satyavādy ati taṃ sṛjantu ‖6‖
(4.16.7’) śatena pāśair abhi dhehi varuṇainaṃ mā te mocy anṛtavāṅ nṛcakṣaḥ |
(4.16.7.c) āstāṃ jālma udaraṃ śraṃśayitvā kośa ivābandhaḥ parikṛtyamānaḥ ‖7‖
(4.16.8’) yaḥ samābhyo varuṇo yo vyābhyo yaḥ saṃdeśyo varuṇo yo videśyo |
(4.16.8.c) yo daivo varuṇo yaś ca mānuṣaḥ ‖8‖
