(4.16.9’) tais tvā sarvair abhi ṣyāmi pāśair asāv āmuṣyāyaṇāmuṣyāḥ putra |
(4.16.9.c) tān u te sarvān anusaṃdiśāmi ‖9‖

(4.17.1’) īśāṇāṃ tvā bheṣajānām ujjeṣa ā rabhāmahe |
(4.17.1.c) cakre sahasravīryam sarvasmā oṣadhe tvā ‖1‖
(4.17.2’) satyajitaṃ śapathayāvanīṃ sahamānāṃ punaḥsarām |
(4.17.2.c) sarvāḥ sam ahvy oṣadhīr ito naḥ pārayād iti ‖2‖
(4.17.3’) yā śaśāpa śapanena yāghaṃ mūram ādadhe |
(4.17.3.c) yā rasasya haraṇāya jātam ārebhe tokam attu sā ‖3‖
(4.17.4’) yāṃ te cakrur āme pātre yāṃ cakrur nīlalohite |
(4.17.4.c) āme māṃse kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakrus tayā kṛtyākṛto jahi ‖4‖
(4.17.5’) dauṣvapnyaṃ daurjīvityaṃ rakṣo abhvam arāyyaḥ |
(4.17.5.c) durṇāmnīḥ sarvā durvācas tā asman nāśayāmasi ‖5‖
(4.17.6’) kṣudhāmāraṃ tṛṣṇāmāram agotām anapatyatām |
(4.17.6.c) apāmārga tvayā vayaṃ sarvaṃ tad apa mṛjmahe ‖6‖
(4.17.7’) tṛṣṇāmāraṃ kṣudhāmāraṃ atho akṣaparājayam |
(4.17.7.c) apāmārga tvayā vayaṃ sarvaṃ tad apa mṛjmahe ‖7‖
(4.17.8’) apāmārga oṣadhīnāṃ sarvāsām eka id vaśī |
(4.17.8.c) tena te mṛjma āsthitam atha tvam agadaś cara ‖8‖

(4.18.1’) samaṃ jyotiḥ sūryeṇāhnā rātrī samāvatī |
(4.18.1.c) kṛṇomi satyam ūtaye 'rasāḥ santu kṛtvarīḥ ‖1‖
(4.18.2’) yo devāḥ kṛtyāṃ kṛtvā harād aviduṣo gṛham |
(4.18.2.c) vatso dhārur iva mātaraṃ taṃ pratyag upa padyatām ‖2‖
(4.18.3’) amā kṛtvā pāpmānaṃ yas tenānyaṃ jighāṃsati |
(4.18.3.c) aśmānas tasyāṃ dagdhāyāṃ bahulāḥ phaṭ karikrati ‖3‖
(4.18.4’) sahasradhāman viśikhān vigrīvāṃ chāyayā tvam |
(4.18.4.c) prati sma cakruṣe kṛtyāṃ priyāṃ priyāvate hara ‖4‖
(4.18.5’) anayāham oṣadhyā sarvāḥ kṛtyā adūduṣam |
(4.18.5.c) yāṃ kṣetre cakrur yāṃ goṣu yāṃ vā te puruṣeṣu ‖5‖
(4.18.6’) yaś cakāra na śaśāka kartuṃ śaśre pādam aṅgurim |
(4.18.6.c) cakāra bhadram asmabhyam ātmane tapanam tu saḥ ‖6‖
(4.18.7’) apāmārgo 'pa mārṣṭu kṣetriyaṃ śapathaś ca yaḥ |
(4.18.7.c) apāha yātudhānīr apa sarvā arāyyaḥ ‖7‖
(4.18.8’) apamṛjya yātudhānān apa sarvā arāyyaḥ |
(4.18.8.c) apāmārga tvayā vayaṃ sarvaṃ tad apa mṛjmahe ‖8‖

(4.19.1’) uto asy abandhukṛd uto asi nu jāmikṛt |
(4.19.1.c) uto kṛtyākṛtaḥ prajāṃ nadam ivā chindhi vārṣikam ‖1‖
(4.19.2’) brāhmaṇena paryuktāsi kaṇvena nārṣadena |
(4.19.2.c) senevaiṣi tviṣīmatī na tatra bhayam asti yatra prāpnoṣy oṣadhe ‖2‖
(4.19.3’) agram eṣy oṣadhīnāṃ jyotiṣevābhidīpayan |
(4.19.3.c) uta trātāsi pākasyātho hantāsi rakṣasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.19.4’) yad ado devā asurāṃs tvayāgre nirakurvata |
(4.19.4.c) tatas tvam adhy oṣadhe 'pāmārgo ajāyathāḥ ‖4‖
(4.19.5’) vibhindatī śataśākhā vibhindan nāma te pitā |
(4.19.5.c) pratyag vi bhindhi tvaṃ taṃ yo asmāṃ abhidāsati ‖5‖
(4.19.6’) asad bhūmyāḥ sam abhavat tad yām eti mahad vyacaḥ |
(4.19.6.c) tad vai tato vidhūpāyat pratyak kartāram ṛchatu ‖6‖
(4.19.7’) pratyaṅ hi saṃbabhūvitha pratīcīnaphalas tvam |
(4.19.7.c) sarvān mac chapathām adhi varīyo yāvayā vadham ‖7‖
(4.19.8’) śatena mā pari pāhi sahasreṇābhi rakṣā mā |
(4.19.8.c) indras te vīrudhāṃ pata ugra ojmānam ā dadhat ‖8‖

(4.20.1’) ā paśyati prati paśyati parā paśyati paśyati |
(4.20.1.c) divam antarikṣam ād bhūmiṃ sarvaṃ tad devi paśyati ‖1‖
(4.20.2’) tisro divas tisraḥ pṛthivīḥ ṣaṭ cemāḥ pradiśāḥ pṛthak |
(4.20.2.c) tvayāhaṃ sarvā bhūtāni paśyāni devy oṣadhe ‖2‖
(4.20.3’) divyasya suparṇasya tasya hāsi kanīnikā |
(4.20.3.c) sā bhūmim ā rurohitha vahyaṃ śrāntā vadhūr iva ‖3‖
(4.20.4’) tāṃ me sahasrākṣo devo dakṣiṇe hasta ā dadhat |
(4.20.4.c) tayāhaṃ sarvaṃ paśyāmi yaś ca śūdra utāryaḥ ‖4‖
(4.20.5’) āviṣ kṛṇuṣva rūpāni mātmānam apa gūhathāḥ |
(4.20.5.c) atho sahasracakṣo tvaṃ prati paśyāḥ kimīdinaḥ ‖5‖
(4.20.6’) darśaya mā yātudhānān darśaya yātudhānyaḥ |
(4.20.6.c) piśācānt sarvān darśayeti tvā rabha oṣadhe ‖6‖
(4.20.7’) kaśyapasya cakṣur asi śunyāś ca caturakṣyāḥ |
(4.20.7.c) vīdhre sūryam iva sarpantaṃ mā piśācaṃ tiras karaḥ ‖7‖
(4.20.8’) ud agrabhaṃ paripāṇād yātudhānaṃ kimīdinam |
(4.20.8.c) tenāhaṃ sarvaṃ paśyāmy uta śūdram utāryam ‖8‖
(4.20.9’) yo antarikṣeṇa patati divam yaś ca atisarpati |
(4.20.9.c) bhūmiṃ yo manyate nāthaṃ taṃ piśācam pra darśaya ‖9‖

(4.21.1’) ā gāvo agmann uta bhadram akrant sīdantu goṣṭhe raṇayantv asme |
(4.21.1.c) prajāvatīḥ pururūpā iha syur indrāya pūrvīr uṣaso duhānāḥ ‖1‖
(4.21.2’) indro yajvane gṛṇate ca śikṣata uped dadāti na svaṃ muṣāyati |
(4.21.2.c) bhūyobhūyo rayim id asya vardhayann abhinne khilye ni dadhāti devayum ‖2‖
(4.21.3’) na tā naśanti na dabhāti taskaro nāsām āmitro vyathir ā dadharṣati |
(4.21.3.c) devāṃś ca yābhir yajate dadāti ca jyog it tābhiḥ sacate gopatiḥ saha ‖3‖
(4.21.4’) na tā arvā reṇukakāṭo 'śnute na saṃskṛtatram upa yanti tā abhi |
(4.21.4.c) urugāyam abhayaṃ tasya tā anu gāvo martasya vi caranti yajvanaḥ ‖4‖
(4.21.5’) gāvo bhago gāva indro ma ichād gāva somasya prathamasya bhakṣaḥ |
(4.21.5.c) imā yā gāvaḥ sa janāsa indra ichāmi hṛdā manasā cid indram ‖5‖
(4.21.6’) yūyaṃ gāvo medayatha kṛśaṃ cid aśrīraṃ cit kṛṇuthā supratīkam |
(4.21.6.c) bhadraṃ gṛhaṃ kṛṇutha bhadravāco bṛhad vo vaya ucyate sabhāsu ‖6‖
(4.21.7’) prajāvatīḥ sūyavase ruśantīḥ śuddhā apaḥ suprapāṇe pibantīḥ |
(4.21.7.c) mā va stena īśata māghaśaṃsaḥ pari vo rudrasya hetir vṛṇaktu ‖7‖

(4.22.1’) imam indra vardhaya kṣatriyaṃ me imaṃ viśām ekavṛṣaṃ kṛṇu tvam |
(4.22.1.c) nir amitrān akṣṇuhy asya sarvāṃs tān randhayāsmā ahamuttareṣu ‖1‖
(4.22.2’) emaṃ bhaja grāme aśveṣu goṣu niṣ ṭaṃ bhaja yo amitro asya |
(4.22.2.c) varṣma kṣatrāṇām ayam astu rājendra śatruṃ randhaya sarvam asmai ‖2‖
(4.22.3’) ayam astu dhanapatir dhanānām ayaṃ viśāṃ viśpatir astu rājā |
(4.22.3.c) asminn indra mahi varcāṃsi dhehy avarcasaṃ kṛṇuhi śatrum asya ‖3‖
(4.22.4’) asmai dyāvāpṛthivī bhūri vāmaṃ duhāthāṃ gharmadughe iva dhenū |
(4.22.4.c) ayaṃ rājā priya indrasya bhūyāt priyo gavām oṣadhīnāṃ paśūnām ‖4‖
(4.22.5’) yunajmi ta uttarāvantam indraṃ yena jayanti na parājayante |
(4.22.5.c) yas tvā karad ekavṛṣaṃ janānām uta rājñām uttamaṃ mānavānām ‖5‖
(4.22.6’) uttaras tvam adhare te sapatnā ye ke ca rājan pratiśatravas te |
(4.22.6.c) ekavṛṣa indrasakhā jigīvāṃ chatrūyatām ā bharā bhojanāni ‖6‖
(4.22.7’) siṃhapratīko viśo addhi sarvā vyāghrapratīko 'va bādhasva śatrūn |
(4.22.7.c) ekavṛṣa indrasakhā jigīvāṃ chatrūyatām ā khidā bhojanāni ‖7‖

(4.23.1’) agner manve prathamasya pracetasaḥ pāñcajanyasya bahudhā yam indhate |
(4.23.1.c) viśoviśaḥ praviśivāṃsam īmahe sa no muñcatv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(4.23.2’) yathā havyaṃ vahasi jātavedo yathā yajñaṃ kalpayasi prajānan |
(4.23.2.c) evā devebhyaḥ sumatiṃ na ā vaha sa no muñcatv aṃhasaḥ ‖2‖
(4.23.3’) yāmanyāmann upayuktaṃ vahiṣṭhaṃ karmaṅkarmann ābhagam agnim īḍe |
(4.23.3.c) rakṣohaṇaṃ yajñavṛdhaṃ ghṛtāhutaṃ sa no muñcatv aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.23.4’) sujātaṃ jātavedasam agniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ vibhum |
(4.23.4.c) havyavāhaṃ havāmahe sa no muñcatv aṃhasaḥ ‖4‖
(4.23.5’) yena ṛṣayo balam adyotayan yujā yenāsurāṇām ayuvanta māyāḥ |
(4.23.5.c) yenāgninā paṇīn indro jigāya sa no muñcatv aṃhasaḥ ‖5‖
(4.23.6’) yena devā amṛtam anvavindan yenauṣadhīr madhumatīr akṛṇvan |
(4.23.6.c) yena devāḥ svar ābharant sa no muñcatv aṃhasaḥ ‖6‖
(4.23.7’) yasyedaṃ pradiśi yad virocate yaj jātaṃ janitavyaṃ ca kevalam |
(4.23.7.c) staumy agniṃ nāthito johavīmi sa no muñcatv aṃhasaḥ ‖7‖

(4.24.1’) indrasya manmahe śaśvad id asya manmahe vṛtraghna stomā upa mema āguḥ |
(4.24.1.c) yo dāśuṣaḥ sukṛto havam eti sa no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(4.24.2’) ya ugrīṇām ugrabāhur yayur yo dānavānāṃ balam āruroja |
(4.24.2.c) yena jitāḥ sindhavo yena gāvaḥ sa no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖2‖
(4.24.3’) yaś carṣaṇipro vṛṣabhaḥ svarvid yasmai grāvāṇaḥ pravadanti nṛmṇam |
(4.24.3.c) yasyādhvaraḥ saptahotā madiṣṭhaḥ sa no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.24.4’) yasya vaśāsa ṛṣabhāsa ukṣaṇo yasmai mīyante svaravaḥ svarvide |
(4.24.4.c) yasmai śukraḥ pavate brahmaśumbhitaḥ sa no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖4‖
(4.24.5’) yasya juṣṭiṃ sominaḥ kāmayante yaṃ havanta iṣumantaṃ gaviṣṭau |
(4.24.5.c) yasminn arkaḥ śiśriye yasminn ojaḥ sa no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖5‖
(4.24.6’) yaḥ prathamaḥ karmakṛtyāya jajñe yasya vīryam prathamasyānubuddham |
(4.24.6.c) yenodyato vajro 'bhyāyatāhiṃ sa no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖6‖
(4.24.7’) yaḥ saṃgrāmān nayati saṃ yudhe vaśī yaḥ puṣṭāni saṃsṛjati dūyāni |
(4.24.7.c) staumīndraṃ nāthito johavīmi sa no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖7‖

(4.25.1’) vāyoḥ savitur vidathāni manmahe yāv ātmanvad viśatho yau ca rakṣathaḥ |
(4.25.1.c) yau viśvasya paribhū babhūvathus tau no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(4.25.2’) yayoḥ saṃkhyātā varimā pārhivāni yābhyāṃ rajo yupitam antarikṣe |
(4.25.2.c) yayoḥ prāyaṃ nānv ānaśe kaś cana tau no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖2‖
(4.25.3’) tava vrate ni viśante janāsas tvayy udite prerate citrabhāno |
(4.25.3.c) yuvaṃ vāyo savitā ca bhuvanāni rakṣathas tau no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.25.4’) apeto vāyo savitā ca duṣkṛtam apa rakṣāṃsi śimidāṃ ca sedhatam |
(4.25.4.c) saṃ hy ūrjayā sṛjathaḥ saṃ balena tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖4‖
(4.25.5’) rayiṃ me poṣaṃ savitota vāyus tanū dakṣam ā suvatāṃ suśevam |
(4.25.5.c) ayakṣmatātiṃ maha iha dhattaṃ tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖5‖
(4.25.6’) pra sumatiṃ savitar vāya ūtaye mahasvantaṃ matsaraṃ mādayāthaḥ |
(4.25.6.c) arvāg vāmasya pravato ni yachataṃ tau no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖6‖
(4.25.7’) upa śreṣṭhā na āśiṣo devayor dhāmann asthiran |
(4.25.7.c) staumi devaṃ savitāraṃ ca vāyuṃ tau no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖7‖

(4.26.1’) manve vāṃ dyāvāpṛthivī subhojasau sacetasau ye aprathethām amitā yojanāni |
(4.26.1.c) pratiṣṭhe hy abhavataṃ vasūnāṃ te no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(4.26.2’) pratiṣṭhe hy abhavataṃ vasūnāṃ pravṛddhe devī subhage urūcī |
(4.26.2.c) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖2‖
(4.26.3’) asantāpe sutapasau huve 'ham urvī gambhīre kavibhir namasye |
(4.26.3.c) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.26.4’) ye amṛtaṃ bibhṛtho ye havīṃṣi ye srotyā bibhṛtho ye manuṣyān |
(4.26.4.c) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖4‖
(4.26.5’) ye usriyā bibhṛtho ye vanaspatīn yayor vāṃ viśvā bhuvanāny antaḥ |
(4.26.5.c) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖5‖
(4.26.6’) ye kīlālena tarpayatho ye ghṛtena yābhyām ṛte na kiṃ cana śaknuvanti |
(4.26.6.c) dyāvāpṛthivī bhavataṃ me syone te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖6‖
(4.26.7’) yan medam abhiśocati yenayena vā kṛtaṃ pauruṣeyān na daivāt |
(4.26.7.c) staumi dyāvāpṛthivī nāthito johavīmi te no muñcatam amhasaḥ ‖7‖

(4.27.1’) marutāṃ manve adhi me bruvantu premaṃ vājaṃ vājasāte avantu |
(4.27.1.c) āśūn iva suyamān ahva ūtaye te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(4.27.2’) utsam akṣitaṃ vyacanti ye sadā ya āsiñcanti rasam oṣadhīṣu |
(4.27.2.c) puro dadhe marutaḥ pṛśnimātṝṃs te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖2‖
(4.27.3’) payo dhenūnāṃ rasam oṣadhīnāṃ javam arvatāṃ kavayo ya invatha |
(4.27.3.c) śagmā bhavantu maruto naḥ syonās te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.27.4’) apaḥ samudrād divam ud vahanti divas pṛthivīm abhi ye sṛjanti |
(4.27.4.c) ye adbhir īśānā marutaś caranti te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖4‖
(4.27.5’) ye kīlālena tarpayanti ye ghṛtena ye vā vayo medasā saṃsṛjanti |
(4.27.5.c) ye adbhir īśānā maruto varṣayanti te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖5‖
(4.27.6’) yadīd idaṃ maruto mārutena yadi devā daivyenedṛg āra |
(4.27.6.c) yūyam īśidhve vasavas tasya niṣkṛtes te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖6‖
(4.27.7’) tigmam anīkam viditaṃ sahasvan mārutaṃ śardhaḥ pṛtanāsūgram |
(4.27.7.c) staumi maruto nāthito johavīmi te no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖7‖

(4.28.1’) bhavāśarvau manve vāṃ tasya vittaṃ yayor vām idaṃ pradiśi yad virocate |
(4.28.1.c) yāv asyeśāthe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(4.28.2’) yayor abhyabhva uta yad dūre cid yau viditāv iṣubhṛtām asiṣṭhau |
(4.28.2.c) yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖2‖
(4.28.3’) sahasrākṣau vṛtrahanā huvehaṃ dūregavyūtī stuvann emy ugrau |
(4.28.3.c) yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.28.4’) yāv ārebhāthe bahu sākam agre pra ced asrāṣṭram abhibhāṃ janeṣu |
(4.28.4.c) yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖4‖
(4.28.5’) yayor vadhān nāpapadyate kaś canāntar deveṣūta mānuṣeṣu |
(4.28.5.c) yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖5‖
(4.28.6’) yaḥ kṛtyākṛn mūlakṛd yātudhāno ni tasmin dhattaṃ vajram ugrau |
(4.28.6.c) yāv asyeśathe dvipado yau catuṣpadas tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖6‖
(4.28.7’) adhi no brūtaṃ pṛtanāsūgrau saṃ vajreṇa sṛjataṃ yaḥ kimīdī |
(4.28.7.c) staumi bhavāśarvau nāthito johavīmi tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖7‖

(4.29.1’) manve vāṃ mitrāvaruṇāv ṛtāvṛdhau sacetasau druhvaṇo yau nudethe |
(4.29.1.c) pra satyāvānam avatho bhareṣu tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(4.29.2’) sacetasau druhvaṇo yau nudethe pra satyāvānam avatho bhareṣu |
(4.29.2.c) yau gachatho nṛcakṣasau babhruṇā sutaṃ tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖2‖
(4.29.3’) yāv aṅgirasam avatho yāv agastiṃ mitrāvaruṇā jamadagnim attrim |
(4.29.3.c) yau kaśyapam avatho yau vasiṣṭhaṃ tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖
(4.29.4’) yau śyāvāśvam avatho vādhryaśvaṃ mitrāvaruṇā purumīḍham attrim |
(4.29.4.c) yau vimadam avatho saptavadhriṃ tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖4‖
(4.29.5’) yau bharadvājam avatho yau gaviṣṭhiraṃ viśvāmitraṃ varuṇa mitra kutsam |
(4.29.5.c) yau kakṣīvantam avatho prota kaṇvaṃ tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖5‖
(4.29.6’) yau medhātithim avatho yau triśokaṃ mitrāvaruṇāv uśanām kāvyaṃ yau |
(4.29.6.c) yau gotamam avatho prota mugdalaṃ tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖6‖
(4.29.7’) yayo rathaḥ satyavartma rjuraśmir mithuyā carantam abhiyāti dūṣayan |
(4.29.7.c) staumi mitrāvaruṇau nāthito johavīmi tau no muñcatam aṃhasaḥ ‖7‖

(4.30.1’) ahaṃ rudrebhir vasubhiś carāmy aham ādityair uta viśvadevaiḥ |
(4.30.1.c) ahaṃ mitrāvarunobhā bibharmy aham indrāgnī aham aśvinobhā ‖1‖
(4.30.2’) ahaṃ rāṣṭrī saṃgamanī vasūnāṃ cikituṣī prathamā yajñiyānām |
(4.30.2.c) tāṃ mā devā vy adadhuḥ purutrā bhūristhātrāṃ bhūry āveśayantaḥ ‖2‖
(4.30.3’) aham eva svayam idaṃ vadāmi juṣṭaṃ devānām uta mānuṣāṇām |
(4.30.3.c) yaṃ kāmaye tantam ugraṃ kṛṇomi taṃ brahmāṇaṃ tam ṛṣiṃ taṃ sumedhām ‖3‖
(4.30.4’) mayā so 'nnam atti yo vipaśyati yaḥ prāṇati ya īm śṛṇoty uktam |
(4.30.4.c) amantavo māṃ ta upa kṣiyanti śrudhi śruta śruddheyaṃ te vadāmi ‖4‖
(4.30.5’) ahaṃ rudrāya dhanur ā tanomi brahmadviṣe śarave hantavā u |
(4.30.5.c) ahaṃ janāya samadaṃ kṛṇomi aham dyāvāpṛthivī ā viveśa ‖5‖
(4.30.6’) ahaṃ somam āhanasaṃ bibharmy ahaṃ tvaṣṭāram uta pūṣaṇaṃ bhagam |
(4.30.6.c) ahaṃ dadhāmi draviṇā haviṣmate suprāvyā yajamānāya sunvate ‖6‖
(4.30.7’) ahaṃ suve pitaram asya mūrdhan mama yonir apsv antaḥ samudre |
(4.30.7.c) tato vi tiṣṭhe bhuvanāni viśvotāmūṃ dyāṃ varṣmaṇopa spṛśāmi ‖7‖
(4.30.8’) aham eva vātaiva pra vāmy ārabhamāṇā bhuvanāni viśvā |
(4.30.8.c) paro divā para enā pṛthivyaitāvatī mahimnā saṃ babhūva ‖8‖

(4.31.1’) tvayā manyo saratham ārujanto harṣamāṇā hṛṣitāso marutvan |
(4.31.1.c) tigmeṣava āyudhā saṃśiśānā upa pra yantu naro agnirūpāḥ ‖1‖
(4.31.2’) agnir iva manyo tviṣitaḥ sahasva senānīr naḥ sahure hūta edhi |
(4.31.2.c) hatvāya śatrūn vi bhajasva veda ojo mimāno vi mṛdho nudasva ‖2‖
(4.31.3’) sahasva manyo abhimātim asmai rujan mṛṇan pramṛṇan prehi śatrūn |
(4.31.3.c) ugraṃ te pājo nanv ā rurudhre vaśī vaśaṃ nayāsā ekaja tvam ‖3‖
(4.31.4’) eko bahūnām asi manyo īḍitā viśaṃviśam yuddhāya saṃ śiśādhi |
(4.31.4.c) akṛttaruk tvayā yujā vayaṃ dyumantaṃ ghoṣam vijayāya kṛṇmasi ‖4‖
(4.31.5’) vijeṣakṛd indra ivānavabravo 'smākaṃ manyo adhipā bhaveha |
(4.31.5.c) priyaṃ te nāma sahure gṛṇīmasi vidmā tam utsaṃ yata ābabhūtha ‖5‖
(4.31.6’) ābhūtyā sahajā vajra sāyaka saho bibharṣi sahabhūte uttaram |
(4.31.6.c) kratvā no manyo saha medy edhi mahādhanasya puruhūta saṃsṛji ‖6‖
(4.31.7’) saṃsṛṣṭaṃ dhanam ubhayaṃ samākṛtam asmabhyaṃ dhattāṃ varuṇaś ca manyuḥ |
(4.31.7.c) bhiyo dadhānā hṛdayeṣu śatravah parājitāso apa ni layantām ‖7‖

(4.32.1’) yas te manyo 'vidhad vajra sāyaka saha ojaḥ puṣyati viśvam ānuṣak |
(4.32.1.c) sāhyāma dāsam āryaṃ tvayā yujā vayaṃ sahaskṛtena sahasā sahasvatā ‖1‖
(4.32.2’) manyur indro manyur evāsa devo manyur hotā varuṇo jātavedāḥ |
(4.32.2.c) manyuṃ viśa īḍate mānuṣīr yāḥ pahi no manyo tapasā sajoṣāḥ ‖2‖
(4.32.3’) abhīhi manyo tavasas tavīyān tapasā yujā vi jahi śatrūn |
(4.32.3.c) amitrahā vṛtrahā dasyuhā ca viśvā vasūny ā bharā tvaṃ naḥ ‖3‖
(4.32.4’) tvaṃ hi manyo abhibhūtyojāḥ svayaṃbhūr bhāmo abhimātiṣāhaḥ |
(4.32.4.c) viśvacarṣaṇiḥ sahuriḥ sahīyān asmāsv ojaḥ pṛtanāsu dhehi ‖4‖
(4.32.5’) abhāgaḥ sann apa pareto asmi tava kratvā taviṣasya pracetaḥ |
(4.32.5.c) taṃ tvā manyo akratur jihīḍāhaṃ svā tanūr baladāvā na ehi ‖5‖
(4.32.6’) ayaṃ te asmy upa na ehy arvāṅ pratīcīnaḥ sahure viśvadāvan |
(4.32.6.c) manyo vajrinn abhi na ā vavṛtsva hanāva dasyūṃr uta bodhy āpeḥ ‖6‖
(4.32.7’) abhi prehi dakṣiṇato bhavā no 'dhā vṛtrāṇi jaṅghanāva bhūri |
(4.32.7.c) juhomi te dharuṇaṃ madhvo agram ubhāv upāṃśu prathamā pibāva ‖7‖

(4.33.1’) apa naḥ śośucad agham agne śuśugdhy ā rayim |
(4.33.1.c) apa naḥ śośucad agham ‖1‖
(4.33.2’) sukṣetriyā sugātuyā vasūyā ca yajāmahe |
(4.33.2.c) apa naḥ śośucad agham ‖2‖
(4.33.3’) pra yad bhandiṣṭha eṣāṃ prāsmākāsaś ca sūrayaḥ |
(4.33.3.c) apa naḥ śośucad agham ‖3‖
(4.33.4’) pra yat te agne sūrayo jāyemahi pra te vayam |
(4.33.4.c) apa naḥ śośucad agham ‖4‖
(4.33.5’) pra yad agneḥ sahasvato viśvato yanti bhānavaḥ |
(4.33.5.c) apa naḥ śośucad agham ‖5‖
(4.33.6’) tvaṃ hi viśvatomukha viśvataḥ paribhūr asi |
(4.33.6.c) apa naḥ śośucad agham ‖6‖
(4.33.7’) dviṣo no viśvatomukhāti nāveva pāraya |
(4.33.7.c) apa naḥ śośucad agham ‖7‖
(4.33.8’) sa naḥ sindhum iva nāvāti parṣa svastaye |
(4.33.8.c) apa naḥ śośucad agham ‖8‖

(4.34.1’) brahmāsya śīrṣam bṛhad asya pṛṣṭhaṃ vāmadevyam udaram odanasya |
(4.34.1.c) chandāṃsi pakṣau mukham asya satyaṃ viṣṭārī jātas tapaso'dhi yajñaḥ ‖1‖
(4.34.2’) anasthāḥ pūtāḥ pavanena śuddhāḥ śucayaḥ śucim api yanti lokam |
(4.34.2.c) naiṣāṃ śiśnaṃ pra dahati jātavedāḥ svarge loke bahu straiṇam eṣām ‖2‖
(4.34.3’) viṣṭāriṇam odanaṃ ye pacanti nainān avartiḥ sacate kadā cana |
(4.34.3.c) āste yama upa yāti devānt sam gandharvair madate somyebhiḥ ‖3‖
(4.34.4’) viṣṭāriṇam odanam ye pacanti nainān yamaḥ pari muṣṇāti retaḥ |
(4.34.4.c) rathī ha bhūtvā rathayāna īyate pakṣī ha bhūtvāti divaḥ sam eti ‖4‖
(4.34.5’) eṣa yajñānāṃ vitato vahiṣṭho viṣṭāriṇaṃ paktvā divam ā viveśa |
(4.34.5.c) āṇḍīkaṃ kumudaṃ saṃ tanoti bisaṃ śālūkaṃ śaphako mulālī |
(4.34.5.e) etās tvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumat pinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ
‖5‖
(4.34.6’) ghṛtahradā madhukūlāḥ surodakāḥ kṣīreṇa pūrṇā udakena dadhnā |
(4.34.6.c) etās tvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumat pinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ
‖6‖
(4.34.7’) caturaḥ kumbhāṃś caturdhā dadāmi kṣīreṇa pūrnā;m udakena dadhnā |
(4.34.7.c) etās tvā dhārā upa yantu sarvāḥ svarge loke madhumat pinvamānā upa tvā tiṣṭhantu puṣkariṇīḥ samantāḥ
‖7‖
(4.34.8’) imam odanaṃ ni dadhe brāhmaṇeṣu viṣṭāriṇaṃ lokajitaṃ svargam |
(4.34.8.c) sa me mā kṣeṣṭa svadhayā pinvamāno viśvarūpā dhenuḥ kāmadughā me astu ‖8‖

(4.35.1’) yam odanaṃ prathamajā ṛtasya prajāpatis tapasā brahmaṇe 'pacat |
(4.35.1.c) yo lokānāṃ vidhṛtir nābhireṣāt tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ‖1‖
(4.35.2’) yenātaran bhūtakṛto 'ti mṛtyuṃ yam anvavindan tapasā śrameṇa |
(4.35.2.c) yaṃ papāca brahmaṇe brahma pūrvaṃ tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ‖2‖
(4.35.3’) yo dādhāra pṛthivīṃ viśvabhojasaṃ yo antarikṣam āpṛṇād rasena |
(4.35.3.c) yo astabhnād divam ūrdhvo mahimnā tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ‖3‖
(4.35.4’) yasmān māsā nirmitās triṃśadarāḥ saṃvatsaro yasmān nirmito dvādaśāraḥ |
(4.35.4.c) ahorātrā yaṃ pariyanto nāpus tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ‖4‖
(4.35.5’) yaḥ prāṇadaḥ prāṇadavān babhūva yasmai lokā ghṛtavantaḥ kṣaranti |
(4.35.5.c) jyotiṣmatīḥ pradiśo yasya sarvās tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ‖5‖
(4.35.6’) yasmāt pakvād amṛtaṃ saṃbabhūva yo gāyatryā adhipatir babhūva |
(4.35.6.c) yasmin vedā nihitā viśvarūpās tenaudanenāti tarāṇi mṛtyum ‖6‖
(4.35.7’) ava bādhe dviṣantaṃ devapīyuṃ sapatnā ye me 'pa te bhavantu |
(4.35.7.c) brahmaudanaṃ viśvajitaṃ pacāmi śṛṇvantu me śraddadhānasya devāḥ ‖7‖

(4.36.1’) tānt satyaujāḥ pra dahatv agnir vaiśvānaro vṛṣā |
(4.36.1.c) yo no durasyād dipsāc cātho yo no arātiyāt ‖1‖
(4.36.2’) yo no dipsād adipsato dipsato yaś ca dipsati |
(4.36.2.c) vaiśvānarasya daṃṣṭrayor agner api dadhāmi tam ‖2‖
(4.36.3’) ya āgare mṛgayante pratikrośe 'māvāsye |
(4.36.3.c) kravyādo anyān dipsataḥ sarvāṃs tānt sahasā sahe ‖3‖
(4.36.4’) sahe piśācānt sahasaiṣāṃ draviṇaṃ dade |
(4.36.4.c) sarvān durasyato hanmi saṃ ma ākūtir ṛdyatām ‖4‖
(4.36.5’) ye devās tena hāsante sūryeṇa mimate javam |
(4.36.5.c) nadīṣu parvateṣu ye saṃ taiḥ paśubhir vide ‖5‖
(4.36.6’) tapano asmi piśācānām vyāghro gomatām iva |
(4.36.6.c) śvānaḥ siṃham iva dṛṣṭvā te na vindante nyañcanam ‖6‖
(4.36.7’) na piśācaiḥ saṃ śaknomi na stenaiḥ na vanargubhiḥ |
(4.36.7.c) piśācās tasmān naśyanti yam ahaṃ grāmam āviśe ‖7‖
(4.36.8’) yaṃ grāmam āviśata idam ugraṃ saho mama |
(4.36.8.c) piśācās tasmān naśyanti na pāpam upa jānate ‖8‖
(4.36.9’) ye mā krodhayanti lapitā hastinaṃ maśakā iva |
(4.36.9.c) tān ahaṃ manye durhitān jane alpaśayūn iva ‖9‖
(4.36.10’) abhi taṃ nirṛtir dhattām aśvam iva aśvābhidhānyā |
(4.36.10.c) malvo yo mahyaṃ krudhyati sa u pāśān na mucyate ‖10‖

(4.37.1’) tvayā pūrvam atharvāṇo jaghnū rakṣāṃsy oṣadhe |
(4.37.1.c) tvayā jaghāna kaśyapas tvayā kaṇvo agastyaḥ ‖1‖
(4.37.2’) tvayā vayam apsaraso gandharvāṃs cātayāmahe |
(4.37.2.c) ajaśṛṅgy aja rakṣaḥ sarvān gandhena nāśaya ‖2‖
(4.37.3’) nadīṃ yantv apsaraso 'pāṃ tāram avaśvasam |
(4.37.3.c) gulgulūḥ pīlā nalady aukṣagandhiḥ pramandanī |
(4.37.3.e) tat paretāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana ‖3‖
(4.37.4’) yatrāśvatthā nyagrodhā mahāvṛkṣāḥ śikhaṇḍinaḥ |
(4.37.4.c) tat paretāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana ‖4‖
(4.37.5’) yatra vaḥ preṅkhā haritā arjunā uta yatrāghātāḥ karkaryaḥ saṃvadanti |
(4.37.5.c) tat paretāpsarasaḥ pratibuddhā abhūtana ‖5‖
(4.37.6’) eyam agann oṣadhīnāṃ vīrudhām vīryāvatī |
(4.37.6.c) ajaśṛṅgy arāṭakī tīkṣṇaśṛṅgī vyṛṣatu ‖6‖
(4.37.7’) ānṛtyataḥ śikhaṇḍino gandharvasyāpsarāpateḥ |
(4.37.7.c) bhinadmi muṣkāv api yāmi śepaḥ ‖7‖
(4.37.8’) bhīmā indrasya hetayaḥ śataṃ ṛṣṭīr ayasmayīḥ |
(4.37.8.c) tābhir haviradān gandharvān avakādān vyṛṣatu ‖8‖
(4.37.9’) bhīmā indrasya hetayaḥ śataṃ ṛṣṭīr hiraṇyayīḥ |
(4.37.9.c) tābhir haviradān gandharvān avakādān vyṛṣatu ‖9‖
(4.37.10’) avakādān abhiśocān apsu jyotaya māmakān |
(4.37.10.c) piśācānt sarvān oṣadhe pra mṛṇīhi sahasva ca ‖10‖
(4.37.11’) śvevaikaḥ kapir ivaikaḥ kumāraḥ sarvakeśakaḥ |
(4.37.11.c) priyo dṛśa iva bhūtvā gandharvaḥ sacate striyas |
(4.37.11.e) tam ito nāśayāmasi brahmaṇā vīryāvatā ‖11‖
(4.37.12’) jāyā id vo apsaraso gandharvāḥ patayo yuyam |
(4.37.12.c) apa dhāvatāmartyā martyān mā sacadhvam ‖12‖

(4.38.1’) udbhindatīṃ saṃjayantīm apsarāṃ sādhudevinīm |
(4.38.1.c) glahe kṛtāni kṛṇvānām apsarāṃ tām iha huve ‖1‖
(4.38.2’) vicinvatīm ākirantīm apsarāṃ sādhudevinīm |
(4.38.2.c) glahe kṛtāni gṛhṇānām apsarāṃ tām iha huve ‖2‖
(4.38.3’) yāyaiḥ parinṛtyaty ādadānā kṛtaṃ glahāt |
(4.38.3.c) sā naḥ kṛtāni sīṣatī prahām āpnotu māyayā |
(4.38.3.e) sā naḥ payasvaty aitu mā no jaiṣur idaṃ dhanam ‖3‖
(4.38.4’) yā akṣeṣu pramodante śucaṃ krodhaṃ ca bibhratī |
(4.38.4.c) ānandinīṃ pramodinīm apsarāṃ tām iha huve ‖4‖
(4.38.5’) sūryasya raśmīn anu yāḥ sañcaranti marīcīr vā yā anusañcaranti |
(4.38.5.c) yāsām ṛṣabho dūrato vājinīvānt sadyaḥ sarvān lokān paryeti rakṣan |
(4.38.5.e) sa na aitu homam imaṃ jusāṇo 'ntarikṣeṇa saha vājinīvān ‖5‖
(4.38.6’) antarikṣena saha vājinīvan karkīṃ vatsām iha rakṣa vājin |
(4.38.6.c) ime te stokā bahulā ehy arvāṅ iyaṃ te karkīha te mano 'stu ‖6‖
(4.38.7’) antarikṣeṇa saha vājinīvan karkīṃ vatsām iha rakṣa vājin |
(4.38.7.c) ayaṃ ghāso ayaṃ vraja iha vatsāṃ ni badhnīmaḥ |
(4.38.7.e) yathānāma va īśmahe svāhā ‖7‖

(4.39.1’) pṛthivyām agnaye sam anamant sa ārdhnot |
(4.39.1.c) yathā pṛthivyām agnaye samanamann evā mahyaṃ saṃnamaḥ saṃ namantu ‖1‖
(4.39.2’) pṛthivī dhenus tasyā agnir vatsaḥ |
(4.39.2.c) sā me 'gninā vatseneṣam ūrjaṃ kāmaṃ duhām |
(4.39.2.e) āyuḥ prathamaṃ prajāṃ poṣaṃ rayiṃ svāhā ‖2‖
(4.39.3’) antarikṣe vāyave sam anamant sa ārdhnot |
(4.39.3.c) yathāntarikṣe vāyave samanamann evā mahyaṃ saṃnamaḥ saṃ namantu ‖3‖
(4.39.4’) antarikṣaṃ dhenus tasyā vatsaḥ |
(4.39.4.c) sā me vāyunā vatseneṣam ūrjaṃ kāmaṃ duhām |
(4.39.4.e) āyuḥ prathamaṃ prajāṃ poṣaṃ rayiṃ svāhā ‖4‖
(4.39.5’) divyādityāya sam anamant sa ārdhnot |
(4.39.5.c) yathā divyādityāya samanamann evā mahyaṃ saṃnamaḥ saṃ namantu ‖5‖
(4.39.6’) dyaur dhenus tasyā ādityo vatsaḥ |
(4.39.6.c) sā ma ādityena vatseneṣam ūrjaṃ kāmaṃ duhām |
(4.39.6.e) āyuḥ prathamaṃ prajāṃ poṣaṃ rayiṃ svāhā ‖6‖
(4.39.7’) dikṣu candrāya sam anamant sa ārdhnot |
(4.39.7.c) yathā dikṣu candrāya samanamann evā mahyaṃ saṃnamaḥ saṃ namantu ‖7‖
(4.39.8’) diśo dhenavas tāsāṃ candro vatsaḥ |
(4.39.8.c) tā me candreṇa vatseneṣam ūrjaṃ kāmaṃ duhām āyuḥ prathamaṃ prajāṃ posaṃ rayiṃ svāhā ‖8‖
(4.39.9’) agnāv agniś carati praviṣṭa ṛṣīṇām putro abhiśastipā u |
(4.39.9.c) namaskāreṇa namasā te juhomi mā devānāṃ mithuyā karma bhāgam ‖9‖
(4.39.10’) hṛdā pūtam manasā jātavedo viśvāni deva vayunāni vidvān |
(4.39.10.c) saptāsyāni tava jātavedas tebhyo juhomi sa juṣasva havyam ‖10‖

(4.40.1’) ye purastāj juhvati jātavedaḥ prācyā diśo 'bhidāsanty asmān |
(4.40.1.c) agnim ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi ‖1‖
(4.40.2’) ye dakṣiṇato juhvati jātavedo dakṣiṇāyā diśo 'bhidāsanty asmān |
(4.40.2.c) yamaṃ ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyag enā pratisareṇa hanmi ‖2‖
(4.40.3’) ye paścāj juhvati jātavedaḥ pratīcyā diśo 'bhidāsanty asmān |
(4.40.3.c) varuṇam ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi ‖3‖
(4.40.4’) ya uttarato juhvati jātaveda udīcyā diśo 'bhidāsanty asmān |
(4.40.4.c) somam ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi ‖4‖
(4.40.5’) ye 'dhastāj juhvati jātaveda udīcyā diśo 'bhidāsanty asmān |
(4.40.5.c) bhūmim ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi ‖5‖
(4.40.6’) ye 'ntarikṣāj juhvati jātavedo vyadhvāyā diśo 'bhidāsanty asmān |
(4.40.6.c) vāyum ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi ‖6‖
(4.40.7’) ya upariṣṭāj juhvati jātaveda ūrdhvāyā diśo 'bhidāsanty asmān |
(4.40.7.c) sūryam ṛtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi ‖7‖
(4.40.8’) ye diśām antardeśebhyo juhvati jātavedaḥ sarvābhyo digbhyo 'bhidāsanti asmān |
(4.40.8.c) brahma rtvā te parāñco vyathantāṃ pratyag enān pratisareṇa hanmi ‖8‖

(5.1.1’) ṛdhaṅmantro yoniṃ ya ābabhūvamṛtāsur vardhamānaḥ sujanmā |
(5.1.1.c) adabdhāsur bhrājamāno 'heva trito dhartā dādhāra trīṇi ‖1‖
(5.1.2’) ā yo dharmāṇi prathamaḥ sasāda tato vapūṃṣi kṛṇuṣe purūṇi |
(5.1.2.c) dhāsyur yoniṃ prathama ā viveśā yo vācam anuditāṃ ciketa ‖2‖
(5.1.3’) yas te śokāya tanvaṃ rireca kṣarad dhiraṇyaṃ śucayo 'nu svāḥ |
(5.1.3.c) atrā dadhete amṛtāni nāmāsme vastrāṇi viśa erayantām ‖3‖
(5.1.4’) pra yad ete prataraṃ pūrvyaṃ guḥ sadaḥsada ātiṣṭhanto ajuryam |
(5.1.4.c) kaviḥ śuṣasya mātarā rihāṇe jāmyai dhuryaṃ patim erayethām ‖4‖
(5.1.5’) tad ū ṣu te mahat pṛthujman namaḥ kaviḥ kāvyena kṛṇomi |
(5.1.5.c) yat samyañcāv abhiyantāv abhi kṣām atrā mahī rodhacakre vāvṛdhete ‖5‖
(5.1.6’) sapta maryādāḥ kavayas tatakṣus tāsām id ekām abhy aṃhuro gāt |
(5.1.6.c) āyor ha skambha upamasya nīḍe pathāṃ visarge dharuṇeṣu tasthau ‖6‖
(5.1.7’) utāmṛtāsur vrata emi kṛnvann asur ātmā tanvas tat sumadguḥ |
(5.1.7.c) uta vā śakro ratnaṃ dadhāty ūrjayā vā yat sacate havirdāḥ ‖7‖
(5.1.8’) uta putraḥ pitaraṃ kṣatram īḍe jyeṣṭhaṃ maryādam ahvayant svastaye |
(5.1.8.c) darśan nu tā varuṇa yās te viṣṭhā āvarvratataḥ kṛṇavo vapūṃṣi ‖8‖
(5.1.9’) ardham ardhena payasā pṛṇakṣy ardhena śuṣma vardhase amura |
(5.1.9.c) aviṃ vṛdhāma śagmiyaṃ sakhāyaṃ varuṇaṃ putram adityā iṣiram |
(5.1.9.e) kaviśastāny asmai vapūṃṣy avocāma rodasī satyavācā ‖9‖

(5.2.1’) tad id āsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭham yato yajña ugras tveṣanṛmṇaḥ |
(5.2.1.c) sadyo jajñāno ni riṇāti śatrūn anu yad enaṃ madanti viśva ūmāḥ ‖1‖
(5.2.2’) vavṛdhānaḥ śavasā bhūryojāḥ śatrur dāsāya bhiyasaṃ dadhāti |
(5.2.2.c) avyanac ca vyanac ca sasni saṃ te navanta prabhṛtā madeṣu ‖2‖
(5.2.3’) tve kratum api pṛñcanti bhūri dvir yad ete trir bhavanty ūmāḥ |
(5.2.3.c) svadoḥ svādīyaḥ svādunā sṛjā sam adaḥ su madhu madhunābhi yodhīḥ ‖3‖
(5.2.4’) yadi cin nu tvā dhanā jayantaṃ raṇeraṇe anumadanti viprāḥ |
(5.2.4.c) ojīyaḥ śuṣmint sthiram ā tanuṣva mā tvā dabhan durevāsaḥ kaśokāḥ ‖4‖
(5.2.5’) tvayā vayaṃ śāśadmahe raṇeṣu prapaśyanto yudhenyāni bhūri |
(5.2.5.c) codayāmi ta āyudhā vacobhiḥ saṃ te śiśāmi brahmaṇā vayāṃsi ‖5‖
(5.2.6’) ni tad dadhiṣe 'vare pare ca yasminn āvithāvasā duroṇe |
(5.2.6.c) ā sthāpayata mātaraṃ jigatnum ata invata karvarāṇi bhūri ‖6‖
(5.2.7’) stuṣva varṣman puruvartmānaṃ sam ṛbhvāṇam inatamam āptam āptyānām |
(5.2.7.c) ā darśati śavasā bhūryojāḥ pra sakṣati pratimānaṃ pṛthivyāḥ ‖7‖
(5.2.8’) imā brahma bṛhaddivaḥ kṛṇavad indrāya śūṣam agriyaḥ svarṣāḥ |
(5.2.8.c) maho gotrasya kṣayati svarājā turaś cid viśvam arṇavat tapasvān ‖8‖
(5.2.9’) evā mahān bṛhaddivo atharvāvocat svāṃ tanvam indram eva |
(5.2.9.c) svasārau mātaribhvarī aripre hinvanti caine śavasā vardhayanti ca ‖9‖

(5.3.1’) mamāgne varco vihaveṣv astu vayaṃ tvendhānās tanvam puṣema |
(5.3.1.c) mahyaṃ namantāṃ pradiśaś catasras tvayādhyakṣeṇa pṛtanā jayema ‖1‖
(5.3.2’) agne manyuṃ pratinudan pareṣāṃ tvaṃ no gopāḥ pari pāhi viśvataḥ |
(5.3.2.c) apāñco yantu nivatā durasyavo 'maiṣāṃ cittaṃ prabudhāṃ vi neśat ‖2‖
(5.3.3’) mama devā vihave santu sarva indravanto maruto viṣṇur agniḥ |
(5.3.3.c) mamāntarikṣam urulokam astu mahyaṃ vātaḥ pavatāṃ kāmāyāsmai ‖3‖
(5.3.4’) mahyaṃ yajantāṃ mama yānīṣṭākūtiḥ satyā manaso me astu |
(5.3.4.c) eno mā ni gāṃ katamac canāhaṃ viśve devā abhi rakṣantu meha ‖4‖
(5.3.5’) mayi devā draviṇam ā yajantāṃ mayi āśīr astu mayi devahūtiḥ |
(5.3.5.c) daivāḥ hotāraḥ saniṣan na etad ariṣṭāḥ syāma tanvā suvīrāḥ ‖5‖
(5.3.6’) daivīḥ ṣaḍ urvīr uru naḥ kṛṇota viśve devāsa iha mādayadhvam |
(5.3.6.c) mā no vidad abhibhā mo aśastir mā no vidad vṛjinā dveṣyā yā ‖6‖
(5.3.7’) tisro devīr mahi naḥ śarma yachata prajāyai nas tanve yac ca puṣṭam |
(5.3.7.c) mā hāsmahi prajayā mā tanūbhir mā radhāma dviṣate soma rājan ‖7‖
(5.3.8’) uruvyacā no mahiṣaḥ śarma yachatv asmin have puruhūtaḥ purukṣu |
(5.3.8.c) sa naḥ prajāyai haryaśva mṛḍendra mā no rīriṣo mā parā dāḥ ‖8‖
(5.3.9’) dhātā vidhātā bhuvanasya yas patir devaḥ savitābhimātiṣāhaḥ |
(5.3.9.c) ādityā rudrā aśvinobhā devāḥ pāntu yajamānaṃ nirṛthāt ‖9‖
(5.3.10’) ye naḥ sapatnā apa te bhavantv indrāgnibhyām ava bādhāmaha enān |
(5.3.10.c) ādityā rudrā uparispṛśo no ugraṃ cettāram adhirājam akrata ‖10‖
(5.3.11’) arvāñcam indram amuto havāmahe yo gojid dhanajid aśvajid yaḥ |
(5.3.11.c) imaṃ no yajñaṃ vihave śṛṇotv asmākam abhūr haryaśva medī ‖11‖

(5.4.1’) yo giriṣv ajāyathā vīrudhāṃ balavattamaḥ |
(5.4.1.c) kuṣṭhehi takmanāśana takmānaṃ nāśayann itaḥ ‖1‖
(5.4.2’) suparṇasuvane girau jātaṃ himavatas pari |
(5.4.2.c) dhanair abhi śrutvā yanti vidur hi takmanāśanam ‖2‖
(5.4.3’) aśvattho devasadanas tṛtīyasyām ito divi |
(5.4.3.c) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ devāḥ kuṣṭham avanvata ‖3‖
(5.4.4’) hiraṇyayī naur acarad dhiraṇyabandhanā divi |
(5.4.4.c) tatrāmṛtasya puṣpaṃ devāḥ kuṣṭham avanvata ‖4‖
(5.4.5’) hiraṇyayāḥ panthāna āsann aritrāṇi hiraṇyayā |
(5.4.5.c) nāvo hiraṇyayīr āsan yābhiḥ kuṣṭhaṃ nirāvahan ‖5‖
(5.4.6’) imaṃ me kuṣṭha pūruṣaṃ tam ā vaha taṃ niṣ kuru |
(5.4.6.c) tam u me agadaṃ kṛdhi ‖6‖
(5.4.7’) devebhyo adhi jāto 'si somasyāsi sakhā hitaḥ |
(5.4.7.c) sa prāṇāya vyānāya cakṣuṣe me asmai mṛḍa ‖7‖
(5.4.8’) udaṅ jāto himavataḥ sa prācyāṃ nīyase janam |
(5.4.8.c) tatra kuṣṭhasya nāmāny uttamāni vi bhejire ‖8‖
(5.4.9’) uttamo nāma kuṣṭhasy uttamo nāma te pitā |
(5.4.9.c) yakṣmaṃ ca sarvaṃ nāśaya takmānaṃ cārasaṃ kṛdhi ‖9‖
(5.4.10’) śīrṣāmayaṃ upahatyām akṣyos tanvo rapaḥ |
(5.4.10.c) kuṣṭhas tat sarvaṃ niṣ karad daivaṃ samaha vṛṣṇyam ‖10‖

(5.5.1’) rātrī mātā nabhaḥ pitāryamā te pitāmahaḥ |
(5.5.1.c) silācī nāma vā asi sā devānām asi svasā ‖1‖
(5.5.2’) yas tvā pibati jīvati trāyase puruṣaṃ tvam |
(5.5.2.c) bhartrī hi śaśvatām asi janānāṃ ca nyañcanī ‖2‖
(5.5.3’) vṛkṣaṃvṛkṣam ā rohasi vṛṣaṇyantīva kanyalā |
(5.5.3.c) jayantī pratyātiṣṭhantī sparaṇī nāma vā asi ‖3‖
(5.5.4’) yad daṇḍena yad iṣvā yad vārur harasā kṛtam |
(5.5.4.c) tasya tvam asi niṣkṛtiḥ semaṃ niṣ kṛdhi pūruṣam ‖4‖
(5.5.5’) bhadrāt plakṣān nis tiṣṭhasy aśvatthāt khadirād dhavāt |
(5.5.5.c) bhadrān nyagrodhāt parṇāt sā na ehy arundhati ‖5‖
(5.5.6’) hiraṇyavarṇe subhage sūryavarṇe vapuṣṭame |
(5.5.6.c) rutaṃ gachāsi niṣkṛte niṣkṛtir nāma vā asi ‖6‖
(5.5.7’) hiraṇyavarṇe subhage śuṣme lomaśavakṣane |
(5.5.7.c) apām asi svasā lākṣe vāto hātmā babhūva te ‖7‖
(5.5.8’) silācī nāma kānīno 'jababhru pitā tava |
(5.5.8.c) aśvo yamasya yaḥ śyāvas tasya hāsnāsy ukṣitā ‖8‖
(5.5.9’) aśvasyāsnaḥ saṃpatitā sā vṛkṣā;m abhi siṣyade |
(5.5.9.c) sarā patatriṇī bhūtvā sā na ehy arundhati ‖9‖

(5.6.1’) brahma jajñānaṃ prathamaṃ purastād vi sīmataḥ suruco vena āvaḥ |
(5.6.1.c) sa budhnyā upamā asya viṣṭhāḥ sataś ca yonim asataś ca vi vaḥ ‖1‖
(5.6.2’) anāptā ye vaḥ prathamā yāni karmāṇi cakrire |
(5.6.2.c) vīrān no atra mā dabhan tad va etat puro dadhe ‖2‖
(5.6.3’) sahasradhāra eva te sam asvaran divo nāke madhujihvā asaścataḥ |
(5.6.3.c) tasya spaśo na ni miṣanti bhūrṇayaḥ padepade pāśinaḥ santi setave ‖3‖
(5.6.4’) pary ū ṣu pra dhanvā vājasātaye pari vṛtrāṇi sakṣaṇiḥ |
(5.6.4.c) dviṣas tad adhy arṇaveneyase sanisraso nāmāsi trayodaśo māsa indrasya gṛhaḥ ‖4‖
(5.6.5’) nv etenārātsīr asau svāhā |
(5.6.5.c) tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suṣevau somārudrāv iha su mṛḍataṃ naḥ ‖5‖
(5.6.6’) avaitenārātsīr asau svāhā |
(5.6.6.c) tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suṣevau somārudrāv iha su mṛḍataṃ naḥ ‖6‖
(5.6.7’) apaitenārātsīr asau svāhā |
(5.6.7.c) tigmāyudhau tigmahetī suṣevau somārudrāv iha su mṛḍataṃ naḥ ‖7‖
(5.6.8’) mumuktam asmān duritād avadyāj juṣethām yajñam amṛtam asmāsu dhattam ‖8‖
(5.6.9’) cakṣuṣo hete manaso hete brahmaṇo hete tapasaś ca hete |
(5.6.9.c) menyā menir asy amenayas te santu ye 'smā;m abhyaghāyanti ‖9‖
(5.6.10’) yo 'smāṃś cakṣuṣā manasā cittyākūtyā ca yo aghāyur abhidāsāt |
(5.6.10.c) tvaṃ tān agne menyāmenīn kṛṇu svāhā ‖10‖
(5.6.11’) indrasya gṛho 'si |
(5.6.11.c) taṃ tvā pra padye taṃ tvā pra viśāmi sarvaguḥ sarvapūruṣaḥ sarvātmā sarvatanūḥ saha yan me 'sti tena ‖

(5.7.1’) ā no bhara mā pari ṣṭhā arāte mā no rakṣīr dakṣiṇāṃ nīyamānām |
(5.7.1.c) namo vīrtsāyā asamṛddhaye namo astv arātaye ‖1‖
(5.7.2’) yam arāte purodhatse puruṣaṃ parirāpiṇam |
(5.7.2.c) namas te tasmai kṛṇmo mā vaniṃ vyathayīr mama ‖2‖
(5.7.3’) pra ṇo vanir devakṛtā divā naktaṃ ca kalpatām |
(5.7.3.c) arātim anupremo vayaṃ namo astv arātaye ‖3‖
(5.7.4’) sarasvatīm anumatim bhagam yanto havāmahe |
(5.7.4.c) vācam juṣṭāṃ madhumatīm avādiṣaṃ devānāṃ devahūtiṣu ‖4‖
(5.7.5’) yaṃ yācāmy ahaṃ vācā sarasvatyā manoyujā |
(5.7.5.c) śraddhā tam adya vindatu dattā somena babhruṇā ‖5‖
(5.7.6’) mā vaniṃ mā vācaṃ no vīrtsīr ubhāv indrāgnī ā bharatāṃ no vasūni |
(5.7.6.c) sarve no adya ditsanto 'rātiṃ prati haryata ‖6‖
(5.7.7’) paro 'pehy asamṛddhe vi te hetim nayāmasi |
(5.7.7.c) veda tvāhaṃ nimīvantīṃ nitudantīm arāte ‖7‖
(5.7.8’) uta nagnā bobhuvatī svapnayā sacase janam |
(5.7.8.c) arāte cittam vīrtsanty ākūtim puruṣasya ca ‖8‖
(5.7.9’) yā mahatī mahonmānā viśvā āśā vyānaśe |
(5.7.9.c) tasyai hiraṇyakeśyai nirṛtyā akaraṃ namaḥ ‖9‖
(5.7.10’) hiraṇyavarṇā subhagā hiraṇyakaśipur mahī |
(5.7.10.c) tasyai hiraṇyadrāpaye 'rātyā akaraṃ namaḥ ‖10‖

(5.8.1’) vaikaṅkatenedhmena devebhya ājyaṃ vaha |
(5.8.1.c) agne tām iha mādaya sarva ā yantu me havam ‖1‖
(5.8.2’) indrā yāhi me havam idaṃ kariṣyāmi tac chṛṇu |
(5.8.2.c) ima aindrā atisarā ākūtiṃ saṃ namantu me |
(5.8.2.e) tebhiḥ śakema vīryaṃ jātavedas tanūvaśin ‖2‖
(5.8.3’) yad asāv amuto devā adevaḥ saṃś cikīrṣati |
(5.8.3.c) mā tasyāgnir havyaṃ vākṣīd dhavaṃ devā asya mopa gur mamaiva havam etana ‖3‖
(5.8.4’) ati dhāvatātisarā indrasya vacasā hata |
(5.8.4.c) aviṃ vṛka iva mathnīta sa vo jīvan mā moci prānam asyāpi nahyata ‖4‖
(5.8.5’) yam amī purodadhire brahmāṇam apabhūtaye |
(5.8.5.c) indra sa te adhaspadaṃ taṃ praty asyāmi mṛtyave ‖5‖
(5.8.6’) yadi preyur devapurā brahma varmāṇi cakrire |
(5.8.6.c) tanūpānaṃ paripāṇaṃ kṛṇvānā yad upocire sarvaṃ tad arasaṃ kṛdhi ‖6‖
(5.8.7’) yān asāv atisarāṃś cakāra kṛṇavac ca yān |
(5.8.7.c) tvaṃ tān indra vṛtrahan pratīcaḥ punar ā kṛdhi yathāmuṃ tṛṇahāṃ janam ‖7‖
(5.8.8’) yathendra udvācanaṃ labdhvā cakre adhaspadam |
(5.8.8.c) kṛṇve 'ham adharān tathā amūñ chaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyaḥ ‖8‖
(5.8.9’) atrainān indra vṛtrahann ugro marmaṇi vidhya |
(5.8.9.c) atraivainān abhi tiṣṭhendra medy ahaṃ tava |
(5.8.9.e) anu tvendrā rabhāmahe syāma sumatau tava ‖9‖

(5.9.1’) dive svāhā ‖1‖
(5.9.2’) pṛthivyai svāhā ‖2‖
(5.9.3’) antarikṣāya svāhā ‖3‖
(5.9.4’) antarikṣāya svāhā ‖4‖
(5.9.5’) dive svāhā ‖5‖
(5.9.6’) pṛthivyai svāhā ‖6‖
(5.9.7’) sūryo me cakṣur vātaḥ prāṇo 'ntarikṣam ātmā pṛthivī śarīram |

(5.9.8’) ud āyur ud balam ut kṛtam ut kṛtyām un manīṣām ud indriyam |

(5.10.1’) aśmavarma me 'si yo mā prācyā diśo 'ghāyur abhidāsāt |
(5.10.1.c) etat sa ṛchāt ‖1‖
(5.10.2’) aśmavarma me 'si yo mā dakṣiṇāyā diśo 'ghāyur abhidāsāt |
(5.10.2.c) etat sa ṛchāt ‖2‖
(5.10.3’) aśmavarma me 'si yo mā pratīcyā diśo 'ghāyur abhidāsāt |
(5.10.3.c) etat sa ṛchāt ‖3‖
(5.10.4’) aśmavarma me 'si yo modīcyā diśo 'ghāyur abhidāsāt |
(5.10.4.c) etat sa ṛchāt ‖4‖
(5.10.5’) aśmavarma me 'si yo mā dhruvāyā diśo 'ghāyur abhidāsāt |
(5.10.5.c) etat sa ṛchāt ‖5‖
(5.10.6’) aśmavarma me 'si yo mordhvāyā diśo 'ghāyur abhidāsāt |
(5.10.6.c) etat sa ṛchāt ‖6‖
(5.10.7’) aśmavarma me 'si yo mā diśām antardeśebhyo 'ghāyur abhidāsāt |
(5.10.7.c) etat sa ṛchāt ‖7‖
(5.10.8’) bṛhatā mana upa hvaye mātariśvanā prāṇāpānau |
(5.10.8.c) sūryāc cakṣur antarikṣāc chrotraṃ pṛthivyāḥ śarīram |
(5.10.8.e) sarasvatyā vācam upa hvayāmahe manoyujā ‖8‖

(5.11.1’) kathaṃ mahe asurāyābravīr iha kathaṃ pitre haraye tveṣanṛmṇaḥ |
(5.11.1.c) pṛśniṃ varuṇa dakṣiṇāṃ dadāvān punarmagha tvaṃ manasācikitsīḥ ‖1‖
(5.11.2’) na kāmena punarmagho bhavāmi saṃ cakṣe kaṃ pṛśnim etām upāje |
(5.11.2.c) kena nu tvam atharvan kāvyena kena jātenāsi jātavedāḥ ‖2‖
(5.11.3’) satyam ahaṃ gabhīraḥ kāvyena satyaṃ jātenāsmi jātavedāḥ |
(5.11.3.c) na me dāso nāryo mahitvā vrataṃ mīmāya yad ahaṃ dhariṣye ‖3‖
(5.11.4’) na tvad anyaḥ kavitaro na medhayā dhīrataro varuṇa svadhāvan |
(5.11.4.c) tvaṃ tā viśvā bhuvanāni vettha sa cin nu tvaj jano māyī bibhāya ‖4‖
(5.11.5’) tvaṃ hy aṅga varuṇa svadhāvan viśvā vettha janima supraṇīte |
(5.11.5.c) kiṃ rajasa enā paro anyad asty enā kiṃ pareṇāvaram amura ‖5‖
(5.11.6’) ekaṃ rajasa enā paro anyad asty enā para ekena durṇaśaṃ cid arvāk |
(5.11.6.c) tat te vidvān varuṇa pra bravīmy adhovacasaḥ paṇayo bhavantu nīcair dāsā upa sarpantu bhūmim ‖6‖
(5.11.7’) tvaṃ hy aṅga varuṇa bravīṣi punarmagheṣv avadyāni bhūri |
(5.11.7.c) mo ṣu paṇī;mr abhy etāvato bhūn mā tvā vocann arādhasaṃ janāsaḥ ‖7‖
(5.11.8’) mā mā vocann arādhasaṃ janāsaḥ punas te pṛśniṃ jaritar dadāmi |
(5.11.8.c) stotraṃ me viśvam ā yāhi śacībhir antar viśvāsu mānuṣīṣu dikṣu ‖8‖
(5.11.9’) ā te stotrāṇy udyatāni yantv antar viśvāsu mānuṣīṣu dikṣu |
(5.11.9.c) dehi nu me yan me adatto asi yujyo me saptapadaḥ sakhāsi ‖9‖
(5.11.10’) samā nau bandhur varuṇa samā jā vedāhaṃ tad yan nāv eṣā samā jā |
(5.11.10.c) dadāmi tad yat te adatto asmi yujyas te saptapadaḥ sakhāsmi ‖10‖
(5.11.11’) devo devāya gṛṇate vayodhā vipro viprāya stuvate sumedhāḥ |
(5.11.11.c) ajījano hi varuṇa svadhāvann atharvāṇaṃ pitaraṃ devabandhum |
(5.11.11.e) tasmā u rādhaḥ kṛṇuhi supraśastaṃ sakhā no asi paramaṃ ca bandhuḥ ‖11‖

(5.12.1’) samiddho adya manuṣo duroṇe devo devān yajasi jātavedaḥ |
(5.12.1.c) ā ca vaha mitramahaś cikitvān tvaṃ dūtaḥ kavir asi pracetāḥ ‖1‖
(5.12.2’) tanūnapāt patha ṛtasya yānān madhvā samañjant svadayā sujihva |
(5.12.2.c) manmāni dhībhir uta yajñam ṛndhan devatrā ca kṛṇuhy adhvaram naḥ ‖2‖
(5.12.3’) ājuhvāna īḍyo bandyaś cā yāhy agne vasubhiḥ sajoṣāḥ |
(5.12.3.c) tvaṃ devānām asi yahva hotā sa enān yakṣīṣito yajīyān ‖3‖
(5.12.4’) prācīnaṃ barhiḥ pradiśā pṛthivyā vastor asyā vṛjyate agre ahnām |
(5.12.4.c) vy u prathate vitaraṃ varīyo devebhyo aditaye syonam ‖4‖
(5.12.5’) vyacasvatīr urviyā vi śrayantāṃ patibhyo na janayaḥ śumbhamānāḥ |
(5.12.5.c) devīr dvāro bṛhatīr viśvaminvā devebhyo bhavata suprāyaṇāḥ ‖5‖
(5.12.6’) ā suṣvayantī yajate upāke uṣāsānaktā sadatāṃ ni yonau |
(5.12.6.c) divye yoṣaṇe bṛhatī surukme adhi śriyaṃ śukrapiśaṃ dadhāne ‖6‖
(5.12.7’) daivyā hotārā prathamā suvācā mimānā yajñaṃ manuṣo yajadhyai |
(5.12.7.c) pracodayantā vidatheṣu kārū prācīnaṃ jyotiḥ pradiśā diśantā ‖7‖
(5.12.8’) ā no yajñaṃ bhāratī tūyam etv iḍā manuṣvad iha cetayantī |
(5.12.8.c) tisro devīr barhir edaṃ syonaṃ sarasvatīḥ svapasaḥ sadantām ‖8‖
(5.12.9’) ya ime dyāvāpṛthivī janitrī rūpair apiṃśad bhuvanāni viśvā |
(5.12.9.c) tam adya hotar iṣito yajīyān devaṃ tvaṣṭāram iha yakṣi vidvān ‖9‖
(5.12.10’) upāvasṛja tmanyā samañjan devānāṃ pātha ṛtuthā havīṃṣi |
(5.12.10.c) vanaspatiḥ śamitā devo agniḥ svadantu havyaṃ madhunā ghṛtena ‖10‖
(5.12.11’) sadyo jāto vy amimīta yajñam agnir devānām abhavat purogāḥ |
(5.12.11.c) asya hotuḥ praśiṣy ṛtasya vāci svāhākṛtaṃ havir adantu devāḥ ‖11‖

(5.13.1’) dadir hi mahyaṃ varuṇo divaḥ kavir vacobhir ugrair ni riṇāmi te viṣam |
(5.13.1.c) khātam akhātam uta saktam agrabham ireva dhanvan ni jajāsa te viṣam ‖1‖
(5.13.2’) yat te apodakaṃ viṣaṃ tat ta etāsv agrabham |
(5.13.2.c) gṛhṇāmi te madhyamam uttamaṃ rasam utāvamam bhiyasā neśad ād u te ‖2‖
(5.13.3’) vṛṣā me ravo nabhasā na tanyatur ugreṇa te vacasā bādha ād u te |
(5.13.3.c) ahaṃ tam asya nṛbhir agrabham rasaṃ tamasa iva jyotir ud etu sūryaḥ ‖3‖
(5.13.4’) cakṣuṣā te cakṣur hanmi viṣeṇa hanmi te viṣam |
(5.13.4.c) ahe mriyasva mā jīvīḥ pratyag abhy etu tvā viṣam ‖4‖
(5.13.5’) kairāta pṛśna upatṛṇya babhra ā me śṛṇutāsitā alīkāḥ |
(5.13.5.c) mā me sakhyuḥ stāmānam api ṣṭhātāśrāvayanto ni viṣe ramadhvam ‖5‖
(5.13.6’) asitasya taimātasya babhror apodakasya ca |
(5.13.6.c) sātrāsāhasyāhaṃ manyor ava jyām iva dhanvano vi muñcāmi rathā;m iva ‖6‖
(5.13.7’) āligī ca viligī ca pitā ca matā ca |
(5.13.7.c) vidma vaḥ sarvato bandhv arasāḥ kiṃ kariṣyatha ‖7‖
(5.13.8’) urugūlāyā duhitā jātā dāsy asiknyā |
(5.13.8.c) prataṅkaṃ dadruṣīṇāṃ sarvāsām arasam viṣam ‖8‖
(5.13.9’) karṇā śvāvit tad abravīd girer avacarantikā |
(5.13.9.c) yāḥ kāś cemāḥ khanitrimās tāsām arasatamaṃ viṣam ‖9‖
(5.13.10’) tābuvaṃ na tābuvaṃ na ghet tvam asi tābuvam |
(5.13.10.c) tābuvenārasaṃ viṣam ‖10‖
(5.13.11’) tastuvaṃ na tastuvaṃ na ghet tvam asi tastuvam |
(5.13.11.c) tastuvenārasaṃ viṣam ‖11‖

(5.14.1’) suparṇas tvānv avindat sūkaras tvākhanan nasā |
(5.14.1.c) dipsauṣadhe tvaṃ dipsantam ava kṛtyākṛtaṃ jahi ‖1‖
(5.14.2’) ava jahi yātudhānān ava kṛtyākṛtaṃ jahi |
(5.14.2.c) atho yo asmān dipsati tam u tvaṃ jahy oṣadhe ‖2‖
(5.14.3’) riśyasyeva parīśāsaṃ parikṛtya pari tvacaḥ |
(5.14.3.c) kṛtyāṃ kṛtyākṛte devā niṣkam iva prati muñcata ‖3‖
(5.14.4’) punaḥ kṛtyāṃ kṛtyākṛte hastagṛhya parā ṇaya |
(5.14.4.c) samakṣam asma ā dhehi yathā kṛtyākṛtam hanat ‖4‖
(5.14.5’) kṛtyāḥ santu kṛtyākṛte śapathaḥ śapathīyate |
(5.14.5.c) sukho ratha iva vartatāṃ kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtaṃ punaḥ ‖5‖
(5.14.6’) yadi strī yadi vā pumān kṛtyāṃ cakāra pāpmane |
(5.14.6.c) tām u tasmai nayāmasy aśvam ivāśvābhidhānyā ‖6‖
(5.14.7’) yadi vāsi devakṛtā yadi vā puruṣaiḥ kṛtā |
(5.14.7.c) tāṃ tvā punar ṇayāmasīndreṇa sayujā vayam ‖7‖
(5.14.8’) agne pṛtanāṣāṭ pṛtanāḥ sahasva |
(5.14.8.c) punaḥ kṛtyāṃ kṛtyākṛte pratiharaṇena harāmasi ‖8‖
(5.14.9’) kṛtavyadhani vidya taṃ yaś cakāra tam ij jahi |
(5.14.9.c) na tvām acakruṣe vayaṃ vadhāya saṃ śiśīmahi ‖9‖
(5.14.10’) putra iva pitaraṃ gacha svaja ivābhiṣṭhito daśa |
(5.14.10.c) bandham ivāvakrāmī gacha kṛtye kṛtyākṛtaṃ punaḥ ‖10‖
(5.14.11’) ud eṇīva vāraṇy abhiskandhaṃ mṛgīva |
(5.14.11.c) kṛtyā kartāram ṛchatu ‖11‖
(5.14.12’) iṣvā ṛjīyaḥ patatu dyāvāpṛthivī taṃ prati |
(5.14.12.c) sā taṃ mṛgam iva gṛhṇatu kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtaṃ punaḥ ‖12‖
(5.14.13’) agnir ivaitu pratikūlam anukūlam ivodakam |
(5.14.13.c) sukho ratha iva vartatāṃ kṛtyā kṛtyākṛtaṃ punaḥ ‖13‖

(5.15.1’) ekā ca me daśa ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.1.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖1‖
(5.15.2’) dve ca me viṃśatiś ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.2.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖2‖
(5.15.3’) tisraś ca me triṃśac ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.3.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖3‖
(5.15.4’) catasraś ca me catvāriṃśac ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.4.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karah ‖4‖
(5.15.5’) pañca ca me pañcāśac ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.5.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖5‖
(5.15.6’) ṣaṭ ca me ṣaṣṭiś ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.6.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖6‖
(5.15.7’) sapta ca me saptatiś ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.7.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖7‖
(5.15.8’) aṣṭa ca me 'śītiś ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.8.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖8‖
(5.15.9’) nava ca me navatiś ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.9.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖9‖
(5.15.10’) daśa ca me śataṃ ca me 'pavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.10.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖10‖
(5.15.11’) śataṃ ca me sahasraṃ cāpavaktāra oṣadhe |
(5.15.11.c) ṛtajāta ṛtāvari madhu me madhulā karaḥ ‖11‖

(5.16.1’) yady ekavṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖1‖
(5.16.2’) yadi dvivṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖2‖
(5.16.3’) yadi trivṛso 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖3‖
(5.16.4’) yadi caturvṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖4‖
(5.16.5’) yadi pañcavṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖5‖
(5.16.6’) yadi ṣaḍvṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖6‖
(5.16.7’) yadi saptavṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖7‖
(5.16.8’) yady aṣṭavṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖8‖
(5.16.9’) yadi navavṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖9‖
(5.16.10’) yadi daśavṛṣo 'si sṛjāraso 'si ‖10‖
(5.16.11’) yady ekādaśo 'si so 'podako 'si ‖11‖

(5.17.1’) te 'vadan prathamā brahmakilbiṣe 'kūpāraḥ salilo mātariśvā |
(5.17.1.c) vīḍuharās tapa ugraṃ mayobhūr āpo devīḥ prathamajā ṛtasya ‖1‖
(5.17.2’) somo rājā prathamo brahmajāyāṃ punaḥ prāyachad ahṛṇīyamānaḥ |
(5.17.2.c) anvartitā varuṇo mitra āsīd agnir hotā hastagṛhyā nināya ‖2‖
(5.17.3’) hastenaiva grāhya ādhir asyā brahmajāyeti ced avocat |
(5.17.3.c) na dūtāya praheyā tastha eṣā tathā rāṣṭraṃ gupitaṃ kṣatriyasya ‖3‖
(5.17.4’) yām āhus tārakaiṣā vikeśīti duchunāṃ grāmam avapadyamānām |
(5.17.4.c) sā brahmajāyā vi dunoti rāṣṭraṃ yatra prāpādi śaśa ulkuṣīmān ‖4‖
(5.17.5’) brahmacārī carati veviṣad viṣaḥ sa devānāṃ bhavaty ekam aṅgam |
(5.17.5.c) tena jāyām anv avindad bṛhaspatiḥ somena nītāṃ juhvaṃ na devāḥ ‖5‖
(5.17.6’) devā vā etasyām avadanta pūrve saptaṛṣayas tapasā ye niṣeduḥ |
(5.17.6.c) bhīmā jāyā brāhmaṇasyāpanītā durdhāṃ dadhāti parame vyoman ‖6‖
(5.17.7’) ye garbhā avapadyante jagad yac cāpalupyate |
(5.17.7.c) vīrā ye tṛhyante mitho brahmajāyā hinasti tān ‖7‖
(5.17.8’) uta yat patayo daśa striyāḥ pūrve abrāhmaṇāḥ |
(5.17.8.c) brahmā ced dhastam agrahīt sa eva patir ekadhā ‖8‖
(5.17.9’) brāhmaṇa eva patir na rājanyo na vaiśyaḥ |
(5.17.9.c) tat sūryaḥ prabruvann eti pañcabhyo mānavebhyaḥ ‖9‖
(5.17.10’) punar vai devā adaduḥ punar manuṣyā adaduḥ |
(5.17.10.c) rājānaḥ satyaṃ gṛhṇānā brahmajāyāṃ punar daduḥ ‖10‖
(5.17.11’) punardāya brahmajāyāṃ kṛtvā devair nikilbiṣam |
(5.17.11.c) ūrjaṃ pṛthivyā bhaktvorugāyam upāsate ‖11‖
(5.17.12’) nāsya jāyā śatavāhī kalyāṇī talpam ā śaye |
(5.17.12.c) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ‖12‖
(5.17.13’) na vikarṇaḥ pṛthuśirās tasmin veśmani jāyate |
(5.17.13.c) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ‖13‖
(5.17.14’) nāsya kṣattā niṣkagrīvaḥ sūnānām ety agrataḥ |
(5.17.14.c) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ‖14‖
(5.17.15’) nāsya śvetaḥ kṛṣṇakarṇo dhuri yukto mahīyate |
(5.17.15.c) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ‖15‖
(5.17.16’) nāsya kṣetre puṣkariṇī nāṇḍīkaṃ jāyate bisam |
(5.17.16.c) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ‖16‖
(5.17.17’) nāsmai pṛśniṃ vi duhanti ye 'syā doham upāsate |
(5.17.17.c) yasmin rāṣṭre nirudhyate brahmajāyācittyā ‖17‖
(5.17.18’) nāsya dhenuḥ kalyāṇī nānaḍvānt sahate dhuram |
(5.17.18.c) vijānir yatra brahmaṇo rātriṃ vasati pāpayā ‖18‖

(5.18.1’) naitāṃ te devā adadus tubhyaṃ nṛpate attave |
(5.18.1.c) mā brāhmaṇasya rājanya gāṃ jighatso anādyām ‖1‖
(5.18.2’) akṣadrugdho rājanyaḥ pāpa ātmaparājitaḥ |
(5.18.2.c) sa brāhmaṇasya gām adyād adya jīvāni mā śvaḥ ‖2‖
(5.18.3’) āviṣṭitāghaviṣā pṛdākūr iva carmaṇā |
(5.18.3.c) sā brāhmaṇasya rājanya tṛṣṭaiṣā gaur anādyā ‖3‖
(5.18.4’) nir vai kṣatraṃ nayati hanti varco 'gnir ivārabdho vi dunoti sarvam |
(5.18.4.c) yo brāhmaṇaṃ manyate annam eva sa viṣasya pibati taimātasya ‖4‖
(5.18.5’) ya enaṃ hanti mṛduṃ manyamāno devapīyur dhanakāmo na cittāt |
(5.18.5.c) saṃ tasyendro hṛdaye 'gnim indhe ubhe enaṃ dviṣṭo nabhasī carantam ‖5‖
(5.18.6’) na brāhmaṇo hiṃsitavyo 'gniḥ priyatanor iva |
(5.18.6.c) somo hy asya dāyāda indro asyābhiśastipāḥ ‖6‖
(5.18.7’) śatāpāṣṭhāṃ ni girati tāṃ na śaknoti niḥkhidam |
(5.18.7.c) annaṃ yo brahmaṇām malvaḥ svādv admīti manyate ‖7‖
(5.18.8’) jihvā jyā bhavati kulmalaṃ vāṅ nāḍīkā dantās tapasābhidigdhāḥ |
(5.18.8.c) tebhir brahmā vidhyati devapīyūn hṛdbalair dhanurbhir devajūtaiḥ ‖8‖
(5.18.9’) tīkṣṇeṣavo brāhmaṇā hetimanto yām asyanti śaravyāṃ na sā mṛṣā |
(5.18.9.c) anuhāya tapasā manyunā cota durād ava bhindanty enam ‖9‖
(5.18.10’) ye sahasram arājann āsan daśaśatā uta |
(5.18.10.c) te brāhmaṇasya gāṃ jagdhvā vaitahavyāḥ parābhavan ‖10‖
(5.18.11’) gaur eva tān hanyamānā vaitahavyā;m avātirat |
(5.18.11.c) ye kesaraprābandhāyāś caramājām apeciran ‖11‖
(5.18.12’) ekaśataṃ tā janatā yā bhūmir vyadhūnuta |
(5.18.12.c) prajāṃ hiṃsitvā brāhmaṇīm asaṃbhavyaṃ parābhavan ‖12‖
(5.18.13’) devapīyuś carati martyeṣu garagīrṇo bhavaty asthibhūyān |
(5.18.13.c) yo brāhmaṇaṃ devabandhuṃ hinasti na sa pitṛyāṇam apy eti lokam ‖13‖
(5.18.14’) agnir vai naḥ padavāyaḥ somo dāyāda ucyate |
(5.18.14.c) hantābhiśastendras tathā tad vedhaso viduḥ ‖14‖
(5.18.15’) iṣur iva digdhā nṛpate pṛdākūr iva gopate |
(5.18.15.c) sā brāhmaṇasyeṣur ghorā tayā vidhyati pīyataḥ ‖15‖

(5.19.1’) atimātram avardhanta nod iva divam aspṛśan |
(5.19.1.c) bhṛguṃ hiṃsitvā sṛñjayā vaitahavyāḥ parābhavan ‖1‖
(5.19.2’) ye bṛhatsāmānam āṅgirasam ārpayan brāhmaṇaṃ janāḥ |
(5.19.2.c) petvas teṣām ubhayādam avis tokāny āvayat ‖2‖
(5.19.3’) ye brāhmaṇaṃ pratyaṣṭhīvan ye vāsmiñ chulkam īṣire |
(5.19.3.c) asnas te madhye kulyāyāḥ keśān khādanta āsate ‖3‖
(5.19.4’) brahmagavī pacyamānā yāvat sābhi vijaṅgahe |
(5.19.4.c) tejo rāṣṭrasya nir hanti na vīro jāyate vṛṣā ‖4‖
(5.19.5’) krūram asyā āśasanaṃ tṛṣṭaṃ piśitam asyate |
(5.19.5.c) kṣīraṃ yad asyāḥ pīyate tad vai pitṛṣu kilbiṣam ‖5‖
(5.19.6’) ugro rājā manyamāno brāhmaṇaṃ yo jighatsati |
(5.19.6.c) parā tat sicyate rāṣṭraṃ brāhmaṇo yatra jīyate ‖6‖
(5.19.7’) aṣṭāpadī caturakṣī catuḥśrotrā caturhanuḥ |
(5.19.7.c) dvyāsyā dvijihvā bhūtvā sā rāṣṭram ava dhūnute brahmajyasya ‖7‖
(5.19.8’) tad vai rāṣṭram ā sravati nāvaṃ bhinnām ivodakam |
(5.19.8.c) brahmāṇaṃ yatra hiṃsanti tad rāṣṭraṃ hanti duchunā ‖8‖
(5.19.9’) taṃ vṛkṣā apa sedhanti chāyāṃ no mopa gā iti |
(5.19.9.c) yo brāhmaṇasya sad dhanam abhi nārada manyate ‖9‖
(5.19.10’) viṣam etad devakṛtaṃ rājā varuṇo 'bravīt |
(5.19.10.c) na brāhmaṇasya gāṃ jagdhvā rāstre jāgāra kaś cana ‖10‖
(5.19.11’) navaiva tā navatayo yā bhūmir vyadhūnuta |
(5.19.11.c) prajāṃ hiṃsitvā brāhmaṇīm asaṃbhavyaṃ parābhavan ‖11‖
(5.19.12’) yām mṛtāyānubadhnanti kūdyaṃ padayopanīm |
(5.19.12.c) tad vai brahmajya te devā upastaraṇam abruvan ‖12‖
(5.19.13’) aśrūṇi kṛpamānasya yāni jītasya vāvṛtuḥ |
(5.19.13.c) taṃ vai brahmajya te devā apāṃ bhāgam adhārayan ‖13‖
(5.19.14’) yena mṛtaṃ snapayanti śmaśrūṇi yenondate |
(5.19.14.c) taṃ vai brahmajya te devā apāṃ bhāgam adhārayan ‖14‖
(5.19.15’) na varṣaṃ maitrāvaruṇaṃ brahmajyam abhi varṣati |
(5.19.15.c) nāsmai samitiḥ kalpate na mitraṃ nayate vaśam ‖15‖

(5.20.1’) uccairghoṣo dundubhiḥ satvanāyan vānaspatyaḥ saṃbhṛta usṛiyābhiḥ |
(5.20.1.c) vācaṃ kṣuṇuvāno damayant sapatnānt siṃha iva jeṣyann abhi taṃstanīhi ‖1‖
(5.20.2’) siṃha ivāstānīd druvayo vibaddho 'bhikrandann ṛṣabho vāsitām iva |
(5.20.2.c) vṛṣā tvaṃ vadhrayas te sapatnā aindras te śuṣmo abhimātiṣāhaḥ ‖2‖
(5.20.3’) vṛṣeva yūthe sahasā vidāno gavyann abhi ruva saṃdhanājit |
(5.20.3.c) śucā vidhya hṛdayaṃ pareṣāṃ hitvā grāmān pracyutā yantu śatravaḥ ‖3‖
(5.20.4’) saṃjayan pṛtanā ūrdhvamāyur gṛhyā gṛhṇāno bahudhā vi cakṣva |
(5.20.4.c) daivīṃ vācaṃ dundubha ā gurasva vedhāḥ śatrūṇām upa bharasva vedaḥ ‖4‖
(5.20.5’) dundubher vācaṃ prayatāṃ vadantīm āśṛṇvatī nāthitā ghoṣabuddhā |
(5.20.5.c) nārī putraṃ dhāvatu hastagṛhyāmitrī bhītā samare vadhānām ‖5‖
(5.20.6’) pūrvo dundubhe pra vadāsi vācaṃ bhūmyāḥ pṛṣṭhe vada rocamānaḥ |
(5.20.6.c) amitrasenām abhijañjabhāno dyumad vada dundubhe sūnṛtāvat ‖6‖
(5.20.7’) antareme nabhasī ghoṣo astu pṛthak te dhvanayo yantu śībham |
(5.20.7.c) abhi kranda stanayotpipānaḥ ślokakṛn mitratūryāya svardhī ‖7‖
(5.20.8’) dhībhiḥ kṛtaḥ pra vadāti vācam ud dharṣaya satvanām āyudhāni |
(5.20.8.c) indramedī satvano ni hvayasva mitrair amitrā;m ava jaṅghanīhi ‖8‖
(5.20.9’) saṃkrandanaḥ pravado dhṛṣṇuṣeṇaḥ pravedakṛd bahudhā grāmaghoṣī |
(5.20.9.c) śriyo vanvano vayunāni vidvān kīrtim bahubhyo vi hara dvirāje ‖9‖
(5.20.10’) śreyaḥketo vasujit sahīyānt saṃgrāmajit saṃśito brahmaṇāsi |
(5.20.10.c) aṃśūn iva grāvādhiṣavaṇe adrir gavyan dundubhe'dhi nṛtya vedaḥ ‖10‖
(5.20.11’) śatrūṣāṇ nīṣād abhimātiṣāho gaveṣaṇaḥ sahamāna udbhit |
(5.20.11.c) vāgvīva mantraṃ pra bharasva vācam sāṃgrāmajityāyeṣam ud vadeha ‖11‖
(5.20.12’) acyutacyut samado gamiṣṭho mṛdho jetā puraetāyodhyaḥ |
(5.20.12.c) indreṇa gupto vidathā nicikyad dhṛddyotano dviṣatāṃ yāhi śībham ‖12‖

(5.21.1’) vihṛdayaṃ vaimanasyaṃ vadāmitreṣu dundubhe |
(5.21.1.c) vidveṣaṃ kaśmaśaṃ bhayam amitreṣu ni dadhmasy ava enān dundubhe jahi ‖1‖
(5.21.2’) udvepamānā manasā cakṣuṣā hṛdayena ca |
(5.21.2.c) dhāvantu bibhyato 'mitrāḥ pratrāsenājye hute ‖2‖
(5.21.3’) vānaspatyaḥ saṃbhṛta usriyābhir viśvagotryaḥ |
(5.21.3.c) pratrāsam amitrebhyo vadājyenābhighāritaḥ ‖3‖
(5.21.4’) yathā mṛgāḥ saṃvijanta āraṇyāḥ puruṣād adhi |
(5.21.4.c) eva tvaṃ dundubhe 'mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya ‖4‖
(5.21.5’) yathā vṛkād ajāvayo dhāvanti bahu bibhyatīḥ |
(5.21.5.c) eva tvaṃ dundubhe 'mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya ‖5‖
(5.21.6’) yathā śyenāt patatriṇaḥ saṃvijante ahardivi siṃhasya stanathor yathā |
(5.21.6.c) eva tvaṃ dundubhe 'mitrān abhi kranda pra trāsayātho cittāni mohaya ‖6‖
(5.21.7’) parāmitrān dundubhinā hariṇasyājinena ca |
(5.21.7.c) sarve devā atitrasan ye saṃgrāmasyeṣate ‖7‖
(5.21.8’) yair indraḥ prakrīḍate padghoṣaiś chāyayā saha |
(5.21.8.c) tair amitrās trasantu no 'mī ye yanty anīkaśaḥ ‖8‖
(5.21.9’) jyāghoṣā dundubhayo 'bhi krośantu yā diśaḥ |
(5.21.9.c) senāḥ parājitā yatīr amitrāṇām anīkaśaḥ ‖9‖
(5.21.10’) āditya cakṣur ā datsva marīcayo 'nu dhāvata |
(5.21.10.c) patsaṅginīr ā sajantu vigate bāhuvīrye ‖10‖
(5.21.11’) yūyam ugrā marutaḥ pṛśnimātara indreṇa yujā pra mṛnīta śatrūn |
(5.21.11.c) somo rājā varuṇo rājā mahādeva uta mṛtyur indraḥ ‖11‖
(5.21.12’) etā devasenāḥ sūryaketavaḥ sacetasaḥ |
(5.21.12.c) amitrān no jayantu svāhā ‖12‖

(5.22.1’) agnis takmānam apa bādhatām itaḥ somo grāvā varuṇaḥ pūtadakṣāḥ |
(5.22.1.c) vedir barhiḥ samidhaḥ śośucānā apa dveṣāṃsy amuyā bhavantu ‖1‖
(5.22.2’) ayaṃ yo viśvān haritān kṛṇoṣy ucchocayann agnir ivābhidunvan |
(5.22.2.c) adhā hi takmann araso hi bhūyā adhā nyaṅṅ adharān vā parehi ‖2‖
(5.22.3’) yaḥ paruṣaḥ pāruṣeyo 'vadhvaṃsa ivāruṇaḥ |
(5.22.3.c) takmānaṃ viśvadhāvīryādharāñcaṃ parā suvā ‖3‖
(5.22.4’) adharāñcam pra hiṇomi namaḥ kṛtvā takmane |
(5.22.4.c) śakambharasya muṣṭihā punar etu mahāvṛṣān ‖4‖
(5.22.5’) oko asya mūjavanta oko asya mahāvṛṣāḥ |
(5.22.5.c) yāvaj jātas takmaṃs tāvān asi balhikeṣu nyocaraḥ ‖5‖
(5.22.6’) takman vyāla vi gada vyaṅga bhūri yāvaya |
(5.22.6.c) dāsīṃ niṣṭakvarīm icha tām vajreṇa sam arpaya ‖6‖
(5.22.7’) takman mūjavato gacha balhikān vā parastarām |
(5.22.7.c) śūdrām icha prapharvyaṃ tāṃ takman vīva dhūnuhi ‖7‖
(5.22.8’) mahāvṛṣān mūjavato bandhv addhi paretya |
(5.22.8.c) praitāni takmane brūmo anyakṣetrāṇi vā imā ‖8‖
(5.22.9’) anyakṣetre na ramase vaśī san mṛḍayāsi naḥ |
(5.22.9.c) abhūd u prārthas takmā sa gamiṣyati balhikān ‖9‖
(5.22.10’) yat tvaṃ śīto 'tho rūraḥ saha kāsāvepayaḥ |
(5.22.10.c) bhīmās te takman hetayas tābhiḥ sma pari vṛṅgdhi naḥ ‖10‖
(5.22.11’) mā smaitānt sakhīn kuruthā balāsaṃ kāsam udyugam |
(5.22.11.c) mā smāto'rvāṅ aiḥ punas tat tvā takmann upa bruve ‖11‖
(5.22.12’) takman bhrātrā balāsena svasrā kāsikayā saha |
(5.22.12.c) pāpmā bhrātṛvyeṇa saha gachāmum araṇaṃ janam ‖12‖
(5.22.13’) tṛtīyakaṃ vitṛtīyaṃ sadandim uta śāradam |
(5.22.13.c) takmānaṃ śītaṃ rūraṃ graiṣmaṃ nāśaya vārṣikam ‖13‖
(5.22.14’) gandhāribhyo mūjavadbhyo 'ṅgebhyo magadhebhyaḥ |
(5.22.14.c) praiṣyan janam iva śevadhiṃ takmānaṃ pari dadmasi ‖14‖

(5.23.1’) ote me dyāvāpṛthivī otā devī sarasvatī |
(5.23.1.c) otau ma indraś cāgniś ca krimiṃ jambhayatām iti ‖1‖
(5.23.2’) asyendra kumārasya krimīn dhanapate jahi |
(5.23.2.c) hatā viśvā arātaya ugreṇa vacasā mama ‖2‖
(5.23.3’) yo akṣyau parisarpati yo nāse parisarpati |
(5.23.3.c) datāṃ yo madyaṃ gachati taṃ krimiṃ jambhayāmasi ‖3‖
(5.23.4’) sarūpau dvau virūpau dvau kṛṣṇau dvau rohitau dvau |
(5.23.4.c) babhruś ca babhrukarṇaś ca gṛdhraḥ kokaś ca te hatāḥ ‖4‖
(5.23.5’) ye krimayaḥ śitikakṣā ye kṛṣṇāḥ śitibāhavaḥ |
(5.23.5.c) ye ke ca viśvarūpās tān krimīn jambhayāmasi ‖5‖
(5.23.6’) ut purastāt sūrya eti viśvadṛṣṭo adṛṣṭahā |
(5.23.6.c) dṛṣṭāṃś ca ghnann adṛṣṭāṃś ca sarvāṃś ca pramṛṇan krimīn ‖6‖
(5.23.7’) yevāṣāsaḥ kaṣkaṣāsa ejatkāḥ śipavitnukāḥ |
(5.23.7.c) dṛṣṭāṃś ca hanyatāṃ krimir utādṛṣṭaś ca hanyatām ‖7‖
(5.23.8’) hato yevāṣaḥ krimīṇāṃ hato nadanimota |
(5.23.8.c) sarvān ni maṣmaṣākaraṃ dṛṣadā khalvā;m iva ‖8‖
(5.23.9’) triśīrṣāṇaṃ trikakudaṃ krimiṃ sāraṅgam arjunam |
(5.23.9.c) śṛṇāmy asya pṛṣṭīr api vṛścāmi yac chiraḥ ‖9‖
(5.23.10’) atrivad vaḥ krimayo hanmi kaṇvavaj jamadagnivat |
(5.23.10.c) agastyasya brahmaṇā saṃ pinaṣmy ahaṃ krimīn ‖10‖
(5.23.11’) hato rājā krimīṇām utaiṣāṃ sthapatir hataḥ |
(5.23.11.c) hato hatamātā krimir hatabhrātā hatasvasā ‖11‖
(5.23.12’) hatāso asya veśaso hatāsaḥ pariveśasaḥ |
(5.23.12.c) atho ye kṣullakā iva sarve te krimayo hatāḥ ‖12‖
(5.23.13’) sarveṣāṃ ca krimīṇāṃ sarvāsāṃ ca krimīnām |
(5.23.13.c) bhinadmy aśmanā śira dahāmy agninā mukham ‖13‖

(5.25.1’) parvatād divo yoner aṅgādaṅgāt samābhṛtam |
(5.25.1.c) śepo garbhasya retodhāḥ sarau parṇam ivā dadhat ‖1‖
(5.25.2’) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī bhūtānāṃ garbham ādadhe |
(5.25.2.c) evā dadhāmi te garbhaṃ tasmai tvām avase huve ‖2‖
(5.25.3’) garbhaṃ dhehi sinīvāli garbhaṃ dhehi sarasvati |
(5.25.3.c) garbhaṃ te aśvinobhā dhattāṃ puṣkarasrajā ‖3‖
(5.25.4’) garbhaṃ te mitrāvaruṇau garbham devo bṛhaspatiḥ |
(5.25.4.c) garbhaṃ ta indraś cāgniś ca garbhaṃ dhātā dadhātu te ‖4‖
(5.25.5’) viṣṇur yoniṃ kalpayatu tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi piṃśatu |
(5.25.5.c) ā siñcatu prajāpatir dhātā garbhaṃ dadhātu te ‖5‖
(5.25.6’) yad veda rājā varuṇo yad vā devī sarasvatī |
(5.25.6.c) yad indro vṛtrahā veda tad garbhakaraṇaṃ piba ‖6‖
(5.25.7’) garbho asy oṣadhīnāṃ garbho vanaspatīnām |
(5.25.7.c) garbho viśvasya bhūtasya so agne garbham eha dhāḥ ‖7‖
(5.25.8’) adhi skanda vīrayasva garbham ā dhehi yonyām |
(5.25.8.c) vṛṣāsi vṛṣṇyāvan prajāyai tvā nayāmasi ‖8‖
(5.25.9’) vi jihīṣva bārhatsāme garbhas te yonim ā śayām |
(5.25.9.c) aduṣ ṭe devāḥ putraṃ somapā ubhayāvinam ‖9‖
(5.25.10’) dhātaḥ śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ |
(5.25.10.c) pumāṃsaṃ putram ā dhehi daśame māsi sūtave ‖10‖
(5.25.11’) tvaṣṭaḥ śreṣṭhena rūpeṇāsyā nāryā gavīnyoḥ |

(5.26.1’) yajūṃṣi yajñe samidhaḥ svāhāgniḥ pravidvān iha vo yunaktu ‖1‖
(5.26.2’) yunaktu devaḥ savitā prajānann asmin yajñe mahiṣaḥ svāhā ‖2‖
(5.26.3’) indra ukthāmadāny asmin yajñe pravidvān yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā ‖3‖
(5.26.4’) praiṣā yajñe nividaḥ svāhā śiṣṭāḥ patnībhir vahateha yuktāḥ ‖4‖
(5.26.5’) chandāṃsi yajñe marutaḥ svāhā māteva putraṃ pipṛteha yuktāḥ ‖5‖
(5.26.6’) eyam agan barhiṣā prokṣaṇībhir yajñaṃ tanvānāditiḥ svāhā ‖6‖
(5.26.7’) viṣṇur yunaktu bahudhā tapāṃsy asmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā ‖7‖
(5.26.8’) tvaṣṭā yunaktu bahudhā nu rūpā asmin yajñe yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā ‖8‖
(5.26.9’) bhago yunaktvāśiṣo nv asmā asmin yajñe pravidvān yunaktu suyujaḥ svāhā ‖9‖
(5.26.10’) somo yunaktu bahudhā payāṃsy asmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā ‖10‖
(5.26.11’) indro yunaktu bahudhā payāṃsy asmin yajñe suyujaḥ svāhā ‖11‖

(5.27.1’) ūrdhvā asya samidho bhavanty ūrdhvā śukrā śocīṣy agneḥ |
(5.27.1.c) dyumattamā supratīkaḥ sasūnus tanūnapād asuro bhūripāṇiḥ ‖1‖
(5.27.2’) devo deveṣu devaḥ patho anakti madhvā ghṛtena ‖2‖
(5.27.3’) madhvā yajñam nakṣati praiṇāno narāśaṃso agniḥ sukṛd devaḥ savitā viśvavāraḥ ‖3‖
(5.27.4’) achāyam eti śavasā ghṛtā cid īdāno vahnir namasā ‖4‖
(5.27.5’) agniḥ sruco adhvareṣu prayakṣu sa yakṣad asya mahimānam agneḥ ‖5‖

(5.27.8’) uruvyacasāgner dhāmnā patyamāne |

(5.27.10’) tan nas turīpam adbhutaṃ purukṣu |

(5.27.11’) vanaspate 'va sṛjā rarāṇaḥ |
(5.27.11.c) tmanā devebhyo agnir havyaṃ śamitā svadayatu ‖11‖
(5.27.12’) agne svāhā kṛṇuhi jātavedaḥ |
