(5.28.1’) nava prāṇān navabhiḥ saṃ mimīte dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya |
(5.28.1.c) harite trīṇi rajate trīṇy ayasi trīṇi tapasāviṣṭhitāni ‖1‖
(5.28.2’) agniḥ sūryaś candramā bhūmir āpo dyaur antarikṣaṃ pradiśo diśaś ca |
(5.28.2.c) ārtavā ṛtubhiḥ saṃvidānā anena mā trivṛtā pārayantu ‖2‖
(5.28.3’) trayaḥ poṣas trivṛti śrayantām anaktu pūṣā payasā ghṛtena |
(5.28.3.c) annasya bhūmā puruṣasya bhūmā bhūmā paśūnāṃ ta iha śrayantām ‖3‖
(5.28.4’) imam ādityā vasunā sam ukṣatemam agne vardhaya vavṛdhānaḥ |
(5.28.4.c) imam indra saṃ sṛja vīryeṇāsmin trivṛc chrayatāṃ poṣayiṣṇu ‖4‖
(5.28.5’) bhūmiṣ ṭvā pātu haritena viśvabhṛd agniḥ pipartv ayasā sajoṣāḥ |
(5.28.5.c) vīrudbhiṣ ṭe arjunaṃ saṃvidānaṃ dakṣaṃ dadhātu sumanasyamānam ‖5‖
(5.28.6’) tredhā jātam janmanedaṃ hiraṇyam agner ekaṃ priyatamaṃ babhūva somasyaikaṃ hiṃsitasya parāpatat |
(5.28.6.c) apām ekaṃ vedhasāṃ reta āhus tat te hiraṇyaṃ trivṛd astv āyuṣe ‖6‖
(5.28.7’) tryāyuṣaṃ jamadagneḥ kaśyapasya tryāyuṣam |
(5.28.7.c) tredhāmṛtasya cakṣaṇaṃ trīṇy āyūṃṣi te 'karam ‖7‖
(5.28.8’) trayah suparṇās trivṛtā yad āyann ekākṣaram abhisaṃbhūya śakrāḥ |
(5.28.8.c) praty auhan mṛtyum amṛtena sākam antardadhānā duritāni viśvā ‖8‖
(5.28.9’) divas tvā pātu haritaṃ madhyāt tvā pātv arjunam |
(5.28.9.c) bhūmyā ayasmayaṃ pātu prāgād devapurā ayam ‖9‖
(5.28.10’) imās tisro devapurās tās tvā rakṣantu sarvataḥ |
(5.28.10.c) tās tvaṃ bibhrad varcasvy uttaro dviṣatāṃ bhava ‖10‖
(5.28.11’) puraṃ devānām amṛtaṃ hiraṇyam ya ābedhe prathamo devo agre |
(5.28.11.c) tasmai namo daśa prācīḥ kṛṇomy anu manyatāṃ trivṛd ābadhe me ‖11‖
(5.28.12’) ā tvā cṛtatv aryamā pūṣā bṛhaspatiḥ |
(5.28.12.c) aharjātasya yan nāma tena tvāti cṛtāmasi ‖12‖
(5.28.13’) ṛtubhiṣ ṭvārtavair āyuṣe varcase tvā |
(5.28.13.c) saṃvatsarasya tejasā tena samhanu kṛṇmasi ‖13‖
(5.28.14’) ghṛtād ulluptam madhunā samaktaṃ bhūmidṛṃham acyutam pārayiṣṇu |
(5.28.14.c) bhindat sapatnān adharāṃś ca kṛṇvad ā mā roha mahate saubhagāya ‖14‖

(5.29.1’) purastād yukto vaha jātavedo 'gne viddhi kriyamāṇam yathedam |
(5.29.1.c) tvaṃ bhiṣag bheṣajasyāsi kartā tvayā gām aśvaṃ puruṣaṃ sanema ‖1‖
(5.29.2’) tathā tad agne kṛṇu jātavedo viśvebhir devaiḥ saha saṃvidānaḥ |
(5.29.2.c) yo no dideva yatamo jaghāsa yathā so asya paridhiṣ patāti ‖2‖
(5.29.3’) yathā so asya paridhiṣ patāti tathā tad agne kṛṇu jātavedaḥ |
(5.29.3.c) viśvebhir devair saha saṃvidānaḥ ‖3‖
(5.29.4’) akṣyau ni vidhya hṛdayaṃ ni vidhya jihvāṃ ni tṛnddhi pra dato mṛṇīhi |
(5.29.4.c) piśāco asya yatamo jaghāsāgne yaviṣṭha prati śṛṇīhi ‖4‖
(5.29.5’) yad asya hṛtaṃ vihṛtaṃ yat parābhṛtam ātmano jagdhaṃ yatamat piśācaiḥ |
(5.29.5.c) tad agne vidvān punar ā bhara tvaṃ śarīre māṃsam asum erayāmaḥ ‖5‖
(5.29.6’) āme supakve śabale vipakve yo mā piśāco aśane dadambha |
(5.29.6.c) tad ātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantām agado 'yam astu ‖6‖
(5.29.7’) kṣire mā manthe yatamo dadambhākṛṣṭapacye aśane dhānye yaḥ |
(5.29.7.c) tad ātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantām agado 'yam astu ‖7‖
(5.29.8’) apāṃ mā pāne yatamo dadambha kravyād yātūnām śayane śayānam |
(5.29.8.c) tad ātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantām agado 'yam astu ‖8‖
(5.29.9’) divā mā naktaṃ yatamo dadambha kravyād yātūnām śayane śayānam |
(5.29.9.c) tad ātmanā prajayā piśācā vi yātayantām agado 'yam astu ‖9‖
(5.29.10’) kravyādam agne rudhiraṃ piśācaṃ manohanaṃ jahi jātavedaḥ |
(5.29.10.c) tam indro vājī vajreṇa hantu chinattu somaḥ śiro asya dhṛṣṇuḥ ‖10‖
(5.29.11’) sanād agne mṛṇasi yātudhānān na tvā rakṣāṃsi pṛtanāsu jigyuḥ |
(5.29.11.c) sahamūrān anu daha kravyādo mā te hetyā mukṣata daivyāyāḥ ‖11‖
(5.29.12’) samāhara jātavedo yad dhṛtaṃ yat parābhṛtam |
(5.29.12.c) gātrāṇy asya vardhantām aṃśur ivā pyāyatām ayam ‖12‖
(5.29.13’) somasyeva jātavedo aṃśur ā pyāyatām ayam |
(5.29.13.c) agne virapśinaṃ medhyam ayakṣmaṃ kṛṇu jīvatu ‖13‖
(5.29.14’) etās te agne samidhaḥ piśācajambhanīḥ |
(5.29.14.c) tās tvaṃ juṣasva prati cainā gṛhāṇa jātavedaḥ ‖14‖
(5.29.15’) tārṣṭāghīr agne samidhaḥ prati gṛhṇāhy arciṣā |
(5.29.15.c) jahātu kravyād rūpaṃ yo asya māṃsaṃ jihīrṣati ‖15‖

(5.30.1’) āvatas ta āvataḥ parāvatas ta āvataḥ |
(5.30.1.c) ihaiva bhava mā nu gā mā pūrvān anu gāḥ pitṝn asuṃ badhnāmi te dṛḍham ‖1‖
(5.30.2’) yat tvābhiceruḥ puruṣaḥ svo yad araṇo janaḥ |
(5.30.2.c) unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te ‖2‖
(5.30.3’) yad dudrohitha śepiṣe striyai puṃse acittyā |
(5.30.3.c) unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te ‖3‖
(5.30.4’) yat enaso mātṛkṛtāc cheṣe pitṛkṛtāc ca yat |
(5.30.4.c) unmocanapramocane ubhe vācā vadāmi te ‖4‖
(5.30.5’) yat te mātā yat te pitā jamir bhrātā ca sarjataḥ |
(5.30.5.c) pratyak sevasva bheṣajaṃ jaradaṣṭiṃ kṛṇomi tvā ‖5‖
(5.30.6’) ihaidhi puruṣa sarveṇa manasā saha |
(5.30.6.c) dūtau yamasya mānu gā adhi jīvapurā ihi ‖6‖
(5.30.7’) anuhūtaḥ punar ehi vidvān udayanaṃ pathaḥ |
(5.30.7.c) ārohaṇam ākramaṇaṃ jīvatojīvato 'yanam ‖7‖
(5.30.8’) mā bibher na mariṣyasi jaradaṣṭiṃ kṛṇomi tvā |
(5.30.8.c) nir avocam ahaṃ yakṣmam aṅgebhyo aṅgajvaraṃ tava ‖8‖
(5.30.9’) aṅgabhedo aṅgajvaro yaś ca te hṛdayāmayaḥ |
(5.30.9.c) yakṣmaḥ śyena iva prāpaptad vacā sāḍhaḥ parastarām ‖9‖
(5.30.10’) ṛṣī bodhapratībodhāv asvapno yaś ca jāgṛviḥ |
(5.30.10.c) tau te prāṇasya goptārau divā naktaṃ ca jāgṛtām ‖10‖
(5.30.11’) ayam agnir upasadya iha sūrya ud etu te |
(5.30.11.c) udehi mṛtyor gambhīrāt kṛṣṇāc cit tamasas pari ‖11‖
(5.30.12’) namo yamāya namo astu mṛtyave namaḥ pitṛbhya uta ye nayanti |
(5.30.12.c) utpāraṇasya yo veda tam agniṃ puro dadhe 'smā ariṣṭatātaye ‖12‖
(5.30.13’) aitu prāṇa aitu mana aitu cakṣur atho balam |
(5.30.13.c) śarīram asya sam vidāṃ tat padbhyāṃ prati tiṣṭhatu ‖13‖
(5.30.14’) prāṇenāgne cakṣuṣā saṃ sṛjemaṃ sam īraya tanvā saṃ balena |
(5.30.14.c) vetthāmṛtasya mā nu gān mā nu bhūmigṛho bhuvat ‖14‖
(5.30.15’) mā te prāṇa upa dasan mo apāno 'pi dhāyi te |
(5.30.15.c) sūryas tvādhipatir mṛtyor udāyachatu raśmibhiḥ ‖15‖
(5.30.16’) ivam antar vadati jihvā baddhā paniṣpadā |
(5.30.16.c) tvayā yakṣmam nir avocaṃ śataṃ ropīś ca takmanaḥ ‖16‖
(5.30.17’) ayaṃ lokaḥ priyatamo devānām aparājitaḥ |
(5.30.17.c) yasmai tvam iha mṛtyave diṣṭaḥ puruṣa jajñiṣe |
(5.30.17.e) sa ca tvānu hvayāmasi mā purā jaraso mṛthāḥ ‖17‖

(5.31.1’) yāṃ te cakrur āme pātre yāṃ cakrur miśradhānye |
(5.31.1.c) āme māṃse kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖1‖
(5.31.2’) yāṃ te cakruḥ kṛkavākāv aje vā yāṃ kurīriṇi |
(5.31.2.c) avyāṃ te kṛtyāṃ yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖2‖
(5.31.3’) yāṃ te cakrur ekaśaphe paśūnām ubhayādati |
(5.31.3.c) gardabhe kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖3‖
(5.31.4’) yāṃ te cakrur amūlāyāṃ valagaṃ vā narācyām |
(5.31.4.c) kṣetre te kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖4‖
(5.31.5’) yāṃ te cakrur gārhapatye pūrvāgnāv uta duścitah |
(5.31.5.c) śālāyāṃ kṛtyāṃ yām cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖5‖
(5.31.6’) yāṃ te cakruḥ sabhāyāṃ yām cakrur adhidevane |
(5.31.6.c) akṣeṣu kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖6‖
(5.31.7’) yāṃ te cakruḥ senāyāṃ yāṃ cakrur iṣvāyudhe |
(5.31.7.c) dundubhau kṛtyāṃ yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖7‖
(5.31.8’) yāṃ te kṛtyām kūpe 'vadadhuḥ śmaśāne vā nicakhnuḥ |
(5.31.8.c) sadmani kṛtyām yāṃ cakruḥ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖8‖
(5.31.9’) yāṃ te cakruḥ puruṣāsthe agnau saṃkasuke ca yām |
(5.31.9.c) mrokaṃ nirdāhaṃ kravyādaṃ punaḥ prati harāmi tām ‖9‖
(5.31.10’) apathenā jabhāraināṃ tāṃ pathetaḥ pra hiṇmasi |
(5.31.10.c) adhīro maryādhīrebhyaḥ saṃ jabhārācittyā ‖10‖
(5.31.11’) yaś cakāra na śaśāka kartuṃ śaśre pādam aṅgurim |
(5.31.11.c) cakāra bhadram asmabhyam abhago bhagavadbhyaḥ ‖11‖
(5.31.12’) kṛtyākṛtam valaginaṃ mūlinaṃ śapatheyyam |
(5.31.12.c) indras taṃ hantu mahatā vadhenāgnir vidhyatv astayā ‖12‖

(6.1.1’) doṣo gāya bṛhad gāya dyumad dhehi |
(6.1.1.c) ātharvaṇa stuhi devaṃ savitāram ‖1‖
(6.1.2’) tam u ṣṭuhi yo antaḥ sindhau sūnuḥ |
(6.1.2.c) satyasya yuvānam adroghavācaṃ suśevam ‖2‖
(6.1.3’) sa ghā no devaḥ savitā sāviṣad amṛtāni bhūri |
(6.1.3.c) ubhe suṣṭutī sugātave ‖3‖

(6.2.1’) indrāya somam ṛtvijaḥ sunotā ca dhāvata |
(6.2.1.c) stotur yo vacaḥ śṛṇavad dhavaṃ ca me ‖1‖
(6.2.2’) ā yaṃ viśantīndavo vayo na vṛkṣam andhasaḥ |
(6.2.2.c) virapśin vi mṛdho jahi rakṣasvinīḥ ‖2‖
(6.2.3’) sunotā somapāvne somam indrāya vajriṇe |
(6.2.3.c) yuvā jeteśānah sa puruṣṭutaḥ ‖3‖

(6.3.1’) pātaṃ na indrāpūṣaṇāditiḥ pāntu marutaḥ |
(6.3.1.c) apāṃ napāt sindhavaḥ sapta pātana pātu no viṣṇur uta dyauḥ ‖1‖
(6.3.2’) pātāṃ no dyāvāpṛthivī abhiṣṭaye pātu grāvā pātu somo no aṃhasaḥ |
(6.3.2.c) pātu no devī subhagā sarasvatī pātv agniḥ śivā ye asya pāyavaḥ ‖2‖
(6.3.3’) pātām no devāśvinā śubhas patī uṣāsānaktota na uruṣyatām |
(6.3.3.c) apāṃ napād abhihrutī gayasya cid deva tvaṣṭar vardhaya sarvatātaye ‖3‖

(6.4.1’) tvaṣṭā me daivyaṃ vacaḥ parjanyo brahmaṇas patiḥ |
(6.4.1.c) putrair bhrātṛbhir aditir nu pātu no duṣṭaraṃ trāyamāṇaṃ sahaḥ ‖1‖
(6.4.2’) aṃśo bhago varuṇo mitro aryamāditiḥ pāntu marutaḥ |
(6.4.2.c) apa tasya dveṣo gamed abhihruto yāvayac chatrum antitam ‖2‖
(6.4.3’) dhiye sam aśvinā prāvataṃ na uruṣyā ṇa urujmann aprayuchan |
(6.4.3.c) dyauṣ pitar yāvaya duchunā yā ‖3‖

(6.5.1’) ud enam uttaraṃ nayāgne ghṛtenāhuta |
(6.5.1.c) sam enaṃ varcasā sṛja prajayā ca bahuṃ kṛdhi ‖1‖
(6.5.2’) indremaṃ prataraṃ kṛdhi sajātānām asad vaśī |
(6.5.2.c) rāyas poṣeṇa saṃ sṛja jīvātave jarase naya ‖2‖
(6.5.3’) yasya kṛṇmo havir gṛhe tam agne vardhayā tvam |
(6.5.3.c) tasmai somo adhi bravad ayaṃ ca brahmaṇas patiḥ ‖3‖

(6.6.1’) yo 'smān brahmaṇas pate 'devo abhimanyate |
(6.6.1.c) sarvaṃ tam randhayāsi me yajamānāya sunvate ‖1‖
(6.6.2’) yo naḥ soma suśaṃsino duḥśaṃsa ādideśati |
(6.6.2.c) vajreṇāsya mukhe jahi sa saṃpiṣṭo apāyati ‖2‖
(6.6.3’) yo naḥ somābhidāsati sanābhir yaś ca niṣṭyaḥ |
(6.6.3.c) apa tasya balaṃ tira mahīva dyaur vadhatmanā ‖3‖

(6.7.1’) yena somāditiḥ pathā mitrā vā yanty adruhaḥ |
(6.7.1.c) tenā no 'vasā gahi ‖1‖
(6.7.2’) yena soma sāhantyāsurān randhayāsi naḥ |
(6.7.2.c) tenā no adhi vocata ‖2‖
(6.7.3’) yena devā asurāṇām ojāṃsy avṛṇīdhvam |
(6.7.3.c) tenā naḥ śarma yachata ‖3‖

(6.8.1’) yathā vṛkṣaṃ libujā samantaṃ pariṣasvaje |
(6.8.1.c) evā pari ṣvajasva māṃ yathā māṃ kāminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ‖1‖
(6.8.2’) yathā suparṇaḥ prapatan pakṣau nihanti bhūmyām |
(6.8.2.c) evā ni hanmi te mano yathā māṃ kāminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ‖2‖
(6.8.3’) yatheme dyāvāpṛthivī sadyaḥ paryeti sūryaḥ |
(6.8.3.c) evā pary emi te mano yathā mām kāminy aso yathā man nāpagā asaḥ ‖3‖

(6.9.1’) vāñcha me tanvaṃ pādau vāñchākṣyau vāñcha sakthyau |
(6.9.1.c) akṣyau vṛṣaṇyantyāḥ keśā māṃ te kāmena śuṣyantu ‖1‖
(6.9.2’) mama tvā doṣaṇiśriṣaṃ kṛṇomi hṛdayaśriṣam |
(6.9.2.c) yathā mama kratāv aso mama cittam upāyasi ‖2‖
(6.9.3’) yāsāṃ nābhir ārehaṇaṃ hṛdi saṃvananaṃ kṛtam gāvo ghṛtasya mātaro 'mūṃ saṃ vānayantu me ‖3‖

(6.10.1’) pṛthivyai śrotrāya vanaspatibhyo 'gnaye 'dhipataye svāhā ‖1‖
(6.10.2’) prāṇāyāntarikṣāya vayobhyo vāyave 'dhipataye svāhā ‖2‖
(6.10.3’) dive cakṣuṣe nakṣatrebhyaḥ sūryāyādhipataye svāhā ‖3‖

(6.11.1’) samīm aśvattha ārūḍhas tatra puṃsuvanaṃ kṛtam |
(6.11.1.c) tad vai putrasya vedanaṃ tat strīṣv ā bharāmasi ‖1‖
(6.11.2’) puṃsi vai reto bhavati tat striyām anu ṣicyate |
(6.11.2.c) tad vai putrasya vedanaṃ tat prajāpatir abravīt ‖2‖
(6.11.3’) prajāpatir anumatiḥ sinīvāly acīkḷpat |
(6.11.3.c) straiṣūyam anyatra dadhat pumāṃsam u dadhat iha ‖3‖

(6.12.1’) pari dyām iva sūryo 'hīnāṃ janimāgamam |
(6.12.1.c) rātrī jagad ivānyad dhaṃsāt tenā te vāraye viṣam ‖1‖
(6.12.2’) yad brahmabhir yad ṛṣibhir yad devair viditaṃ purā |
(6.12.2.c) yad bhūtaṃ bhavyam āsanvat tenā te vāraye viṣam ‖2‖
(6.12.3’) madhvā pṛñce nadyaḥ parvatā girayo madhu |
(6.12.3.c) madhu paruṣṇī śīpālā śam āsne astu śaṃ hṛde ‖3‖

(6.13.1’) namo devavadhebhyo namo rājavadhebhyaḥ |
(6.13.1.c) atho ye viśyānāṃ vadhās tebhyo mṛtyo namo 'stu te ‖1‖
(6.13.2’) namas te adhivākāya parāvākāya te namaḥ |
(6.13.2.c) sumatyai mṛtyo te namo durmatyai te idaṃ namaḥ ‖2‖
(6.13.3’) namas te yātudhānebhyo namas te bheṣajebhyaḥ |
(6.13.3.c) namas te mṛtyo mūlebhyo brāhmaṇebhya idaṃ namaḥ ‖3‖

(6.14.1’) asthisraṃsaṃ parusraṃsam āsthitam hṛdayāmayam |
(6.14.1.c) balāsaṃ sarvaṃ nāśayāṅgeṣṭhā yaś ca parvasu ‖1‖
(6.14.2’) nir balāsaṃ balāsinaḥ kṣiṇomi muṣkaraṃ yathā |
(6.14.2.c) chinadmy asya bandhanaṃ mūlam urvārvā iva ‖2‖
(6.14.3’) nir balāsetaḥ pra patāśuṅgaḥ śiśuko yathā |
(6.14.3.c) atho ita iva hāyano 'pa drāhy avīrahā ‖3‖

(6.15.1’) uttamo asy oṣadhīnāṃ tava vṛkṣā upastayaḥ |
(6.15.1.c) upastir astu so 'smākaṃ yo asmā;m abhidāsati ‖1‖
(6.15.2’) sabandhuś cāsabandhuś ca yo asmā;m abhidāsati |
(6.15.2.c) teṣāṃ sā vṛkṣāṇām ivāhaṃ bhūyāsam uttamaḥ ‖2‖
(6.15.3’) yathā soma oṣadhīnām uttamo haviṣāṃ kṛtaḥ |
(6.15.3.c) talāśā vṛkṣānām ivāhaṃ bhūyāsam uttamaḥ ‖3‖

(6.16.1’) ābayo anābayo rasas ta ugra ābayo |
(6.16.1.c) ā te karambham admasi ‖1‖
(6.16.2’) vihahlo nāma te pitā madāvatī nāma te mātā |
(6.16.2.c) sa hina tvam asi yas tvam ātmānam āvayaḥ ‖2‖
(6.16.3’) tauvilike 'velayāvāyam ailaba ailayīt |
(6.16.3.c) babhruś ca babhrukarṇaś cāpehi nir āla ‖3‖
(6.16.4’) alasālāsi pūrva silāñjālāsy uttarā |
(6.16.4.c) nīlāgalasāla ‖4‖

(6.17.1’) yatheyam pṛthivī mahī bhūtānāṃ garbham ādadhe |
(6.17.1.c) evā te dhriyatāṃ garbho anu sūtuṃ savitave ‖1‖
(6.17.2’) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī dādhāremān vanaspatīn |
(6.17.2.c) evā te dhriyatāṃ garbho anu sūtuṃ savitave ‖2‖
(6.17.3’) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī dādhāra parvatān girīn |
(6.17.3.c) evā te dhriyatāṃ garbho anu sūtuṃ savitave ‖3‖
(6.17.4’) yatheyaṃ pṛthivī mahī dādhāra viṣṭhitaṃ jagat |
(6.17.4.c) evā te dhriyatāṃ garbho anu sūtuṃ savitave ‖4‖

(6.18.1’) īrṣyāyā dhrājiṃ prathamāṃ prathamasyā utāparām |
(6.18.1.c) agniṃ hṛdayyaṃ śokaṃ taṃ te nir vāpayāmasi ‖1‖
(6.18.2’) yathā bhūmir mṛtamanā mṛtān mṛtamanastarā |
(6.18.2.c) yathota mamruṣo mana everṣyor mṛtaṃ manaḥ ‖2‖
(6.18.3’) ado yat te hṛdi śritaṃ manaskaṃ patayiṣṇukam |
(6.18.3.c) tatas ta īrṣyāṃ muñcāmi nir ūṣmāṇaṃ dṛter iva ‖3‖

(6.19.1’) punantu mā devajanāḥ punantu manavo dhiyā |
(6.19.1.c) punantu viśvā bhūtāni pavamānaḥ punātu mā ‖1‖
(6.19.2’) pavamānaḥ punātu mā kratve dakṣāya jīvase |
(6.19.2.c) atho ariṣṭatātaye ‖2‖
(6.19.3’) ubhābhyāṃ deva savitaḥ pavitreṇa savena ca |
(6.19.3.c) asmān punīhi cakṣase ‖3‖

(6.20.1’) agner ivāsya dahata eti śuṣmiṇa uteva matto vilapann apāyati |
(6.20.1.c) anyam asmad ichatu kaṃ cid avratas tapurvadhāya namo astu takmane ‖1‖
(6.20.2’) namo rudrāya namo astu takmane namo rājñe varuṇāya tviṣīmate |
(6.20.2.c) namo dive namaḥ pṛthivyai nama oṣadhībhyaḥ ‖2‖
(6.20.3’) ayaṃ yo abhiśocayiṣṇur viśvā rūpāṇi haritā kṛṇoṣi |
(6.20.3.c) tasmai te 'ruṇāya babhrave namaḥ kṛṇomi vanyāya takmane ‖3‖

(6.21.1’) imā yās tisraḥ pṛthivīs tāsāṃ ha bhūmir uttamā |
(6.21.1.c) tāsām adhi tvaco ahaṃ bheṣajaṃ sam u jagrabham ‖1‖
(6.21.2’) śreṣṭham asi bheṣajānāṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ vīrudhānām |
(6.21.2.c) somo bhaga iva yāmeṣu deveṣu varuṇo yathā ‖2‖
(6.21.3’) revatīr anādhṛṣaḥ siṣāsavaḥ siṣāsatha |
(6.21.3.c) uta stha keśadṛmhaṇīr atho ha keśavardhanīḥ ‖3‖

(6.22.1’) kṛṣṇaṃ niyānaṃ harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divam ut patanti |
(6.22.1.c) ta āvavṛtrant sadanād ṛtasyād id ghṛtena pṛthivīṃ vy ūduḥ ‖1‖
(6.22.2’) payasvatīḥ kṛṇuthāpa oṣadhīḥ śivā yad ejathā maruto rukmavakṣasaḥ |
(6.22.2.c) ūrjaṃ ca tatra sumatiṃ ca pinvata yatrā naro marutaḥ siñcathā madhu ‖2‖
(6.22.3’) udapruto marutas tā;m iyarta vṛṣṭir yā viśvā nivatas pṛṇāti |
(6.22.3.c) ejāti glahā kanyeva tunnairuṃ tundānā patyeva jāyā ‖3‖

(6.23.1’) sasruṣīs tad apaso divā naktaṃ ca sasruṣīḥ |
(6.23.1.c) vareṇyakratur aham apo devīr upa hvaye ‖1‖
(6.23.2’) otā āpaḥ karmaṇyā muñcantv itaḥ praṇītaye |
(6.23.2.c) sadyaḥ kṛṇvantv etave ‖2‖
(6.23.3’) devasya savituḥ save karma kṛṇvantu mānuṣāḥ |
(6.23.3.c) śaṃ no bhavantv apa oṣadhīḥ śivāḥ ‖3‖

(6.24.1’) himavataḥ pra sravanti sindhau samaha saṅgamaḥ |
(6.24.1.c) āpo ha mahyaṃ tad devīr dadan hṛddyotabheṣajam ‖1‖
(6.24.2’) yan me akṣyor ādidyota pārṣṇyoḥ prapadoś ca yat |
(6.24.2.c) āpas tat sarvaṃ niṣ karan bhiṣajāṃ subhiṣaktamāḥ ‖2‖
(6.24.3’) sindhupatnīḥ sindhurājñīḥ sarvā yā nadya sthana |
(6.24.3.c) datta nas tasya bheṣajaṃ tenā vo bhunajāmahai ‖3‖

(6.25.1’) pañca ca yāḥ pañcāśac ca saṃyanti manyā abhi |
(6.25.1.c) itas tāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitām iva ‖1‖
(6.25.2’) sapta ca yāḥ saptatiś ca saṃyanti graivyā abhi |
(6.25.2.c) itas tāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitām iva ‖2‖
(6.25.3’) nava ca yā navatiś ca saṃyanti skandhyā abhi |
(6.25.3.c) itas tāḥ sarvā naśyantu vākā apacitām iva ‖3‖

(6.26.1’) ava mā pāpman sṛja vaśī san mṛdayāsi naḥ |
(6.26.1.c) ā mā bhadrasya loke pāpman dhehy avihrutam ‖1‖
(6.26.2’) yo naḥ pāpman na jahāsi tam u tvā jahimo vayam |
(6.26.2.c) pathām anu vyāvartane 'nyaṃ pāpmānu padyatām ‖2‖
(6.26.3’) anyatrāsman ny ucyatu sahasrākṣo amartyaḥ |
(6.26.3.c) yaṃ dveṣāma tam ṛchatu yam u dviṣmas tam ij jahi ‖3‖

(6.27.1’) devāḥ kapota iṣito yad ichan dūto nirṛtyā idam ājagāma |
(6.27.1.c) tasmā arcāma kṛṇavāma niṣkṛtiṃ śaṃ no astu dvipade śaṃ catuṣpade ‖1‖
(6.27.2’) śivaḥ kapota iṣito no astv anāgā devāḥ śakuno gṛhaṃ naḥ |
(6.27.2.c) agnir hi vipro juṣatām havir naḥ pari hetiḥ pakṣiṇī no vṛṇaktu ‖2‖
(6.27.3’) hetiḥ pakṣiṇī na dabhāty asmān āṣṭrī padaṃ kṛṇute agnidhāne |
(6.27.3.c) śivo gobhya uta puruṣebhyo no astu mā no devā iha hiṃsīt kapota ‖3‖

(6.28.1’) ṛcā kapotaṃ nudata praṇodam iṣaṃ madantaḥ pari gāṃ nayāmaḥ |
(6.28.1.c) saṃlobhayanto duritā padāni hitvā na ūrjaṃ pra padāt pathiṣṭhaḥ ‖1‖
(6.28.2’) parīme 'gnim arṣata parīme gām aneṣata |
(6.28.2.c) deveṣv akrata śravaḥ ka imā;m ā dadharṣati ‖2‖
(6.28.3’) yaḥ prathamaḥ pravatam āsasāda bahubhyaḥ panthām anupaspaśānaḥ |
(6.28.3.c) yo 'syeśe dvipado yaś catuṣpadas tasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave ‖3‖

(6.29.1’) amūn hetiḥ patatriṇī ny etu yad ulūko vadati mogham etat |
(6.29.1.c) yad vā kapota padam agnau kṛṇoti ‖1‖
(6.29.2’) yau te dūtau nirṛta idam eto 'prahitau prahitau vā gṛhaṃ naḥ |
(6.29.2.c) kapotolūkābhyām apadaṃ tad astu ‖2‖
(6.29.3’) avairahatyāyedam ā papatyāt suvīratāyā idam ā sasadyāt |
(6.29.3.c) parāṅ eva parā vada parācīm anu saṃvatam |
(6.29.3.e) yathā yamasya tvā gṛhe 'rasaṃ praticākaśān ābhūkaṃ praticākaśān ‖3‖

(6.30.1’) devā imaṃ madhunā saṃyutaṃ yavaṃ sarasvatyām adhi maṇāv acarkṛṣuḥ |
(6.30.1.c) indra āsīt sīrapatiḥ śatakratuḥ kīnāśā āsan marutaḥ sudānavaḥ ‖1‖
(6.30.2’) yas te mado 'vakeśo vikeśo yenābhihasyaṃ puruṣaṃ kṛṇoṣi |
(6.30.2.c) ārāt tvad anyā vanāni vṛkṣi tvaṃ śami śatavalśā vi roha ‖2‖
(6.30.3’) bṛhatpalāśe subhage varṣavṛddha ṛtāvari |
(6.30.3.c) māteva putrebhyo mṛḍa keśebhyaḥ śami ‖3‖

(6.31.1’) āyaṃ gauḥ pṛśnir akramīd asadan mātaram puraḥ |
(6.31.1.c) pitaram ca prayant svaḥ ‖1‖
(6.31.2’) antaś carati rocanā asya prāṇād apānataḥ |
(6.31.2.c) vy akhyan mahiṣaḥ svaḥ ‖2‖
(6.31.3’) triṃśad dhāmā vi rājati vāk pataṅgo aśiśriyat |
(6.31.3.c) prati vastor ahar dyubhiḥ ‖3‖

(6.32.1’) antardāve juhuta sv etad yātudhānakṣayaṇaṃ ghṛtena |
(6.32.1.c) ārād rakṣāṃsi prati daha tvam agne na no gṛhāṇām upa tītapāsi ‖1‖
(6.32.2’) rudro vo grīvā aśarait piśācāh pṛṣṭīr vo 'pi śṛṇātu yātudhānāḥ |
(6.32.2.c) vīrud vo viśvatovīryā yamena sam ajīgamat ‖2‖
(6.32.3’) abhayaṃ mitrāvaruṇāv ihāstu no 'rciṣāttriṇo nudataṃ pratīcaḥ |
(6.32.3.c) mā jñātāraṃ mā pratiṣṭhāṃ vidanta mitho vighnānā upa yantu mṛtyum ‖3‖

(6.33.1’) yasyedam ā rajo yujas tuje janā navaṃ svaḥ |
(6.33.1.c) indrasya rantyaṃ bṛhat ‖1‖
(6.33.2’) nādhṛṣa ā dadhṛṣate dhṛṣāṇo dhṛṣitaḥ śavaḥ |
(6.33.2.c) purā yathā vyathiḥ śrava indrasya nādhṛṣe śavaḥ ‖2‖
(6.33.3’) sa no dadātu tāṃ rayim uruṃ piśaṅgasaṃdṛśam |
(6.33.3.c) indraḥ patis tuviṣṭamo janeṣv ā ‖3‖

(6.34.1’) prāgnaye vācam īraya vṛṣabhāya kṣitīnām |
(6.34.1.c) sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ ‖1‖
(6.34.2’) yo rakṣāṃsi nijūrvaty agnis tigmena śociṣā |
(6.34.2.c) sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ ‖2‖
(6.34.3’) yaḥ parasyāḥ parāvatas tiro dhanvātirocate |
(6.34.3.c) sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ ‖3‖
(6.34.4’) yo viśvābhi vipaśyati bhuvanā saṃ ca paśyati |
(6.34.4.c) sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ ‖4‖
(6.34.5’) yo asya pāre rajasaḥ śukro agnir ajāyata |
(6.34.5.c) sa naḥ parṣad ati dviṣaḥ ‖5‖

(6.35.1’) vaiśvānaro na ūtaya ā pra yātu parāvataḥ |
(6.35.1.c) agnir naḥ suṣṭutīr upa ‖1‖
(6.35.2’) vaiśvānaro na āgamad imaṃ yajñaṃ sajūr upa |
(6.35.2.c) agnir uktheṣv aṃhasu ‖2‖
(6.35.3’) vaiśvānaro 'ṅgirasāṃ stomam ukthaṃ ca cākḷpat |
(6.35.3.c) aiṣu dyumnaṃ svar yamat ‖3‖

(6.36.1’) ṛtāvānaṃ vaiśvānaram ṛtasya jyotiṣas patim |
(6.36.1.c) ajasraṃ gharmam īmahe ‖1‖
(6.36.2’) sa viśvā prati cākḷpa ṛtūṃr ut sṛjate vaśī |
(6.36.2.c) yajñasya vaya uttiran ‖2‖
(6.36.3’) agniḥ pareṣu dhāmasu kāmo bhūtasya bhavyasya |
(6.36.3.c) samrād eko vi rājati ‖3‖

(6.37.1’) upa prāgāt sahasrākṣo yuktvā śapatho ratham |
(6.37.1.c) śaptāram anvichan mama vṛka ivāvimato gṛham ‖1‖
(6.37.2’) pari ṇo vṛṅgdhi śapatha hradam agnir ivā dahan |
(6.37.2.c) śaptāram atra no jahi divo vṛkṣam ivāśaniḥ ‖2‖
(6.37.3’) yo naḥ śapād aśapataḥ śapato yaś ca naḥ śapāt |
(6.37.3.c) śune peṣṭram ivāvakṣāmaṃ taṃ praty asyāmi mṛtyave ‖3‖

(6.38.1’) simhe vyāghra uta yā pṛdākau tviṣir agnau brāhmaṇe sūrye yā |
(6.38.1.c) indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā saṃvidānā ‖1‖
(6.38.2’) yā hastini dvīpini yā hiraṇye tviṣir apsu goṣu yā puruṣeṣu |
(6.38.2.c) indraṃ yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā saṃvidānā ‖2‖
(6.38.3’) rathe akṣeṣv ṛṣabhasya vāje vāte parjanye varuṇasya śuṣme |
(6.38.3.c) indram yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā saṃvidānā ‖3‖
(6.38.4’) rājanye dundubhāv āyatāyām aśvasya vāje puruṣasya māyau |
(6.38.4.c) indram yā devī subhagā jajāna sā na aitu varcasā samvidānā ‖4‖

(6.39.1’) yaśo havir vardhatām indrajūtaṃ sahasravīryaṃ subhṛtaṃ sahaskṛtam |
(6.39.1.c) prasarsrāṇam anu dīrghāya cakṣase haviṣmantaṃ mā vardhaya jyeṣṭhatātaye ‖1‖
(6.39.2’) achā na indraṃ yaśasaṃ yaśobhir yaśasvinaṃ namasānā vidhema |
(6.39.2.c) sa no rāsva rāṣṭram indrajūtaṃ tasya te rātau yaśasaḥ syāma ‖2‖
(6.39.3’) yaśā indro yaśā agnir yaśāḥ somo ajāyata |
(6.39.3.c) yaśā viśvasya bhūtasya aham asmi yaśastamaḥ ‖3‖

(6.40.1’) abhayaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī ihāstu no 'bhayaṃ somaḥ savitā naḥ kṛṇotu |
(6.40.1.c) abhayaṃ no 'stūrv antarikṣaṃ saptaṛṣīṇāṃ ca haviṣābhayaṃ no astu ‖1‖
(6.40.2’) asmai grāmāya pradiśaś catasra ūrjaṃ subhūtaṃ svasti savitā naḥ kṛṇotu |
(6.40.2.c) aśatrv indro abhayaṃ naḥ kṛṇotv anyatra rājñām abhi yātu manyuḥ ‖2‖
(6.40.3’) anamitraṃ no adharād anamitraṃ na uttarāt |
(6.40.3.c) indrānamitraṃ naḥ paścād anamitraṃ puras kṛdhi ‖3‖

(6.41.1’) manase cetase dhiya ākūtaya uta cittaye |
(6.41.1.c) matyai śrutāya cakṣase vidhema haviṣā vayam ‖1‖
(6.41.2’) apānāya vyānāya prāṇāya bhūridhāyase |
(6.41.2.c) sarasvatyā uruvyace vidhema haviṣā vayam ‖2‖
(6.41.3’) mā no hāsiṣur ṛṣayo daivyā ye tanūpā ye nas tanvas tanūjāḥ |
(6.41.3.c) amartyā martyāṃ abhi naḥ sacadhvam āyur dhatta prataraṃ jīvase naḥ ‖3‖

(6.42.1’) ava jyām iva dhanvano manyuṃ tanomi te hṛdaḥ |
(6.42.1.c) yathā saṃmanasau bhūtvā sakhāyāv iva sacāvahai ‖1‖
(6.42.2’) sakhāyāv iva sacāvahā ava manyuṃ tanomi te |
(6.42.2.c) adhas te aśmano manyum upāsyāmasi yo guruḥ ‖2‖
(6.42.3’) abhi tiṣṭhāmi te manyuṃ pārṣṇyā prapadena ca |
(6.42.3.c) yathāvaśo na vādiṣo mama cittam upāyasi ‖3‖

(6.43.1’) ayaṃ darbho vimanyukaḥ svāya cāraṇāya ca |
(6.43.1.c) manyor vimanyukasyāyaṃ manyuśamana ucyate ‖1‖
(6.43.2’) ayaṃ yo bhūrimūlaḥ samudram avatiṣṭhati |
(6.43.2.c) darbhaḥ pṛthivyā utthito manyuśamana ucyate ‖2‖
(6.43.3’) vi te hanavyāṃ śaraṇiṃ vi te mukhyāṃ nayāmasi |
(6.43.3.c) yathāvaśo na vādiṣo mama cittam upāyasi ‖3‖

(6.44.1’) asthād dyaur asthāt pṛthivy asthād viśvam idaṃ jagat |
(6.44.1.c) asthur vṛkṣā ūrdhvasvapnās tiṣṭhād rogo ayaṃ tava ‖1‖
(6.44.2’) śataṃ yā bheṣajāni te sahasraṃ saṃgatāni ca |
(6.44.2.c) śreṣṭham āsrāvabheṣajaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ roganāśanam ‖2‖
(6.44.3’) rudrasya mūtram asy amṛtasya nābhiḥ |
(6.44.3.c) viṣāṇakā nāma vā asi pitṝṇāṃ mūlād utthitā vātīkṛtanāśanī ‖3‖

(6.45.1’) paro 'pehi manaspāpa kim aśastāni śaṃsasi |
(6.45.1.c) parehi na tvā kāmaye vṛkṣāṃ vanāni saṃ cara gṛheṣu goṣu me manaḥ ‖1‖
(6.45.2’) avaśasā niḥśasā yat parāśasopārima jāgrato yat svapantaḥ |
(6.45.2.c) agnir viśvāny apa duṣkṛtāny ajuṣṭāny āre asmad dadhātu ‖2‖
(6.45.3’) yad indra brahmaṇas pate 'pi mṛṣā carāmasi |
(6.45.3.c) pracetā na āṅgiraso duritāt pātv aṃhasaḥ ‖3‖

(6.46.1’) yo na jīvo 'si na mṛto devānām amṛtagarbho 'si svapna |
(6.46.1.c) varuṇānī te mātā yamaḥ pitārarur nāmāsi ‖1‖
(6.46.2’) vidma te svapna janitraṃ devajāmīnāṃ putro 'si yamasya karaṇaḥ |
(6.46.2.c) antako 'si mṛtyur asi taṃ tvā svapna tathā saṃ vidma sa naḥ svapna duṣvapnyāt pāhi ‖2‖
(6.46.3’) yathā kalāṃ yathā śaphaṃ yatharṇaṃ saṃnayanti |
(6.46.3.c) evā duṣvapnyaṃ sarvaṃ dviṣate saṃ nayāmasi ‖3‖

(6.47.1’) agniḥ prātaḥsavane pātv asmān vaiśvānaro viśvakṛd viśvaśaṃbhūḥ |
(6.47.1.c) sa naḥ pāvako draviṇe dadhātv āyuṣmantaḥ sahabhakṣāḥ syāma ‖1‖
(6.47.2’) viśve devā maruta indro asmān asmin dvitīye savane na jahyuḥ |
(6.47.2.c) āyuṣmantaḥ priyam eṣāṃ vadanto vayaṃ devānāṃ sumatau syāma ‖2‖
(6.47.3’) idaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanaṃ kavīnām ṛtena ye camasam airayanta |
(6.47.3.c) te saudhanvanāḥ svar ānaśānāḥ sviṣṭiṃ no abhi vasyo nayantu ‖3‖

(6.48.1’) śyeno 'si gāyatrachandā anu tvā rabhe |
(6.48.1.c) svasti mā saṃ vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā ‖1‖
(6.48.2’) ṛbhur asi jagachandā anu tvā rabhe |
(6.48.2.c) svasti mā saṃ vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā ‖2‖
(6.48.3’) vṛṣāsi triṣṭupchandā anu tvā rabhe |
(6.48.3.c) svasti mā saṃ vahāsya yajñasyodṛci svāhā ‖3‖

(6.49.1’) nahi te agne tanvaḥ krūram ānaṃśa martyaḥ |
(6.49.1.c) kapir babhasti tejanaṃ svaṃ jarāyu gaur iva ‖1‖
(6.49.2’) meṣa iva vai saṃ ca vi corv acyase yad uttaradrāv uparaś ca khādataḥ |
(6.49.2.c) śīrṣṇā śiro 'psasāpso ardayann aṃśūn babhasti haritebhir āsabhiḥ ‖2‖
(6.49.3’) suparṇā vācam akratopa dyavy ākhare kṛṣṇā iṣirā anartiṣuḥ |
(6.49.3.c) ni yan niyanti uparasya niṣkṛtiṃ purū reto dadhire sūryaśritaḥ ‖3‖

(6.50.1’) hataṃ tardaṃ samaṅkam ākhum aśvinā chintaṃ śiro api pṛṣṭīḥ śṛṇītam |
(6.50.1.c) yavān ned adān api nahyataṃ mukham athābhayaṃ kṛṇutaṃ dhānyāya ‖1‖
(6.50.2’) tarda hai pataṅga hai jabhya hā upakvasa |
(6.50.2.c) brahmevāsaṃsthitaṃ havir anadanta imān yavān ahiṃsanto apodita ‖2‖
(6.50.3’) tardāpate vaghāpate tṛṣṭajambhā ā śṛṇota me |
(6.50.3.c) ya āraṇyā vyadvarā ye ke ca stha vyadvarās tānt sarvān jambhayāmasi ‖3‖

(6.51.1’) vāyoḥ pūtaḥ pavitreṇa pratyaṅ somo ati drutaḥ |
(6.51.1.c) indrasya yujaḥ sakhā ‖1‖
(6.51.2’) āpo asmān mātaraḥ sūdayantu ghṛtena no ghṛtapvah punantu |
(6.51.2.c) viśvaṃ hi ripraṃ pravahanti devīr ud id ābhyaḥ śucir ā pūta emi ‖2‖
(6.51.3’) yat kiṃ cedaṃ varuṇa daivye jane 'bhidrohaṃ manuṣyāś caranti |
(6.51.3.c) acittyā cet tava dharma yuyopima mā nas tasmād enaso deva rīriṣaḥ ‖3‖

(6.52.1’) ut sūryo diva eti puro rakṣāṃsi nijūrvan |
(6.52.1.c) ādityaḥ parvatebhyo viśvadṛṣṭo adṛṣṭahā ‖1‖
(6.52.2’) ni gāvo goṣṭhe asadan ni mṛgāso avikṣata |
(6.52.2.c) ny ūrmayo nadīnaṃ ny adṛṣṭā alipsata ‖2‖
(6.52.3’) āyurdadaṃ vipaścitaṃ śrutāṃ kaṇvasya vīrudham |
(6.52.3.c) ābhāriṣaṃ viśvabheṣajīm asyādṛṣṭān ni śamayat ‖3‖

(6.53.1’) dyauś ca ma idaṃ pṛthivī ca pracetasau śukro bṛhan dakṣiṇayā pipartu |
(6.53.1.c) anu svadhā cikitāṃ somo agnir vāyur naḥ pātu savitā bhagaś ca ‖1‖
(6.53.2’) punaḥ prāṇaḥ punar ātmā na aitu punaś cakṣuḥ punar asur na aitu |
(6.53.2.c) vaiśvānaro no adabdhas tanūpā antas tiṣṭhāti duritāni viśvā ‖2‖
(6.53.3’) saṃ varcasā payasā saṃ tanūbhir aganmahi manasā saṃ śivena |
(6.53.3.c) tvaṣṭā no atra varīyaḥ kṛṇotv anu no mārṣṭu tanvo yad viriṣṭam ‖3‖

(6.54.1’) idaṃ tad yuja uttaram indraṃ śumbhāmy aṣṭaye |
(6.54.1.c) asya kṣatraṃ śriyaṃ mahīṃ vṛṣṭir iva vardhayā tṛṇam ‖1‖
(6.54.2’) asmai kṣatram agnīṣomāv asmai dhārayataṃ rayim |
(6.54.2.c) imaṃ rāṣṭrasyābhīvarge kṛṇutam yuja uttaram ‖2‖
(6.54.3’) sabandhuś cāsabandhuś ca yo asmā;m abhidāsati |
(6.54.3.c) sarvaṃ taṃ randhayāsi me yajamānāya sunvate ‖3‖

(6.55.1’) ye panthāno bahavo devayānā antarā dyāvāpṛthivī saṃcaranti |
(6.55.1.c) teṣām ajyāniṃ yatamo vahāti tasmai mā devāḥ pari datteha sarve ‖1‖
(6.55.2’) grīṣmo hemantaḥ śiśiro vasantaḥ śarad varṣāḥ svite no dadhāta |
(6.55.2.c) ā no goṣu bhajatā prajāyāṃ nivāta id vaḥ śaraṇe syāma ‖2‖
(6.55.3’) idāvatsarāya parivatsarāya saṃvatsarāya kṛṇutā bṛhan namaḥ |
(6.55.3.c) teṣāṃ vayaṃ sumatau yajñiyānām api bhadre sau manase syāma ‖3‖

(6.56.1’) mā no devā ahir vadhīt satokānt sahapuruṣān |
(6.56.1.c) samyataṃ na vi ṣparad vyāttaṃ na saṃ yaman namo devajanebhyaḥ |
(6.56.2’) namo 'stv asitāya namas tiraścirājaye |
(6.56.2.c) svajāya babhrave namo namo devajanebhyaḥ ‖2‖
(6.56.3’) saṃ te hanmi datā dataḥ sam u te hanvā hanū |
(6.56.3.c) saṃ te jihvayā jihvāṃ sam v āsnāha āsyam ‖3‖

(6.57.1’) idam id vā u bheṣajam idaṃ rudrasya bheṣajam |
(6.57.1.c) yeneṣum ekatejanāṃ śataśalyām apabravat ‖1‖
(6.57.2’) jālāṣeṇābhi ṣiñcata jālāṣeṇopa siñcata |
(6.57.2.c) jālāṣam ugraṃ bheṣajaṃ tena no mṛḍa jīvase ‖2‖
(6.57.3’) śaṃ ca no mayaś ca no mā ca naḥ kiṃ canāmamat |
(6.57.3.c) kṣamā rapo viśvaṃ no astu bheṣajaṃ sarvaṃ no astu bheṣajam ‖3‖

(6.58.1’) yaśasaṃ mendro maghavān kṛṇotu yaśasaṃ dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime |
(6.58.1.c) yaśasaṃ mā devaḥ savitā kṛṇotu priyo dātur dakṣiṇāyā iha syām ‖1‖
(6.58.2’) yathendro dyāvāpṛthivyor yaśasvān yathāpa oṣadhīṣu yaśasvatīḥ |
(6.58.2.c) evā viśveṣu deveṣu vayaṃ sarveṣu yaśasaḥ syāma ‖2‖
(6.58.3’) yaśā indro yaśā agnir yaśāḥ somo ajāyata |
(6.58.3.c) yaśā viśvasya bhūtasyāham asmi yaśastamaḥ ‖3‖

(6.59.1’) anaḍudbhyas tvaṃ prathamaṃ dhenubhyas tvam arundhati |
(6.59.1.c) adhenave vayase śarma yacha catuṣpade ‖1‖
(6.59.2’) śarma yachatv oṣadhiḥ saha devīr arundhatī |
(6.59.2.c) karat payasvantaṃ goṣṭham ayakṣmā;m uta pūruṣān ‖2‖
(6.59.3’) viśvarūpāṃ subhagām achāvadāmi jīvalām |
(6.59.3.c) sā no rudrasyāstāṃ hetiṃ dūraṃ nayatu gobhyaḥ ‖3‖

(6.60.1’) ayam ā yāty aryamā purastād viṣitastupaḥ |
(6.60.1.c) asyā ichann agruvai patim uta jāyām ajānaye ‖1‖
(6.60.2’) aśramad iyam aryamann anyāsāṃ samanaṃ yatī |
(6.60.2.c) aṅgo nv aryamann asyā anyāḥ samanam āyati ‖2‖
(6.60.3’) dhātā dādhāra pṛthivīm dhātā dyām uta sūryam |
(6.60.3.c) dhātāsyā agruvai patim dadhātu pratikāmyam ‖3‖

(6.61.1’) mahyam āpo madhumad erayantāṃ mahyaṃ sūro abharaj jyotiṣe kam |
(6.61.1.c) mahyaṃ devā uta viśve tapojā mahyaṃ devaḥ savitā vyaco dhāt ‖1‖
(6.61.2’) ahaṃ viveca pṛthivīm uta dyām aham ṛtūṃr ajanayaṃ sapta sākam |
(6.61.2.c) ahaṃ satyam anṛtaṃ yad vadāmy ahaṃ daivīṃ pari vācam viśaś ca ‖2‖
(6.61.3’) ahaṃ jajāna pṛthivīm uta dyām aham ṛtūṃr ajanayaṃ sapta sindhūn |
(6.61.3.c) ahaṃ satvam anṛtaṃ yad vadāmi yo agnīṣomāv ajuṣe sakhāyā ‖3‖

(6.62.1’) vaiśvānaro raśmibhir naḥ punātu vātaḥ prāṇeneṣiro nabhobhiḥ |
(6.62.1.c) dyāvāpṛthivī payasā payasvatī ṛtāvarī yajñiye na punītām ‖1‖
(6.62.2’) vaiśvānarīṃ sūnṛtām ā rabhadhvaṃ yasyā āśās tanvo vītapṛṣṭhāḥ |
(6.62.2.c) tayā gṛṇantaḥ sadhamādeṣu vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīnām ‖2‖
(6.62.3’) vaiśvānarīṃ varcasa ā rabhadhvaṃ śuddhā bhavantaḥ śucayaḥ pāvakāḥ |
(6.62.3.c) iheḍayā sadhamādaṃ madanto jyok paśyema sūryam uccarantam ‖3‖

(6.63.1’) yat te devī nirṛtir ābabandha dāma grīvāsv avimokyaṃ yat |
(6.63.1.c) tat te vi ṣyāmy āyuṣe varcase balāyādomadam annam addhi prasūtaḥ ‖1‖
(6.63.2’) namo 'stu te nirṛte tigmatejo 'yasmayān vi cṛtā bandhapāśān |
(6.63.2.c) yamo mahyam punar it tvām dadāti tasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave ‖2‖
(6.63.3’) ayasmaye drupade bedhiṣa ihābhihito mṛtyubhir ye sahasram |
(6.63.3.c) yamena tvaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidāna uttamaṃ nākam adhi rohayemam ‖3‖
(6.63.4’) saṃsam id yuvase vṛṣann agne viśvāny arya ā |
(6.63.4.c) iḍas pade sam idhyase sa no vasūny ā bhara ‖4‖

(6.64.1’) saṃ jānīdhvaṃ saṃ pṛcyadhvaṃ saṃ vo manāṃsi jānatām |
(6.64.1.c) devā bhāgaṃ yathā pūrve samjānānā upāsate ‖1‖
(6.64.2’) samāno mantraḥ samitiḥ samānī samānaṃ vrataṃ saha cittam eṣām |
(6.64.2.c) samānena vo haviṣā juhomi samānaṃ ceto abhisaṃviśadhvam ‖2‖
(6.64.3’) samānī va ākūtiḥ samānā hṛdayāni vaḥ |
(6.64.3.c) samānam astu vo manaḥ yathā vaḥ susahāsati ‖3‖

(6.65.1’) ava manyur avāyatāva bāhū manoyujā |
(6.65.1.c) parāśara tvaṃ teṣām parāñcaṃ śuṣmam ardayādhā no rayim ā kṛdhi ‖1‖
(6.65.2’) nirhastebhyo nairhastam yaṃ devāḥ śarum asyatha |
(6.65.2.c) vṛścāmi śatrūṇāṃ bāhūn anena haviṣā 'ham ‖2‖
(6.65.3’) indraś cakāra prathamaṃ nairhastam asurebhyaḥ |
(6.65.3.c) jayantu satvāno mama sthireṇendreṇa medinā ‖3‖

(6.66.1’) nirhastaḥ śatrur abhidāsann astu ye senābhir yudham āyanty asmān |
(6.66.1.c) sam arpayendra mahatā vadhena drātv eṣām aghahāro vividdhaḥ ‖1‖
(6.66.2’) ātanvānā āyachanto 'syanto ye ca dhāvatha |
(6.66.2.c) nirhastāḥ śatravaḥ sthanendro vo 'dya parāśarīt ‖2‖
(6.66.3’) nirhastāḥ santu śatravo 'ṅgaiṣāṃ mlāpayāmasi |
(6.66.3.c) athaiṣām indra vedāṃsi śataśo vi bhajāmahai ‖3‖

(6.67.1’) pari vartmāni sarvata indraḥ pūṣā ca sasratuḥ |
(6.67.1.c) muhyantv adyāmūḥ senā amitrāṇāṃ parastarām ‖1‖
(6.67.2’) mūḍhā amitrāś caratāśīrṣāṇa ivāhayaḥ |
(6.67.2.c) teṣāṃ vo agnimūḍhānām indro hantu varaṃvaram ‖2‖
(6.67.3’) aiṣu nahya vṛṣājinaṃ hariṇasya bhiyaṃ kṛdhi |
(6.67.3.c) parāṅ amitra eṣatv arvācī gaur upeṣatu ‖3‖

(6.68.1’) āyam agant savitā kṣureṇoṣṇena vāya udakenehi |
(6.68.1.c) ādityā rudrā vasava undantu sacetasaḥ somasya rājño vapata pracetasaḥ ‖1‖
(6.68.2’) aditiḥ śmaśru vapatv āpa undantu varcasā |
(6.68.2.c) cikitsatu prajāpatir dīrghāyutvāya cakṣase ‖2‖
(6.68.3’) yenāvapat savitā kṣureṇa somasya rājño varuṇasya vidvān |
(6.68.3.c) tena brahmāṇo vapatedam asya gomān aśvavān ayam astu prajāvān ‖3‖

(6.69.1’) girāv aragarāṭeṣu hiranye goṣu yad yaśaḥ |
(6.69.1.c) surāyāṃ sicyamānāyāṃ kīlāle madhu tan mayi ‖1‖
(6.69.2’) aśvinā sāragheṇa mā madhunāṅktaṃ śubhas patī |
(6.69.2.c) yathā bhargasvatīṃ vācam āvadāni janā;m anu ‖2‖
(6.69.3’) mayi varco atho yaśo 'tho yajñasya yat payaḥ |
(6.69.3.c) tan mayi prajāpatir divi dyām iva dṛṃhatu ‖3‖

(6.70.1’) yathā māṃsam yathā surā yathākṣā adhidevane |
(6.70.1.c) yathā puṃso vṛṣaṇyata striyāṃ nihanyate manaḥ |
(6.70.1.e) evā te aghnye mano 'dhi vatse ni hanyatām ‖1‖
(6.70.2’) yathā hastī hastinyāḥ padena padam udyuje |
(6.70.2.c) yathā puṃso vṛṣaṇyata striyāṃ nihanyate manaḥ |
(6.70.2.e) evā te aghnye mano 'dhi vatse ni hanyatām ‖2‖
(6.70.3’) yathā pradhir yathopadhir yathā nabhyaṃ pradhāv adhi |
(6.70.3.c) yathā puṃso vṛṣaṇyata striyāṃ nihanyate manaḥ |
(6.70.3.e) evā te aghnye mano 'dhi vatse ni hanyatām ‖3‖

(6.71.1’) yad annam admi bahudhā virūpaṃ hiraṇyam aśvam uta gām ajām avim |
(6.71.1.c) yad eva kiṃ ca pratijagrahāham agniṣ ṭad dhotā suhutaṃ kṛnotu ‖1‖
(6.71.2’) yan mā hutam ahutam ājagāma dattaṃ pitṛbhir anumataṃ manuṣyaiḥ |
(6.71.2.c) yasmān me mana ud iva rārajīty agniṣ ṭad dhotā suhutaṃ kṛṇotu ‖2‖
(6.71.3’) yad annam admy anṛtena devā dāsyann adāsyann uta saṃgṛṇāmi |
(6.71.3.c) vaiśvānarasya mahato mahimnā śivaṃ mahyaṃ madhumad astv annam ‖3‖

(6.72.1’) yathāsitaḥ prathayate vaśām anu vapūṃṣi kṛṇvann asurasya māyayā |
(6.72.1.c) evā te śepaḥ sahasāyam arko 'ṅgenāṅgaṃ saṃsamakaṃ kṛṇotu ‖1‖
(6.72.2’) yathā pasas tāyādaraṃ vātena sthūlabhaṃ kṛtam |
(6.72.2.c) yāvat parasvataḥ pasas tāvat te vardhatāṃ pasaḥ ‖2‖
(6.72.3’) yāvad aṅgīnaṃ pārasvataṃ hāstinaṃ gārdabham ca yat |
(6.72.3.c) yāvad aśvasya vajinas tāvat te vardhatāṃ pasaḥ ‖3‖

(6.73.1’) eha yātu varuṇaḥ somo agnir bṛhaspatir vasubhir eha yātu |
(6.73.1.c) asya śriyam upasaṃyāta sarva ugrasya cettuḥ saṃmanasaḥ sajātāḥ ‖1‖
(6.73.2’) yo vaḥ śuṣmo hṛdayeṣv antar ākūtir yā vo manasi praviṣṭā |
(6.73.2.c) tānt sīvayāmi haviṣā ghṛtena mayi sajātā ramatir vo astu ‖2‖
(6.73.3’) ihaiva sta māpa yātādhy asmat pūṣā parastād apatham vaḥ kṛṇotu |
(6.73.3.c) vāstoṣ patir anu vo johavītu mayi sajātā ramatiḥ vo astu ‖3‖

(6.74.1’) saṃ vaḥ pṛcyantāṃ tanvaḥ saṃ manāṃsi sam u vratā |
(6.74.1.c) sam vo 'yam brahmaṇas patir bhagaḥ saṃ vo ajīgamat ‖1‖
(6.74.2’) samjñapanaṃ vo manaso 'tho samjñapanam hṛdaḥ |
(6.74.2.c) atho bhagasya yac chrāntaṃ tena saṃjñapayāmi vaḥ ‖2‖
(6.74.3’) yathādityā vasubhiḥ sambabhūvur marudbhir ugrā ahṛṇīyamānāḥ |
(6.74.3.c) evā triṇāmann ahṛṇīyamāna imān janānt saṃmanasas kṛdhīha ‖3‖

(6.75.1’) nir amuṃ nuda okasaḥ sapatno yaḥ pṛtanyati |
(6.75.1.c) nairbādhyena haviṣendra enaṃ parāśarīt ‖1‖
(6.75.2’) paramāṃ taṃ parāvatam indro nudatu vṛtrahā |
(6.75.2.c) yato na punar āyati śaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyaḥ ‖2‖
(6.75.3’) etu tisraḥ parāvata etu pañca janā;m ati |
(6.75.3.c) etu tisro 'ti rocanā yato na punar āyati |
(6.75.3.e) śaśvatībhyaḥ samābhyo yāvat sūryo asad divi ‖3‖

(6.76.1’) ya enaṃ pariṣīdanti samādadhati cakṣase |
(6.76.1.c) saṃpreddho agnir jihvābhir ud etu hṛdayād adhi ‖1‖
(6.76.2’) agneḥ sāmtapanasyāham āyuṣe padam ā rabhe |
(6.76.2.c) addhātir yasya paśyati dhūmam udyantam āsyataḥ ‖2‖
(6.76.3’) yo asya samidhaṃ veda kṣatriyeṇa samāhitām |
(6.76.3.c) nābhihvāre padaṃ ni dadhāti sa mṛtyave ‖3‖
(6.76.4’) nainaṃ ghnanti paryāyino na sannā;m ava gachati |
(6.76.4.c) agner yaḥ kṣatriyo vidvān nāma gṛhnāti āyuṣe ‖4‖

(6.77.1’) asthād dyaur asthāt pṛthivy asthād viśvam idaṃ jagat |
(6.77.1.c) āsthāne parvatā asthu sthāmny aśvā;m atiṣṭhipam ‖1‖
(6.77.2’) ya udānat parāyaṇaṃ ya udānaṇ nyāyanam |
(6.77.2.c) āvartanam nivartanaṃ yo gopā api taṃ huve ‖2‖
(6.77.3’) jātavedo ni vartaya śataṃ te santv āvṛtaḥ |
(6.77.3.c) sahasraṃ ta upāvṛtas tabhir naḥ punar ā kṛdhi ‖3‖

(6.78.1’) tena bhūtena haviṣāyam ā pyāyatāṃ punaḥ |
(6.78.1.c) jāyām yām asmā āvākṣus tām rasenābhi vardhatām ‖1‖
(6.78.2’) abhi vardhatāṃ payasābhi rāṣṭreṇa vardhatām |
(6.78.2.c) rayyā sahasravarcasemau stām anupakṣitau ‖2‖
(6.78.3’) tvaṣṭā jāyām ajanayat tvaṣṭāsyai tvāṃ patim |
(6.78.3.c) tvaṣṭā sahasram āyuṃṣi dīrgham āyuḥ kṛṇotu vām ‖3‖

(6.79.1’) ayaṃ no nabhasas patiḥ saṃsphāno abhi rakṣatu |
(6.79.1.c) asamātim gṛheṣu naḥ ‖1‖
(6.79.2’) tvaṃ no nabhasas pate ūrjaṃ gṛhesu dhāraya |
(6.79.2.c) ā puṣṭam etv ā vasu ‖2‖
(6.79.3’) deva saṃsphāna sahasrāpoṣasyeśiṣe |
(6.79.3.c) tasya no rāsva tasya no dhehi tasya te bhaktivāmsaḥ syāma ‖3‖

(6.80.1’) antarikṣeṇa patati viśvā bhūtāvacākaśat |
(6.80.1.c) śuno divyasya yan mahas tenā te haviṣā vidhema ‖1‖
(6.80.2’) ye trayaḥ kālakāñjā divi devā iva śritāḥ |
(6.80.2.c) tān sarvān ahva ūtaye 'smā ariṣṭatātaye ‖2‖
(6.80.3’) apsu te janma divi te sadhasthaṃ samudre antar mahimā te pṛthivyām |
(6.80.3.c) śuno divyasya yan mahas tenā te haviṣā vidhema ‖3‖

(6.81.1’) yantāsi yachase hastāv apa rakṣāṃsi sedhasi |
(6.81.1.c) prajāṃ dhanaṃ ca gṛhṇānaḥ parihasto abhūd ayam ‖1‖
(6.81.2’) parihasta vi dhāraya yoniṃ garbhāya dhātave |
(6.81.2.c) maryāde putram ā dhehi taṃ tvam ā gamayāgame ‖2‖
(6.81.3’) yaṃ parihastam abibhar aditiḥ putrakāmyā |
(6.81.3.c) tvaṣṭā tam asyā ā badhnād yathā putraṃ janād ‖3‖

(6.82.1’) āgachata āgatasya nāma gṛhṇāmy āyataḥ |
(6.82.1.c) indrasya vṛtraghno vanve vāsavasya śatakratoḥ ‖1‖
(6.82.2’) yena sūryāṃ sāvitrīm aśvinohatuḥ pathā |
(6.82.2.c) tena mām abravīd bhago jayām ā vahatād iti ‖2‖
(6.82.3’) yas te 'ṅkuśo vasudāno bṛhann indra hiraṇyayaḥ |
(6.82.3.c) tenā janiyate jāyāṃ mahyaṃ dhehi śacīpate ‖3‖

(6.83.1’) apacitaḥ pra patata suparṇo vasater iva |
(6.83.1.c) sūryaḥ kṛṇotu bheṣajaṃ candramā vo 'pochatu ‖1‖
(6.83.2’) eny ekā śyeny ekā kṛṣṇaikā rohiṇī dve |
(6.83.2.c) sarvāsām agrabhaṃ nāmāvīraghnīr apetana ‖2‖
(6.83.3’) asūtikā rāmāyaṇy apacit pra patiṣyati |
(6.83.3.c) glaur itaḥ pra patiṣyati sa galunto naśiṣyati ‖3‖
(6.83.4’) vīhi svām āhutiṃ juṣāno manasā svāhā manasā yad idaṃ juhomi ‖4‖

(6.84.1’) yasyās ta āsani ghore juhomy eṣāṃ baddhānām avasarjanāya kam |
(6.84.1.c) bhūmir iti tvābhipramanvate janā nirṛtir iti tvāhaṃ pari veda sarvataḥ ‖1‖
(6.84.2’) bhūte haviṣmatī bhavaiṣa te bhāgo yo asmāsu |
(6.84.2.c) muñcemān amūn enasaḥ svāhā ‖2‖
(6.84.3’) evo ṣv asman nirṛte 'nehā tvam ayasmayān vi cṛtā bandhapāśān |
(6.84.3.c) yamo mahyaṃ punar it tvāṃ dadāti tasmai yamāya namo astu mṛtyave ‖3‖
(6.84.4’) ayasmaye drupade bedhiṣa ihābhihito mṛtyubhir ye sahasram |
(6.84.4.c) yamena tvaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidāna uttamaṃ nākam adhi rohayemam ‖4‖

(6.85.1’) varaṇo vārayātā ayaṃ devo vanaspatiḥ |
(6.85.1.c) yakṣmo yo asminn āviṣṭas tam u devā avīvaran ‖1‖
(6.85.2’) indrasya vacasā vayaṃ mitrasya varuṇasya ca |
(6.85.2.c) devānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vācā yakṣmaṃ te vārayāmahe ‖2‖
(6.85.3’) yathā vṛtra imā āpas tastambha viśvadhā yatīḥ |
(6.85.3.c) evā te agninā yakṣmaṃ vaiśvānareṇa vāraye ‖3‖

(6.86.1’) vṛṣendrasya vṛṣā divo vṛsā pṛthivyā ayam |
(6.86.1.c) vṛṣā viśvasya bhūtasya tvam ekavṛṣo bhava ‖1‖
(6.86.2’) samudra īśe sravatām agniḥ pṛthivyā vaśī |
(6.86.2.c) candramā nakṣatrāṇām īśe tvam ekavṛṣo bhava ‖2‖
(6.86.3’) samrāḍ asy asurāṇāṃ kakun manuṣyānām |
(6.86.3.c) devānām ardhabhāg asi tvam ekavṛṣo bhava ‖3‖

(6.87.1’) ā tvāhārṣam antar abhūr dhruvas tiṣṭhāvicācalat |
(6.87.1.c) viśas tvā sarvā vāñchantu mā tvad rāṣṭram adhi bhraśat ‖1‖
(6.87.2’) ihaivaidhi māpa cyoṣṭhāḥ parvata ivāvicācalat |
(6.87.2.c) indra iveha dhruvas tiṣṭheha rāṣṭram u dhāraya ‖2‖
(6.87.3’) indra etam adīdharat dhruvaṃ dhruveṇa haviṣā |
(6.87.3.c) tasmai somo adhi bravad ayaṃ ca brahmaṇas patiḥ ‖3‖

(6.88.1’) dhruvā dyaur dhruvā pṛthivī dhruvaṃ viśvam idaṃ jagat |
(6.88.1.c) dhruvāsaḥ parvatā ime dhruvo rājā viśām ayam ‖1‖
(6.88.2’) dhruvaṃ te rājā varuṇo dhruvam devo bṛhaspatiḥ |
(6.88.2.c) dhruvaṃ ta indraś cāgniś ca rāṣṭraṃ dhārayatāṃ dhruvam ‖2‖
(6.88.3’) dhruvo 'cyutaḥ pra mṛṇīhi śatrūn chatrūyato 'dharān pādayasva |
(6.88.3.c) sarvā diśaḥ saṃmanasaḥ sadhrīcīr dhruvāya te samitiḥ kalpatām iha ‖3‖

(6.89.1’) idaṃ yat preṇyaḥ śiro dattaṃ somena vṛṣṇyam |
(6.89.1.c) tataḥ pari prajātena hārdiṃ te śocayāmasi ‖1‖
(6.89.2’) śocayāmasi te hārdiṃ śocayāmasi te manaḥ |
(6.89.2.c) vātaṃ dhūma iva sadhryaṅ mām evānv etu ye manaḥ ‖2‖
(6.89.3’) mahyaṃ tvā mitrāvaruṇau mahyaṃ devī sarasvatī |
(6.89.3.c) mahyaṃ tvā madhyaṃ bhūmyā ubhāv antau sam asyatām ‖3‖

(6.90.1’) yāṃ te rudra iṣum āsyad aṅgebhyo hṛdayāya ca |
(6.90.1.c) idaṃ tām adya tvad vayaṃ viṣūcīṃ vi vṛhāmasi ‖1‖
(6.90.2’) yās te śataṃ dhamanayo 'ṅgāny anu viṣṭhitāḥ |
(6.90.2.c) tāsāṃ te sarvāsām vayaṃ nir viṣāṇi hvayāmasi ‖2‖
(6.90.3’) namas te rudrāsyate namaḥ pratihitāyai |
(6.90.3.c) namo visṛjyamānāyai namo nipatitāyai ‖3‖

(6.91.1’) imaṃ yavam aṣṭāyogaiḥ ṣadyogebhir acarkṛṣuḥ |
(6.91.1.c) tenā te tanvo rapo 'pācīnam apa vyaye ‖1‖
(6.91.2’) nyag vāto vāti nyak tapati sūryaḥ |
(6.91.2.c) nīcīnam aghnyā duhe nyag bhavatu te rapaḥ ‖2‖
(6.91.3’) āpa id vā u bheṣajīr āpo amīvacātanīḥ |
(6.91.3.c) āpo viśvasya bheṣajīs tās te kṛṇvantu bheṣajam ‖3‖

(6.92.1’) vātaramhā bhava vājin yujamāna indrasya yāhi prasave manojavāḥ |
(6.92.1.c) yuñjantu tvā maruto viśvavedasa ā te tvastā patsu javaṃ dadhātu ‖1‖
(6.92.2’) javas te arvan nihito guhā yaḥ śyene vāte uta yo 'carat parīttaḥ |
(6.92.2.c) tena tvaṃ vājin balavān balenājiṃ jaya samane parayiṣṇuḥ ‖2‖
(6.92.3’) tanūṣ ṭe vājin tanvaṃ nayantī vāmam asmabhyaṃ dhāvatu śarma tubhyam |
(6.92.3.c) ahruto maho dharuṇāya devo divīva jyotih svam ā mimīyāt ‖3‖

(6.93.1’) yamo mṛtyur aghamāro nirṛtho babhruḥ śarvo 'stā nīlaśikhaṇḍaḥ |
(6.93.1.c) devajanāḥ senayottasthivāṃsas te asmākaṃ pari vṛñjantu vīrān ‖1‖
(6.93.2’) manasā homair harasā ghṛtena śarvāyāstra uta rājñe bhavāya |
(6.93.2.c) namasyebhyo nama ebhyaḥ kṛṇomy anyatrāsmad aghaviṣā nayantu ‖2‖
(6.93.3’) trāyadhvaṃ no aghaviṣābhyo vadhād viśve devā maruto viśvavedasaḥ |
(6.93.3.c) agnīṣomā varuṇaḥ pūtadakṣā vātāparjanyayoḥ sumatau syāma ‖3‖

(6.94.1’) saṃ vo manāṃsi saṃ vratā sam ākūtīr namāmasi |
(6.94.1.c) amī ye vivratā sthana tān vaḥ saṃ namayāmasi ‖1‖
(6.94.2’) ahaṃ gṛbhṇāmi manasā manāṃsi mama cittam anu cittebhir eta |
(6.94.2.c) mama vaśeṣu hṛdayāni vaḥ kṛṇomi mama yātam anuvartmāna eta ‖2‖
(6.94.3’) ote me dyāvāpṛthivī otā devī sarasvatī |
(6.94.3.c) otau ma indraś cāgniś ca rdhyāsmedaṃ sarasvati ‖3‖

(6.95.1’) aśvattho devasadanas tṛtīyasyām ito divi |
(6.95.1.c) tatrāmṛtasya cakṣaṇam devāḥ kuṣṭham avanvata ‖1‖
(6.95.2’) hiraṇyayī naur acarad dhiraṇyabandhanā divi |
(6.95.2.c) tatrāmṛtasya puṣpaṃ devāḥ kuṣṭham avanvata ‖2‖
(6.95.3’) garbho asy oṣadhīnāṃ garbho himavatām uta |
(6.95.3.c) garbho viśvasya bhūtasyemaṃ me agadaṃ kṛdhi ‖3‖

(6.96.1’) yā oṣadhayaḥ somarājñīr bahvīḥ śatavicakṣaṇāḥ |
(6.96.1.c) bṛhaspatiprasūtās tā no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(6.96.2’) muñcantu mā śapathyād atho varuṇyād uta |
(6.96.2.c) atho yamasya paḍvīṣād viśvasmād devakilbiṣāt ‖2‖
(6.96.3’) yac cakṣuṣā manasā yac ca vācopārima jāgrato yat svapantaḥ |
(6.96.3.c) somas tāni svadhayā naḥ punātu ‖3‖

(6.97.1’) abhibhūr yajño abhibhūr agnir abhibhūḥ somo abhibhūr indraḥ |
(6.97.1.c) abhy ahaṃ viśvāḥ pṛtanā yathāsāny evā vidhemāgnihotrā idaṃ haviḥ ‖1‖
(6.97.2’) svadhāstu mitrāvaruṇā vipaścitā prajāvat kṣatraṃ madhuneha pinvatam |
(6.97.2.c) bādhethāṃ dūraṃ nirṛtiṃ parācaiḥ kṛtaṃ cid enaḥ pra mumuktam asmat ‖2‖
(6.97.3’) imaṃ vīram anu harṣadhvam ugram indraṃ sakhāyo anu saṃ rabhadhvam |
(6.97.3.c) grāmajitaṃ gojitaṃ vajrabāhuṃ jayantam ajma pramṛṇantam ojasā ‖3‖

(6.98.1’) indro jayāti na parā jayātā adhirājo rājasu rājayātai |
(6.98.1.c) carkṛtya īḍyo vandyaś copasadyo namasyś bhaveha ‖1‖
(6.98.2’) tvam indrādhirājaḥ śravasyus tvaṃ bhūr abhibhūtir janānām |
(6.98.2.c) tvaṃ daivīr viśa imā vi rājāyuṣmat kṣatram ajaraṃ te astu ‖2‖
(6.98.3’) prācyā diśas tvam indrāsi rājotodīcyā diśo vṛtrahan chatruho 'si |
(6.98.3.c) yatra yanti srotyās taj jitaṃ te dakṣiṇato vṛṣabha eṣi havyaḥ ‖3‖

(6.99.1’) abhi tvendra varimataḥ purā tvāṃhūraṇād dhuve |
(6.99.1.c) hvayāmy ugraṃ cettāraṃ puruṇāmānam ekajam ‖1‖
(6.99.2’) yo adya senyo vadho jighāṃsan na udīrate |
(6.99.2.c) indrasya tatra bāhū samantaṃ pari dadmaḥ ‖2‖
(6.99.3’) pari dadma indrasya bāhū samantaṃ trātus trāyatāṃ naḥ |
(6.99.3.c) deva savitaḥ soma rājan sumanasaṃ mā kṛṇu svastaye ‖3‖

(6.100.1’) devā aduḥ sūryo dyaur adāt pṛthivy adāt |
(6.100.1.c) tisraḥ sarasvatir aduḥ sacittā viṣadūṣaṇam ‖1‖
(6.100.2’) yad vo devā upajīkā āsiñcan dhanvany udakam |
(6.100.2.c) tena devaprasūtenedaṃ dūṣayatā viṣam ‖2‖
(6.100.3’) asurāṇāṃ duhitāsi sā devānām asi svasā |
(6.100.3.c) divas pṛthivyāḥ saṃbhūtā sā cakarthārasaṃ viṣam ‖3‖

(6.101.1’) ā vṛṣāyasva śvasihi vardhasva prathayasva ca |
(6.101.1.c) yathāṅgaṃ vardhatāṃ śepas tena yoṣitam ij jahi ‖1‖
(6.101.2’) yena kṛṣaṃ vājayanti yena hinvanty āturam |
(6.101.2.c) tenāsya brahmaṇas pate dhanur ivā tānayā pasaḥ ‖2‖
(6.101.3’) āhaṃ tanomi te paso adhi jyām iva dhanvani |
(6.101.3.c) kramasva ṛṣa iva rohitam anavaglāyatā sadā ‖3‖

(6.102.1’) yathayaṃ vāho aśvinā samaiti saṃ ca vartate |
(6.102.1.c) evā mām abhi te manaḥ samaitu saṃ ca vartatām ‖1‖
(6.102.2’) āhaṃ khidāmi te mano rājāśvaḥ pṛṣṭyām iva |
(6.102.2.c) reṣmachinnam yathā tṛṇaṃ mayi te veṣṭatāṃ manaḥ ‖2‖
(6.102.3’) āñjanasya madughasya kuṣṭhasya naladasya ca |
(6.102.3.c) turo bhagasya hastābhyām anurodhanam ud bhare ‖3‖

(6.103.1’) saṃdānaṃ vo bṛhaspatiḥ saṃdānaṃ savitā karat |
(6.103.1.c) saṃdānaṃ mitro aryamā saṃdānaṃ bhago aśvinā ‖1‖
(6.103.2’) sam paramānt sam avamān atho saṃ dyāmi madhyamān |
(6.103.2.c) indras tān pary ahār dāmnā tān agne saṃ dyā tvam ‖2‖
(6.103.3’) amī ye yudham āyanti ketūn kṛtvānīkaśaḥ |
(6.103.3.c) indras tān pary ahār dāmna tān agne saṃ dyā tvam ‖3‖

(6.104.1’) ādānena saṃdānenāmitrān ā dyāmasi |
(6.104.1.c) apānā ye caiṣāṃ prāṇā asunāsūnt sam achidan ‖1‖
(6.104.2’) idam ādānam akaraṃ tapasendreṇa saṃśitam |
(6.104.2.c) amitrā ye 'tra naḥ santi tān agna ā dyā tvam ‖2‖
(6.104.3’) ainān dyatām indrāgnī somo rājā ca medinau |
(6.104.3.c) indro marutvān ādānam amitrebhyaḥ kṛṇotu naḥ ‖3‖

(6.105.1’) yathā mano manasketaiḥ parāpataty āśumat |
(6.105.1.c) evā tvaṃ kāse pra pata manaso 'nu pravāyyam ‖1‖
(6.105.2’) yathā bāṇaḥ susaṃśitaḥ parāpataty āśumat |
(6.105.2.c) evā tvaṃ kāse pra pata pṛthivyā anu saṃvatam ‖2‖
(6.105.3’) yathā sūryasya raśmayaḥ parāpatanty āśumat |
(6.105.3.c) evā tvaṃ kāse pra pata samudrasyānu vikṣaram ‖3‖

(6.106.1’) āyane te parāyane dūrvā rohantu puṣpiṇīḥ |
(6.106.1.c) utso vā tatra jāyatām hrado vā puṇḍarīkavān ‖1‖
(6.106.2’) apām idaṃ nyayanaṃ samudrasya niveśanam |
(6.106.2.c) madhye hradasya no gṛhāḥ parācīnā mukhā kṛdhi ‖2‖
(6.106.3’) himasya tvā jarāyuṇā śāle pari vyayāmasi |
(6.106.3.c) śītahradā hi no bhuvo 'gniṣ kṛṇotu bheṣajam ‖3‖

(6.107.1’) viśvajit trāyamāṇāyai mā pari dehi |
(6.107.1.c) trāyamāṇe dvipāc ca sarvaṃ no rakṣa catuṣpāḍ yac ca naḥ svam ‖1‖
(6.107.2’) trāyamāṇe viśvajite mā pari dehi |
(6.107.2.c) viśvajid dvipāc ca sarvaṃ no rakṣa catuṣpāḍ yac ca naḥ svam ‖2‖
(6.107.3’) viśvajit kalyāṇyai mā pari dehi |
(6.107.3.c) kalyāṇi dvipāc ca sarvaṃ no rakṣa catuṣpāḍ yac ca naḥ svam ‖3‖
(6.107.4’) kalyāṇi sarvavide mā pari dehi |
(6.107.4.c) sarvavid dvipāc ca sarvaṃ no rakṣa catuṣpāḍ yac ca naḥ svam ‖4‖

(6.108.1’) tvaṃ no medhe prathamā gobhir aśvebhir ā gahi |
(6.108.1.c) tvaṃ sūryasya raśmibhis tvam no asi yajñiyā ‖1‖
(6.108.2’) medhām ahaṃ prathamāṃ brahmaṇvatīṃ brahmajūtām ṛṣiṣṭutām |
(6.108.2.c) prapītāṃ brahmacāribhir devānām avase huve ‖2‖
(6.108.3’) yāṃ medhām ṛbhavo vidur yāṃ medhām asurā viduḥ |
(6.108.3.c) ṛṣayo bhadrāṃ medhāṃ yāṃ vidus tāṃ mayy ā veśayāmasi ‖3‖
(6.108.4’) yām ṛṣayo bhūtakṛto medhāṃ medhāvino viduḥ |
(6.108.4.c) tayā mām adya medhayāgne medhāvinaṃ kṛṇu ‖4‖
(6.108.5’) medhāṃ sāyaṃ medhāṃ prātar medhāṃ madhyandinaṃ pari |
(6.108.5.c) medhāṃ sūryasya raśmibhir vacasā veśayāmahe ‖5‖

(6.109.1’) pippalī kṣiptabheṣajy utātividdhabheṣajī |
(6.109.1.c) tāṃ devāḥ sam akalpayann iyaṃ jīvitavā alam ‖1‖
(6.109.2’) pippalyaḥ sam avadantāyatīr jananād adhi |
(6.109.2.c) yaṃ jīvam aśnavāmahai na sa riṣyāti pūruṣaḥ ‖2‖
(6.109.3’) asurās tvā ny akhanan devās tvod avapan punaḥ |
(6.109.3.c) vātīkṛtasya bheṣajīm atho kṣiptasya bheṣajīm ‖3‖

(6.110.1’) pratno hi kam īḍyo adhvareṣu sanāc ca hotā navyaś ca satsi |
(6.110.1.c) svām cāgne tanvaṃ piprāyasvāsmabhyaṃ ca saubhagam ā yajasva ‖1‖
(6.110.2’) jyeṣṭhaghnyāṃ jāto vicṛtor yamasya mūlabarhaṇāt pari pāhy enam |
(6.110.2.c) aty enam neṣad duritāni viśvā dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāya ‖2‖
(6.110.3’) vyāghre 'hny ajaniṣṭa vīro nakṣatrajā jāyamānaḥ suvīraḥ |
(6.110.3.c) sa mā vadhīt pitaraṃ vardhamāno mā mātaraṃ pra minīj janitrīm ‖3‖

(6.111.1’) imam me agne puruṣam mumugdhy ayaṃ yo baddhaḥ suyato lālapīti |
(6.111.1.c) ato 'dhi te kṛṇavad bhāgadheyaṃ yadānunmadito 'sati ‖1‖
(6.111.2’) agniṣ ṭe ni śamayatu yadi te mana udyutam |
(6.111.2.c) kṛṇomi vidvān bheṣajaṃ yathānunmadito 'sasi ‖2‖
(6.111.3’) devainasād unmaditam unmattam rakṣasas pari |
(6.111.3.c) kṛṇomi vidvān bheṣajaṃ yadānunmadito 'sati ‖3‖
(6.111.4’) punas tvā dur apsarasaḥ punar indraḥ punar bhagaḥ |
(6.111.4.c) punas tvā dur viśve devā yathānunmadito 'sasi ‖4‖

(6.112.1’) mā jyeṣṭhaṃ vadhīd ayam agna eṣām mūlabarhaṇāt pari pāhy enam |
(6.112.1.c) sa grāhyāḥ pāśān vi cṛta prajānan tubhyaṃ devā anu jānantu viśve ‖1‖
(6.112.2’) un muñca pāśāṃs tvam agna eṣāṃ trayas tribhir utsitā yebhir āsan |
(6.112.2.c) sa grāhyāḥ pāśān vi cṛta prajānan pitāputrau mātaraṃ muñca sarvān ‖2‖
(6.112.3’) yebhiḥ pāśaiḥ parivitto vibaddho 'ṅgeaṅga ārpita utsitaś ca |
(6.112.3.c) vi te mucyantaṃ vimuco hi santi bhrūṇaghni pūṣan duritāni mṛkṣva ‖3‖

(6.113.1’) trite devā amṛjataitad enas trita enan manuṣyeṣu mamṛje |
(6.113.1.c) tato yadi tvā grāhir ānaśe tāṃ te devā brahmaṇā nāśayantu ‖1‖
(6.113.2’) marīcīr dhūmān pra viśānu pāpmann udārān gachota vā nīhārān |
(6.113.2.c) nadīnaṃ phenām anu tān vi naśya bhrūṇaghni pūṣan duritāni mṛkṣva ‖2‖
(6.113.3’) dvādaśadhā nihitaṃ tritasyāpamṛṣṭam manuṣyainasāni |
(6.113.3.c) tato yadi tvā grāhir ānaśe tāṃ te devā brahmaṇā nāśayantu ‖3‖

(6.114.1’) yad devā devahedanaṃ devāsaś cakṛma vayam |
(6.114.1.c) ādityās tasmān no yuyam ṛtasya ṛtena muñcata ‖1‖
(6.114.2’) ṛtasya ṛtenādityā yajatrā muñcateha naḥ |
(6.114.2.c) yajñaṃ yad yajñavāhasaḥ śikṣanto nopaśekima ‖2‖
(6.114.3’) medasvatā yajamānāḥ srucājyāni juhvataḥ |
(6.114.3.c) akāmā viśve vo devāḥ śikṣanto nopa śekima ‖3‖

(6.115.1’) yad vidvāmso yad avidvāṃsa enāṃsi cakṛmā vayam |
(6.115.1.c) yūyaṃ nas tasmān muñcata viśve devāḥ sajoṣasah ‖1‖
(6.115.2’) yadi jāgrad yadi svapann ena enasyo 'karam |
(6.115.2.c) bhūtaṃ mā tasmād bhavyaṃ ca drupadād iva muñcatām ‖2‖
(6.115.3’) drupadād iva mumucānaḥ svinnaḥ snātvā malād iva |
(6.115.3.c) pūtaṃ pavitreṇevājyaṃ viśve śumbhantu mainasaḥ ‖3‖

(6.116.1’) yad yāmam cakrur nikhananto agre kārṣīvaṇā annavido na vidyayā |
(6.116.1.c) vaivasvate rājani taj juhomy atha yajñiyaṃ madhumad astu no 'nnam ‖1‖
(6.116.2’) vaivasvataḥ kṛṇavad bhāgadheyaṃ madhubhāgo madhunā saṃ sṛjāti |
(6.116.2.c) mātur yad ena iṣitaṃ na āgan yad vā pitā 'parāddho jihīde ‖2‖
(6.116.3’) yadīdaṃ mātur yadi pitur naḥ pari bhrātuḥ putrāc cetasa ena āgan |
(6.116.3.c) yāvanto asmān pitaraḥ sacante teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ śivo astu manyuḥ ‖3‖

(6.117.1’) apamityam apratīttaṃ yad asmi yamasya yena balinā carāmi |
(6.117.1.c) idaṃ tad agne anṛṇo bhavāmi tvaṃ pāśān vicṛtaṃ vettha sarvān ‖1‖
(6.117.2’) ihaiva santaḥ prati dadma enaj jīvā jīvebhyo ni harāma enat |
(6.117.2.c) apamitya dhānyaṃ yaj jaghasāham idaṃ tad agne anṛṇo bhavāmi ‖2‖
(6.117.3’) anṛṇā asminn anṛṇāḥ parasmin tṛtīye loke anṛṇāḥ syāma |
(6.117.3.c) ye devayānāḥ pitṛyāṇaś ca lokāḥ sarvān patho anṛṇā ā kṣiyema ‖3‖

(6.118.1’) yad dhastābhyāṃ cakṛma kilbiṣāny akṣānāṃ gatnum upalipsamānāḥ |
(6.118.1.c) ugraṃpaśye ugrajitau tad adyāpsarasāv anu dattām ṛṇaṃ naḥ ‖1‖
(6.118.2’) ugraṃpaśye rāṣṭrabhṛt kilbiṣāṇi yad akṣavṛttam anu dattam na etat |
(6.118.2.c) ṛṇān no na ṛṇam ertsamāno yamasya loke adhirajjur āyat ‖2‖
(6.118.3’) yasmā ṛṇaṃ yasya jāyām upaimi yaṃ yācamāno abhyaimi devāḥ |
(6.118.3.c) te vācaṃ vādiṣur mottarāṃ mad devapatnī apsarasāv adhītam ‖3‖

(6.119.1’) yad adīvyann ṛṇam ahaṃ kṛṇomy adāsyann agne uta saṃgṛṇāmi |
(6.119.1.c) vaiśvānaro no adhipā vasiṣṭha ud in nayāti sukṛtasya lokam ‖1‖
(6.119.2’) vaiśvānarāya prati vedayāmi yadi ṛṇaṃ saṃgaro devatāsu |
(6.119.2.c) sa etān pāśān vicṛtam veda sarvān atha pakvena saha saṃ bhavema ‖2‖
(6.119.3’) vaiśvānaraḥ pavitā mā punātu yat saṃgaram abhidhāvāmy āśām |
(6.119.3.c) anājānan manasā yācamāno yat tatraino apa tat suvāmi ‖3‖

(6.120.1’) yad antarikṣaṃ pṛthivīm uta dyām yan mātaraṃ pitaraṃ vā jihiṃsima |
(6.120.1.c) ayaṃ tasmād gārhapatyo no agnir ud in nayāti sukṛtasya lokam ‖1‖
(6.120.2’) bhūmir mātāditir no janitraṃ bhrātāntarikṣam abhiśastyā naḥ |
(6.120.2.c) dyaur naḥ pitā pitryāc chaṃ bhavāti jāmim ṛtvā māva patsi lokāt ‖2‖
(6.120.3’) yatrā suhārdaḥ sukṛto madanti vihāya rogaṃ tanvaḥ svāyāḥ |
(6.120.3.c) aślonā aṅgair ahrutāḥ svarge tatra paśyema pitarau ca putrān ‖3‖

(6.121.1’) viṣāṇā pāśān vi ṣyādhy asmad ya uttamā adhamā vāruṇā ye |
(6.121.1.c) duṣvapnyaṃ duritaṃ ni ṣvāsmad atha gachema sukṛtasya lokam ‖1‖
(6.121.2’) yad dāruṇi badhyase yac ca rajjvāṃ yad bhūmyāṃ badhyase yac ca vācā |
(6.121.2.c) ayaṃ tasmād gārhapatyo no agnir ud in nayāti sukṛtasya lokam ‖2‖
(6.121.3’) ud agātāṃ bhagavatī vicṛtau nāma tārake |
(6.121.3.c) prehāmṛtasya yachatāṃ praitu baddhakamocanam ‖3‖
(6.121.4’) vi jihīṣva lokam kṛṇu bandhān muñcāsi baddhakam |
(6.121.4.c) yonyā iva pracyuto garbhaḥ pathaḥ sarvā;m anu kṣiya ‖4‖

(6.122.1’) etaṃ bhāgaṃ pari dadāmi vidvān viśvakarman prathamajā ṛtasya |
(6.122.1.c) asmābhir dattaṃ jarasaḥ parastād achinnaṃ tantum anu saṃ tarema ‖1‖
(6.122.2’) tataṃ tantum anv eke taranti yeṣāṃ dattaṃ pitryam āyanena |
(6.122.2.c) abandhv eke dadataḥ prayachanto dātuṃ cec chikṣānt sa svarga eva ‖2‖
(6.122.3’) anvārabhethām anusaṃrabhethām etaṃ lokaṃ śraddadhānāḥ sacante |
(6.122.3.c) yad vāṃ pakvaṃ pariviṣṭam agnau tasya guptaye dampatī saṃ śrayethām ‖3‖
(6.122.4’) yajñam yantaṃ manasā bṛhantam anvārohāmi tapasā sayoniḥ |
(6.122.4.c) upahūtā agne jarasaḥ parastāt tṛtīye nāke sadhamādaṃ madema ‖4‖
(6.122.5’) śuddhāḥ pūtā yoṣito yajñiyā imā brahmaṇāṃ hasteṣu prapṛthak sādayāmi |
(6.122.5.c) yatkāma idaṃ abhiṣiñcāmi vo 'haṃ indro marutvānt sa dadātu tan me ‖5‖

(6.123.1’) etaṃ sadhasthāḥ pari vo dadāmi yaṃ śevadhim āvahāj jātavedaḥ |
(6.123.1.c) anvāgantā yajamānaḥ svasti taṃ sma jānīta parame vyoman ‖1‖
(6.123.2’) jānīta smainaṃ parame vyoman devāḥ sadhasthā vida lokam atra |
(6.123.2.c) anvāgantā yajamānaḥ svastīṣṭāpūrtaṃ sma kṛṇutāvir asmai ‖2‖
(6.123.3’) devāḥ pitaraḥ pitaro devāḥ |
(6.123.3.c) yo asmi so asmi ‖3‖
(6.123.4’) sa pacāmi sa dadāmi |
(6.123.4.c) sa yaje sa dattān mā yūṣam ‖4‖
(6.123.5’) nāke rājan prati tiṣṭha tatraitat prati tiṣṭhatu |
(6.123.5.c) viddhi pūrtasya no rājant sa deva sumanā bhava ‖5‖

(6.124.1’) divo nu mām bṛhato antarikṣād apāṃ stoko abhy apaptad rasena |
(6.124.1.c) sam indriyena payasāham agne chandobhir yajñaiḥ sukṛtāṃ kṛtena ‖1‖
(6.124.2’) yadi vṛkṣād abhyapaptat phalaṃ tad yady antarikṣāt sa u vāyur eva |
(6.124.2.c) yatrāspṛkṣat tanvo yac ca vāsasa āpo nudantu nirṛtiṃ parācaiḥ ‖2‖
(6.124.3’) abhyañjanaṃ surabhi sā samṛddhir hiraṇyaṃ varcas tad u pūtrimam eva |
(6.124.3.c) sarvā pavitrā vitatādhy asmat tan mā tārīn nirṛtir mo arātiḥ ‖3‖

(6.125.1’) vanaspate vīḍvaṅgo hi bhūyā asmatsakhā prataraṇaḥ suvīraḥ |
(6.125.1.c) gobhiḥ saṃnaddho asi vīḍayasvāsthātā te jayatu jetvāni ‖1‖
(6.125.2’) divas pṛthivyāḥ pary oja udbhṛtaṃ vanaspatibhyaḥ pary ābhṛtaṃ sahaḥ |
(6.125.2.c) apām ojmānaṃ pari gobhir āvṛtam indrasya vajraṃ haviṣā rathaṃ yaja ‖2‖
(6.125.3’) indrasyaujo marutām anīkaṃ mitrasya garbho varuṇasya nābhiḥ |
(6.125.3.c) sa imāṃ no havyadātiṃ juṣāṇo deva ratha prati havyā gṛbhāya ‖3‖

(6.126.1’) upa śvāsaya pṛthivīm uta dyaṃ purutrā te vanvatāṃ viṣṭhitam jagat |
(6.126.1.c) sa dundubhe sajūr indreṇa devair dūrād davīyo apa sedha śatrūn ‖1‖
(6.126.2’) ā krandaya balam ojo na ā dhā abhi ṣṭana duritā bādhamānaḥ |
(6.126.2.c) apa sedha dundubhe duchunām ita indrasya muṣṭir asi vīḍayasva ‖2‖
(6.126.3’) prāmūṃ jayābhīme jayantu ketumad dundubhir vāvadītu |
(6.126.3.c) sam aśvaparṇāḥ patantu no naro 'smākam indra rathino jayantu ‖3‖

(6.127.1’) vidradhasya balāsasya lohitasya vanaspate |
(6.127.1.c) visalpakasyoṣadhe moc chiṣaḥ piśitaṃ cana ‖1‖
(6.127.2’) yau te balāsa tiṣṭhataḥ kakṣe muṣkāv apaśritau |
(6.127.2.c) vedāhaṃ tasya bheṣajaṃ cīpudrur abhicakṣaṇam ‖2‖
(6.127.3’) yo aṅgyo yaḥ karṇyo yo akṣyor visalpakaḥ |
(6.127.3.c) vi vṛhāmo visalpakaṃ vidradhaṃ hṛdayāmayam |
(6.127.3.e) parā tam ajñātam yakṣmam adharāñcaṃ suvāmasi ‖3‖

(6.128.1’) śakadhūmaṃ nakṣatrāṇi yad rājānam akurvata |
(6.128.1.c) bhadrāham asmai prāyachan idaṃ rāṣṭram asād iti ‖1‖
(6.128.2’) bhadrāhaṃ no madhyaṃdine bhadrāhaṃ sāyam astu naḥ |
(6.128.2.c) bhadrāhaṃ no ahnāṃ prātā rātrī bhadrāham astu naḥ ‖2‖
(6.128.3’) ahorātrābhyāṃ nakṣatrebhyaḥ suryācandramasābhyām |
(6.128.3.c) bhadrāham asmabhyaṃ rājan chakadhūma tvaṃ kṛdhi ‖3‖
(6.128.4’) yo no bhadrāham akaraḥ sāyaṃ naktam atho divā |
(6.128.4.c) tasmai te nakṣatrarāja śakadhūma sadā namaḥ ‖4‖

(6.129.1’) bhagena mā śāṃśapena sākam indreṇa medinā |
(6.129.1.c) kṛṇomi bhaginaṃ māpa drāntv arātayaḥ ‖1‖
(6.129.2’) yena vṛkṣā;m abhyabhavo bhagena varcasā saha |
(6.129.2.c) tena mā bhaginaṃ kṛṇv apa drāntv arātayaḥ ‖2‖
(6.129.3’) yo andho yaḥ punaḥsaro bhago vṛkṣeṣv āhitaḥ |
(6.129.3.c) tena mā bhaginaṃ kṛṇv apa drāntv arātayaḥ ‖3‖

(6.130.1’) rathajitāṃ rāthajiteyīnām apsarasām ayaṃ smaraḥ |
(6.130.1.c) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu ‖1‖
(6.130.2’) asau me smaratād iti priyo me smaratād iti |
(6.130.2.c) devāh pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu ‖2‖
(6.130.3’) yathā mama smarād asau nāmuṣyāhaṃ kadā cana |
(6.130.3.c) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu ‖3‖
(6.130.4’) un mādayata maruta ud antarikṣa mādaya |
(6.130.4.c) agna un mādayā tvam asau mām anu śocatu ‖4‖

(6.131.1’) ni śīrṣato ni pattata ādhyo ni tirāmi te |
(6.131.1.c) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu ‖1‖
(6.131.2’) anumate 'nv idaṃ manyasvākute sam idaṃ namaḥ |
(6.131.2.c) devāḥ pra hiṇuta smaram asau mām anu śocatu ‖2‖
(6.131.3’) yad dhāvasi triyojanaṃ pañcayojanam āśvinam |
(6.131.3.c) tatas tvaṃ punar āyasi putrāṇāṃ no asaḥ pitā ‖3‖

(6.132.1’) yaṃ devāḥ smaram asiñcann apsv antaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6.132.1.c) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ‖1‖
(6.132.2’) yaṃ viśve devāḥ smaram asiñcann apsv antaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6.132.2.c) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ‖2‖
(6.132.3’) yam indrāṇī smaram asiñcad apsv antaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6.132.3.c) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ‖3‖
(6.132.4’) yam indrāgnī smaram asiñcatām apsv antaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6.132.4.c) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ‖4‖
(6.132.5’) yam mitrāvaruṇau smaram asiñcatām apsv antaḥ śośucānaṃ sahādhyā |
(6.132.5.c) taṃ te tapāmi varuṇasya dharmaṇā ‖5‖

(6.133.1’) ya imāṃ devo mekhalām ābabandha yaḥ saṃnanāha ya u no yuyoja |
(6.133.1.c) yasya devasya praśiṣā carāmaḥ sa pāram ichāt sa u no vi muñcāt ‖1‖
(6.133.2’) āhutāsy abhihuta ṛṣīṇām asy āyudham |
(6.133.2.c) pūrvā vratasya prāśnatī vīraghnī bhava mekhale ‖2‖
(6.133.3’) mṛtyor ahaṃ brahmacārī yad asmi niryācan bhūtāt puruṣaṃ yamāya |
(6.133.3.c) tam ahaṃ brahmaṇā tapasā śrameṇānayainaṃ mekhalayā sināmi ‖3‖
(6.133.4’) śraddhāyā duhitā tapaso 'dhi jātā svasā ṛṣīṇāṃ bhūtakṛtāṃ babhūva |
(6.133.4.c) sā no mekhale matim ā dhehi medhām atho no dhehi tapa indriyaṃ ca ‖4‖
(6.133.5’) yāṃ tvā pūrve bhūtakṛta ṛṣayaḥ paribedhire |
(6.133.5.c) sā tvaṃ pari ṣvajasva māṃ dīrghāyutvāya mekhale ‖5‖

(6.134.1’) ayaṃ vajras tarpayatām ṛtasyāvāsya ṛāṣṭram apa hantu jīvitam |
(6.134.1.c) śṛṇātu grīvāḥ pra śṛṇātūṣṇihā vṛtrasyeva śacīpatiḥ ‖1‖
(6.134.2’) adharo'dhara uttarebhyo gūḍhaḥ pṛthivyā mot sṛpat |
(6.134.2.c) vajreṇāvahataḥ śayām ‖2‖
(6.134.3’) yo jināti tam anvicha yo jināti tam ij jahi |
(6.134.3.c) jinato vajra tvaṃ sīmantam anvañcam anu pātaya ‖3‖

(6.135.1’) yad aśnāmi balaṃ kurva itthaṃ vajram ā dade |
(6.135.1.c) skandhān amuṣya śātayan vṛtrasyeva śacīpatiḥ ‖1‖
(6.135.2’) yat pibāmi saṃ pibāmi samudra iva saṃpibaḥ |
(6.135.2.c) prāṇān amuṣya saṃpāya saṃ pibāmo amuṃ vayam ‖2‖
(6.135.3’) yad girāmi saṃ girāmi samudra iva saṃgiraḥ |
(6.135.3.c) prāṇān amuṣya saṃgīrya saṃ girāmo amum vayam ‖3‖

(6.136.1’) devī devyām adhi jātā pṛthivyām asy oṣadhe |
(6.136.1.c) tāṃ tvā nitatni keśebhyo dṛṃhaṇāya khanāmasi ‖1‖
(6.136.2’) dṛṃha pratnān janayājātān jātān u varṣīyasas kṛdhi ‖2‖
(6.136.3’) yas te keśo 'vapadyate samūlo yaś ca vṛścate |
(6.136.3.c) idaṃ taṃ viśvabheṣajyābhi ṣiñcāmi vīrudhā ‖3‖

(6.137.1’) yāṃ jamadagnir akhanad duhitre keśavardhanīm |
(6.137.1.c) tāṃ vītahavya ābharad asitasya gṛhebhyaḥ ‖1‖
(6.137.2’) abhīśunā meyā āsan vyāmenānumeyāḥ |
(6.137.2.c) keśā naḍā iva vardhantāṃ śīrṣṇas te asitāḥ pari ‖2‖
(6.137.3’) dṛṃha mūlam āgraṃ yacha vi madhyaṃ yāmayauṣadhe |
(6.137.3.c) keśā naḍā iva vardhantām śīrṣṇas te asitāḥ pari ‖3‖

(6.138.1’) tvaṃ vīrudhāṃ śreṣṭhatamābhiśrutāsy oṣadhe |
(6.138.1.c) imaṃ me adya puruṣaṃ klībam opaśinaṃ kṛdhi ‖1‖
(6.138.2’) klībaṃ kṛdhy opaśinam atho kurīriṇaṃ kṛdhi |
(6.138.2.c) athāsyendro grāvabhyām ubhe bhinattv āṇḍyau ‖2‖
(6.138.3’) klība klībaṃ tvākaraṃ vadhre vadhriṃ tvākaram arasārasaṃ tvākaram |
(6.138.3.c) kurīram asya śīrṣaṇi kumbaṃ cādhinidadhmasi ‖3‖
(6.138.4’) ye te nādyau devakṛte yayos tiṣṭhati vṛṣṇyam |
(6.138.4.c) te te bhinadmi śamyayāmuṣyā adhi muṣkayoḥ ‖4‖
(6.138.5’) yathā naḍam kaśipune striyo bhindanty aśmanā |
(6.138.5.c) evā bhinadmi te śepo 'muṣyā adhi muṣkayoḥ ‖5‖

(6.139.1’) nyastikā rurohitha subhagaṃkaraṇī mama |
(6.139.1.c) śataṃ tava pratānās trayastriṃśan nitānāḥ ‖
(6.139.1.e) tayā sahasraparṇyā hṛdayaṃ śoṣayāmi te ‖1‖
(6.139.2’) śuṣyatu mayi te hṛdayam atho śuṣyatv āsyam |
(6.139.2.c) atho ni śuṣya māṃ kāmenātho śuṣkāsyā cara ‖2‖
(6.139.3’) saṃvananī samuṣpalā babhru kalyāṇi saṃ nuda |
(6.139.3.c) amūṃ ca māṃ ca saṃ nuda samānaṃ hṛdayaṃ kṛdhi ‖3‖
(6.139.4’) yathodakam apapuṣo 'paśuṣyaty āsyam |
(6.139.4.c) evā ni śuṣya māṃ kāmenātho śuṣkāsyā cara ‖4‖
(6.139.5’) yathā nakulo vichidya saṃdadhāty ahiṃ punaḥ |
(6.139.5.c) evā kāmasya vichinnaṃ saṃ dhehi vīryāvati ‖5‖

(6.140.1’) yau vyāghrāv avarūdhau jighatsataḥ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca |
(6.140.1.c) tau dantaṃ brahmaṇas pate śivau kṛṇu jātavedaḥ ‖1‖
(6.140.2’) vrīhim attaṃ yavam attam atho māṣam atho tilam |
(6.140.2.c) eṣa vāṃ bhāgo nihito ratnadheyāya dantau mā hiṃsiṣṭaṃ pitaram mātaraṃ ca ‖2‖
(6.140.3’) upahūtau sayujau syonau dantau sumaṅgalau |
(6.140.3.c) anyatra vāṃ ghoraṃ tanvaḥ paraitu dantau mā hiṃsiṣṭaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca ‖3‖

(6.141.1’) vāyur enāḥ samākarat tvaṣṭā poṣāya dhriyatām |
(6.141.1.c) indra ābhyo adhi bravad rudro bhūmne cikitsatu ‖1‖
(6.141.2’) lohitena svadhitinā mithunaṃ karṇayoḥ kṛdhi |
(6.141.2.c) akartām aśvinā lakṣma tad astu prajayā bahu ‖2‖
(6.141.3’) yathā cakrur devāsurā yathā manuṣyā uta |
(6.141.3.c) evā sahasrapoṣāya kṛṇutaṃ lakṣmāśvinā ‖3‖

(6.142.1’) uc chrayasva bahur bhava svena mahasā yava |
(6.142.1.c) mṛṇīhi viśvā pātrāṇi mā tvā divyāśanir vadhīt ‖1‖
(6.142.2’) āśṛṇvantaṃ yavaṃ devaṃ yatra tvāchāvadāmasi |
(6.142.2.c) tad uc chrayasva dyaur iva samudra ivaidhy akṣitaḥ ‖2‖
(6.142.3’) akṣitās ta upasado 'kṣitāḥ santu rāśayaḥ |
(6.142.3.c) pṛṇanto akṣitāḥ santv attāraḥ santv akṣitāḥ ‖3‖

(7.1.1’) dhītī vā ye anayan vāco agraṃ manasā vā ye 'vadann ṛtāni |
(7.1.1.c) tṛtīyena brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānās turīyeṇāmanvata nāma dhenoḥ ‖1‖
(7.1.2’) sa veda putraḥ pitaraṃ sa mātaraṃ sa sūnur bhuvat sa bhuvat punarmaghaḥ |
(7.1.2.c) sa dyām aurṇod antarikṣaṃ svaḥ sa idaṃ viśvam abhavat sa ābharat ‖2‖

(7.2.1’) atharvāṇaṃ pitaraṃ devabandhuṃ mātur garbhaṃ pitur asuṃ yuvānam |
(7.2.1.c) ya imaṃ yajñam manasā ciketa pra ṇo vocas tam iheha bravaḥ ‖1‖

(7.3.1’) ayā viṣṭhā janayan karvarāṇi sa hi ghṛṇir urur varāya gātuḥ |
(7.3.1.c) sa pratyudaid dharuṇaṃ madhvo agraṃ svayā tanvā tanvam airayata ‖1‖

(7.4.1’) ekayā ca daśabhiś ca suhute dvābhyām iṣṭaye viṃśatyā ca |
(7.4.1.c) tisṛbhiś ca vahase triṃśatā ca viyugbhir vāya iha tā vi muñca ‖1‖

(7.5.1’) yajñena yajñam ayajanta devās tāni dharmāṇi prathamāny āsan |
(7.5.1.c) te ha nākaṃ mahimānaḥ sacanta yatra pūrve sādhyāḥ santi devāḥ ‖1‖
(7.5.2’) yajño babhūva sa ā babhūva sa pra jajñe sa u vāvṛdhe punaḥ |
(7.5.2.c) sa devānām adhipatir babhūva so asmāsu draviṇam ā dadhātu ‖2‖
(7.5.3’) yad devā devān haviṣā 'yajantāmartyān manasā martyena |
(7.5.3.c) madema tatra parame vyoman paśyema tad uditau sūryasya ‖3‖
(7.5.4’) yat puruṣeṇa haviṣā yajñaṃ devā atanvata |
(7.5.4.c) asti nu tasmād ojīyo yad vihavyenejire ‖4‖
(7.5.5’) mugdhā devā uta śunā 'yajantota gor aṅgaiḥ purudhā 'yajanta |
(7.5.5.c) ya imaṃ yajñaṃ manasā ciketa pra ṇo vocas tam iheha bravaḥ ‖5‖

(7.6.1’) aditir dyaur aditir antarikṣam aditir mātā sa pitā sa putraḥ |
(7.6.1.c) viśve devā aditir pañca janā aditir jātam aditir janitvam ‖1‖
(7.6.2’) mahīm ū ṣu mātaraṃ suvratānām ṛtasya patnīm avase havāmahe |
(7.6.2.c) tuvikṣatrām ajarantīm urūcīṃ suśarmāṇam aditiṃ supraṇītim ‖2‖
(7.6.3’) sutrāmāṇaṃ pṛthivīṃ dyām anehasaṃ suśarmāṇam aditiṃ supraṇītim |
(7.6.3.c) daivīṃ nāvaṃ svaritrām anāgaso asravantīm ā ruhemā svastaye ‖1‖
(7.6.4’) vājasya nu prasave mātaraṃ mahīm aditiṃ nāma vacasā karāmahe |
(7.6.4.c) yasyā upastha urv antarikṣaṃ sā naḥ śarma trivarūthaṃ ni yachāt ‖2‖

(7.7.1’) diteḥ putrāṇām aditer akāriṣam ava devānāṃ bṛhatām anarmaṇām |
(7.7.1.c) teṣāṃ hi dhāma gabhiṣak samudriyaṃ nainān namasā paro asti kaścana ‖1‖

(7.8.1’) bhadrād adhi śreyaḥ prehi bṛhaspatiḥ puraetā te astu |

(7.9.1’) prapathe pathām ajaniṣṭa pūṣā prapathe divaḥ prapathe pṛthivyāḥ |
(7.9.1.c) ubhe abhi priyatame sadhasthe ā ca parā ca carati prajānan ‖1‖
(7.9.2’) pūṣemā āśā anu veda sarvāḥ so asmā;m abhayatamena neṣat |
(7.9.2.c) svastidā āghṛṇiḥ sarvavīro 'prayuchan pura etu prajānan ‖2‖
(7.9.3’) pūṣan tava vrate vayaṃ na riṣyema kadā cana |
(7.9.3.c) stotāras ta iha smasi ‖3‖
(7.9.4’) pari pūṣā parastād dhastaṃ dadhātu dakṣiṇam |
(7.9.4.c) punar no naṣṭam ājatu saṃ naṣṭena gamemahi ‖4‖

(7.10.1’) yas te stanaḥ śaśayur yo mayobhūr yaḥ sumnayuḥ suhavo yaḥ sudatraḥ |

(7.11.1’) yas te pṛthu stanayitnur ya ṛṣvo daivaḥ ketur viśvam ābhūṣatīdam |

(7.12.1’) sabhā ca mā samitiś cāvatāṃ prajāpater duhitarau saṃvidāne |

(7.12.2’) vidma te sabhe nāma nariṣṭā nāma vā asi |
(7.12.2.c) ye te ke ca sabhāsadas te me santu savācasaḥ ‖2‖
(7.12.3’) eṣām ahaṃ samāsīnānāṃ varco vijñānam ā dade |

(7.13.1’) yathā sūryo nakṣatrāṇām udyaṃs tejāṃsy ādade |

(7.14.1’) abhi tyaṃ devaṃ savitāram oṇyoḥ kavikratum |

(7.14.2’) urdhvā yasyāmatir bhā adidyutat savīmani |

(7.14.3’) sāvīr hi deva prathamāya pitre varṣmāṇam asmai varimāṇam asmai |

(7.15.1’) tāṃ savitaḥ satyasavāṃ sucitrām āhaṃ vṛṇe sumatiṃ viśvavārām |

(7.16.1’) bṛhaspate savitar vardhayainaṃ jyotayainaṃ mahate saubhagāya |

(7.17.1’) dhātā dadhātu no rayim īśāno jagatas patiḥ |

(7.17.2’) dhātā dadhātu dāśuṣe prācīṃ jīvātum akṣitām |
(7.17.2.c) vayam devasya dhīmahi sumatiṃ viśvarādhasaḥ ‖2‖
(7.17.3’) dhātā viśvā vāryā dadhātu prajākāmāya dāśuṣe duroṇe |

(7.18.1’) pra nabhasva pṛthivi bhinddhīdaṃ divyaṃ nabhaḥ |
(7.18.1.c) udno divyasya no dhātar īśāno vi ṣyā dṛtim ‖1‖

(7.19.1’) prajāpatir janayati prajā imā dhātā dadhātu sumanasyamānaḥ |

(7.20.1’) anv adya no 'numatir yajñaṃ deveṣu manyatām |
(7.20.1.c) agniś ca havyavāhano bhavatāṃ dāśuṣe mama ‖1‖
(7.20.2’) anv id anumate tvaṃ maṃsase śaṃ ca nas kṛdhi |
(7.20.2.c) juṣasva havyam āhutaṃ prajāṃ devi rarāsva naḥ ‖2‖
(7.20.3’) anu manyatām anumanyamānaḥ prajāvantaṃ rayim akṣīyamāṇam |
(7.20.3.c) tasya vayaṃ heḍasi māpi bhūma sumṛḍīke asya sumatau syāma ‖3‖
(7.20.4’) yat te nāma suhavaṃ supraṇīte 'numate anumataṃ sudānu |
(7.20.4.c) tenā no yajñaṃ pipṛhi viśvavāre rayiṃ no dhehi subhage suvīram ‖4‖
(7.20.5’) emaṃ yajñam anumatir jagāma sukṣetratāyai suvīratāyai sujātam |

(7.21.1’) sameta viśve vacasā patiṃ diva eko vibhūr atithir janānām |

(7.25.1’) yayor ojasā skabhitā rajāṃsi yau vīryair vīratamā śaviṣṭhā |

(7.26.1’) viṣṇor nu kaṃ prā vocaṃ vīryāṇi yaḥ pārthivāni vimame rajāṃsi |

(7.26.6’) viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi paśyata yato vratāni paspaśe |

(7.26.7’) tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ |

(7.27.1’) iḍaivāsmā;m anu vastāṃ vratena yasyāḥ pade punate devayantaḥ |

(7.29.1’) agnāviṣṇū mahi tad vāṃ mahitvam pātho ghṛtasya guhyasya nāma |

(7.31.1’) indrotibhir bahulābhir no adya yāvacchreṣṭhābhir maghavan chūra jinva |

(7.32.1’) upa priyaṃ panipnatam yuvānam āhutīvṛdham |

(7.33.1’) saṃ mā siñcantu marutaḥ saṃ pūṣa saṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |

(7.34.1’) agne jātān pra ṇudā me sapatnān praty ajātān jātavedo nudasva |

(7.35.1’) prānyānt sapatnānt sahasā sahasva praty ajātān jātavedo nudasva |
(7.35.1.c) idaṃ rāṣṭraṃ pipṛhi saubhagāya viśva enam anu madantu devāḥ ‖1‖
(7.35.2’) imā yās te śataṃ hirāḥ sahasraṃ dhamanīr uta |
(7.35.2.c) tāsāṃ te sarvāsām aham aśmanā bilam apy adhām ‖2‖
(7.35.3’) paraṃ yoner avaraṃ te kṛṇomi mā tvā prajābhi bhūn mota sūtuḥ |
(7.35.3.c) asvaṃ tvāprajasaṃ kṛṇomy aśmānaṃ te apidhānam kṛṇomi ‖3‖

(7.36.1’) akṣyau nau madhusaṃkāśe anīkam nau samañjanam |

(7.37.1’) abhi tvā manujātena dadhāmi mama vāsasā |
(7.37.1.c) yāthā 'so mama kevalo nānyāsāṃ kīrtayāś cana ‖1‖

(7.38.1’) idam khanāmi bheṣajaṃ māṃpaśyam abhirorudam |

(7.38.2’) yenā nicakra āsurīndraṃ devebhyas pari |
(7.38.2.c) tenā ni kurve tvām ahaṃ yathā te 'sāni supriyā ‖2‖
(7.38.3’) pratīcī somam asi pratīcī uta sūryam |
(7.38.3.c) pratīcī viśvān devān tāṃ tvāchāvadāmasi ‖3‖
(7.38.4’) ahaṃ vadāmi net tvaṃ sabhāyām aha tvaṃ vada |
(7.38.4.c) mamed asas tvaṃ kevalo nānyāsāṃ kīrtayāś cana ‖4‖

(7.72.2’) śrātam havir o ṣv indra pra yāhi jagāma sūro adhvano vi madhyam |
(7.72.2.c) pari tvāsate nidhibhiḥ sakhāyaḥ kulapā na vrājapatim carantam ‖2‖
(7.72.3’) śrātaṃ manya ūdhani śrātam agnau suśṛtaṃ manye tad ṛtaṃ navīyaḥ |
(7.72.3.c) mādhyandinasya savanasya dadhnaḥ pibendra vajrin purukṛj juṣāṇaḥ ‖1‖

(7.73.1’) samiddho agnir vṛṣaṇā rathī divas tapto gharmo duhyate vām iṣe madhu |
(7.73.1.c) vayaṃ hi vāṃ purudamāso aśvinā havāmahe sadhamādeṣu kāravaḥ ‖1‖
(7.73.2’) samiddho agnir aśvinā tapto vāṃ gharma ā gatam |
(7.73.2.c) duhyante nūnaṃ vṛṣaṇeha dhenavo dasrā madanti vedhasaḥ ‖2‖
(7.73.3’) ivāhākṛtaḥ śucir deveṣu yajño yo aśvinoś camaso devapānaḥ |
(7.73.3.c) tam u viśve amṛtāso juṣāṇā gandharvasya praty āsnā rihanti ‖3‖
(7.73.4’) yad usriyāsv āhutaṃ ghṛtaṃ payo 'yaṃ sa vām aśvinā bhāga ā gatam |
(7.73.4.c) mādhvī dhartārā vidathasya satpatī taptaṃ gharmaṃ pibatam divaḥ ‖4‖
(7.73.5’) tapto vāṃ gharmo nakṣatu svahotā pra vām adhvaryuś caratu payasvān |
(7.73.5.c) madhor dugdhasyāśvinā tanāyā vītaṃ pātaṃ payasa usriyāyāḥ ‖5‖
(7.73.6’) upa drava payasā godhug oṣam ā gharme siñca paya usriyāyāḥ |
(7.73.6.c) vi nākam akhyat savitā vareṇyo 'nuprayāṇam uṣaso vi rājati ‖6‖
(7.73.7’) upa hvaye sudughāṃ dhenum etāṃ suhasto godhug uta dohad enām |
(7.73.7.c) śreṣṭhaṃ savaṃ savitā sāviṣan no 'bhīddho gharmas tad u ṣu pra vocat ‖7‖
(7.73.8’) hiṅkṛṇvatī vasupatnī vasūnāṃ vatsam ichantī manasā nyāgan |
(7.73.8.c) duhām aśvibhyāṃ payo aghnyeyaṃ sā vardhatāṃ mahate saubhagāya ‖8‖
(7.73.9’) juṣṭo damūnā atithir duroṇa imaṃ no yajñam upa yāhi vidvān |
(7.73.9.c) viśvā agne abhiyujo vihatya śatrūyatām ā bharā bhojanāni ‖9‖
(7.73.10’) agne śardha mahate saubhagāya tava dyumnāny uttamāni santu |
(7.73.10.c) saṃ jāspatyaṃ suyamam ā kṛṇuṣva śatrūyatām abhi tiṣṭhā mahāṃsi ‖10‖
(7.73.11’) sūyavasād bhagavatī hi bhūyā adhā vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |
(7.73.11.c) addhi tṛṇam aghnye viśvadānīṃ piba śuddham udakam ācarantī ‖11‖

(7.74.1’) apacitāṃ lohinīnāṃ kṛṣṇā māteti śuśruma |
(7.74.1.c) muner devasya mūlena sarvā vidhyāmi tā aham ‖1‖
(7.74.2’) vidhyāmy āsāṃ prathamāṃ vidhyāmi uta madhyamām |
(7.74.2.c) idaṃ jaghanyām āsām ā chinadmi stukām iva ‖2‖
(7.74.3’) tvāṣṭreṇāhaṃ vacasā vi ta īrṣyām amīmadam |
(7.74.3.c) atho yo manyuṣ ṭe pate tam u te śamayāmasi ‖3‖
(7.74.4’) vratena tvaṃ vratapate samakto viśvāhā sumanā dīdihīha |
(7.74.4.c) taṃ tvā vayaṃ jātavedaḥ samiddhaṃ prajāvanta upa sadema sarve ‖4‖

(7.75.1’) prajāvatīḥ sūyavase ruśantīḥ śuddhā apaḥ suprapāṇe pibantīḥ |

(7.76.1’) ā susrasaḥ susraso asatībhyo asattarāḥ |
(7.76.1.c) sehor arasatarā havaṇād vikledīyasīḥ ‖1‖
(7.76.2’) yā graivyā apacito 'tho yā upapakṣyāḥ |
(7.76.2.c) vijāmni yā apacitaḥ svayaṃsrasaḥ ‖2‖
(7.76.3’) yaḥ kīkasāḥ praśṛṇāti talīdyam avatiṣṭhati |

(7.76.4’) pakṣī jāyānyah patati sa ā viśati pūruṣam |

(7.77.2’) yo no marto maruto durhṛṇāyus tiraś cittāni vasavo jighāṃsati |
(7.77.2.c) druhaḥ pāśān prati muñcatāṃ sas tapiṣṭhena tapasā hantanā tam ‖2‖

(7.79.1’) yat te devā akṛṇvan bhāgadheyam amāvāsye saṃvasanto mahitvā |
(7.79.1.c) tenā no yajñaṃ pipṛhi viśvavāre rayiṃ no dhehi subhage suvīram ‖1‖
(7.79.2’) aham evāsmy amāvāsyā mām ā vasanti sukṛto mayīme |
(7.79.2.c) mayi devā ubhaye sādyāś cendrajyeṣṭhāḥ sam agachanta sarve ‖2‖
(7.79.3’) āgan rātrī saṅgamanī vasūnām ūrjaṃ puṣṭaṃ vasv āveśayantī |
(7.79.3.c) amāvāsyāyai haviṣa vidhemorjaṃ duhānā payasā na āgan ‖3‖

(7.79.4.c) yatkāmās te juhumas tan no astu vayaṃ syāma patayo rayiṇām ‖4‖

(7.80.1’) paurṇamāsī jigāya |
(7.80.1.c) tasyāṃ devaiḥ saṃvasanto mahitvā nākasya pṛṣṭhe sam iṣā madema ‖1‖
(7.80.2’) vṛṣabhaṃ vājinaṃ vayaṃ paurṇamāsaṃ yajāmahe |
(7.80.2.c) sa no dadātv akṣitāṃ rayim anupadasvatīm ‖2‖
(7.80.3’) prajāpate na tvad etāny anyo viśvā rūpāṇi paribhūr jajāna |

(7.81.1’) pūrvāparaṃ carato mayayaitau śiśū krīḍantau pari yāto 'rṇavam |

(7.81.3’) somasyāmśo yudhāṃ pate 'nūno nāma vā asi |
(7.81.3.c) anūnam darśa mā kṛdhi prajayā ca dhanena ca ‖3‖
(7.81.4’) darśo 'si darśato 'si samagro 'si samantaḥ |
(7.81.4.c) samagraḥ samanto bhūyāsaṃ gobhir aśvaiḥ prajayā paśubhir gṛhair dhanena ‖4‖

(7.81.6.c) tenāsmān indro varuṇo bṛhaspatir ā pyāyayantu bhuvanasya gopāḥ ‖6‖

(7.82.1’) abhy arcata suṣṭutiṃ gavyam ājim asmāsu bhadrā draviṇāni dhatta |
(7.82.1.c) imaṃ yajñaṃ nayata devatā no ghṛtasya dhārā madhumat pavantām ‖1‖
(7.82.2’) mayy agre agniṃ gṛhṇāmi saha kṣatreṇa varcasā balena |
(7.82.2.c) mayi prajāṃ mayy āyur dadhāmi svāhā mayy agnim ‖2‖
(7.82.3’) ihaivāgne adhy dhārayā rayim mā tvā ni kran pūrvacittā nikāriṇaḥ |
(7.82.3.c) kṣatreṇāgne suyamam astu tubhyam upasattā vardhatāṃ te aniṣṭṛtaḥ ‖3‖
(7.82.4’) anv agnir uṣasām agram akhyad anv ahāni prathamo jātavedāḥ |

(7.83.1’) apsu te rājan varuṇa gṛho hiraṇyayo mithaḥ |

(7.84.1’) anādhṛṣyo jātavedā amartyo virāḍ agne kṣatrabhṛd dīdihīha |
