(7.106.1.c) tataḥ pāhi tvaṃ naḥ pracetaḥ śubhe sakhibhyo amṛtatvam astu naḥ ‖1‖

(7.107.1’) ava divas tārayanti sapta sūryasya raśmayaḥ |
(7.107.1.c) āpaḥ samudriyā dhārās tās śalyam asisrasan ‖1‖

(7.108.1’) yo na stāyad dipsati yo na āviḥ svo vidvān araṇo vā no agne |
(7.108.1.c) pratīcy etv araṇī datvatī tān maiṣām agne vāstu bhūn mo apatyam ‖1‖
(7.108.2’) yo naḥ suptān jāgrato vābhidāsāt tiṣṭhato vā carato jātavedaḥ |
(7.108.2.c) vaiśvānareṇa sayujā sajoṣās tān pratīco nir daha jātavedaḥ ‖2‖

(7.109.1’) idam ugrāya babhrave namo yo akṣeṣu tanūvaśī |
(7.109.1.c) ghṛtena kaliṃ śikṣāmi sa no mṛḍātīdṛśe ‖1‖
(7.109.2’) ghṛtam apsarābhyo vaha tvam agne pāṃsūn akṣebhyaḥ sikatā apaś ca |
(7.109.2.c) yathābhagaṃ havyadātiṃ juṣāṇā madanti devā ubhayāni havyā ‖2‖
(7.109.3’) apsarasaḥ sadhamādaṃ madanti havirdhānam antarā sūryaṃ ca |
(7.109.3.c) tā me hastau saṃ sṛjantu ghṛtena sapatnaṃ me kitavam randhayantu ‖3‖
(7.109.4’) ādinavaṃ pratidīvne ghṛtenāsmā;m abhi kṣara |
(7.109.4.c) vṛkṣam ivāśanyā jahi yo asmān pratidīvyati ‖4‖
(7.109.5’) yo no dyuve dhanam idaṃ cakāra yo akṣāṇāṃ glahanaṃ śeṣaṇaṃ ca |
(7.109.5.c) sa no devo havir idaṃ juṣāṇo gandharvebhiḥ sadhamādaṃ madema ‖5‖
(7.109.6’) saṃvasava iti vo nāmadheyam ugraṃpaśyā rāṣṭrabhṛto hy akṣāḥ |
(7.109.6.c) tebhyo va indavo haviṣā vidhema vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ‖6‖
(7.109.7’) devān yan nāthito huve brahmacaryaṃ yad ūṣima |
(7.109.7.c) akṣān yad babhrūn ālabhe te no mṛḍantv īdṛśe ‖7‖

(7.110.1’) agna indraś ca dāśuṣe hato vṛtrāṇy aprati |
(7.110.1.c) ubhā hi vṛtrahantamā ‖1‖
(7.110.2’) yābhyām ajayant svar agra eva yāv ātasthatur bhuvanāni viśvā |
(7.110.2.c) pra carṣaṇīvṛṣaṇā vajrabāhū agnim indram vṛtrahaṇā huve 'ham ‖2‖
(7.110.3’) upa tvā devo agramīc camasena bṛhaspatiḥ |
(7.110.3.c) indra gīrbhir na ā viśa yajamānāya sunvate ‖3‖

(7.111.1’) indrasya kukṣir asi somadhāna ātmā devānām uta mānuṣāṇām |
(7.111.1.c) iha prajā janaya yās ta āsu yā anyatreha tās te ramantām ‖1‖

(7.112.1’) śumbhanī dyāvāpṛthivī antisumne mahivrate |
(7.112.1.c) āpaḥ sapta susruvur devīs tā no muñcantv aṃhasaḥ ‖1‖
(7.112.2’) muñcantu mā śapathyād atho varuṇyād uta |
(7.112.2.c) atho yamasya paḍvīśād viśvasmād devakilbiṣāt ‖2‖

(7.113.1’) tṛṣṭike tṛṣṭavandana ud amūṃ chindhi tṛṣṭike |
(7.113.1.c) yathā kṛtadviṣṭāso 'muṣmai śepyāvate ‖1‖
(7.113.2’) tṛṣṭāsi tṛṣṭikā viṣā viṣātaky asi |
(7.113.2.c) parivṛktā yathāsasy ṛṣabhasya vaśeva ‖2‖

(7.114.1’) ā te dade vakṣaṇābhya ā te 'haṃ hṛdayād dade |
(7.114.1.c) ā te mukhasya saṅkāśāt sarvaṃ te varca ā dade ‖1‖
(7.114.2’) preto yantu vyādhyaḥ prānudhyāḥ pro aśastayaḥ |
(7.114.2.c) agnī rakṣasvinīr hantu somo hantu durasyatīḥ ‖2‖

(7.115.1’) pra patetaḥ pāpi lakṣmi naśyetaḥ prāmutah pata |
(7.115.1.c) ayasmayenāṅkena dviṣate tvā sajāmasi ‖1‖
(7.115.2’) yā mā lakṣmīḥ patayālūr ajuṣṭābhicaskanda vandaneva vṛkṣam |
(7.115.2.c) anyatrāsmat savitas tām ito dhā hiraṇyahasto vasu no rarāṇaḥ ‖2‖
(7.115.3’) ekaśataṃ lakṣmyo martyasya sākaṃ tanvā januṣo 'dhi jātāḥ |
(7.115.3.c) tāsāṃ pāpiṣṭhā nir itaḥ pra hiṇmaḥ śivā asmabhyaṃ jātavedo niyacha ‖3‖
(7.115.4’) etā enā vyākaraṃ khile gā viṣṭhitā iva |
(7.115.4.c) ramantāṃ puṇyā lakṣmīr yāḥ pāpīs tā anīnaśam ‖4‖

(7.116.1’) namo rūrāya cyavanāya nodanāya dhṛṣṇave |
(7.116.1.c) namaḥ śītāya pūrvakāmakṛtvane ‖1‖
(7.116.2’) yo anyedyur ubhayadyur abhyetīmaṃ maṇḍūkam |
(7.116.2.c) abhy etv avrataḥ ‖2‖

(7.117.1’) ā mandrair indra haribhir yāhi mayūraromabhiḥ |
(7.117.1.c) mā tvā ke cid vi yaman viṃ na pāśino 'ti dhanveva tā;m ihi ‖1‖

(7.118.1’) marmāṇi te varmaṇā chādayāmi somas tvā rājāmṛtenānu vastām |
(7.118.1.c) uror varīyo varuṇas te kṛṇotu jayantaṃ tvānu devā madantu ‖1‖

(8.1.1’) antakāya mṛtyave namaḥ prānā apānā iha te ramantām |
(8.1.1.c) ihāyam astu puruṣaḥ sahāsunā sūryasya bhāge amṛtasya loke ‖1‖
(8.1.2’) ud enaṃ bhago agrabhīd ud enaṃ somo aṃśumān |
(8.1.2.c) ud enaṃ maruto devā ud indrāgnī svastaye ‖2‖
(8.1.3’) iha te 'sur iha prāṇa ihāyur iha te manaḥ |
(8.1.3.c) ut tvā nirṛtyāḥ pāśebhyo daivyā vacā bharāmasi ‖3‖
(8.1.4’) ut krāmātaḥ puruṣa māva patthā mṛtyoḥ paḍvīṣam avamuñcamānaḥ |
(8.1.4.c) mā chitthā asmāl lokād agneḥ sūryasya saṃdṛśaḥ ‖4‖
(8.1.5’) tubhyaṃ vātaḥ pavatāṃ mātariśvā tubhyaṃ varṣantv amṛtāny āpaḥ |
(8.1.5.c) sūryas te tanve śaṃ tapāti tvām mṛtyur dayatāṃ mā pra meṣṭhāḥ ‖5‖
(8.1.6’) udyānaṃ te puruṣa nāvayānaṃ jīvātuṃ te dakṣatātiṃ kṛnomi |
(8.1.6.c) ā hi rohemam amṛtaṃ sukhaṃ ratham atha jirvir vidatham ā vadāsi ‖6‖
(8.1.7’) mā te manas tatra gān mā tiro bhūn mā jīvebhyaḥ pra mado mānu gāḥ pitṝn |
(8.1.7.c) viśve devā abhi rakṣantu tveha ‖7‖
(8.1.8’) mā gatānām ā dīdhīthā ye nayanti parāvatam |
(8.1.8.c) ā roha tamaso jyotir ehy ā te hastau rabhāmahe ‖8‖
(8.1.9’) śyāmaś ca tvā mā śabalaś ca preṣitau yamasya yau pathirakṣī śvānau |
(8.1.9.c) arvāṅ ehi mā vi dīdhyo mātra tiṣṭhaḥ parāṅmanāḥ ‖9‖
(8.1.10’) maitaṃ panthām anu gā bhīma eṣa yena pūrvaṃ neyatha taṃ bravīmi |
(8.1.10.c) tama etat puruṣa mā pra patthā bhayaṃ parastād abhayaṃ te arvāk ‖10‖ {1}
(8.1.11’) rakṣantu tvāgnayo ye apsv antā rakṣatu tvā manuṣyā yam indhate |
(8.1.11.c) vaiśvānaro rakṣatu jātavedā divyas tvā mā pra dhāg vidyutā saha ‖11‖
(8.1.12’) mā tvā kravyād abhi maṃstārāt saṃkasukāc cara rakṣatu tvā dyau rakṣatu |
(8.1.12.c) pṛthivī sūryaś ca tvā rakṣatāṃ candramāś ca |
(8.1.12.e) antarikṣaṃ rakṣatu devahetyāḥ ‖12‖
(8.1.13’) bodhaś ca tvā pratibodhaś ca rakṣatām asvapnaś ca tvānavadrāṇaś ca rakṣatām |
(8.1.13.c) gopāyaṃś ca tvā jāgṛviś ca rakṣatām ‖13‖
(8.1.14’) te tvā rakṣantu te tvā gopāyantu tebhyo namas tebhyaḥ svāhā ‖14‖
(8.1.15’) jīvebhyas tvā samude vāyur indro dhātā dadhātu savitā trāyamāṇaḥ |
(8.1.15.c) mā tvā prāṇo balaṃ hāsīd asuṃ te 'nu hvayāmasi ‖15‖
(8.1.16’) mā tvā jambhaḥ saṃhanur mā tamo vidan mā jihvā barhis pramayuḥ kathā syāḥ |
(8.1.16.c) ut tvādityā vasavo bharantūd indrāgnī svastaye ‖16‖
(8.1.17’) ut tvā dyaur ut pṛthivy ut prajāpatir agrabhīt |
(8.1.17.c) ut tvā mṛtyor oṣadhayaḥ somarājñīr apīparan ‖17‖
(8.1.18’) ayaṃ devā ihaivāstv ayaṃ māmutra gād itaḥ |
(8.1.18.c) imaṃ sahasravīryeṇa mṛtyor ut pārayāmasi ‖18‖
(8.1.19’) ut tvā mṛtyor apīparaṃ saṃ dhamantu vayodhasaḥ |
(8.1.19.c) mā tvā vyastakeśyo mā tvāgharudo rudan ‖19‖
(8.1.20’) āhārṣam avidaṃ tvā punar āgāḥ punarṇavaḥ |
(8.1.20.c) sarvāṅga sarvaṃ te cakṣuḥ sarvam āyuś ca te 'vidam ‖20‖
(8.1.21’) vy avāt te jyotir abhūd apa tvat tamo akramīt |
(8.1.21.c) apa tvan mṛtyuṃ nirṛtim apa yakṣmaṃ ni dadhmasi ‖21‖ {2}

(8.2.1’) ā rabhasvemām amṛtasya śnuṣṭim achidyamānā jaradaṣṭir astu te |
(8.2.1.c) asuṃ ta āyuḥ punar ā bharāmi rajas tamo mopa gā mā pra meṣṭhāh ‖1‖
(8.2.2’) jīvatāṃ jyotir abhyehy arvāṅ ā tvā harāmi śataśāradāya |
(8.2.2.c) avamuñcan mṛtyupāśān aśastiṃ drāghīya āyuḥ prataraṃ te dadhāmi ‖2‖
(8.2.3’) vātāt te prānam avidaṃ sūryāc cakṣur ahaṃ tava |
(8.2.3.c) yat te manas tvayi tad dhārayāmi saṃ vitsvāṅgair vada jihvayālapan ‖3‖
(8.2.4’) prāṇena tvā dvipadāṃ catuṣpadām agnim iva jātam abhi saṃ dhamāmi |
(8.2.4.c) namas te mṛtyo cakṣuṣe namaḥ prāṇāya te 'karam ‖4‖
(8.2.5’) ayaṃ jīvatu mā mṛtemaṃ sam īrayāmasi |
(8.2.5.c) kṛṇomy asmai bheṣajaṃ mṛtyo mā puruṣaṃ vadhīḥ ‖5‖
(8.2.6’) jīvalāṃ naghāriṣāṃ jīvantīm oṣadhīm aham |
(8.2.6.c) trāyamāṇāṃ sahamānāṃ sahasvatīm iha huve 'smā ariṣṭatātaye ‖6‖
(8.2.7’) adhi brūhi mā rabhathāh sṛjemaṃ tavaiva sant sarvahāyāḥ ihāstu |
(8.2.7.c) bhavāśarvau mṛḍataṃ śarma yachatam apasidhya duritaṃ dhattam āyuḥ ‖7‖
(8.2.8’) asmai mṛtyo adhi brūhīmaṃ dayasvod ito 'yam etu |
(8.2.8.c) ariṣṭaḥ sarvāṅgaḥ suśruj jarasā śatahāyana ātmanā bhujam aśnutām ‖8‖
(8.2.9’) devānāṃ hetih pari tvā vṛṇaktu pārayāmi tvā rajasa ut tvā mṛtyor apīparam |
(8.2.9.c) ārād agniṃ kravyādaṃ nirūhaṃ jīvātave te paridhiṃ dadhāmi ‖9‖
(8.2.10’) yat te niyānaṃ rajasaṃ mṛtyo anavadharṣyam |
(8.2.10.c) patha imaṃ tasmād rakṣanto brahmāsmai varma kṛṇmasi ‖10‖ {3}
(8.2.11’) kṛṇomi te prāṇāpānau jarāṃ mṛtyuṃ dīrgham āyuḥ svasti |
(8.2.11.c) vaivasvatena prahitān yamadūtāṃś carato 'pa sedhāmi sarvān ‖11‖
(8.2.12’) ārād arātiṃ nirṛtiṃ paro grāhiṃ kravyādaḥ piśācān |
(8.2.12.c) rakṣo yat sarvaṃ durbhūtaṃ tat tama ivāpa hanmasi ‖12‖
(8.2.13’) agneṣ ṭa prānam amṛtād āyuṣmato vanve jātavedasaḥ |
(8.2.13.c) yathā na riṣyā amṛtaḥ sajūr asas tat te kṛṇomi tad u te sam ṛdhyatām ‖13‖
(8.2.14’) śive te stāṃ dyāvāpṛthivī asaṃtāpe abhiśriyau |
(8.2.14.c) śaṃ te sūrya ā tapatu śaṃ vāto vātu te hṛde |
(8.2.14.e) śivā abhi kṣarantu tvāpo divyāḥ payasvatīḥ ‖14‖
(8.2.15’) śivās te santv oṣadhaya ut tvāhārṣam adharasyā uttarāṃ pṛthivīm abhi |
(8.2.15.c) tatra tvādityau rakṣatāṃ sūryācandramasāv ubhā ‖15‖
(8.2.16’) yat te vāsaḥ paridhānaṃ yāṃ nīviṃ kṛṇuṣe tvam |
(8.2.16.c) śivaṃ te tanve tat kṛṇmaḥ saṃsparśe 'drūkṣṇam astu te ‖16‖
(8.2.17’) yat kṣureṇa marcayatā sutejasā vaptā vapasi keśaśmaśru |
(8.2.17.c) śubhaṃ mukhaṃ mā na āyuḥ pra moṣīḥ ‖17‖
(8.2.18’) śivau te stāṃ vrīhiyavāv abalāsāv adomadhau |
(8.2.18.c) etau yakṣmaṃ vi bādhete etau muñcato amhasaḥ ‖18‖
(8.2.19’) yad aśnāsi yat pibasi dhānyaṃ kṛṣyāḥ payaḥ |
(8.2.19.c) yad ādyaṃ yad anādyaṃ sarvaṃ te annam aviṣaṃ kṛṇomi ‖19‖
(8.2.20’) ahne ca tvā rātraye cobhābhyāṃ pari dadmasi |
(8.2.20.c) arāyebhyo jighatsubhya imaṃ me pari rakṣata ‖20‖ {4}
(8.2.21’) śataṃ te 'yutaṃ hāyanān dve yuge trīṇi catvāri kṛṇmaḥ |
(8.2.21.c) indrāgnī viśve devās te 'nu manyantām ahṛṇīyamānāḥ ‖21‖
(8.2.22’) śarade tvā hemantāya vasantāya grīṣmāya pari dadmasi |
(8.2.22.c) varṣāṇi tubhyaṃ syonāni yeṣu vardhanta oṣadhīḥ ‖22‖
(8.2.23’) mṛtyur īśe dvipadāṃ mṛtyur īśe catuṣpadām |
(8.2.23.c) tasmāt tvāṃ mṛtyor gopater ud bharāmi sa mā bibheḥ ‖23‖
(8.2.24’) so 'riṣṭa na mariṣyasi na mariṣyasi mā bibheḥ |
(8.2.24.c) na vai tatra mriyante no yanti adhamaṃ tamaḥ ‖24‖
(8.2.25’) sarvo vai tatra jīvati gaur aśvaḥ puruṣaḥ paśuḥ |
(8.2.25.c) yatredaṃ brahma kriyate paridhir jīvanāya kam ‖25‖
(8.2.26’) pari tvā pātu samānebhyo 'bhicārāt sabandhubhyaḥ |
(8.2.26.c) amamrir bhavāmṛto 'tijīvo mā te hāsiṣur asavaḥ śarīram ‖26‖
(8.2.27’) ye mṛtyava ekaśataṃ yā nāṣṭrā atitāryāḥ |
(8.2.27.c) muñcantu tasmāt tvāṃ devā agner vaiśvānarād adhi ‖27‖
(8.2.28’) agneḥ śarīram asi pārayiṣṇu rakṣohāsi sapatnahā |
(8.2.28.c) atho amīvacātanaḥ pūtudrur nāma bheṣajam ‖28‖ {5}

(8.3.1’) rakṣohaṇaṃ vājinam ā jigharmi mitraṃ prathiṣṭham upa yāmi śarma |
(8.3.1.c) śiśāno agniḥ kratubhiḥ samiddhaḥ sa no divā sa riṣaḥ pātu naktam ‖1‖
(8.3.2’) ayodaṃṣṭro arciṣā yātudhānān upa spṛśa jātavedaḥ samiddhaḥ |
(8.3.2.c) ā jihvayā mūradevān rabhasva kravyādo vṛṣṭvāpi dhatsvāsan ‖2‖
(8.3.3’) ubhobhayāvinn upa dhehi daṃṣṭrau hiṃsraḥ śiśāno 'varaṃ paraṃ ca |
(8.3.3.c) utāntarikṣe pari yāhy agne jambhaiḥ saṃ dhehy abhi yātudhānān ‖3‖
(8.3.4’) agne tvacaṃ yātudhānasya bhindhi hiṃsrāśanir harasā hantv enam |
(8.3.4.c) pra parvāṇi jātavedaḥ śṛṇīhi kravyāt kraviṣṇur vi cinotv enam ‖4‖
(8.3.5’) yatredānīṃ paśyasi jātavedas tiṣṭhantam agna uta vā carantam |
(8.3.5.c) utāntarikṣe patantaṃ yātudhānaṃ tam astā vidhya śarvā śiśānaḥ ‖5‖
(8.3.6’) yajñair iṣūḥ saṃnamamāno agne ivācā śalyā;m aśanibhir dihānaḥ |
(8.3.6.c) tābhir vidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān pratīco bāhūn prati bhaṅgdhy eṣām ‖6‖
(8.3.7’) utārabdhānt spṛnuhi jātaveda utārebhāṇā;m ṛṣṭibhir yātudhānān |
(8.3.7.c) agne pūrvo ni jahi śośucāna āmādaḥ kṣviṅkās tam adantv enīḥ ‖7‖
(8.3.8’) iha pra brūhi yatamaḥ so agne yātudhāno ya idaṃ kṛṇoti |
(8.3.8.c) tam ā rabhasva samidhā yaviṣṭha nṛcakṣasaś cakṣuṣe randhayainam ‖8‖
(8.3.9’) tīkṣṇenāgne cakṣuṣā rakṣa yajñaṃ prāñcaṃ vasubhyaḥ pra ṇaya pracetaḥ |
(8.3.9.c) hiṃsraṃ rakṣāṃsy abhi śośucānaṃ mā tvā dabhan yātudhānā nṛcakṣaḥ ‖9‖
(8.3.10’) nṛcakṣā rakṣaḥ pari paśya vikṣu tasya trīṇi prati śṛṇīhy agrā |
(8.3.10.c) tasyāgne pṛṣṭīr harasā śṛṇīhi tredhā mūlaṃ yātudhānasya vṛśca ‖10‖ {6}
(8.3.11’) trir yātudhānaḥ prasitiṃ ta etv ṛtaṃ yo agne anṛtena hanti |
(8.3.11.c) tam arciṣā sphūrjayan jātavedaḥ samakṣam enam gṛṇate ni yuṅgdhi ‖11‖
(8.3.12’) yad agne adya mithunā śapāto yad vācas tṛṣṭaṃ janayanta rebhāḥ |
(8.3.12.c) manyor manasaḥ śaravyā jāyate yā tayā vidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān ‖12‖
(8.3.13’) parā śṛṇīhi tapasā yātudhānān parāgne rakṣo harasā śṛṇīhi |
(8.3.13.c) parārciṣā mūradevān chṛṇīhi parāsutṛpaḥ śośucataḥ śṛṇīhi ‖13‖
(8.3.14’) parādya devā vṛjinaṃ śṛṇantu pratyag enaṃ śapathā yantu sṛṣṭāḥ |
(8.3.14.c) vācāstenaṃ śarava ṛchantu marman viśvasyaitu prasitiṃ yātudhānaḥ ‖14‖
(8.3.15’) yaḥ pauruṣeyeṇa kraviṣā samaṅkte yo aśvyena paśunā yātudhānaḥ |
(8.3.15.c) yo aghnyāyā bharati kṣīram agne teṣāṃ śīrṣāṇi harasāpi vṛśca ‖15‖
(8.3.16’) viṣaṃ gavāṃ yātudhānā bharantām ā vṛścantām aditaye durevāḥ |
(8.3.16.c) paraiṇān devaḥ savitā dadātu parā bhāgam oṣadhīnāṃ jayantām ‖16‖
(8.3.17’) saṃvatsarīṇaṃ paya usriyāyās tasya māśīd yātudhāno nṛcakṣaḥ |
(8.3.17.c) pīyūṣam agne yatamas titṛpsāt taṃ pratyañcam arciṣā vidhya marmaṇi ‖17‖
(8.3.18’) sanād agne mṛṇasi yātudhānān na tvā rakṣāṃsi pṛtanāsu jigyuḥ |
(8.3.18.c) sahamūrān anu daha kravyādo mā te hetyā mukṣata daivyāyāḥ ‖18‖
(8.3.19’) tvaṃ no agne adharād udaktas tvaṃ paścād uta rakṣā purastāt |
(8.3.19.c) prati tye te ajarāsas tapiṣṭhā aghaśaṃsaṃ śośucato dahantu ‖19‖
(8.3.20’) paścāt purastād adharād utottarāt kaviḥ kāvyena pari pāhy agne |
(8.3.20.c) sakhā sakhāyam ajaro jarimne agne martā;m amartyas tvaṃ naḥ ‖20‖ {7}
(8.3.21’) tad agne cakṣuḥ prati dhehi rebhe śaphārujo yena paśyasi yātudhānān |
(8.3.21.c) atharvavaj jyotiṣā daivyena satyaṃ dhūrvantam acitaṃ nyoṣa ‖21‖
(8.3.22’) pari tvāgne puraṃ vayaṃ vipraṃ sahasya dhīmahi |
(8.3.22.c) dhṛṣadvarṇaṃ divedive hantāraṃ bhaṅgurāvataḥ ‖22‖
(8.3.23’) viṣeṇa bhaṅgurāvataḥ prati sma rakṣaso jahi |
(8.3.23.c) agne tigmena śociṣā tapuragrābhir arcibhiḥ ‖23‖
(8.3.24’) vi jyotiṣā bṛhatā bhāty agnir āvir viśvāni kṛṇute mahitvā |
(8.3.24.c) prādevīr māyāḥ sahate durevāḥ śiśīte śṛṅge rakṣobhyo vinikṣe ‖24‖
(8.3.25’) ye te śṛṅge ajare jātavedas tigmahetī brahmasaṃśite |
(8.3.25.c) tābhyāṃ durhārdam abhidāsantaṃ kimīdinaṃ |
(8.3.25.e) pratyañcam arciṣā jātavedo vi nikṣva ‖25‖
(8.3.26’) agnī rakṣāṃsi sedhati śukraśocir amartyaḥ |
(8.3.26.c) śuciḥ pāvaka īḍyaḥ ‖26‖ {8}

(8.4.1’) indrāsomā tapataṃ rakṣa ubjataṃ ny arpayataṃ vṛṣaṇā tamovṛdhaḥ |
(8.4.1.c) parā śṛṇītam acito ny oṣataṃ hataṃ nudethāṃ ni śiśītam attriṇaḥ ‖1‖
(8.4.2’) indrāsomā sam aghaśaṃsam abhy aghaṃ tapur yayastu carur agnimā;m iva |
(8.4.2.c) brahmadviṣe kravyāde ghoracakṣase dveṣo dhattam anavāyaṃ kimīdine ‖2‖
(8.4.3’) indrāsomā duṣkṛto vavre antar anārambhaṇe tamasi pra vidhyatam |
(8.4.3.c) yato naiṣāṃ punar ekaś canodayat tad vām astu sahase manyumac chavaḥ ‖3‖
(8.4.4’) indrāsomā vartayataṃ divo vadhaṃ saṃ pṛthivyā aghaśaṃsāya tarhaṇam |
(8.4.4.c) ut takṣataṃ svaryaṃ1 parvatebhyo yena rakṣo vāvṛdhānaṃ nijūrvathaḥ ‖4‖
(8.4.5’) indrāsomā vartayataṃ divas pary agnitaptebhir yuvam aśmahanmabhiḥ |
(8.4.5.c) tapurvadhebhir ajarebhir attriṇo ni parśāne vidhyataṃ yantu nisvaram ‖5‖
(8.4.6’) indrāsomā pari vāṃ bhūtu viśvata iyaṃ matiḥ kakṣyāśveva vājinā |
(8.4.6.c) yāṃ vāṃ hotrāṃ parihinomi medhayemā brahmāṇi nṛpatī iva jinvatam ‖6‖
(8.4.7’) prati smarethāṃ tujayadbhir evair hataṃ druho rakṣaso bhaṅgurāvataḥ |
(8.4.7.c) indrāsomā duṣkṛte mā sugaṃ bhūd yo mā kadā cid abhidāsati druhuḥ ‖7‖
(8.4.8’) yo mā pākena manasā carantam abhicaṣṭe anṛtebhir vacobhiḥ |
(8.4.8.c) āpa iva kāśinā samgṛbhītā asann astv asataḥ indra vaktā ‖8‖
(8.4.9’) ye pākaśaṃsaṃ viharanta evair ye vā bhadraṃ dūṣayanti svadhābhiḥ |
(8.4.9.c) ahaye vā tān pradadātu soma ā vā dadhātu nirṛter upaṣṭhe ‖9‖
(8.4.10’) yo no rasaṃ dipsati pitvo agne aśvānāṃ gavāṃ yas tanūnām |
(8.4.10.c) ripu stena steyakṛd dabhram etu ni ṣa hīyatāṃ tanvā tanā ca ‖10‖ {9}
(8.4.11’) paraḥ so astu tanvā tanā ca tisraḥ pṛthivīr adho astu viśvāḥ |
(8.4.11.c) prati śuṣyatu yaśo asya devā yo mā divā dipsati yaś ca naktam ‖11‖
(8.4.12’) suvijñānaṃ cikituṣe janāya sac cāsac ca vacasī paspṛdhāte |
(8.4.12.c) tasyor yat satyaṃ yatarad ṛjīyas tad it somo 'vati hanty asat ‖12‖
(8.4.13’) na vā u somo vṛjinaṃ hinoti na kṣatriyam mithuyā dhārayantam |
(8.4.13.c) hanti rakṣo hanty āsad vadantam ubhāv indrasya prasitau śayāte ‖13‖
(8.4.14’) yadi vāham anṛtadevo asmi moghaṃ vā devā;m apyūhe agne |
(8.4.14.c) kim asmabhyaṃ jātavedo hṛṇīṣe droghavācas te nirṛthaṃ sacantām ‖14‖
(8.4.15’) adyā murīya yadi yātudhāno asmi yadi vāyus tatapa puruṣasya |
(8.4.15.c) adhā sa vīrair daśabhir vi yūyā yo mā moghaṃ yātudhānety āha ‖15‖
(8.4.16’) yo māyātuṃ yātudhānety āha yo vā rakṣāḥ śicir asmīty āha |
(8.4.16.c) indras taṃ hantu mahatā vadhena viśvasya jantor adhamas padīṣṭa ‖16‖
(8.4.17’) pra yā jigāti khargaleva naktam apa druhus tanvaṃ1 gūhamānā |
(8.4.17.c) vavram anantam ava sā padīṣṭia grāvāṇo ghnantu rakṣasa upabdaiḥ ‖17‖
(8.4.18’) vi tiṣṭhadhvam maruto vikṣv ichata gṛbhāyata rakṣasaḥ saṃ pinaṣṭan |
(8.4.18.c) vayo ye bhūtvā patayanti naktabhir ye vā ripo dadhire deve adhvare ‖18‖
(8.4.19’) pra vartaya divo 'śmānam indra somaśitaṃ maghavant saṃ śiśādhi |
(8.4.19.c) prākto apākto adharād udakto 'bhi jahi rakṣasaḥ parvatena ‖19‖
(8.4.20’) eta u tye patayanti śvayātava indraṃ dipsanti dipsavo 'dābhyam |
(8.4.20.c) śiśīte śakraḥ piśunebhyo vadhaṃ nunaṃ sṛjad aśaniṃ yātumadbhyaḥ ‖20‖ {10}
(8.4.21’) indro yātūnām abhavat parāśaro havirmathīnām abhy āvivāsatām |
(8.4.21.c) abhīd u śakraḥ paraśur yathā vanaṃ pātreva bhindant sata etu rakṣasaḥ ‖21‖
(8.4.22’) ulūkayātuṃ śuśulūkayātuṃ jahi śvayātum uta kokayātum |
(8.4.22.c) suparṇayātum uta gṛdhrayātuṃ dṛṣadeva pra mṛṇa rakṣa indra ‖22‖
(8.4.23’) mā no rakṣo abhi naḍ yātumāvad apochantu mithunā ye kimīdinaḥ |
(8.4.23.c) pṛthivī naḥ pārthivāt pātv aṃhaso 'ntarikṣaṃ divyāt pātv asmān ‖23‖
(8.4.24’) indra jahi pumāṃsaṃ yātudhānam uta striyaṃ māyayā śāśadānām |
(8.4.24.c) vigrīvāso mūradevā ṛdantu mā te dṛśant sūryam uccarantam ‖24‖
(8.4.25’) prati cakṣva vi cakṣvendraś ca soma jāgṛtam |
(8.4.25.c) rakṣobhyo vadham asyatam aśaniṃ yātumadbhyaḥ ‖25‖ {11}

(8.5.1’) ayaṃ pratisaro maṇir vīro vīrāya badhyate |
(8.5.1.c) vīryavānt sapatnahā śūravīraḥ paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ ‖1‖
(8.5.2’) ayaṃ maṇiḥ sapatnahā suvīraḥ sahasvān vājī sahamāna ugraḥ |
(8.5.2.c) pratyak kṛtyā dūṣayann eti vīraḥ ‖2‖
(8.5.3’) anenendro maṇinā vṛtram ahann anenāsurān parābhāvayan manīṣī |
(8.5.3.c) anenājayad dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime anenājayat pradiśaś catasraḥ ‖3‖
(8.5.4’) ayaṃ srāktyo maṇiḥ pratīvartaḥ pratisaraḥ |
(8.5.4.c) ojasvān vimṛdho vaśī so asmān pātu sarvataḥ ‖4‖
(8.5.5’) tad agnir āha tad u soma āha bṛhaspatiḥ savitā tad indraḥ |
(8.5.5.c) te me devāḥ purohitāḥ pratīcīḥ kṛtyāḥ pratisarair ajantu ‖5‖
(8.5.6’) antar dadhe dyāvāpṛthivī utāhar uta sūryam |
(8.5.6.c) te me devāḥ purohitāḥ pratīcīḥ kṛtyāḥ pratisarair ajantu ‖6‖
(8.5.7’) ye srāktyaṃ maṇiṃ janā varmāṇi kṛṇvate |
(8.5.7.c) sūrya iva divam āruhya vi kṛtyā bādhate vaśī ‖7‖
(8.5.8’) srāktyena maṇinā ṛṣiṇeva manīṣiṇā |
(8.5.8.c) ajaiṣaṃ sarvāḥ pṛtanā vi mṛdho hanmi rakṣasaḥ ‖8‖
(8.5.9’) yāḥ kṛtyā āṅgirasīr yāḥ kṛtyā āsurīr yāḥ |
(8.5.9.c) kṛtyāḥ svayaṃkṛtā yā u cānyebhir ābhṛtāḥ |
(8.5.9.e) ubhayīs tāḥ parā yantu parāvato navatiṃ nāvyā ati ‖9‖
(8.5.10’) asmai maṇiṃ varma badhnantu devā indro viṣṇuḥ savitā rudro agniḥ |
(8.5.10.c) prajāpatiḥ parameṣṭhī virāḍ vaiśvānara ṛṣayaś ca sarve ‖10‖ {12}
(8.5.11’) uttamo asy oṣadhīnām anaḍvān jagatām iva vyāghraḥ śvapadām iva |
(8.5.11.c) yam aichāmāvidāma taṃ pratispāśanam antitam ‖11‖
(8.5.12’) sa id vyāghro bhavaty atho siṃho atho vṛṣā |
(8.5.12.c) atho sapatnakarśano yo bibhartīmaṃ maṇim ‖12‖
(8.5.13’) nainaṃ ghnanty apsaraso na gandharvā na martyāḥ |
(8.5.13.c) sarvā diśo vi rājati yo bibhartīmaṃ maṇim ‖13‖
(8.5.14’) kaśyapas tvām asṛjata kaśyapas tvā sam airayat |
(8.5.14.c) abibhas tvendro mānuṣe bibhrat saṃśreṣiṇe 'jayat |
(8.5.14.e) maṇiṃ sahasravīryaṃ varma devā akṛṇvata ‖14‖
(8.5.15’) yas tvā kṛtyābhir yas tvā dīkṣābhir yajñair yas tvā jighāṃsati |
(8.5.15.c) pratyak tvam indra taṃ jahi vajreṇa śataparvaṇā ‖15‖
(8.5.16’) ayam id vai pratīvarta ojasvān saṃjayo maṇiḥ |
(8.5.16.c) prajāṃ dhanaṃ ca rakṣatu paripāṇaḥ sumaṅgalaḥ ‖16‖
(8.5.17’) asapatnaṃ no adharād asapatnaṃ na uttarāt |
(8.5.17.c) indrāsapatnaṃ naḥ paścāj jyotiḥ śūra puras kṛdhi ‖17‖
(8.5.18’) varma me dyāvāpṛthivī varmāhar varma sūryaḥ |
(8.5.18.c) varma ma indraś cāgniś ca varma dhātā dadhātu me ‖18‖
(8.5.19’) aindrāgnaṃ varma bahulaṃ yad ugraṃ viśve devā nātividhyanti sarve |
(8.5.19.c) tan me tanvaṃ trāyatāṃ sarvato bṛhad āyuṣmāṃ jaradaṣṭir yathāsāni ‖19‖
(8.5.20’) ā mārukṣad devamaṇir mahyā ariṣṭatātaye |
(8.5.20.c) imaṃ methim abhisaṃviśadhvaṃ tanūpānaṃ trivarūtham ojase ‖20‖
(8.5.21’) asminn indro ni dadhātu nṛmṇam imaṃ devāso abhisaṃviśadhvam |
(8.5.21.c) dīrghāyutvāya śataśāradāyāyuṣmān jaradaṣṭir yathāsat ‖21‖
(8.5.22’) svastidā viśāṃ patir vṛtrahā vimṛdho vaśī |
(8.5.22.c) indro badhnātu te maṇiṃ jigīvā;m aparājitaḥ |
(8.5.22.e) somapā abhayaṅkaro vṛṣā |

(8.6.1’) yau te mātonmamārja jātāyāḥ pativedanau |
(8.6.1.c) durṇāmā tatra mā gṛdhad aliṃśa uta vatsapaḥ ‖1‖
(8.6.2’) palālānupalālau śarkuṃ kokaṃ malimlucaṃ palījakam |
(8.6.2.c) āśreṣaṃ vavrivāsasam ṛkṣagrīvaṃ pramīlinam ‖2‖
(8.6.3’) mā saṃ vṛto mopa sṛpa ūrū māva sṛpo 'ntarā |
(8.6.3.c) kṛṇomy asyai bheṣajaṃ bajaṃ durṇāmacātanam ‖3‖
(8.6.4’) durṇāmā ca sunāmā cobhā samvṛtam ichataḥ |
(8.6.4.c) arāyān apa hanmaḥ sunāmā straiṇam ichatām ‖4‖
(8.6.5’) yaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ keśy asura stambaja uta tuṇḍikaḥ |
(8.6.5.c) arāyān asyā muṣkābhyāṃ bhaṃsaso 'pa hanmasi ‖5‖
(8.6.6’) anujighraṃ pramṛśantaṃ kravyādam uta reriham |
(8.6.6.c) arāyāṃ chvakiṣkiṇo bajaḥ piṅgo anīnaśat ‖6‖
(8.6.7’) yas tvā svapne nipadyate bhrātā bhūtvā piteva ca |
(8.6.7.c) bajas tānt sahatām itaḥ klībarūpāṃs tirītinaḥ ‖7‖
(8.6.8’) yas tvā svapantīṃ tsarati yas tvā dipsati jāgratīm |
(8.6.8.c) chāyām iva pra tānt sūryaḥ parikrāmann anīnaśat ‖8‖
(8.6.9’) yaḥ kṛṇoti mṛtavatsām avatokām imāṃ striyam |
(8.6.9.c) tam oṣadhe tvaṃ nāśayāsyāḥ kamalam añjivam ‖9‖
(8.6.10’) ye śālāḥ parinṛtyanti sāyaṃ gardabhanādinaḥ |
(8.6.10.c) kusūlā ye ca kukṣilāḥ kakubhāḥ karumāḥ srimāḥ |
(8.6.10.e) tān oṣadhe tvaṃ gandhena viṣūcīnān vi nāśaya ‖10‖ {14}
(8.6.11’) ye kukundhāḥ kukirabhāḥ kṛttīr dūrśāni bibhrati |
(8.6.11.c) klībā iva pranṛtyanto vane ye kurvate ghoṣaṃ tān ito nāśayāmasi ‖11‖
(8.6.12’) ye sūryaṃ na titikṣanta ātapantam amuṃ divaḥ |
(8.6.12.c) arāyān bastavāsino durgandhīṃl lohitāsyān makakān nāśayāmasi ‖12‖
(8.6.13’) ya ātmānam atimātram aṃsa ādhāya bibhrati |
(8.6.13.c) strīṇāṃ śroṇipratodina indra rakṣāṃsi nāśaya ‖13‖
(8.6.14’) ye pūrve badhvo yanti haste śṛṅgāni bibhrataḥ |
(8.6.14.c) āpākesthāḥ prahāsina stambe ye kurvate jyotis tān ito nāśayāmasi ‖14‖
(8.6.15’) yeṣām paścāt prapadāni puraḥ pārṣṇīḥ puro mukhā |
(8.6.15.c) khalajāḥ śakadhūmajā uruṇḍā ye ca maṭmaṭāḥ kumbhamuṣkā ayāśavaḥ |
(8.6.15.e) tān asyā brahmaṇas pate pratībodhena nāśaya ‖15‖
(8.6.16’) paryastākṣā apracaṅkaśā astraiṇāḥ santu paṇḍagāḥ |
(8.6.16.c) ava bheṣaja pādaya ya imāṃ saṃvivṛtsaty apatiḥ svapatiṃ striyam ‖16‖
(8.6.17’) uddharṣiṇaṃ munikeśaṃ jambhayantaṃ marīmṛśam |
(8.6.17.c) upeṣantam udumbalaṃ tuṇḍelam uta śāluḍam |
(8.6.17.e) padā pra vidhya pārṣṇyā sthālīṃ gaur iva spandanā ‖17‖
(8.6.18’) yas te garbhaṃ pratimṛśāj jātaṃ vā mārayāti te |
(8.6.18.c) piṅgas tam ugradhanvā kṛṇotu hṛdayāvidham ‖18‖
(8.6.19’) ye amno jatān mārayanti sūtikā anuśerate |
(8.6.19.c) strībhāgān piṅgo gandharvān vāto abhram ivājatu ‖19‖
(8.6.20’) parisṛṣṭaṃ dharayatu yad dhitaṃ māva pādi tat |
(8.6.20.c) garbhaṃ ta ugrau rakṣatām bheṣajau nīvibhāryau ‖20‖ {15}
(8.6.21’) pavīnasāt taṅgalvāc chāyakād uta nagnakāt |
(8.6.21.c) prajāyai patye tvā piṅgaḥ pari pātu kimīdinaḥ ‖21‖
(8.6.22’) dvyāsyāc caturakṣāt pañcapadād anaṅgureḥ |
(8.6.22.c) vṛntād abhi prasarpataḥ pari pāhi varīvṛtāt ‖22‖
(8.6.23’) ya āmaṃ māmsam adanti pauruṣeyaṃ ca ye kraviḥ |
(8.6.23.c) garbhān khādanti keśavās tān ito nāśayāmasi ‖23‖
(8.6.24’) ye sūryāt parisarpanti snuṣeva śvaśurād adhi |
(8.6.24.c) bajaś ca teṣāṃ piṅgaś ca hṛdaye 'dhi ni vidhyatām ‖24‖
(8.6.25’) piṅga rakṣa jāyamānaṃ mā pumāṃsaṃ striyaṃ kran |
(8.6.25.c) āṇḍādo garbhān mā dabhan bādhasvetaḥ kimīdinaḥ ‖25‖
(8.6.26’) aprajāstvaṃ mārtavatsam ād rodam agham āvayam |
(8.6.26.c) vṛkṣād iva srajam kṛtvāpriye prati muñca tat ‖26‖ {16}

(8.7.1’) yā babhravo yāś ca śukrā rohiṇīr uta pṛśnayaḥ |
(8.7.1.c) asiknīḥ kṛṣṇā oṣadhīḥ sarvā achāvadāmasi ‖1‖
(8.7.2’) trāyantām imaṃ purusaṃ yakṣmād deveṣitād adhi |
(8.7.2.c) yāsām dyauḥ pitā pṛthivī mātā samudro mūlaṃ vīrudhāṃ babhūva ‖2‖
(8.7.3’) āpo agraṃ divyā oṣadhayaḥ |

(8.10.19’) sod akrāmat sā pitṝn āgachat tāṃ pitaro 'ghnata sā māsi sam abhavat |
(8.10.19.c) tasmāt pitṛbhyo māsy upamāsyaṃ dadati pra pitṛyāṇaṃ panthāṃ jānāti ya evaṃ veda ‖19‖
(8.10.20’) sod akrāmat sā devān āgachat tāṃ devā aghnata sārdhamāse sam abhavat |
(8.10.20.c) tasmād devebhyo 'rdhamāse vaṣaṭ kurvanti pra devayānaṃ panthāṃ jānāti ya evaṃ veda ‖20‖
(8.10.21’) sod akrāmat sā manuṣyān āgachat tāṃ manuṣyā aghnata sā sadyaḥ sam abhavat |
(8.10.21.c) tasmān manuṣyebhya ubhayadyur upa haranty upāsya gṛhe haranti ya evaṃ veda ‖21‖ {27}
(8.10.21’) sod akrāmat sāsurān āgachat tām asurā upāhvayanta māya ehīti |
(8.10.21.c) tasyā virocanaḥ prāhrādir vatsa āsīd ayaspātraṃ pātram |
(8.10.21.e) tāṃ dvimūrdhārtvyo 'dhok tāṃ māyām evādhok |
(8.10.23’)  sod akrāmat sā pitṝn āgachat tāṃ pitara upāhvayanta svadha ehīti |
(8.10.23.c) tasyā yamo rājā vatsa āsīd rajatapātraṃ pātram |
(8.10.23.e) tām antako mārtyavo 'dhok tāṃ svadhām evādhok |
(8.10.24’) 	 sod akrāmat sā manuṣyān āgachat tāṃ manuṣyā upāhvayanterāvaty ehīti |
(8.10.24.c) tasyā manur vaivasvato vatsa āsīt pṛthivī pātram |
(8.10.24.e) tāṃ pṛthī vainyo 'dhok tāṃ kṛṣiṃ ca sasyaṃ cādhok |
(8.10.25’)  sod akrāmat sā saptaṛṣīn āgachat tāṃ saptaṛṣaya upāhvayanta brahmaṇvaty ehīti |
(8.10.25.c) tasyāḥ somo rājā vatsa āsīc chandaḥ pātram |
(8.10.25.e) tāṃ bṛhaspatir āṅgiraso 'dhok tāṃ brahma ca tapaś cādhok |
(8.10.25’) sod akrāmat sā devān āgachat tāṃ devā upāhvayantorja ehīti |
(8.10.25.c) tasyā indro vatsa āsīc camasaḥ pātram |
(8.10.25.e) tāṃ devaḥ savitādhok tām ūrjām evādhok |
(8.10.27’) sod akrāmat sā gandharvāpsarasa āgachat tāṃ gandharvāpsarasa upāhvayanta puṇyagandha ehīti |
(8.10.27.c) tasyāś citrarathaḥ sauryavarcaso vatsa āsīt puṣkaraparṇaṃ pātram |
(8.10.27.e) tāṃ vasuruciḥ sauryavarcaso 'dhok tāṃ puṇyam eva gandham adhok |
(8.10.28’) sod akrāmat setarajanān āgachat tām itarajanā upāhvayanta tirodha ehīti |
(8.10.28.c) tasyāḥ kubero vaiśravaṇo vatsa āsīd āmapātraṃ pātram |
(8.10.28.e) tāṃ rajatanābhiḥ kaberako 'dhok tāṃ tirodhām evādhok |
(8.10.29’) sod akrāmat sā sarpān āgachat tāṃ sarpā upāhvayanta viṣavaty ehīti |
(8.10.29.c) tasyās takṣako vaiśaleyo vatsa āsīd alābupātraṃ pātraṃ |
(8.10.29.e) tāṃ dhṛtarāṣṭra airāvato 'dhok tāṃ viṣam evādhok |
(8.10.29’) tad yasmā evaṃ viduṣe 'lābunābhiṣiñcet pratyāhanyāt ‖30‖
(8.10.31’) na ca pratyāhanyān manasā tvā pratyāhanmīti pratyāhanyāt ‖31‖
(8.10.32’) yat pratyāhanti viṣam eva tat pratyāhanti ‖32‖
(8.10.33’) viṣam evāsyāpriyaṃ bhrātṛvyam anuviṣicyate ya evaṃ veda ‖33‖ {30}

(9.1.1’) divas pṛthivyā antarikṣāt samudrād agner vātān madhukaśā hi jajñe |
(9.1.1.c) tāṃ cāyitvāmṛtaṃ vasānāṃ hṛdbhiḥ prajāḥ prati nandanti sarvāḥ ‖1‖
(9.1.2’) mahat payo viśvarūpam asyāḥ samudrasya tvota reta āhuḥ |
(9.1.2.c) yata aiti madhukaśā rarāṇā tat prāṇas tad amṛtaṃ niviṣṭam ‖2‖
(9.1.3’) paśyanty asyāś caritaṃ pṛthivyāṃ pṛthaṅ naro bahudhā mīmāṃsamānāḥ |
(9.1.3.c) agner vātān madhukaśā hi jajñe marutām ugrā naptiḥ ‖3‖
(9.1.4’) mātādityānāṃ duhitā vasūnāṃ prāṇaḥ prajānām amṛtasya nābhiḥ |
(9.1.4.c) hiraṇyavarṇā madhukaśā ghṛtācī mahān bhargaś carati martyeṣu ‖4‖
(9.1.5’) madhoḥ kaśām ajanayanta devās tasyā garbho abhavad viśvarūpaḥ |
(9.1.5.c) taṃ jātaṃ taruṇaṃ piparti mātā sa jāto viśvā bhuvanā vi caṣṭe ‖5‖
(9.1.6’) kas taṃ pra veda ka u taṃ ciketa yo asyā hṛdaḥ kalaśaḥ somadhāno akṣitaḥ |
(9.1.6.c) brahmā sumedhāḥ so asmin madeta ‖6‖
(9.1.7’) sa tau pra veda sa u tau ciketa yāv asyāḥ stanau sahasradhārāv akṣitau |
(9.1.7.c) ūrjaṃ duhāte anapasphurantau ‖7‖
(9.1.8’) hiṅkarikratī bṛhatī vayodhā uccairghoṣābhyeti yā vratam |
(9.1.8.c) trīn gharmān abhi vāvaśānā mimāti māyuṃ payate payobhiḥ ‖8‖
(9.1.9’) yām āpīnām upasīdanty āpaḥ śākvarā vṛṣabhā ye svarājaḥ |
(9.1.9.c) te varṣanti te varṣayanti tadvide kāmam ūrjam āpaḥ ‖9‖
(9.1.10’) stanayitnus te vāk prajāpate vṛṣā śuṣmaṃ kṣipasi bhūmyām adhi |
(9.1.10.c) agner vātān madhukaśā hi jajñe marutām ugrā naptiḥ ‖10‖ {1}
(9.1.11’) yathā somaḥ prātaḥsavane aśvinor bhavati priyaḥ |
(9.1.11.c) evā me aśvinā varca ātmani dhriyatām ‖11‖
(9.1.12’) yathā somo dvitīye savana indrāgnyor bhavati priyaḥ |
(9.1.12.c) evā ma indrāgnī varca ātmani dhriyatām ‖12‖
(9.1.13’) yathā somas tṛtīye savana ṛbhūṇāṃ bhavati priyaḥ |
(9.1.13.c) evā ma ṛbhavo varca ātmani dhriyatām ‖13‖
(9.1.14’) madhu janiṣīya madhu vaṃsiṣīya |
(9.1.14.c) payasvān agna āgamaṃ taṃ mā saṃ sṛja varcasā ‖14‖
(9.1.15’) saṃ māgne varcasā sṛja saṃ prajayā sam āyuṣā |
(9.1.15.c) vidyur me asya devā indro vidyāt saha ṛṣibhiḥ ‖15‖
(9.1.16’) yathā madhu madhukṛtaḥ saṃbharanti madhāv adhi |
(9.1.16.c) evā me aśvinā varca ātmani dhriyatām ‖16‖
(9.1.17’) yathā makṣāḥ idaṃ madhu nyañjanti madhāv adhi |
(9.1.17.c) evā me aśvinā varcas tejo balam ojaś ca dhriyatām ‖17‖
(9.1.18’) yad giriṣu parvateṣu goṣv aśveṣu yan madhu |
(9.1.18.c) surāyāṃ sicyamānāyāṃ yat tatra madhu tan mayi ‖18‖
(9.1.19’) aśvinā sāragheṇa mā madhunāṅktaṃ śubhas patī |
(9.1.19.c) yathā varcasvatīṃ vācam āvadāni janā;m anu ‖19‖
(9.1.20’) stanayitnus te vāk prajāpate vṛṣā śuṣmaṃ kṣipasi bhūmyāṃ divi |
(9.1.20.c) tāṃ paśava upa jīvanti sarve teno seṣam ūrjaṃ piparti ‖20‖
(9.1.21’) pṛthivī daṇḍo 'ntarikṣaṃ garbho dyauḥ kaśā vidyut prakaśo hiraṇyayo binduḥ ‖21‖
(9.1.22’) yo vai kaśāyāḥ sapta madhūni veda madhumān bhavati |
(9.1.22.c) brāhmaṇaś ca rājā ca dhenuś cānaḍvāṃś ca vrīhiś ca yavaś ca madhu saptamam ‖22‖
(9.1.23’) madhumān bhavati madhumad asyāhāryaṃ bhavati |
(9.1.23.c) madhumato lokān jayati ya evaṃ veda ‖23‖
(9.1.24’) yad vīdhre stanayati prajāpatir eva tat prajābhyaḥ prādur bhavati |
(9.1.24.c) tasmāt prācīnopavītas tiṣṭhe prajāpate 'nu mā budhyasveti |
(9.1.24.e) anv enaṃ prajā anu prajāpatir budhyate ya evaṃ veda ‖24‖ {2}

(9.2.1’) sapatnahanam ṛṣabhaṃ ghṛtena kāmaṃ śikṣāmi haviṣājyena |
(9.2.1.c) nīcaiḥ sapatnān mama padaya tvam abhiṣṭuto mahatā vīryeṇa ‖1‖
(9.2.2’) yan me manaso na priyaṃ cakṣuṣo yan me babhasti nābhinandati |
(9.2.2.c) tad duṣvapnyaṃ prati muñcāmi sapatne kāmaṃ stutvod ahaṃ bhideyam ‖2‖
(9.2.3’) duṣvapnyaṃ kāma duritaṃ ca kamāprajastām asvagatām avartim |
(9.2.3.c) ugra īśānaḥ prati muñca tasmin yo asmabhyam aṃhūraṇā cikitsāt ‖3‖
(9.2.4’) nudasva kāma pra ṇudasva kāmāvartiṃ yantu mama ye sapatnāḥ |
(9.2.4.c) teṣāṃ nuttānām adhamā tamāṃsy agne vāstūni nir daha tvam ‖4‖
(9.2.5’) sā te kāma duhitā dhenur ucyate yām āhur vācaṃ kavayo virājam |
(9.2.5.c) tayā sapatnān pari vṛṅgdhi ye mama pary enān prāṇaḥ paśavo jīvanaṃ vṛṇaktu ‖5‖
(9.2.6’) kāmasyendrasya varuṇasya rājño viṣṇor balena savituḥ savena |
(9.2.6.c) agner hotreṇa pra ṇude sapatnāṃ chambīva nāvam udakeṣu dhīraḥ ‖6‖
(9.2.7’) adhyakṣo vājī mama kāma ugraḥ kṛṇotu mahyam asapatnam eva |
(9.2.7.c) viśve devā mama nāthaṃ bhavantu sarve devā havam ā yantu ma imam ‖7‖
(9.2.8’) idam ājyaṃ ghṛtavaj juṣāṇāḥ kāmajyeṣṭhā iha mādayadhvam |
(9.2.8.c) kṛṇvanto mahyam asapatnam eva ‖8‖
(9.2.9’) indrāgnī kāma sarathaṃ hi bhūtvā nīcaiḥ sapatnān mama pādayāthaḥ |
(9.2.9.c) teṣāṃ pannānām adhamā tamāṃsy agne vāstūny anunirdaha tvam ‖9‖
(9.2.10’) jahi tvam kāma mama ye sapatnā andhā tamāṃsy ava pādayainān |
(9.2.10.c) nirindriyā arasāḥ santu sarve mā te jīviṣuḥ katamac canāhaḥ ‖10‖ {3}
(9.2.11’) avadhīt kāmo mama ye sapatnā uruṃ lokam akaran mahyam edhatum |
(9.2.11.c) mahyaṃ namantāṃ pradiśaś catasro mahyaṃ ṣaḍ urvīr ghṛtam ā vahantu ‖11‖
(9.2.12’) te 'dharāñcaḥ pra plavantāṃ chinnā naur iva bandhanāt |
(9.2.12.c) na sāyakapraṇuttānāṃ punar asti nivartanam ‖12‖
(9.2.13’) agnir yava indro yavaḥ somo yavaḥ |
(9.2.13.c) yavayāvāno devā yavayantv enam ‖13‖
(9.2.14’) asarvavīraś caratu praṇutto dveṣyo mitrānāṃ parivargyaḥ svānām |
(9.2.14.c) uta pṛthivyām ava syanti vidyuta ugro vo devaḥ pra mṛṇat sapatnān ‖14‖
(9.2.15’) cyutā ceyaṃ bṛhaty acyutā ca vidyud bibharti stanayitnūṃś ca sarvān |
(9.2.15.c) udyann ādityo draviṇena tejasā nīcaiḥ sapatnān nudatāṃ me sahasvān ‖15‖
(9.2.16’) yat te kāma śarma trivarūtham udbhu brahma varma vitatam anativyādhyaṃ kṛtam |
(9.2.16.c) tena sapatnān pari vṛṅgdhi ye mama pary enān prāṇaḥ paśāvo jīvanaṃ vṛṇaktu ‖16‖
(9.2.17’) yena devā asurān prāṇudanta yenendro dasyūn adhamaṃ tamo nināya |
(9.2.17.c) tena tvaṃ kāma mama ye sapatnās tān asmāl lokāt pra ṇudasva dūram ‖17‖
(9.2.18’) yathā devā asurān prāṇudanta yathendro dasyūn adhamaṃ tamo babādhe |
(9.2.18.c) tathā tvaṃ kāma mama ye sapatnās tān asmāl lokāt pra ṇudasva dūram ‖18‖
(9.2.19’) kāmo jajñe prathamo nainaṃ devā āpuḥ pitaro na martyāḥ |
(9.2.19.c) tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃs tasmai te kāma nama it kṛnomi ‖19‖
(9.2.20’) yāvatī dyāvāpṛthivī varimṇā yāvad āpaḥ siṣyadur yāvad agniḥ |
(9.2.20.c) tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃs tasmai te kāma nama it kṛṇomi ‖20‖ {4}
(9.2.21’) yāvatīr diśaḥ pradiśo viṣūcīr yāvatīr āśā abhicakṣaṇā divaḥ |
(9.2.21.c) tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃs tasmai te kāma nama it kṛṇomi ‖21‖
(9.2.22’) yāvatīr bhṛṅgā jatvaḥ kurūravo yāvatīr vaghā vṛkṣasarpyo babhūvuḥ |
(9.2.22.c) tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃs tasmai te kāma nama it kṛṇomi ‖22‖
(9.2.23’) jyāyān nimiṣato 'si tiṣṭhato jyāyānt samudrād asi kāma manyo |
(9.2.23.c) tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃs tasmai te kāma nama it kṛnomi ‖23‖
(9.2.24’) na vai vātaś cana kāmam āpnoti nāgniḥ sūryo nota candramāḥ |
(9.2.24.c) tatas tvam asi jyāyān viśvahā mahāṃs tasmai te kāma nama it kṛṇomi ‖24‖
(9.2.25’) yās te śivās tanvaḥ kāma bhadrā yābhiḥ satyaṃ bhavati yad vṛṇiṣe |
(9.2.25.c) tābhiṣ ṭvam asmā;m abhisaṃviśasvānyatra pāpīr apa veśayā dhiyaḥ ‖25‖ {5}

(9.3.1’) upamitāṃ pratimitām atho parimitām uta |
(9.3.1.c) śālāyā viśvavārāyā naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi ‖1‖
(9.3.2’) yat te naddhaṃ viśvavāre pāśo granthiś ca yaḥ kṛtaḥ |
(9.3.2.c) bṛhaspatir ivāhaṃ balaṃ vācā vi sraṃsayāmi tat ‖2‖
(9.3.3’) ā yayāma saṃ babarha granthīṃś cakāra te dṛḍhān |
(9.3.3.c) parūṃṣi vidvāṃ chastevendreṇa vi cṛtāmasi ‖3‖
(9.3.4’) vaṃśānāṃ te nahanānāṃ prāṇāhasya tṛṇasya ca |
(9.3.4.c) pakṣāṇāṃ viśvavāre te naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi ‖4‖
(9.3.5’) saṃdaṃśānāṃ paladānāṃ pariṣvañjalyasya ca |
(9.3.5.c) idaṃ mānasya patnyā naddhāni vi cṛtāmasi ‖5‖
(9.3.6’) yāni te 'ntaḥ śikyāny ābedhū raṇyāya kam |
(9.3.6.c) pra te tāni cṛtāmasi śivā mānasya patni na uddhitā tanve bhava ‖6‖
(9.3.7’) havirdhānam agniśālaṃ patnīnāṃ sadanaṃ sadaḥ |
(9.3.7.c) sado devānām asi devi śāle ‖7‖
(9.3.8’) akṣum opaśaṃ vitataṃ sahasrākṣaṃ viṣūvati |
(9.3.8.c) avanaddham abhihitaṃ brahmaṇā vi cṛtāmasi ‖8‖
(9.3.9’) yas tvā śāle pratigṛhṇāti yena cāsi mitā tvam |
(9.3.9.c) ubhau mānasya patni tau jīvatāṃ jaradaṣṭī ‖9‖
(9.3.10’) amutrainam ā gachatād dṛḍhā naddhā pariṣkṛtā |
(9.3.10.c) yasyās te vicṛtāmasy aṅgamaṅgaṃ paruṣparuḥ ‖10‖ {6}
(9.3.11’) yas tvā śāle nimimāya saṃjabhāra vanaspatīn |
(9.3.11.c) prajāyai cakre tvā śāle parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ ‖11‖
(9.3.12’) namas tasmai namo dātre śālāpataye ca kṛṇmaḥ |
(9.3.12.c) namo 'gnaye pracarate puruṣāya ca te namaḥ ‖12‖
(9.3.13’) gobhyo aśvebhyo namo yac chālāyāṃ vijāyate |
(9.3.13.c) vijāvati prajāvati vi te pāśāṃś cṛtāmasi ‖13‖
(9.3.14’) agnim antaś chādayasi puruṣān paśubhiḥ saha |
(9.3.14.c) vijāvati prajāvati vi te pāśāṃś cṛtāmasi ‖14‖
(9.3.15’) antarā dyāṃ ca pṛthivīṃ ca yad vyacas tena śālāṃ prati gṛhṇāmi ta imām |
(9.3.15.c) yad antarikṣaṃ rajaso vimānaṃ tat kṛṇve 'ham udaraṃ śevadhibhyaḥ |
(9.3.15.e) tena śālāṃ prati gṛhṇāmi tasmai ‖15‖
(9.3.16’) ūrjasvatī payasvatī pṛthivyāṃ nimitā mitā |
(9.3.16.c) viśvānnaṃ bibhratī śāle mā hiṃsīḥ pratigṛhṇataḥ ‖16‖
(9.3.17’) tṛṇair āvṛtā paladān vasānā rātrīva śālā jagato niveśanī |
(9.3.17.c) mitā pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhasi hastinīva padvatī ‖17‖
(9.3.18’) itasya te vi cṛtāmy apinaddham aporṇuvan |
(9.3.18.c) varuṇena samubjitāṃ mitraḥ prātar vy ubjatu ‖18‖
(9.3.19’) brahmaṇā śālāṃ nimitāṃ kavibhir nimitāṃ mitām |
(9.3.19.c) indrāgnī rakṣatāṃ śālām amṛtau somyaṃ sadah ‖19‖
(9.3.20’) kulāye 'dhi kulāyaṃ kośe kośaḥ samubjitaḥ |
(9.3.20.c) tatra marto vi jāyate yasmād viśvaṃ prajāyate ‖20‖ {7}
(9.3.21’) yā dvipakṣā catuṣpakṣā ṣaṭpakṣā yā nimīyate |
(9.3.21.c) aṣṭāpakṣāṃ daśapakṣāṃ śālāṃ mānasya patnīm agnir garbha ivā śaye ‖21‖
(9.3.22’) pratīcīṃ tvā pratīcīnaḥ śāle praimy ahiṃsatīm |
(9.3.22.c) agnir hy antar āpaś ca ṛtasya prathamā dvāḥ ‖22‖
(9.3.23’) imā āpaḥ pra bharāmy ayakṣmā yakṣmanāśanīḥ |
(9.3.23.c) gṛhān upa pra sīdāmy amṛtena sahāgninā ‖23‖
(9.3.24’) mā naḥ pāśaṃ prati muco gurur bhāro laghur bhava |
(9.3.24.c) vadhūm iva tvā śāle yatrakāmaṃ bharāmasi ‖24‖
(9.3.25’) prācyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ‖25‖
(9.3.26’) dakṣiṇāyā diśaḥ śālāyā namo mahimne svāhā devebhyaḥ svāhyebhyaḥ ‖26‖

(9.4.1’) sāhasras tveṣa ṛṣabhaḥ payasvān viśvā rūpāṇi vakṣaṇāsu bibhrat |
(9.4.1.c) bhadraṃ dātre yajamānāya śīkṣan bārhaspatya usriyas tantum ātān ‖1‖
(9.4.2’) apāṃ yo agne pratimā babhūva prabhūḥ sarvasmai pṛthivīva devī |
(9.4.2.c) pitā vatsānāṃ patir aghnyānāṃ sāhasre poṣe api naḥ kṛṇotu ‖2‖
(9.4.3’) pumān antarvānt sthaviraḥ payasvān vasoḥ kabandhaṃ ṛṣabho bibharti |
(9.4.3.c) tam indrāya pathibhir devayānair hutam agnir vahatu jātavedāḥ ‖3‖

(9.4.5’) devānāṃ bhāga upanāha eṣo 'pāṃ rasa oṣadhīnāṃ ghṛtasya |

(9.4.11’) ya indra iva deveṣu goṣv eti vivāvadat |
(9.4.11.c) tasya ṛṣabhasyāṅgāni brahmā saṃ stautu bhadrayā ‖11‖
(9.4.12’) pārśve āstām anumatyā bhagasyāstām anūvṛjau |
(9.4.12.c) aṣṭhīvantāv abravīn mitro mamaitau kevalāv iti ‖12‖
(9.4.13’) bhasad āsīd ādityānāṃ śroṇī āstāṃ bṛhaspateḥ |
(9.4.13.c) puchaṃ vātasya devasya tena dhūnoty oṣadhīḥ ‖13‖
(9.4.14’) gudā āsant sinīvālyāḥ sūryāyās tvacam abruvan |
(9.4.14.c) utthātur abruvan pada ṛṣabhaṃ yad akalpayan ‖14‖
(9.4.15’) kroḍa āsīj jāmiśaṃsasya somasya klaśo dhṛtaḥ |
(9.4.15.c) devāḥ saṃgatya yat sarva ṛṣabhaṃ vyakalpayan ‖15‖

(9.4.17.c) śṛṇoti bhadraṃ karṇābhyāṃ gavāṃ yaḥ patir aghnyaḥ ‖17‖

(9.6.39’) etad vā u svādīyo yad adhigavaṃ kṣīraṃ vā māṃsaṃ vā tad eva nāśnīyāt ‖39‖ {17}
(9.6.40’) sa ya evaṃ vidvān kṣīram upasicyopaharati |
(9.6.40.c) yāvad agniṣṭomeneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvad enenāva runddhe ‖40‖
(9.6.41’) sa ya evaṃ vidvānt sarpir upasicyopaharati |
(9.6.41.c) yāvad atirātreṇeṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvad enenāva runddhe ‖41‖
(9.6.42’) sa ya evaṃ vidvān madhūpasicyopaharati |
(9.6.42.c) yāvad sattrasadyeneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvad enenāva runddhe ‖42‖
(9.6.43’) sa ya evaṃ vidvān māṃsam upasicyopaharati |
(9.6.43.c) yāvad dvādaśāheneṣṭvā susamṛddhenāvarunddhe tāvad enenāva runddhe ‖43‖
(9.6.44’) sa ya evaṃ vidvān udakam upasicyopaharati |
(9.6.44.c) prajānāṃ prajananāya gachati pratiṣṭhāṃ priyah prajānāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ vidvān upasicyopaharati ‖44‖ {18
}
(9.6.45’) tasmā uṣā hiṅ kṛṇoti savitā pra stauti |
(9.6.45.c) bṛhaspatir ūrjayod gāyati tvaṣṭā puṣṭyā prati harati viśve devā nidhanam ‖2‖
(9.6.45.e) nidhanaṃ bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖45‖
(9.6.46’) tasmā udyant sūryo hiṅ kṛṇoti saṃgavaḥ pra stauti |
(9.6.46.c) madhyandina ud gāyaty aparāhṇaḥ prati haraty astaṃyan nidhanam |
(9.6.46.e) nidhanaṃ bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖46‖
(9.6.47’) tasmā abhro bhavan hiṅ kṛṇoti stanayan pra stauti |
(9.6.47.c) vidyotamānaḥ prati harati varṣann ud gāyaty udgṛhṇan nidhanam |
(9.6.47.e) nidhanaṃ bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda ‖47‖
(9.6.48’) atithīn prati paśyati hiṅ kṛṇoty abhi vadati pra stauty udakam yācaty ud gāyati |
(9.6.48.c) upa harati prati haraty ucchiṣṭaṃ nidhanam |
(9.6.48.e) nidhanaṃ bhūtyāḥ prajāyāḥ paśūnāṃ bhavati ya evam veda ‖48‖ {19}
(9.6.48’) yat kṣattāraṃ hvayaty ā śrāvayaty eva tat ‖49‖
(9.6.50’) yat pratiśṛṇoti pratyāśrāvayaty eva tat ‖50‖
(9.6.51’) yat pariveṣṭāraḥ pātrahastāḥ pūrve cāpare ca prapadyante camasādhvaryava eva te ‖51‖
(9.6.52’) teṣāṃ na kaś canāhotā ‖52‖
(9.6.53’) yad vā atithipatir atithīn pariviṣya gṛhān upodaity avabhṛtham eva tad upāvaiti ‖53‖
(9.6.54’) yat sabhāgayati dakṣiṇāḥ sabhāgayati yad anutiṣṭhata udavasyaty eva tat ‖54‖
(9.6.55’) sa upahūtaḥ pṛthivyāṃ bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yat pṛthivyāṃ viśvarūpam ‖55‖
(9.6.56’) sa upahūto 'ntarikṣe bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yad divi viśvarūpam ‖56‖
(9.6.57’) sa upahūto divi bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yad divi viśvarūpam ‖57‖
(9.6.58’) sa upahūto deveṣu bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yad divi viśvarūpam ‖58‖
(9.6.59’) sa upahūto lokeṣu bhakṣayaty upahūtas tasmin yad divi viśvarūpam ‖59‖
(9.6.60’) sa upahūta upahūtaḥ ‖60‖
(9.6.61’) āpnotīmaṃ lokam āpnoty amum ‖61‖
(9.6.62’) jyotiṣmato lokān jayati ya evaṃ veda ‖62‖ {20}

(9.7.1’) prajāpatiś ca parameṣṭhī ca śṛṅge indraḥ śiro agnir lalāṭaṃ yamaḥ kṛkāṭam ‖1‖
(9.7.2’) somo rājā mastiṣko dyaur uttarahanuḥ pṛthivy adharahanuḥ ‖2‖
(9.7.3’) vidyuj jihvā maruto dantā revatir grīvāḥ kṛttikā skandhā gharmo vahaḥ ‖3‖
(9.7.4’) viśvaṃ vāyuḥ svargo lokaḥ kṛṣṇadraṃ vidharaṇī niveṣyaḥ ‖4‖
(9.7.5’) śyenaḥ kroto 'ntarikṣaṃ pājasyaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ kakud bṛhatīḥ kīkasāḥ ‖5‖
(9.7.6’) devānāṃ patnīḥ pṛṣṭaya upasadaḥ parśavaḥ ‖6‖
(9.7.7’) mitraś ca varuṇaś cāṃsau tvaṣṭā cāryamā ca doṣaṇī mahādevo bāhū ‖7‖
(9.7.8’) indrāṇī bhasad vāyuḥ puchaṃ pavamāno bālāḥ ‖8‖
(9.7.9’) brahma ca kṣatraṃ ca śroṇī balam ūrū ‖9‖
(9.7.10’) dhātā ca savitā cāṣṭhīvantau jaṅghā gandharvā apsarasaḥ kuṣṭhikā aditiḥ śaphāḥ ‖10‖
(9.7.11’) ceto hṛdayaṃ yakṛn medhā vrataṃ purītat ‖11‖
(9.7.12’) kṣut kukṣir irā vaniṣṭhuḥ parvatāḥ plāśayaḥ ‖12‖
(9.7.13’) krodho vṛkkau manyur āṇḍau prajā śepaḥ ‖13‖
(9.7.14’) nadī sūtrī varṣasya pataya stanā stanayitnur ūdhaḥ ‖14‖
(9.7.15’) viśvavyacās carmauṣadhayo lomāni nakṣatrāṇi rūpam ‖15‖
(9.7.16’) devajanā gudā manuṣyā āntrāṇy atrā udaram ‖16‖
(9.7.17’) rakṣāṃsi lohitam itarajanā ūbadhyam ‖17‖
(9.7.18’) abhraṃ pībo majjā nidhanam ‖18‖
(9.7.19’) agnir āsīna utthito 'śvinā ‖19‖
(9.7.20’) indraḥ prāṅ tiṣṭhan dakṣiṇā tiṣṭhan yamaḥ ‖20‖
(9.7.21’) pratyaṅ tiṣṭhan dhātodaṅ tiṣṭhant savitā ‖21‖
(9.7.22’) tṛṇāmi prāptaḥ somo rājā ‖22‖
(9.7.23’) mitra īkṣamāṇa āvṛtta ānandaḥ ‖23‖
(9.7.24’) yujyamāno vaiśvadevo yuktaḥ prajāpatir vimuktaḥ sarvam ‖24‖
(9.7.25’) etad vai viśvarūpaṃ sarvarūpaṃ gorūpam ‖25‖
(9.7.26’) upainaṃ viśvarūpāḥ sarvarūpāḥ paśavas tiṣṭhanti ya evaṃ veda ‖26‖ {21}

(9.8.1’) śīrṣaktiṃ śīrṣāmayaṃ karṇaśūlaṃ vilohitam |
(9.8.1.c) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖1‖
(9.8.2’) karṇābhyāṃ te kaṅkūṣebhyaḥ karṇaśūlaṃ visalpakam |
(9.8.2.c) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖2‖
(9.8.3’) yasya hetoḥ pracyavate yakṣmaḥ karṇato āsyataḥ |
(9.8.3.c) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖3‖
(9.8.4’) yaḥ kṛṇoti pramotam andhaṃ kṛṇoti pūruṣam |
(9.8.4.c) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖4‖
(9.8.5’) aṅgabhedam aṅgajvaram viśvāṅgyaṃ visalpakam |
(9.8.5.c) sarvaṃ śīrṣanyaṃ te rogaṃ bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖5‖
(9.8.6’) yasya bhīmaḥ pratīkāśa udvepayati pūruṣam |
(9.8.6.c) takmānaṃ viśvaśāradaṃ bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖6‖
(9.8.7’) ya ūrū anusarpaty atho eti gavīnike |
(9.8.7.c) yakṣmaṃ te antar aṅgebhyo bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖7‖
(9.8.8’) yadi kāmād apakāmād dhṛdayāj jāyate pari |
(9.8.8.c) hṛdo balāsam aṅgebhyo bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖8‖
(9.8.9’) harimāṇaṃ te aṅgebhyo 'pvām antarodarāt |
(9.8.9.c) yakṣmodhām antar ātmano bahir nir mantrayāmahe ‖9‖
(9.8.10’) āso balāso bhavatu mūtraṃ bhavatv āmayat |
(9.8.10.c) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ nir avocam ahaṃ tvat ‖10‖ {22}
(9.8.11’) bahir bilaṃ nir dravatu kāhābāhaṃ tavodarāt |
(9.8.11.c) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ nir avocam ahaṃ tvat ‖11‖
(9.8.12’) udarāt te klomno nābhyā hṛdayād adhi |
(9.8.12.c) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ nir avocam ahaṃ tvat ‖12‖
(9.8.13’) yāḥ sīmānaṃ virujanti mūrdhānaṃ praty arṣanīḥ |
(9.8.13.c) ahiṃsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam ‖13‖
(9.8.14’) yā hṛdayam uparṣanty anutanvanti kīkasāḥ |
(9.8.14.c) ahiṃsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam ‖14‖
(9.8.15’) yāḥ pārśve uparṣanty anunikṣanti pṛṣṭīḥ |
(9.8.15.c) ahiṃsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam ‖15‖
(9.8.16’) yās tiraścīḥ uparṣanty arṣaṇīr vakṣaṇāsu te |
(9.8.16.c) ahiṃsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam ‖16‖
(9.8.17’) yā gudā anusarpanty āntrāṇi mohayanti ca |
(9.8.17.c) ahiṃsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam ‖17‖
(9.8.18’) yā majjño nirdhayanti parūṃṣi virujanti ca |
(9.8.18.c) ahiṃsantīr anāmayā nir dravantu bahir bilam ‖18‖
(9.8.19’) ye aṅgāni madayanti yakṣmāso ropaṇās tava |
(9.8.19.c) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ nir avocam ahaṃ tvat ‖19‖
(9.8.20’) visalpasya vidradhasya vātīkārasya vālajeḥ |
(9.8.20.c) yakṣmāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ nir avocam ahaṃ tvat ‖20‖
(9.8.21’) pādābhyāṃ te jānubhyāṃ śroṇibhyāṃ pari bhaṃsasaḥ |
(9.8.21.c) anūkād arṣaṇīr uṣṇihābhyaḥ śīrṣṇo rogam anīnaśam ‖21‖
(9.8.22’) saṃ te śīrṣṇaḥ kapālāni hṛdayasya ca yo vidhuḥ |
(9.8.22.c) udyann āditya raśmibhiḥ śīrṣṇo rogam anīnaśo 'ṅgabhedam aśīśamaḥ ‖22‖ {23}

(9.9.1’) asya vāmasya palitasya hotus tasya bhrātā madhyamo asty aśnaḥ |
(9.9.1.c) tṛtīyo bhrātā ghṛtapṛṣṭho asyātrāpaśyaṃ viśpatiṃ saptaputram ‖1‖
(9.9.2’) sapta yuñjanti ratham ekacakram eko aśvo vahati saptanāmā |
(9.9.2.c) trinābhi cakram ajaram anarvaṃ yatremā viśvā bhuvanādhi tasthuḥ ‖2‖
(9.9.3’) imaṃ ratham adhi ye sapta tasthuḥ saptacakraṃ sapta vahanty aśvāḥ |
(9.9.3.c) sapta svasāro abhi saṃ navanta yatra gavām nihitā sapta nāma ‖3‖
(9.9.4’) ko dadarśa prathamaṃ jāyamānam asthanvantaṃ yad anasthā bibharti |
(9.9.4.c) bhūmyā asur asṛg ātmā kva svit ko vidvāṃsam upa gāt praṣṭum etat ‖4‖
(9.9.5’) iha bravītu ya īm aṅga vedāsya vāmasya nihitaṃ padaṃ veḥ |
(9.9.5.c) śīrṣṇaḥ kṣīraṃ duhrate gāvo asya vavriṃ vasānā udakaṃ padā 'puḥ ‖5‖
(9.9.6’) pākaḥ pṛchāmi manasā 'vijānan devānām enā nihitā padāni |
(9.9.6.c) vatse baṣkaye 'dhi sapta tantūn vi tatnire kavaya otavā u ‖6‖
(9.9.7’) acikitvāṃs cikituṣaś cid atra kavīn pṛchāmi vidvano na vidvān |
(9.9.7.c) vi yas tastambha ṣaṭ imā rajāṃsy ajasya rūpe kiṃ api svid ekam ‖7‖
(9.9.8’) mātā pitaram ṛta ā babhāja 'dhīty agre manasā saṃ hi jagme |
(9.9.8.c) sā bībhatsur garbharasā nividdhā namasvanta id upavākam īyuḥ ‖8‖
(9.9.9’) yuktā mātāsid dhuri dakṣiṇāyā atiṣṭhad garbho vṛjanīṣv antaḥ |
(9.9.9.c) amīmed vatso anu gām apaśyad viśvarūpyaṃ triṣu yoganeṣu ‖9‖
(9.9.10’) tisro matṝs trīn pitṝn bibhrad eka urdhvas tasthau nem ava glāpayanta |
(9.9.10.c) mantrayante divo amuṣya pṛṣṭhe viśvavido vācam aviśvavinnām ‖10‖ {24}
(9.9.11’) pañcāre cakre parivartamāne yasminn ātasthur bhuvanāni viśvā |
(9.9.11.c) tasya nākṣas tapyate bhūribhāraḥ sanād eva na chidyate sanābhiḥ ‖11‖
(9.9.12’) pañcapādaṃ pitaraṃ dvādaśākṛtiṃ diva āhuḥ pare ardhe purīṣiṇam |
(9.9.12.c) atheme anya upare vicakṣaṇe saptacakre ṣaḍara āhur arpitam ‖12‖
(9.9.13’) dvādaśāraṃ nahi taj jarāya varvarti cakraṃ pari dyām ṛtasya |
(9.9.13.c) ā putrā agne mithunāso atra sapta śatāni viṃśatiś ca tasthuḥ ‖13‖
(9.9.14’) sanemi cakram ajaraṃ vi vavṛta uttānāyāṃ daśa yuktā vahanti |
(9.9.14.c) sūryasya cakṣū rajasaity āvṛtaṃ yasminn ātasthur bhuvanāni viśvā ‖14‖
(9.9.15’) striyaḥ satīs tām u me puṃsaḥ āhuḥ paśyad akṣaṇvānn vi cetad andhaḥ |
(9.9.15.c) kavir yaḥ putraḥ sa īm ā ciketa yas tā vijānāt sa pituṣ pitāsat ‖15‖
(9.9.16’) sākaṃjānāṃ saptatham āhur ekajaṃ ṣaḍ id yamā ṛṣayo devajā iti |
(9.9.16.c) teṣām iṣṭāni vihitāni dhāmaśa sthātre rejante vikṛtāni rūpaśaḥ ‖16‖
(9.9.17’) avaḥ pareṇa para enā avareṇa padā vatsaṃ bibhratī gaur ud asthāt |
(9.9.17.c) sā kadrīcī kaṃ svid ardhaṃ parāgāt kva svit sūte nahi yūthe asmin ‖17‖
(9.9.18’) avaḥ pareṇa pitaraṃ yo asya vedāvaḥ pareṇa para enāvareṇa |
(9.9.18.c) kavīyamānaḥ ka iha pra vocad devaṃ manaḥ kuto adhi prajātam ‖18‖
(9.9.19’) ye arvāñcas tām u parāca āhur ye parāñcas tā;m u arvāca āhuḥ |
(9.9.19.c) indraś ca yā cakrathuḥ soma tāni dhurā na yuktā rajaso vahanti ‖19‖
(9.9.20’) dvā suparṇā sayujā sakhāyā samānaṃ vṛkṣaṃ pari ṣasvajāte |
(9.9.20.c) tayor anyaḥ pippalaṃ svādv atty anaśnann anyo abhi cākaśīti ‖20‖
(9.9.21’) yasmin vṛkṣe madhvadaḥ suparṇā niviśante suvate cādhi viśve |
(9.9.21.c) tasya yad āhuḥ pippalaṃ svādv agre tan non naśad yaḥ pitaraṃ na veda ‖21‖
(9.9.22’) yatrā suparṇā amṛtasya bhakṣam animeṣaṃ vidathābhisvaranti |
(9.9.22.c) enā viśvasya bhuvanasya gopāḥ sa mā dhīraḥ pākam atrā viveśa ‖22‖ {25}

(9.10.1’) yad gāyatre adhi gāyatram āhitaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ vā traiṣṭubhān niratakṣata |
(9.10.1.c) yad vā jagaj jagaty āhitaṃ padaṃ ya it tad vidus te amṛtatvam ānuśuḥ ‖1‖
(9.10.2’) gāyatreṇa prati mimīte arkam arkeṇa sāma traiṣṭubhena vākam |
(9.10.2.c) vākena vākaṃ dvipadā catuṣpadākṣareṇa mimate sapta vāṇīḥ ‖2‖
(9.10.3’) jagatā sindhuṃ divy askabhāyad rathaṃtare sūryaṃ pary apaśyat |
(9.10.3.c) gāyatrasya samidhas tisra āhus tato mahnā pra ririce mahitvā ‖3‖
(9.10.4’) upa hvaye sudughāṃ dhenum etāṃ suhasto godhug uta dohad enām |
(9.10.4.c) śreṣṭhaṃ savaṃ savitā sāviṣan no 'bhīddho gharmas tad u ṣu pra vocat ‖4‖
(9.10.5’) hiṅkṛṇvatī vasupatnī vasūnāṃ vatsam ichantī manasābhyāgāt |
(9.10.5.c) duhām aśvibhyāṃ payo aghnyeyaṃ sā vardhatāṃ mahate saubhagāya ‖5‖
(9.10.6’) gaur amīmed abhi vatsaṃ miṣantaṃ mūrdhānaṃ hiṅṅ akṛṇon mātavā u |
(9.10.6.c) sṛkvāṇaṃ gharmam abhi vāvaśānā mimāti māyuṃ payate payobhiḥ ‖6‖
(9.10.7’) ayaṃ sa śiṅkte yena gaur abhivṛtā mimāti mayuṃ dhvasanāv adhi śritā |
(9.10.7.c) sā cittibhir ni hi cakāra martyān vidyud bhavantī prati vavrim auhata ‖7‖
(9.10.8’) anac chaye turagātu jīvam ejad dhruvaṃ madhya ā pastyānām |
(9.10.8.c) jīvo mṛtasya carati svadhābhir amartyo martyenā sayoniḥ ‖8‖
(9.10.9’) vidhuṃ dadrāṇaṃ salilasya pṛṣṭhe yuvānaṃ santaṃ palito jagāra |
(9.10.9.c) devasya paśya kāvyaṃ mahitvādya mamāra sa hyaḥ sam āna ‖9‖
(9.10.10’) ya īṃ cakāra na so asya veda ya īṃ dadarśa hirug in nu tasmāt |
(9.10.10.c) sa mātur yonā parivīto antar bahuprajā nirṛtir ā viveśa ‖10‖ {26}
(9.10.11’) apaśyaṃ gopām anipadyamānam ā ca parā ca pathibhiś carantam |
(9.10.11.c) sa sadhrīcīḥ sa viṣūcīr vasāna ā varīvarti bhuvaneṣv antaḥ ‖11‖
(9.10.12’) dyaur naḥ pitā janitā nābhir atra bandhur no mātā pṛthivī mahīyam |
(9.10.12.c) uttānayoś camvor yonir antar atrā pitā duhitur garbham ādhāt ‖12‖
(9.10.13’) pṛchāmi tvā param antaṃ pṛthivyāḥ pṛchāmi vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ |
(9.10.13.c) pṛchāmi viśvasya bhuvanasya nābhiṃ pṛchāmi vācaḥ paramaṃ vyoma ‖13‖
(9.10.14’) iyaṃ vediḥ paro antaḥ pṛthivyā ayaṃ somo vṛṣṇo aśvasya retaḥ |
(9.10.14.c) ayaṃ yajño viśvasya bhuvanasya nābhir brahmāyaṃ vācaḥ paramaṃ vyoma ‖14‖
(9.10.15’) na vi jānāmi yad ivedam asmi niṇyaḥ saṃnaddho manasā carāmi |
(9.10.15.c) yadā māgan prathamajā ṛtasyād id vāco aśnuve bhāgam asyāḥ ‖15‖
(9.10.16’) apāṅ prāṅ eti svadhayā gṛbhīto 'martyo martyenā sayoniḥ |
(9.10.16.c) tā śaśvantā viṣūcīnā viyantā ny anyaṃ cikyur na ni cikyur anyam ‖16‖
(9.10.17’) saptārdhagarbhā bhuvanasya reto viṣṇos tiṣṭhanti pradiśā vidharmaṇi |
(9.10.17.c) te dhītibhir manasā te vipaścitaḥ paribhuvaḥ pari bhavanti viśvataḥ ‖17‖
(9.10.18’) ṛco akṣare parame vyoman yasmin devā adhi viśve niṣeduḥ |
(9.10.18.c) yas tan na veda kim ṛcā kariṣyati ya it tad vidus te amī sam āsate ‖18‖
(9.10.19’) ṛcaḥ padaṃ mātrayā kalpayanto 'rdharcena cakḷpur viśvam ejat |
(9.10.19.c) tripād brahma pururūpaṃ vi taṣṭhe tena jīvanti pradiśaś catasraḥ ‖19‖
(9.10.20’) sūyavasād bhagavatī hi bhūyā adhā vayaṃ bhagavantaḥ syāma |
(9.10.20.c) addhi tṛṇam aghnye viśvadānīṃ piba śuddham udakam ācarantī ‖20‖ {27}
(9.10.21’) gaur in mimāya salilāni takṣatī ekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadī |
(9.10.21.c) aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī sahasrākṣarā bhuvanasya paṅktis tasyāḥ samudrā adhi vi kṣaranti ‖21‖
(9.10.22’) kṛṣṇaṃ niyānaṃ harayaḥ suparṇā apo vasānā divam ut patanti |
(9.10.22.c) taṃ āvavṛtrant sadanād ṛtasyād id ghṛtena pṛthivīṃ vy ūduḥ ‖22‖
(9.10.23’) apād eti prathamā padvatīnāṃ kas tad vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā ciketa |
(9.10.23.c) garbho bhāraṃ bharaty ā cid asyā ṛtaṃ piparti anṛtaṃ ni pāti ‖23‖
(9.10.24’) virāḍ vāg virāṭ pṛthivī virāḍ antarikṣaṃ virāṭ prajāpatiḥ |
(9.10.24.c) virāṇ mṛtyuḥ sādhyānām adhirājo babhūva tasya bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ vaśe sa me bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ vaśe kṛṇot
u ‖24‖
(9.10.25’) śakamayaṃ dhūmam ārād apaśyaṃ viṣūvatā para enāvareṇa |
(9.10.25.c) ukṣāṇaṃ pṛśnim apacanta vīrās tāni dharmāṇi prathamāny āsan ‖25‖
(9.10.26’) trayaḥ keśina ṛtuthā vi cakṣate saṃvatsare vapata eka eṣām |
(9.10.26.c) viśvam anyo abhicaṣṭe śacībhir dhrājir ekasya dadṛśe na rūpam ‖26‖
(9.10.27’) catvāri vāk parimitā padāni tāni vidur brāhmaṇā ye manīṣiṇaḥ |
(9.10.27.c) guhā trīṇi nihitā neṅgayanti turīyaṃ vāco manuṣyā vadanti ‖27‖
(9.10.28’) indraṃ mitraṃ varuṇam agnim āhur atho divyaḥ sa suparṇo garutmān |
(9.10.28.c) ekaṃ sad viprā bahudhā vadanty agniṃ yamaṃ mātariśvānam āhuḥ ‖28‖ {28}

(10.1.1’) yāṃ kalpayanti vahatau vadhūm iva viśvarūpāṃ hastakṛtāṃ cikitsavaḥ |
(10.1.1.c) sārād etv apa nudāma enām ‖1‖
(10.1.2’) śīrṣaṇvatī nasvatī karṇiṇī kṛtyākṛtā saṃbhṛtā viśvarūpā |
(10.1.2.c) sārād etv apa nudāma enām ‖2‖
(10.1.3’) śūdrakṛtā rājakṛtā strīkṛtā brahmabhiḥ kṛtā |
(10.1.3.c) jāyā patyā nutteva kartāraṃ bandhv ṛchatu ‖3‖
(10.1.4’) anayāham oṣadhyā sarvāḥ kṛtyā adūduṣam |
(10.1.4.c) yāṃ kṣetre cakrur yāṃ goṣu yāṃ vā te puruṣeṣu ‖4‖
(10.1.5’) agham astv aghakṛte śapathaḥ śapathīyate |
(10.1.5.c) pratyak pratiprahiṇmo yathā kṛtyākṛtaṃ hanat ‖5‖
(10.1.6’) pratīcīna āṅgiraso 'dhyakṣo naḥ purohitaḥ |
(10.1.6.c) pratīcīḥ kṛtyā ākṛtyāmūn kṛtyākṛto jahi ‖6‖
(10.1.7’) yas tvovāca parehīti pratikūlam udāyyam |
(10.1.7.c) taṃ kṛtye 'bhinivartasva māsmān icho anāgasaḥ ‖7‖
(10.1.8’) yas te parūṃṣi saṃdadhau rathasyeva rbhur dhiyā |
(10.1.8.c) taṃ gacha tatra te 'yanam ajñātas te 'yaṃ janaḥ ‖8‖
(10.1.9’) ye tvā kṛtvālebhire vidvalā abhicāriṇaḥ |
(10.1.9.c) śaṃbhv idaṃ kṛtyādūṣaṇaṃ prativartma punaḥsaraṃ tena tvā snapayāmasi ‖9‖
(10.1.10’) yad durbhagāṃ prasnapitāṃ mṛtavatsām upeyima |
(10.1.10.c) apaitu sarvaṃ mat pāpaṃ draviṇaṃ mopa tiṣṭhatu ‖10‖ {1}
(10.1.11’) yat te pitṛbhyo dadato yajñe vā nāma jagṛhuḥ |
(10.1.11.c) saṃdeśyāt sarvasmāt pāpād imā muñcantu tvauṣadhīḥ ‖11‖
(10.1.12’) devainasāt pitryān nāmagrāhāt saṃdeśyād abhiniṣkṛtāt |
(10.1.12.c) muñcantu tvā vīrudho vīryeṇa brahmaṇā ṛgbhiḥ payasā ṛṣīṇām ‖12‖
(10.1.13’) yathā vātaś cyāvayati bhūmyā reṇum antarikṣāc cābhram |
(10.1.13.c) evā mat sarvaṃ durbhūtaṃ brahmanuttam apāyati ‖13‖
(10.1.14’) apa krāma nānadatī vinaddhā gardabhīva |
(10.1.14.c) kartṝn nakṣasveto nuttā brahmaṇā vīryāvatā ‖14‖
(10.1.15’) ayaṃ panthāḥ kṛtyeti tvā nayāmo 'bhiprahitāṃ prati tvā pra hiṇmaḥ |
(10.1.15.c) tenābhi yāhi bhañjaty anasvatīva vāhinī viśvarūpā kurūtinī ‖15‖
(10.1.16’) parāk te jyotir apathaṃ te arvāg anyatrāsmad ayanā kṛṇuṣva |
(10.1.16.c) pareṇehi navatiṃ nāvyā ati durgāḥ srotyā mā kṣaṇiṣṭhāḥ parehi ‖16‖
(10.1.17’) vāta iva vṛkṣān ni mṛṇīhi pādaya mā gām aśvaṃ puruṣam uc chiṣa eṣām |
(10.1.17.c) kartṝn nivṛtyetaḥ kṛtye 'prajāstvāya bodhaya ‖17‖
(10.1.18’) yāṃ te barhiṣi yāṃ śmaśāne kṣetre kṛtyāṃ valagaṃ vā nicakhnuḥ |
(10.1.18.c) agnau vā tvā gārhapatye 'bhiceruḥ pākaṃ santaṃ dhīratarā anāgasam ‖18‖
(10.1.19’) upāhṛtam anubuddhaṃ nikhātaṃ vairaṃ tsāry anv avidāma kartram |
(10.1.19.c) tad etu yata ābhṛtaṃ tatrāśva iva vi vartatāṃ hantu kṛtyākṛtaḥ prajām ‖19‖
(10.1.20’) svāyasā asayaḥ santi no gṛhe vidmā te kṛtye yatidhā parūṃṣi |
(10.1.20.c) ut tiṣṭhaiva parehīto 'jñāte kim ihechasi ‖20‖ {2}
(10.1.21’) grīvās te kṛtye pādau cāpi kartsyāmi nir drava |
(10.1.21.c) indrāgnī asmān rakṣatāṃ yau prajānāṃ prajāvatī ‖21‖
(10.1.22’) somo rājādhipā mṛḍitā ca bhūtasya naḥ patayo mṛḍayantu ‖22‖
(10.1.23’) bhavāśarvāv asyatāṃ pāpakṛte kṛtyākṛte |
(10.1.23.c) duṣkṛte vidyutaṃ devahetim ‖23‖
(10.1.24’) yady eyatha dvipadī catuṣpadī kṛtyākṛtā saṃbhṛtā viśvarūpā |
(10.1.24.c) seto 'ṣṭāpadī bhūtvā punaḥ parehi duchune ‖24‖
(10.1.25’) abhyaktāktā svaraṃkṛtā sarvaṃ bharantī duritaṃ parehi |
(10.1.25.c) jānīhi kṛtye kartāraṃ duhiteva pitaraṃ svam ‖25‖
(10.1.26’) parehi kṛtye mā tiṣṭho viddhasyeva padaṃ naya |
(10.1.26.c) mṛgaḥ sa mṛgayus tvaṃ na tvā nikartum arhati ‖26‖
(10.1.27’) uta hanti pūrvāsinaṃ pratyādāyāpara iṣvā |
(10.1.27.c) uta pūrvasya nighnato ni hanty aparaḥ prati ‖27‖
(10.1.28’) etad dhi śṛṇu me vaco 'thehi yata eyatha |
(10.1.28.c) yas tvā cakāra taṃ prati ‖28‖
(10.1.29’) anāgohatyā vai bhīmā kṛtye mā no gām aśvaṃ puruṣaṃ vadhīḥ |
(10.1.29.c) yatrayatrāsi nihitā tatas tvot thāpayāmasi parṇāl laghīyasī bhava ‖29‖
(10.1.30’) yadi stha tamasāvṛtā jālenabhihitā iva |
(10.1.30.c) sarvāḥ saṃlupyetaḥ kṛtyāḥ punaḥ kartre pra hiṇmasi ‖30‖
(10.1.31’) kṛtyākṛto valagino 'bhiniṣkāriṇaḥ prajām |
(10.1.31.c) mṛṇīhi kṛtye moc chiṣo 'mūn kṛtyākṛto jahi ‖31‖
(10.1.32’) yathā sūryo mucyate tamasas pari rātriṃ jahāty uṣasaśca ketūn |
(10.1.32.c) evāhaṃ sarvaṃ durbhūtaṃ kartraṃ kṛtyākṛtā kṛtaṃ hastīva rajo duritaṃ jahāmi ‖32‖ {3}

(10.2.1’) kena pārṣṇī ābhṛte pūruṣasya kena māṃsaṃ saṃbhṛtaṃ kena gulphau |
(10.2.1.c) kenāṅgulīḥ peśanīḥ kena khāni kenocchlaṅkhau madhyataḥ kaḥ pratiṣṭhām ‖1‖
(10.2.2’) kasmān nu gulphāv adharāv akṛṇvann aṣṭhīvantāv uttarau puruṣasya |
(10.2.2.c) jaṅghe nirṛtya ny adadhuḥ kva svij jānunoḥ saṃdhī ka u tac ciketa ‖2‖
(10.2.3’) catuṣṭayaṃ yujate saṃhitāntaṃ jānubhyām ūrdhvaṃ śithiraṃ kabandham |
(10.2.3.c) śroṇī yad ūrū ka u taj jajāna yābhyāṃ kusindhaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ babhūva ‖3‖
(10.2.4’) kati devāḥ katame ta āsan ya uro grīvāś cikyuḥ puruṣasya |
(10.2.4.c) kati stanau vy adadhuḥ kaḥ kaphodau kati skandhān kati pṛṣṭīr acinvan ‖4‖
(10.2.5’) ko asya bāhū sam abharad vīryāṃ karavād iti |

(10.2.6’) kaḥ sapta khāni vi tatarda śīrṣaṇi karṇāv imau nāsike cakṣaṇī mukham |
(10.2.6.c) yeṣāṃ purutrā vijayasya mahnani catuṣpādo dvipado yanti yāmam ‖6‖
(10.2.7’) hanvor hi jihvām adadhāt purūcīm adhā mahīm adhi śiśrāya vācam |

(10.2.8’) mastiṣkam asya yatamo lalātaṃ kakāṭikāṃ prathamo yaḥ kapālam |
(10.2.8.c) citvā cityaṃ hanvoḥ pūruṣasya divaṃ ruroha katamaḥ sa devaḥ ‖8‖
(10.2.9’) priyāpriyāṇi bahulā svapnaṃ saṃbādhatandyaḥ |
(10.2.9.c) ānandān ugro nandāṃś ca kasmād vahati pūruṣaḥ ‖9‖
(10.2.10’) ārtir avartir nirṛtiḥ kuto nu puruṣe 'matiḥ |
(10.2.10.c) rāddhiḥ samṛddhir avyṛddhir matir uditayaḥ kutaḥ ‖10‖ {4}
(10.2.11’) ko asminn āpo vy adadhāt viṣūvṛtaḥ purūvṛtaḥ sindhusṛtyāya jātāḥ |
(10.2.11.c) tīvrā aruṇā lohinīs tāmradhūmrā ūrdhvā avācīḥ puruṣe tiraścīḥ ‖11‖
(10.2.12’) ko asmin rūpam adadhāt ko mahmānaṃ ca nāma ca |
(10.2.12.c) gātuṃ ko asmin kaḥ ketuṃ kaś caritrāni puruṣe ‖12‖
(10.2.13’) ko asmin prāṇaṃ avayat ko apānaṃ vyānam u |

(10.2.14’) ko asmin yajñam adadhād eko devo 'dhi puruṣe |
(10.2.14.c) ko asmint satyaṃ ko 'nṛtaṃ kuto mṛtyuḥ kuto 'mṛtam ‖14‖
(10.2.15’) ko asmai vāsaḥ pary adadhāt ko asyāyur akalpayat |
(10.2.15.c) balaṃ ko asmai prāyachat ko asyākalpayaj javam ‖15‖
(10.2.16’) kenāpo anv atanuta kenāhar akarod ruce |

(10.2.18’) kenemāṃ bhūmim aurṇot kena pary abhavad divam |

(10.2.19’) kena parjanyam anv eti kena somaṃ vicakṣaṇam |

(10.2.21.c) brahmemam agniṃ pūruṣo brahma saṃvatsaraṃ mame ‖21‖
(10.2.22’) kena devā;m anu kṣiyati kena daivajanīr viśaḥ |

(10.2.23’) brahma devā;m anu kṣiyati brahma daivajanīr viśaḥ |

(10.2.32’) tasmin hiraṇyaye kośe tryare tripratiṣṭhite |

(10.3.1’) ayaṃ me varaṇo maṇiḥ sapatnakṣayaṇo vṛṣā |
(10.3.1.c) tenā rabhasva tvaṃ śatrūn pra mṛṇīhi durasyataḥ ‖1‖
(10.3.2’) praiṇān chṛṇīhi pra mṛṇā rabhasva maṇis te astu puraetā purastāt |
(10.3.2.c) avārayanta varaṇena devā abhyācāram asurāṇāṃ śvaḥśvaḥ ‖2‖
(10.3.3’) ayaṃ maṇir varaṇo viśvabheṣajaḥ sahasrākṣo harito hiraṇyayaḥ |
(10.3.3.c) sa te śatrūn adharān pādayāti pūrvas tān dabhnuhi ye tvā dviṣanti ‖3‖
(10.3.4’) ayaṃ te kṛtyāṃ vitatām pauruṣeyād ayaṃ bhayāt |
(10.3.4.c) ayaṃ tvā sarvasmāt pāpād varaṇo vārayiṣyate ‖4‖
(10.3.5’) varaṇo vārayātā ayaṃ devo vanaspatiḥ |
(10.3.5.c) yakṣmo yo asminn āviṣṭas tam u devā avīvaran ‖5‖
(10.3.6’) svapnaṃ suptvā yadi paśyāsi pāpaṃ mṛgaḥ sṛtiṃ yati dhāvād ajuṣṭām |
(10.3.6.c) parikṣavāc chakuneḥ pāpavādād ayaṃ maṇir varaṇo vārayiṣyate ‖6‖
(10.3.7’) arātyās tvā nirṛtyā abhicārād atho bhayāt |
(10.3.7.c) mṛtyor ojīyaso vadhād varaṇo vārayiṣyate ‖7‖
(10.3.8’) yan me mātā yan me pitā bhrātaro yac ca me svā yad enaś cakṛmā vayam |
(10.3.8.c) tato no vārayiṣyate 'yaṃ devo vanaspatiḥ ‖8‖
(10.3.9’) varaṇena pravyathitā bhrātṛvyā me sabandhavaḥ |
(10.3.9.c) asūrtaṃ rajo apy agus te yantv adhamaṃ tamaḥ ‖9‖
(10.3.10’) ariṣṭo 'ham ariṣṭagur āyuṣmānt sarvapūruṣaḥ |
(10.3.10.c) tam māyaṃ varaṇo maṇiḥ pari pātu diśodiśaḥ ‖10‖ {7}
(10.3.11’) ayaṃ me varaṇa urasi rājā devo vanaspatiḥ |
(10.3.11.c) sa me śatrūn vi bādhatām indro dasyūn ivāsurān ‖11‖
(10.3.12’) imaṃ bibharmi varaṇam āyuṣmān chataśāradaḥ |
(10.3.12.c) sa me rāṣṭraṃ ca kṣatraṃ ca paśūn ojaś ca me dadhat ‖12‖
(10.3.13’) yathā vāto vanaspatīn vṛkṣān bhanakty ojasā |
(10.3.13.c) evā sapatnān me bhaṅgdhi pūrvān jātā;m utāparān varaṇas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖13‖
(10.3.14’) yathā vātaś cāgniś ca vṛkṣān psāto vanaspatīn |
(10.3.14.c) evā sapatnān me psāhi pūrvān jātā;m utāparān varaṇas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖14‖
(10.3.15’) yathā vātena prakṣīṇā vṛkṣāḥ śere nyarpitāḥ |
(10.3.15.c) evā sapatnāṃs tvaṃ mama pra kṣiṇīhi ny arpaya |
(10.3.15.e) pūrvān jātā;m utāparān varaṇas tvābhi rakṣatu ‖15‖
(10.3.16’) tāṃs tvaṃ pra chinddhi varaṇa purā diṣṭāt purāyuṣaḥ |
(10.3.16.c) ya enaṃ paśuṣu dipsanti ye cāsya rāṣṭradipsavaḥ ‖16‖
(10.3.17’) yathā sūryo atibhāti yathāsmin teja āhitam |
(10.3.17.c) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10.3.17.e) tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā ‖17‖
(10.3.18’) yathā yaśaś candramasy āditye ca nṛcakṣasi |
(10.3.18.c) evā me varaṇo maṇiḥ kīrtiṃ bhūtiṃ ni yachatu |
(10.3.18.e) tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā ‖18‖
(10.3.19’) yathā yaśaḥ pṛthivyāṃ yathāsmin jātavedasi |

(10.3.19.e) tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā ‖19‖
(10.3.20’) yathā yaśaḥ kanyāyāṃ yathāsmint saṃbhṛte rathe |

(10.3.20.e) tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā ‖20‖ {8}

(10.3.21.e) tejasā mā sam ukṣatu yaśasā sam anaktu mā ‖21‖

(10.4.1’) indrasya prathamo ratho devānām aparo ratho varuṇasya tṛtīya it |
(10.4.1.c) ahīnām apamā ratha sthānum ārad athārṣat ‖1‖
(10.4.2’) darbhaḥ śocis tarūṇakam aśvasya vāraḥ paruṣasya vāraḥ |
(10.4.2.c) rathasya bandhuram ‖2‖
(10.4.3’) ava śveta padā jahi pūrveṇa cāpareṇa ca |
(10.4.3.c) udaplutam iva dārv ahīnām arasaṃ viṣaṃ vār ugram ‖3‖
(10.4.4’) araṃghuṣo nimajyonmaja punar abravīt |
(10.4.4.c) udaplutam iva dārv ahīnām arasaṃ viṣaṃ vār ugram ‖4‖
(10.4.5’) paidvo hanti kasarṇīlaṃ paidvaḥ śvitram utāsitam |
(10.4.5.c) paidvo ratharvyāḥ śiraḥ saṃ bibheda pṛdākvāḥ ‖5‖
(10.4.6’) paidva prehi prathamo 'nu tvā vayam emasi |
(10.4.6.c) ahīn vy asyatāt patho yena smā vayam emasi ‖6‖
(10.4.7’) idaṃ paidvo ajāyatedam asya parāyaṇam |
(10.4.7.c) imāny arvataḥ padāhighnyo vājinīvataḥ ‖7‖
(10.4.8’) saṃyataṃ na vi ṣparad vyāttaṃ na saṃ yamat |
(10.4.8.c) asmin kṣetre dvāv ahī strī ca pumāṃś ca tāv ubhāv arasā ‖8‖
(10.4.9’) arasāsa ihāhayo ye anti ye ca dūrake |
(10.4.9.c) ghanena hanmi vṛścikam ahiṃ daṇḍenāgatam ‖9‖
(10.4.10’) aghāśvasyedaṃ bheṣajam ubhayo svajasya ca |
(10.4.10.c) indro me 'him aghāyantam ahiṃ paidvo arandhayat ‖10‖ {10}
(10.4.11’) paidvasya manmahe vayaṃ sthirasya sthiradhāmnaḥ |
(10.4.11.c) ime paścā pṛdākavaḥ pradīdhyata āsate ‖11‖
(10.4.12’) naṣṭāsavo naṣṭaviṣā hatā indreṇa vajriṇā |
(10.4.12.c) jaghānendro jaghnimā vayam ‖12‖
(10.4.13’) hatās tiraścirājayo nipiṣṭāsaḥ pṛdākavaḥ |
(10.4.13.c) darviṃ karikrataṃ śvitraṃ darbheṣv asitaṃ jahi ‖13‖
(10.4.14’) kairātikā kumārikā sakā khanati bheṣajam |
(10.4.14.c) hiraṇyayībhir abhribhir girīnām upa sānuṣu ‖14‖
(10.4.15’) āyam agan yuvā bhiṣak pṛśnihāparājitaḥ |
(10.4.15.c) sa vai svajasya jambhana ubhayor vṛścikasya ca ‖15‖
(10.4.16’) indro me 'him arandhayan mitraś ca varuṇaś ca |
(10.4.16.c) vātāparjanyobhā ‖16‖
(10.4.17’) indro me 'him arandhayat pṛdākuṃ ca pṛdākvam |
(10.4.17.c) svajaṃ tiraścirājiṃ kasarṇīlaṃ daśonasim ‖17‖
(10.4.18’) indro jaghāna prathamaṃ janitāram ahe tava |
(10.4.18.c) teṣām u tṛhyamāṇānāṃ kaḥ svit teṣām asad rasaḥ ‖18‖
(10.4.19’) saṃ hi śīrṣāṇy agrabhaṃ pauñjiṣṭha iva karvaram |
(10.4.19.c) sindhor madhyaṃ paretya vy anijam aher viṣam ‖19‖
(10.4.20’) ahīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣaṃ parā vahantu sindhavaḥ |
(10.4.20.c) hatās tiraścirājayo nipiṣṭāsaḥ pṛdākavaḥ ‖20‖ {11}
(10.4.21’) oṣadhīnām ahaṃ vṛṇa urvarīr iva sādhuyā |
(10.4.21.c) nayāmy arvatīr ivāhe niraitu viṣam ‖21‖
(10.4.22’) yad agnau sūrye viṣaṃ pṛthivyām oṣadhīṣu yat |
(10.4.22.c) kāndāviṣaṃ kanaknakaṃ niraitv aitu te viṣam ‖22‖
(10.4.23’) ye agnijā oṣadhijā ahīnāṃ ye apsujā vidyuta ābabhūvuḥ |
(10.4.23.c) yeṣāṃ jātāni bahudhā mahānti tebhyaḥ sarpebhyo namasā vidhema ‖23‖
(10.4.24’) taudī nāmāsi kanyā ghṛtācī nāma vā asi |
(10.4.24.c) adhaspadena te padam ā dade viṣadūṣaṇam ‖24‖
(10.4.25’) aṅgādaṅgāt pra cyāvaya hṛdayam pari varjaya |
(10.4.25.c) adhā viṣasya yat tejo 'vācīnaṃ tad etu te ‖25‖
