(10.5.44’) rājño varuṇasya bandho 'si |
(10.5.44.c) so 'mum āmuṣyāyaṇam amuṣyāḥ putram anne prāṇe badhāna ‖44‖
(10.5.45’) yat te annaṃ bhuvas pata ākṣiyati pṛthivīm anu |
(10.5.45.c) tasya nas tvaṃ bhuvas pate saṃprayacha prajāpate ‖45‖
(10.5.46’) apo divyā acāyiṣaṃ rasena sam apṛkṣmahi |
(10.5.46.c) payasvān agna āgamaṃ taṃ mā saṃ sṛja varcasā ‖46‖
(10.5.47’) saṃ māgne varcasā sṛja saṃ prajayā sam āyuṣā |
(10.5.47.c) vidyur me asya devā indro vidyāt saha ṛṣibhiḥ ‖47‖
(10.5.48’) yad agne adya mithunā śapato yad vācas tṛṣṭaṃ janayanta rebhāḥ |
(10.5.48.c) manyor manasaḥ śaravyā jāyate yā tayā vidhya hṛdaye yātudhānān ‖48‖
(10.5.49’) parā śṛṇīhi tapasā yātudhānān parāgne rakṣo harasā śṛṇīhi |
(10.5.49.c) parārciṣā mūradevāṃ chṛṇīhi parāsutṛpaḥ śośucataḥ śṛṇīhi ‖49‖
(10.5.50’) apām asmai vajraṃ pra harāmi caturbhṛṣṭiṃ śīrṣabhidyāya vidvān |
(10.5.50.c) so asyāṅgāni pra śṛṇātu sarvā tan me devā anu jānantu viśve ‖50‖ {17}

(10.6.1’) arātīyor bhrātṛvyasya durhārdo dviṣataḥ śiraḥ |
(10.6.1.c) api vṛścāmy ojasā ‖1‖
(10.6.2’) varma mahyam ayaṃ maṇiḥ phālāj jātaḥ kariṣyati |
(10.6.2.c) pūrṇo manthena māgamad rasena saha varcasā ‖2‖
(10.6.3’) yat tvā śikvaḥ parāvadhīt takṣā hastena vāsyā |
(10.6.3.c) āpas tvā tasmaj jīvalāḥ punantu śucayaḥ śucim ‖3‖
(10.6.4’) hiraṇyasrag ayaṃ maṇiḥ śraddhāṃ yajñaṃ maho dadhat |
(10.6.4.c) gṛhe vasatu no 'tithiḥ ‖4‖
(10.6.5’) tasmai ghṛtaṃ suraṃ madhv annamannam kṣadāmahe |
(10.6.5.c) sa naḥ piteva putrebhyaḥ śreyaḥśreyaś cikitsatu bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvo devebhyo maṇir etya ‖5‖
(10.6.6’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutam ugraṃ kadhiram ojase |
(10.6.6.c) tam agniḥ praty amuñcata so asmai duha ājyaṃ bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖6‖
(10.6.7’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutam ugraṃ kadhiram ojase |
(10.6.7.c) tam indraḥ praty amuñcataujase vīryāya kam |
(10.6.7.e) so asmai balam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖7‖
(10.6.8’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutam ugram kadhiram ojase |
(10.6.8.c) taṃ somaḥ praty amuñcata mahe śrotrāya cakṣase |
(10.6.8.e) so asmai varca id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvam dviṣato jahi ‖8‖
(10.6.9’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutam ugraṃ khadiram ojase |
(10.6.9.c) taṃ sūryaḥ praty amuñcata tenemā ajayad diśaḥ |
(10.6.9.e) so asmai bhūtim id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖9‖
(10.6.10’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir maṇiṃ phālaṃ ghṛtaścutam ugram khadiram ojase |
(10.6.10.c) tam bibhrac candramā maṇim asurāṇāṃ puro 'jayad dānavānāṃ hiraṇyayīḥ |
(10.6.10.e) so asmai śriyam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖10‖ {18}
(10.6.11’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave |
(10.6.11.c) so asmai vājinam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖11‖
(10.6.12’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave |
(10.6.12.c) tenemāṃ maṇinā kṛṣim aśvināv abhi rakṣataḥ |
(10.6.12.e) sa bhiṣagbhyāṃ maho duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖12‖
(10.6.13’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave |
(10.6.13.c) tam bibhrat savitā maṇiṃ tenedam ajayat svaḥ |
(10.6.13.e) so asmai sūnṛtāṃ duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖13‖
(10.6.14’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave |
(10.6.14.c) tam āpo bibhratīr maṇiṃ sadā dhāvanty akṣitāḥ |
(10.6.14.e) sa ābhyo 'mṛtam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖14‖
(10.6.15’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave |
(10.6.15.c) tam rājā varuṇo maṇiṃ praty amuñcata śaṃbhuvam |
(10.6.15.e) so asmai satyam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖15‖
(10.6.16’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave |
(10.6.16.c) taṃ devā bibhrato maṇiṃ sarvāṃl lokān yudhājayan |
(10.6.16.e) sa ebhyo jitim id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖16‖
(10.6.17’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir vātāya maṇim āśave |
(10.6.17.c) tam imaṃ devatā maṇiṃ praty amuñcanta śambhuvam |
(10.6.17.e) sa ābhyo viśvam id duhe bhūyobhūyaḥ śvaḥśvas tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖17‖
(10.6.18’) ṛtavas tam abadhnatārtavās tam abadhnata |
(10.6.18.c) saṃvatsaras taṃ baddhvā sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ vi rakṣati ‖18‖
(10.6.19’) antardeśā abadhnata pradiśas tam abadhnata |
(10.6.19.c) prajāpatisṛṣṭo maṇir dviṣato me 'dharā;m akaḥ ‖19‖
(10.6.20’) atharvāṇo abadhnatātharvaṇā abadhnata |
(10.6.20.c) tair medino aṅgiraso dasyūnāṃ bibhiduḥ puras tena tvam dviṣato jahi ‖20‖ {19}
(10.6.21’) taṃ dhātā praty amuñcata sa bhūtaṃ vy akalpayat |
(10.6.21.c) tena tvaṃ dviṣato jahi ‖21‖
(10.6.22’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10.6.22.c) sa māyaṃ maṇir āgamad rasena saha varcasā ‖22‖
(10.6.23’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10.6.23.c) sa māyaṃ maṇir āgamat saha gobhir ajāvibhir annena prajayā saha ‖23‖
(10.6.24’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10.6.24.c) sa māyaṃ maṇir āgamat saha vrīhiyavābhyāṃ mahasā bhūtyā saha ‖24‖
(10.6.25’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10.6.25.c) sa māyaṃ maṇir āgaman madhor ghṛtasya dhārayā kīlālena maṇiḥ saha ‖25‖
(10.6.26’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10.6.26.c) sa māyaṃ maṇir āgamad ūrjayā payasā saha draviṇena śriyā saha ‖26‖
(10.6.27’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10.6.27.c) sa māyaṃ maṇir āgamat tejasā tviṣyā saha yaśasā kīrtyā saha ‖27‖
(10.6.28’) yam abadhnād bṛhaspatir devebhyo asurakṣitim |
(10.6.28.c) sa māyaṃ maṇir āgamat sarvābhir bhūtibhiḥ saha ‖28‖
(10.6.29’) tam imaṃ devatā maṇiṃ mahyaṃ dadatu puṣṭaye |
(10.6.29.c) abhibhuṃ kṣatravardhanaṃ sapatnadambhanaṃ maṇim ‖29‖
(10.6.30’) brahmaṇā tejasā saha prati muñcāmi me śivam |
(10.6.30.c) asapatnaḥ sapatnahā sapatnān me 'dharā;m akaḥ ‖30‖ {20}
(10.6.31’) uttaraṃ dviṣato mām ayaṃ maṇiḥ kṛṇotu devajāḥ |
(10.6.31.c) yasya lokā ime trayaḥ payo dugdham upāsate |
(10.6.31.e) sa māyam adhi rohatu maṇiḥ śraiṣṭhyāya mūrdhataḥ ‖31‖
(10.6.32’) yaṃ devāḥ pitaro manuṣyā upajīvanti sarvadā |
(10.6.32.c) sa māyam adhi rohatu maṇiḥ śraiṣṭhyāya mūrdhataḥ ‖32‖
(10.6.33’) yathā bījam urvarāyāṃ kṛṣṭe phālena rohati |
(10.6.33.c) evā mayi prajā paśavo 'nnamannaṃ vi rohatu ‖33‖
(10.6.34’) yasmai tvā yajñavardhana maṇe pratyamucaṃ śivam |
(10.6.34.c) taṃ tvaṃ śatadakṣiṇa maṇe śraiṣṭhyāya jinvatāt ‖34‖
(10.6.35’) etam idhmaṃ samāhitaṃ juṣaṇo agne prati harya homaiḥ |
(10.6.35.c) tasmin vidhema sumatiṃ svasti prajām cakṣuḥ paśūnt samiddhe jātavedasi brahmaṇā ‖35‖ {21}

(10.7.1’) kasminn aṅge tapo asyādhi tiṣṭhati kasminn aṅga ṛtam asyādhy āhitam |
(10.7.1.c) kva vrataṃ kva śraddhāsya tiṣṭhati kasminn aṅge satyam asya pratiṣṭhitam ‖1‖
(10.7.2’) kasmād aṅgād dīpyate agnir asya kasmād aṅgāt pavate mātariśva |
(10.7.2.c) kasmād aṅgād vi mimīte 'dhi candramā maha skambhasya mimāno aṅgam ‖2‖
(10.7.3’) kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhati bhūmir asya kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhaty antarikṣam |
(10.7.3.c) kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhaty āhitā dyauḥ kasminn aṅge tiṣṭhaty uttaraṃ divaḥ ‖3‖
(10.7.4’) kva prepsan dīpyata ūrdhvo agniḥ kva prepsan pavate mātariśvā |
(10.7.4.c) yatra prepsantīr abhiyanty āvṛtaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖4‖
(10.7.5’) kvārdhamāsāḥ kva yanti māsāḥ saṃvatsareṇa saha saṃvidānāḥ |
(10.7.5.c) yatra yanty ṛtavo yatrārtavāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖5‖
(10.7.6’) kva prepsantī yuvatī virūpe ahorātre dravataḥ saṃvidāne |
(10.7.6.c) yatra prepsantīr abhiyanty āpaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖6‖
(10.7.7’) yasmint stabdhvā prajāpatir lokānt sarvā;m adhārayat |
(10.7.7.c) skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖7‖
(10.7.8’) yat paramam avamam yac ca madhyamaṃ prajāpatiḥ sasṛje viśvarūpam |
(10.7.8.c) kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa tatra yan na prāviśat kiyat tad babhūva ‖8‖
(10.7.9’) kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa bhūtam kiyad bhaviṣyad anvāśaye 'sya |
(10.7.9.c) ekaṃ yad aṅgam akṛṇot sahasradhā kiyatā skambhaḥ pra viveśa tatra ‖9‖
(10.7.10’) yatra lokāmś ca kośāṃś cāpo brahma janā viduḥ |
(10.7.10.c) asac ca yatra sac cānta skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖10‖ {22}
(10.7.11’) yatra tapaḥ parākramya vrataṃ dhārayaty uttaram |
(10.7.11.c) ṛtaṃ ca yatra śraddhā cāpo brahma samāhitāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖11‖
(10.7.12’) yasmin bhūmir antarikṣaṃ dyaur yasminn adhy āhitā |
(10.7.12.c) yatrāgniś candramāḥ sūryo vātas tiṣṭhanty ārpitāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖12‖
(10.7.13’) yasya trayastriṃśad devā aṅge sarve samāhitāḥ |
(10.7.13.c) skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖13‖
(10.7.14’) yatra ṛṣayaḥ prathamajā ṛcaḥ sāma yajur mahī |
(10.7.14.c) ekarṣir yasminn ārpitaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖14‖
(10.7.15’) yatrāmṛtaṃ ca mṛtyuś ca puruṣe 'dhi samāhite |
(10.7.15.c) samudro yasya nāḍyaḥ puruṣe 'dhi samāhitāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖15‖
(10.7.16’) yasya catasraḥ pradiśo nāḍyas tiṣṭhanti prathamāḥ |
(10.7.16.c) yajño yatra parākrāntaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖16‖
(10.7.17’) ye puruṣe brahma vidus te viduḥ parameṣṭhinam |
(10.7.17.c) yo veda parameṣṭhinaṃ yaś ca veda prajāpatim |
(10.7.17.e) jyeṣṭhaṃ ye brāhmaṇaṃ vidus te skambham anusaṃviduḥ ‖17‖
(10.7.18’) yasya śiro vaiśvānaraś cakṣur aṅgiraso 'bhavan |
(10.7.18.c) aṅgāni yasya yātavaḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖18‖
(10.7.19’) yasya brahma mukham āhur jihvāṃ madhukaśām uta |
(10.7.19.c) virājam ūdho yasyāhuḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖19‖
(10.7.20’) yasmād ṛco apātakṣan yajur yasmād apākaṣan |
(10.7.20.c) sāmāni yasya lomāny atharvāṅgiraso mukhaṃ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖20‖ {23}
(10.7.21’) asaccākhāṃ pratiṣṭhantīṃ paramam iva janā viduḥ |
(10.7.21.c) uto san manyante 'vare ye te śākhām upāsate ‖21‖
(10.7.22’) yatrādityāś ca rudrāś ca vasavaś ca samāhitāḥ |
(10.7.22.c) bhūtaṃ ca yatra bhavyaṃ ca sarve lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖22‖
(10.7.23’) yasya trayastriṃśad devā nidhiṃ rakṣanti sarvadā |
(10.7.23.c) nidhiṃ tam adya ko veda yaṃ devā abhirakṣatha ‖23‖
(10.7.24’) yatra devā brahmavido brahma jyeṣṭham upāsate |
(10.7.24.c) yo vai tān vidyāt pratyakṣaṃ sa brahmā veditā syāt ‖24‖
(10.7.25’) bṛhanto nāma te devā ye 'sataḥ pari jajñire |
(10.7.25.c) ekaṃ tad aṅgaṃ skambhasyāsad āhuḥ paro janāḥ ‖25‖
(10.7.26’) yatra skambhaḥ prajanayan purāṇaṃ vyavartayat |
(10.7.26.c) ekaṃ tad aṅgaṃ skambhasya purāṇam anusaṃviduḥ ‖26‖
(10.7.27’) yasya trayastriṃśad devā aṅge gātrā vibhejire |
(10.7.27.c) tān vai trayastriṃśad devān eke brahamvido viduḥ ‖27‖
(10.7.28’) hiraṇyagarbham paramam anatyudyaṃ janā viduḥ |
(10.7.28.c) skambhas tad agre prāsiñcad dhiraṇyaṃ loke antarā ‖28‖
(10.7.29’) skambhe lokāḥ skambhe tapaḥ skambhe 'dhy ṛtam āhitam |
(10.7.29.c) skambha tvā veda pratyakṣam indre sarvaṃ samāhitam ‖29‖
(10.7.30’) indre lokā indre tapa indre 'dhy ṛtam āhitam |
(10.7.30.c) indraṃ tvā veda pratyakṣaṃ skambhe sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ‖30‖ {24}
(10.7.31’) nāma nāmnā johavīti purā sūryāt puroṣasaḥ |
(10.7.31.c) yad ajaḥ prathamaṃ saṃbabhūva sa ha tat svarājyam iyāya yasmān nānyat param asti bhūtam ‖31‖
(10.7.32’) yasya bhūmiḥ pramāntarikṣam utodaram |
(10.7.32.c) divaṃ yaś cakre mūrdhānaṃ tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ‖32‖
(10.7.33’) yasya sūryaś cakṣuś candramāś ca punarṇavaḥ |
(10.7.33.c) agniṃ yaś cakra āsyaṃ tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ‖33‖
(10.7.34’) yasya vātaḥ prāṇāpānau cakṣur aṅgiraso 'bhavan |
(10.7.34.c) diśo yaś cakre prajñānīs tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ‖34‖
(10.7.35’) skambho dādhāra dyāvāpṛthivī ubhe ime skambho dādhārorv antarikṣam |
(10.7.35.c) skambho dādhāra pradiśaḥ ṣaḍ urvīḥ skambha idaṃ viśvaṃ bhuvanam ā viveśa ‖35‖
(10.7.36’) yaḥ śramāt tapaso jāto lokānt sarvānt samānaśe |
(10.7.36.c) somaṃ yaś cakre kevalaṃ tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ‖36‖
(10.7.37’) kathaṃ vāto nelayati kathaṃ na ramate manaḥ |
(10.7.37.c) kim āpaḥ satyaṃ prepsantīr nelayanti kadā cana ‖37‖
(10.7.38’) mahad yakṣaṃ bhuvanasya madhye tapasi krāntaṃ salilasya pṛṣṭhe |
(10.7.38.c) tasmin chrayante ya u ke ca devā vṛkṣasya skandhaḥ parita iva śākhāḥ ‖38‖
(10.7.39’) yasmai hastābhyāṃ pādābhyāṃ vācā śrotreṇa cakṣuṣā |
(10.7.39.c) yasmai devāḥ sadā baliṃ prayachanti vimite 'mitaṃ skambhaṃ taṃ brūhi katamaḥ svid eva saḥ ‖39‖
(10.7.40’) apa tasya hataṃ tamo vyāvṛttaḥ sa pāpmanā |
(10.7.40.c) sarvāṇi tasmin jyotīṃṣi yāni trīṇi prajāpatau ‖40‖
(10.7.41’) yo vetasaṃ hiraṇyayaṃ tiṣṭhantaṃ salile veda |
(10.7.41.c) sa vai guhyaḥ prajāpatiḥ ‖41‖
(10.7.42’) tantram eke yuvatī virūpe abhyākrāmaṃ vayataḥ ṣaṇmayūkham |
(10.7.42.c) prānyā tantūṃs tirate dhatte anyā nāpa vṛñjāte na gamāto antam ‖42‖
(10.7.43’) tayor ahaṃ parinṛtyantyor iva na vi jānāmi yatarā parastāt |
(10.7.43.c) pumān enad vayaty ud gṛṇanti pumān enad vi jabhārādhi nāke ‖43‖
(10.7.44’) ime mayūkhā upa tastabhur divaṃ sāmāni cakrus tasarāṇi vātave ‖44‖ {25}

(10.8.1’) yo bhūtaṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca sarvaṃ yaś cādhitiṣṭhati |
(10.8.1.c) sv aryasya ca kevalaṃ tasmai jyeṣṭhāya brahmaṇe namaḥ ‖1‖
(10.8.2’) skambheneme viṣṭabhite dyauś ca bhūmiś ca tiṣṭhataḥ |
(10.8.2.c) skambha idaṃ sarvam ātmanvad yat prāṇan nimiṣac ca yat ‖2‖
(10.8.3’) tisro ha prajā atyāyam āyan ny anyā arkam abhito 'viśanta |
(10.8.3.c) bṛhan ha tasthau rajaso vimāno harito hariṇīr ā viveśa ‖3‖
(10.8.4’) dvādaśa pradhayaś cakram ekaṃ trīṇi nabhyāni ka u tac ciketa |
(10.8.4.c) tatrāhatās trīṇi śatāni śaṅkavaḥ ṣaṣṭiś ca khīlā avicācalā ye ‖4‖
(10.8.5’) idaṃ savitar vi jānīhi ṣaḍ yamā eka ekajaḥ |
(10.8.5.c) tasmin hāpitvam ichante ya eṣām eka ekajaḥ ‖5‖
(10.8.6’) āviḥ san nihitaṃ guhā jaran nāma mahat padam |
(10.8.6.c) tatredaṃ sarvam ārpitam ejat prāṇat pratiṣṭhitam ‖6‖
(10.8.7’) ekacakraṃ vartata ekanemi sahasrākṣaraṃ pra puro ni paścā |
(10.8.7.c) ardhena viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ jajāna yad asyārdhaṃ kva tad babhūva ‖7‖
(10.8.8’) pañcavāhī vahatyagram eṣāṃ praṣṭayo yuktā anusaṃvahanti |
(10.8.8.c) ayātam asya dadṛśe na yātaṃ paraṃ nedīyo 'varaṃ davīyaḥ ‖8‖
(10.8.9’) tiryagbilaś camasa ūrdhvabudhnas tasmin yaśo nihitaṃ viśvarūpam |
(10.8.9.c) tad āsata ṛṣayaḥ sapta sākaṃ ye asya gopā mahato babhūvuḥ ‖9‖
(10.8.10’) yā purastād yujyate yā ca paścād yā viśvato yujyate yā ca sarvataḥ |
(10.8.10.c) yayā yajñaḥ prāṅ tāyate tāṃ tvā pṛchāmi katamā sā ṛcām ‖10‖ {26}
(10.8.11’) yad ejati patati yac ca tiṣṭhati prāṇad aprāṇan nimiṣac ca yad bhuvat |
(10.8.11.c) tad dādhāra pṛthivīṃ viśvarūpaṃ tat saṃbhūya bhavaty ekam eva ‖11‖
(10.8.12’) anantaṃ vitataṃ purutrānantam antavac cā samante |
(10.8.12.c) te nākapālaś carati vicinvan vidvān bhūtam uta bhavyam asya ‖12‖
(10.8.13’) prajāpatiś carati garbhe antar adṛśyamāno bahudhā vi jāyate |
(10.8.13.c) ardhena viśvaṃ bhuvanaṃ jajāna yad asyārdhaṃ katamaḥ sa ketuḥ ‖13‖
(10.8.14’) ūrdhvaṃ bharantam udakaṃ kumbhenevodahāryam |
(10.8.14.c) paśyanti sarve cakṣuṣā na sarve manasā viduḥ ‖14‖
(10.8.15’) dūre pūrṇena vasati dūra ūnena hīyate |
(10.8.15.c) mahad yakṣaṃ bhuvanasya madhye tasmai baliṃ rāṣṭrabhṛto bharanti ‖15‖
(10.8.16’) yataḥ sūryaḥ udety astaṃ yatra ca gachati |
(10.8.16.c) tad eva manye 'haṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ tad u nāty eti kiṃ cana ‖16‖
(10.8.17’) ye arvāṅ madhya uta vā purāṇaṃ vedaṃ vidvāṃsam abhito vadanti |
(10.8.17.c) ādityam eva te pari vadanti sarve agniṃ dvitīyaṃ trivṛtaṃ ca haṃsam ‖17‖
(10.8.18’) sahasrāhṇyaṃ viyatāv asya pakṣau harer haṃsasya patataḥ svargam |
(10.8.18.c) sa devānt sarvān urasy upadadya saṃpaśyan yāti bhuvanāni viśvā ‖18‖
(10.8.19’) satyenordhvas tapati brahmaṇārvāṅ vi paśyati |
(10.8.19.c) prāṇena tiryaṅ prāṇati yasmin jyeṣṭham adhi śritam ‖19‖
(10.8.20’) yo vai te vidyād araṇī yābhyāṃ nirmathyate vasu |
(10.8.20.c) sa vidvān jyeṣṭhaṃ manyeta sa vidyād brāhmaṇaṃ mahat ‖20‖ {27}
(10.8.21’) apād agre sam abhavat so agre svar ābharat |
(10.8.21.c) catuṣpād bhūtvā bhogyaḥ sarvam ādatta bhojanam ‖21‖
(10.8.22’) bhogyo bhavad atho annam adad bahu |
(10.8.22.c) yo devam uttarāvantam upāsātai sanātanam ‖22‖
(10.8.23’) sanātanam enam āhur utādya syāt punarṇavaḥ |
(10.8.23.c) ahorātre pra jāyete anyo anyasya rūpayoḥ ‖23‖
(10.8.24’) śataṃ sahasram ayutaṃ nyarbudam asaṃkhyeyaṃ svam asmin niviṣṭam |
(10.8.24.c) tad asya ghnanty abhipaśyata eva tasmād devo rocat eṣa etat ‖24‖
(10.8.25’) bālād ekam aṇīyaskam utaikaṃ neva dṛśyate |
(10.8.25.c) tataḥ pariṣvajīyasī devatā sā mama priyā ‖25‖
(10.8.26’) iyaṃ kalyāṇy ajarā martyasyāmṛtā gṛhe |
(10.8.26.c) yasmai kṛtā śaye sa yaś cakāra jajāra saḥ ‖26‖
(10.8.27’) tvaṃ strī tvaṃ pumān asi tvaṃ kumāra uta vā kumārī‖
(10.8.27.b) tvaṃ jīrṇo daṇḍena vañcasi tvaṃ jāto bhavasi viśvatomukhaḥ ‖27‖
(10.8.28’) utaiṣāṃ pitota vā putra eṣām utaiṣāṃ jyeṣṭha uta vā kaniṣṭhah |
(10.8.28.c) eko ha devo manasi praviṣṭaḥ prathamo jātaḥ sa u garbhe antah ‖28‖
(10.8.29’) pūrṇāt pūrṇam ud acati pūrṇaṃ pūrṇena sicyate |
(10.8.29.c) uto tad adya vidyāma yatas tat pariṣicyate ‖29‖
(10.8.30’) eṣā sanatnī sanam eva jātaiṣā purāṇī pari sarvaṃ babhūva |
(10.8.30.c) mahī devy uṣaso vibhātī saikenaikena miṣatā vi caṣṭe ‖30‖ {28}
(10.8.31’) avir vai nāma devatartenāste parīvṛtā |
(10.8.31.c) tasyā rūpeṇeme vṛkṣā haritā haritasrajaḥ ‖31‖
(10.8.32’) anti santaṃ na jahāty anti santaṃ na paśyati |
(10.8.32.c) devasya paśya kāvyaṃ na mamāra na jīryati ‖32‖
(10.8.33’) apūrveṇeṣitā vācas tā vadanti yathāyatham |
(10.8.33.c) vadantīr yatra gachanti tad āhur brāhmaṇaṃ mahat ‖33‖
(10.8.34’) yatra devāś ca manuṣyāś cārā nābhāv iva śritāḥ |
(10.8.34.c) apāṃ tvā puṣpaṃ pṛchāmi yatra tan māyayā hitam ‖34‖
(10.8.35’) yebhir vāta iṣitaḥ pravāti ye dadante pañca diśaḥ sadhrīcīḥ |
(10.8.35.c) ya āhutim atyamanyanta devā apāṃ netāraḥ katame ta āsan ‖35‖
(10.8.36’) imām eṣāṃ pṛthivīṃ vasta eko 'ntarikṣaṃ pary eko babhūva |
(10.8.36.c) divam eṣāṃ dadate yo vidhartā viśvā āśāḥ prati rakṣanty eke ‖36‖
(10.8.37’) yo vidyāt sūtraṃ vitataṃ yasminn otāḥ prajā imāḥ |
(10.8.37.c) sūtraṃ sūtrasya yo vidyād sa vidyād brāhmaṇaṃ mahat ‖37‖
(10.8.38’) vedāhaṃ sūtraṃ vitataṃ yasminn otāḥ prajā imāḥ |
(10.8.38.c) sūtraṃ sūtrasyāhaṃ vedātho yad brāhmaṇaṃ mahad ‖38‖
(10.8.39’) yad antarā dyāvāpṛthivī agnir ait pradahan viśvadāvyaḥ |
(10.8.39.c) yatrātiṣṭhann ekapatnīḥ parastāt kvevāsīn mātariśvā tadānīm ‖39‖
(10.8.40’) apsv āsīn mātariśvā praviṣṭaḥ praviṣṭā devāḥ salilāny āsan‖
(10.8.40.b) bṛhan ha tasthau rajaso vimānaḥ pavamāno harita ā viveśa ‖40‖
(10.8.41’) uttareṇeva gayatrīm amṛte 'dhi vi cakrame |
(10.8.41.c) sāmnā ye sāma saṃvidur ajas tad dadṛśe kva ‖41‖
(10.8.42’) niveśanaḥ saṃgamano vasūnāṃ deva iva savitā satyadharmā |
(10.8.42.c) indro na tasthau samare dhanānām ‖42‖
(10.8.43’) puṇḍarīkaṃ navadvāraṃ tribhir guṇebhir āvṛtam |
(10.8.43.c) tasmin yad yakṣam ātmanvat tad vai brahmavido viduh ‖43‖
(10.8.44’) akāmo dhīro amṛtaḥ svayaṃbhū rasena tṛpto na kutaś canonaḥ |
(10.8.44.c) tam eva vidvān na bibhāya mṛtyor ātmānaṃ dhīram ajaraṃ yuvānam ‖44‖ {29}

(10.9.1’) aghāyatām api nahyā mukhāni sapatneṣu vajram arpayaitam |
(10.9.1.c) indreṇa dattā prathamā śataudanā bhrātṛvyaghnī yajamānasya gātuḥ ‖1‖
(10.9.2’) vediṣ ṭe carma bhavatu barhir lomāni yāni te |
(10.9.2.c) eṣā tvā raśanāgrabhīd grāvā tvaiṣo 'dhi nṛtyatu ‖2‖
(10.9.3’) bālās te prokṣaṇīḥ santu jīhvā saṃ mārṣṭu aghnye |
(10.9.3.c) śuddhā tvaṃ yajñiyā bhūtvā divaṃ prehi śataudane ‖3‖
(10.9.4’) yaḥ śataudanāṃ pacati kāmapreṇa sa kalpate |
(10.9.4.c) prītā hy asya ṛtvijaḥ sarve yanti yathāyatham ‖4‖
(10.9.5’) sa svargam ā rohati yatrādas tridivaṃ divaḥ |
(10.9.5.c) apūpanābhiṃ kṛtvā yo dadāti śataudanām ‖5‖
(10.9.6’) sa tāṃl lokānt sam āpnoti ye divyā ye ca pārthivāḥ |
(10.9.6.c) hiraṇyajyotiṣaṃ kṛtvā yo dadāti śataudanām ‖6‖
(10.9.7’) ye te devi śamitāraḥ paktāro ye ca te janāḥ |
(10.9.7.c) te tvā sarve gopsyanti maibhyo bhaiṣīḥ śataudane ‖7‖
(10.9.8’) vasavas tvā dakṣiṇata uttarān marutas tvā |
(10.9.8.c) ādityāḥ paścād gopsyanti sāgniṣṭomam ati drava ‖8‖
(10.9.9’) devāḥ pitaro manuṣyā gandharvāpsarasaś ca ye |
(10.9.9.c) te tvā sarve gopsyanti sātirātram ati drava ‖9‖
(10.9.10’) antarikṣaṃ divaṃ bhūmim ādityān maruto diśaḥ |
(10.9.10.c) lokānt sa sarvān āpnoti yo dadāti śataudanām ‖10‖ {30}
(10.9.11’) ghṛtaṃ prokṣantī subhagā devī devān gamiṣyati |
(10.9.11.c) paktāram aghnye mā hiṃsīr divaṃ prehi śataudane ‖11‖
(10.9.12’) ye devā diviṣado antarikṣasadaś ca ye ye ceme bhūmyām adhi |
(10.9.12.c) tebhyas tvaṃ dhukṣva sarvadā kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖12‖
(10.9.13’) yat te śiro yat te mukhaṃ yau karṇau ye ca te hanū |
(10.9.13.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖13‖
(10.9.14’) yau ta oṣṭhau ye nāsike ye śṛṅge ye ca te 'kṣiṇī |
(10.9.14.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖14‖
(10.9.15’) yat te klomā yad dhṛdayaṃ purītat sahakaṇṭhikā |
(10.9.15.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖15‖
(10.9.16’) yat te yakṛd ye matasne yad āntram yāś ca te gudāḥ |
(10.9.16.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖16‖
(10.9.17’) yas te plāśir yo vaniṣṭhur yau kukṣī yac ca carma te |
(10.9.17.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖17‖
(10.9.18’) yat te majjā yad asthi yan maṃsaṃ yac ca lohitam |
(10.9.18.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖18‖
(10.9.19’) yau te bāhū ye doṣaṇī yāv aṃsau yā ca te kakut |
(10.9.19.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖19‖
(10.9.20’) yās te grīvā ye skandhā yāḥ pṛṣṭīr yāś ca parśavaḥ |
(10.9.20.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖20‖ {31}
(10.9.21’) yau ta urū aṣṭhīvantau ye śroṇī yā ca te bhasat |
(10.9.21.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖21‖
(10.9.22’) yat te puchaṃ ye te bālā yad ūdho ye ca te stanāḥ |
(10.9.22.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖22‖
(10.9.23’) yās te jaṅghāḥ yāḥ kuṣṭhikā ṛcharā ye ca te śaphāḥ |
(10.9.23.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖23‖
(10.9.24’) yat te carma śataudane yāni lomāny aghnye |
(10.9.24.c) āmikṣāṃ duhratāṃ dātre kṣīraṃ sarpir atho madhu ‖24‖
(10.9.25’) kroḍau te stāṃ purodāśāv ājyenābhighāritau |
(10.9.25.c) tau pakṣau devi kṛtvā sā paktāraṃ divaṃ vaha ‖25‖
(10.9.26’) ulūkhale musale yaś ca carmaṇi yo vā śūrpe taṇḍulaḥ kaṇaḥ |
(10.9.26.c) yaṃ vā vāto mātariśvā pavamāno mamāthāgniṣ ṭad dhotā suhutaṃ kṛṇotu ‖26‖
(10.9.27’) apo devīr madhumatīr ghṛtaścuto brahmaṇāṃ hasteṣu prapṛthak sādayāmi |
(10.9.27.c) yatkāma idam abhiṣiñcāmi vo 'haṃ tan me sarvaṃ saṃ padyatāṃ vayaṃ syāma patayo rayīṇām ‖27‖ {32}

(10.10.1’) namas te jāyamānāyai jātāyā uta te namaḥ |
(10.10.1.c) bālebhyaḥ śaphebhyo rūpāyāghnye te namaḥ ‖1‖
(10.10.2’) yo vidyāt sapta pravataḥ sapta vidyāt parāvataḥ |
(10.10.2.c) śiro yajñasya yo vidyāt sa vaśāṃ prati gṛhṇīyāt ‖2‖
(10.10.3’) vedāhaṃ sapta pravataḥ sapta veda parāvataḥ |
(10.10.3.c) śiro yajñasyāhaṃ veda somaṃ cāsyāṃ vicakṣaṇam ‖3‖
(10.10.4’) yayā dyaur yayā pṛthivī yayāpo gupitā imāḥ |
(10.10.4.c) vaśāṃ sahasradhārāṃ brahmaṇāchāvadāmasi ‖4‖
(10.10.5’) śataṃ kaṃsāḥ śataṃ dogdhāraḥ śataṃ goptāro adhi pṛṣṭhe asyāḥ |
(10.10.5.c) ye devās tasyāṃ prāṇanti te vaśāṃ vidur ekadhā ‖5‖
(10.10.6’) yajñapadīrākṣīrā svadhāprāṇā mahīlukā |
(10.10.6.c) vaśā parjanyapatnī devā;m apy eti brahmaṇā ‖6‖
(10.10.7’) anu tvāgniḥ prāviśad anu somo vaśe tvā |
(10.10.7.c) ūdhas te bhadre parjanyo vidyutas te stanā vaśe ‖7‖
(10.10.8’) apas tvaṃ dhukṣe prathamā urvarā aparā vaśe |
(10.10.8.c) tṛtīyaṃ rāṣṭraṃ dhukṣe 'nnaṃ kṣīraṃ vaśe tvam ‖8‖
(10.10.9’) yad ādityair hūyamānopātiṣṭha ṛtavari |
(10.10.9.c) indraḥ sahasraṃ pātrānt somaṃ tvāpāyayad vaśe ‖9‖
(10.10.10’) yad anūcīndram air āt tvā ṛṣabho 'hvayat |
(10.10.10.c) tasmāt te vṛtrahā payaḥ kṣīraṃ kruddho 'harad vaśe ‖10‖ {33}
(10.10.11’) yat te kruddho dhanapatir ā kṣīram aharad vaśe |
(10.10.11.c) idaṃ tad adya nākas triṣu pātreṣu rakṣati ‖11‖
(10.10.12’) triṣu pātreṣu taṃ somam ā devy aharad vaśā |
(10.10.12.c) atharvā yatra dīkṣito barhiṣy āsta hiraṇyaye ‖12‖
(10.10.13’) saṃ hi somenāgata sam u sarveṇa padvatā |
(10.10.13.c) vaśā samudram adhy aṣṭhad gandharvaiḥ kalibhiḥ saha ‖13‖
(10.10.14’) saṃ hi vātenāgata sam u sarvaiḥ patatribhiḥ |
(10.10.14.c) vaśā samudre prānṛtyad ṛcaḥ sāmāni bibhratī ‖14‖
(10.10.15’) saṃ hi sūryeṇāgata sam u sarveṇa cakṣuṣā |
(10.10.15.c) vaśā samudram aty akhyad bhadrā jyotīṃṣi bibhratī ‖15‖
(10.10.16’) abhīvṛtā hiraṇyena yad atiṣṭha ṛtāvari |
(10.10.16.c) aśvaḥ samudro bhūtvādhy askandad vaśe tvā ‖16‖
(10.10.17’) tad bhadrāḥ sam agachanta vaśā deṣṭry atho svadhā |
(10.10.17.c) atharvā yatra dīkṣito barhiṣy āsta hiraṇyaye ‖17‖
(10.10.18’) vaśā mātā rājanyasya vaśā mātā svadhe tava |
(10.10.18.c) vaśāyā yajña āyudhaṃ tataś cittam ajāyata ‖18‖
(10.10.19’) ūrdhvo bindur ud acarad brahmaṇaḥ kakudād adhi |
(10.10.19.c) tatas tvaṃ jajñiṣe vaśe tato hotājāyata ‖19‖
(10.10.20’) āsnas te gāthā abhavann uṣṇihābhyo balaṃ vaśe |
(10.10.20.c) pājasyāj jajñe yajña stanebhyo raśmayas tava ‖20‖ {34}
(10.10.21’) īrmābhyām ayanaṃ jātaṃ sakthibhyāṃ ca vaśe tava |
(10.10.21.c) āntrebhyo jajñire atrā udarād adhi vīrudhaḥ ‖21‖
(10.10.22’) yad udaraṃ varuṇasyānuprāviśathā vaśe |
(10.10.22.c) tatas tvā brahmod ahvayat sa hi netram avet tava ‖22‖
(10.10.23’) sarve garbhād avepanta jāyamānād asūsvaḥ |
(10.10.23.c) sasūva hi tām āhu vaśeti brahmabhiḥ kḷptaḥ sa hy asyā bandhuḥ ‖23‖
(10.10.24’) yudha ekaḥ saṃ sṛjati yo asyā eka id vaśī |
(10.10.24.c) tarāṃsi yajñā abhavan tarasāṃ cakṣur abhavad vaśā ‖24‖
(10.10.25’) vaśā yajñaṃ praty agṛhṇād vaśā sūryam adhārayat |
(10.10.25.c) vaśāyām antar aviśad odano brahmaṇā saha ‖25‖
(10.10.26’) vaśām evāmṛtam āhur vaśāṃ mṛtyum upāsate |
(10.10.26.c) vaśedaṃ sarvam abhavad devā manuṣyā asurāḥ pitara ṛṣayaḥ ‖26‖
(10.10.27’) ya evaṃ vidyāt sa vaśāṃ prati gṛhṇīyāt |
(10.10.27.c) tathā hi yajñaḥ sarvapād duhe dātre 'napasphuran ‖27‖
(10.10.28’) tisro jihvā varuṇasyāntar dīdyaty āsani |
(10.10.28.c) tāsāṃ yā madhye rājati sā vaśā duṣpratigrahā ‖28‖
(10.10.29’) caturdhā reto abhavad vaśāyāḥ |
(10.10.29.c) āpas turīyam amṛtaṃ turīyaṃ yajñas turīyaṃ paśavas turīyam ‖29‖
(10.10.30’) vaśā dyaur vaśā pṛthivī vaśā viṣṇuḥ prajāpatiḥ |
(10.10.30.c) vaśāyā dugdham apibant sādhyā vasavaś ca ye ‖30‖
(10.10.31’) vaśāyā dugdhaṃ pītvā sādhyā vasavaś ca ye |
(10.10.31.c) te vai bradhnasya viṣṭapi payo asyā upāsate  ‖31‖
(10.10.32’) somam enām eke duhre ghṛtam eka upāsate |
(10.10.32.c) ya evaṃ viduṣe vaśāṃ dadus te gatās tridivaṃ divaḥ ‖32‖
(10.10.33’) brāhmaṇebhyo vaśāṃ dattvā sarvāṃl lokānt sam aśnute |
(10.10.33.c) ṛtaṃ hy asyām ārpitam api brahmātho tapaḥ ‖33‖
(10.10.34’) vaśāṃ devā upa jīvanti vaśāṃ manuṣyā uta |
(10.10.34.c) vaśedaṃ sarvam abhavad yāvat sūryo vipaśyati ‖34‖ {35}
